### [1] [AdaGATE: Adaptive Gap-Aware Token-Efficient Evidence Assembly for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05245)
*Yilin Guo,Yinshan Wang,Yixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaGATE是一个无需训练的证据控制器，通过将证据选择建模为token约束修复问题，结合缺口追踪、微查询生成和效用选择机制，在多跳RAG场景中提升鲁棒性并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG控制器在多跳问题场景下存在局限性：仅加性扩展上下文、固定top-k选择，或优化相关性时不显式修复缺失的桥接事实，导致在噪声或冗余证据下鲁棒性不足。

Method: AdaGATE将证据选择建模为token约束修复问题，融合三组件：1) 实体中心缺口追踪；2) 定向微查询生成；3) 基于效用的选择机制（平衡缺口覆盖、佐证、新颖性、去冗余和直接问题相关性）。

Result: AdaGATE在HotpotQA三种检索条件下均达到最优证据F1：清洁数据62.3%、冗余注入71.2%；同时仅使用Adaptive-k的2.6倍少的输入token，实现效率与效果的双重提升。

Conclusion: AdaGATE通过显式缺口感知修复结合token高效证据选择，在不完美检索条件下提升了多跳RAG的鲁棒性，且显著降低了token消耗（比Adaptive-k少2.6倍输入token）。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) remains brittle on multi-hop questions in realistic deployment settings, where retrieved evidence may be noisy or redundant and only limited context can be passed to the generator. Existing controllers address parts of this problem, but typically either expand context additively, select from a fixed top-k set, or optimize relevance without explicitly repairing missing bridge facts. We propose AdaGATE, a training-free evidence controller for multi-hop RAG that frames evidence selection as a token-constrained repair problem. AdaGATE combines entity centric gap tracking, targeted micro-query generation, and a utility based selection mechanism that balances gap coverage, corroboration, novelty, redundancy, and direct question relevance. We evaluate AdaGATE on HotpotQA under clean, redundancy, and noise injected retrieval conditions. Across all three settings, AdaGATE achieves the best evidence F1 among the compared controllers, reaching 62.3% on clean data and 71.2% under redundancy injection, while using 2.6x fewer input tokens than Adaptive-k. These results suggest that explicit gap-aware repair, combined with token-efficient evidence selection, improves robustness in multi-hop RAG under imperfect retrieval. Our code and evaluation pipeline are available at https://github.com/eliguo/AdaGATE.

</details>


### [2] [Counterargument for Critical Thinking as Judged by AI and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05353)
*Tosin Adewumi,Marcus Liwicki,Foteini Simistira Liwicki,Lama Alkhaled,Hamam Mokayed,Esra Sümer-Arpak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A study with 36 university students found that writing counterarguments to AI-generated content effectively promotes critical thinking, and that frontier LLMs can serve as reliable assessors of student writing with strong alignment to human evaluations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rise of Generative AI (GenAI) in education presents both opportunities and risks, including concerns about cheating and cognitive offloading. Leveraging GenAI as an educational tool rather than merely a threat requires exploring how students can engage critically with AI-generated content. Specifically, using GenAI outputs as prompts for counterargument writing may foster critical thinking development.

Method: 36 university students wrote counterarguments to AI-generated content on 4 selected thesis statements. Submissions (n=35) were assessed using 6 rubrics (focus, logic, content, style, correctness, reference) on a 5-point Likert scale via two student peer-reviews, one experienced teacher, and six frontier LLMs. Mixed-method design combined qualitative feedback with quantitative analysis, using Gwets AC2 for inter-rater reliability.

Result: Results show that (1) students' self-written counterarguments to AI-generated content demonstrate logical reasoning—a key component of critical thinking; (2) frontier LLMs can successfully assess written work at scale with high alignment to human assessments, with Gwets AC2 inter-rater reliability values of 0.33 for most models.

Conclusion: GenAI can be effectively integrated into educational assessment workflows, with frontier LLMs demonstrating reliable inter-rater agreement comparable to human assessments. Students who generate counterarguments to AI content engage in meaningful critical thinking exercises, suggesting that GenAI tools can serve as both prompts for and evaluators of critical thinking skills.

Abstract: This intervention study investigates the use of counterarguments in writing for critical thinking by students in the context of Generative AI (GenAI). This is especially as risks of cheating and cognitive offloading exist with the use of GenAI. We presented 36 students in a particular university course with 4 carefully selected thesis statements (from a set of popular debates) to write about anyone of them. We used six established rubrics (focus, logic, content, style, correctness and reference) to conduct three human assessments (two student peer-reviews and one experienced teacher) per writeup on a 5-point Likert scale for all the qualified samples (n) of 35 submissions (after disqualifying one for irregularity). Using the same rubrics and guidelines, we also assessed the submissions using six frontier LLMs as judges. Our mixed-method design included qualitative open-ended feedback per assessment and quantitative methods. The results reveal that (1) the students' self-written counterarguments to AI-generated content contains logic, among other things, which is a key component of critical thinking, and (2) GenAI can be successfully used at scale to assess students' written work, based on clear rubrics, and these assessments generally align with human assessments as shown with Gwets AC2 inter-rater reliability values of 0.33 for all the models except one.

</details>


### [3] [Generating Query-Focused Summarization Datasets from Query-Free Summarization Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05392)
*Yllias Chali,Deen Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对查询聚焦摘要任务，提出从无查询数据集中自动生成证据驱动查询的方法。通过内在相似度比较和外在摘要任务评估验证了该方法的有效性，生成的查询能够支持QFS任务并产生具有竞争力的摘要质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模摘要数据集往往缺乏配套的查询，导致难以直接用于查询聚焦摘要任务（QFS）。因此需要探索能否从无查询数据集中自动生成证据驱动的查询，并验证这种查询生成方式对QFS任务的有效性。

Method: 该方法首先提出了一种证据驱动的模型，用于从无查询数据集中自动生成查询关键词。实验采用双重评估策略：内在评估方面，比较系统生成的查询与原始查询的相似度；外在评估方面，使用多种预训练模型和先进的QFS模型执行摘要任务，通过ROUGE评分衡量性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用证据驱动查询生成的摘要能够达到与使用原始查询相近的ROUGE分数，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 证据驱动查询生成方法能够有效支持QFS任务，使用生成查询产生的摘要与使用原始查询产生的摘要相比，能够达到具有竞争力的ROUGE分数。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets are widely used to perform summarization tasks, but they may not include queries alongside documents and summaries. In the search for suitable datasets for Query-Focused Summarization (QFS), we identify two research questions: Is it possible to automatically generate evidence-based query keywords from query-free datasets? Does evidence-based query generation support the QFS task? This paper proposes an evidence-based model to generate queries from query-free datasets. To evaluate our model intrinsically, we compare the similarity between the original queries and the system-generated queries of two QFS datasets. We also perform summarization tasks using different pre-trained models, as well as a state-of-the-art (SOTA) QFS model, to measure the extrinsic performance of our query generation approach. Experimental results indicate that summaries generated using evidence-based queries achieve competitive ROUGE scores compared to those generated from the original queries.

</details>


### [4] [SLAM: Structural Linguistic Activation Marking for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05443)
*Fabrice Harel-Canada,Amit Sahai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLAM是一种新型白盒水印方案，通过稀疏自编码器识别残差流中的语言结构方向并引导生成，在Gemma-2模型上实现100%检测率，质量损失仅1-2分（传统方法为7.5-11.5分），但对句法改写攻击抵抗力较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方案通过偏置下一个token分布来嵌入水印，导致文本质量明显下降（质量损失达7.5-11.5分），这种检测能力与质量之间的权衡制约了水印技术的实用价值。

Method: SLAM通过稀疏自编码器识别残差流中编码语言结构（如时态、从句顺序）方向的向量，在生成时因果性地引导这些方向，同时保持词汇采样和语义不受约束。

Result: 在Gemma-2 2B和9B上，SLAM达到100%检测准确率，质量损失仅1-2分（对比KGW、EWD、Unigram的7.5-11.5分），自然性和多样性接近无水印水平。

Conclusion: SLAM通过结构几何编码而非token频率实现了检测质量权衡的突破，其鲁棒性特征与token分布方法互补：能抵御词级编辑攻击，但对重构句法的改写攻击敏感（代价是质量损失）。

Abstract: LLM watermarks must be detectable without compromising text quality, yet most existing schemes bias the next-token distribution and pay for detection with measurable quality loss. We present SLAM (Structural Linguistic Activation Marking), a novel white-box watermarking scheme that sidesteps this cost by writing the mark into structural geometry rather than token frequencies: sparse autoencoders identify residual-stream directions encoding linguistic structure (e.g., voice, tense, clause order), and we causally steer those directions at generation time, leaving lexical sampling and semantics unconstrained. On Gemma-2 2B and 9B, SLAM achieves 100% detection accuracy with a quality cost of only 1-2 reward points - compared to 7.5-11.5 for KGW, EWD, and Unigram - with naturalness and diversity preserved at near-unwatermarked levels across both models. The trade-off is a complementary robustness profile: SLAM resists word-level edits but is vulnerable to paraphrase that restructures syntax (at a quality cost), the converse of token-distribution methods.

</details>


### [5] [Cooperative Profiles Predict Multi-Agent LLM Team Performance in AI for Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20658)
*Shivani Kumar,Adarsh Bharathwaj,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Multi-agent LLM systems require cooperation under shared constraints, yet it was unclear if stylized game behaviors predict realistic task performance. This study benchmarks 35 LLMs across 6 behavioral economics games and finds that cooperative profiles robustly predict performance in AI-for-Science tasks. Models that coordinate and invest in team production produce better scientific reports across accuracy, quality, and completion. This suggests cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property usable as a diagnostic tool.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-agent LLM systems require cooperative behavior under shared constraints (e.g., GPUs, credit balances), but it was unknown whether a model's behavior in stylized behavioral economics games predicts its performance in realistic collaborative tasks.

Method: Benchmarked 35 open-weight LLMs across 6 behavioral economics games, then tested their performance in AI-for-Science tasks (collaborative data analysis, model building, and report generation) under shared budget constraints.

Result: Game-derived cooperative profiles robustly predict downstream AI-for-Science performance. Models that coordinate games and invest in multiplicative team production produce better scientific reports across three outcomes (accuracy, quality, and completion). These associations hold after controlling for multiple factors.

Conclusion: Cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property of LLMs not reducible to general ability. Behavioral economics games offer a fast and inexpensive diagnostic tool for screening cooperative fitness before costly multi-agent deployment.

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from teams of large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for collaborative scientific reasoning and problem-solving. These systems require agents to coordinate under shared constraints, such as GPUs or credit balances, where cooperative behavior matters. Behavioral economics provides a rich toolkit of games that isolate distinct cooperation mechanisms, yet it remains unknown whether a model's behavior in these stylized settings predicts its performance in realistic collaborative tasks. Here, we benchmark 35 open-weight LLMs across six behavioral economics games and show that game-derived cooperative profiles robustly predict downstream performance in AI-for-Science tasks, where teams of LLM agents collaboratively analyze data, build models, and produce scientific reports under shared budget constraints. Models that effectively coordinate games and invest in multiplicative team production (rather than greedy strategies) produce better scientific reports across three outcomes, accuracy, quality, and completion. These associations hold after controlling for multiple factors, indicating that cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property of LLMs not reducible to general ability. Our behavioral games framework thus offers a fast and inexpensive diagnostic for screening cooperative fitness before costly multi-agent deployment.

</details>


### [6] [ReaComp: Compiling LLM Reasoning into Symbolic Solvers for Efficient Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05485)
*Atharva Naik,Yash Mathur,Prakam,Carolyn Rose,David Mortensen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将少量推理轨迹编译成符号程序合成器的方法，实现零LLM推理成本的强求解能力，在多个基准测试上显著超越LLM，并可零样本迁移到历史语言学等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)在需要大规模组合搜索的困难程序综合任务上效率低且不可靠。

Method: 使用编码智能体(coding agents)将少量推理轨迹编译成约束DSL上的可重用符号程序合成器。该方法无需测试时LLM调用，形成独立的强求解系统。

Result: 符号求解器集成在PBEBench-Lite上达到91.3%准确率，在PBEBench-Hard上达到84.7%，比使用测试时扩展的LLMs高出16.3个百分点；在神经符号混合设置下，将PBEBench-Hard准确率从68.4%提升至85.8%，同时减少78%的token使用量，SLR-Bench困难层级从34.4%提升至58.0%；零样本迁移到历史语言学任务（预测自然语言数据中的音变）达80.1%准确率，并能恢复一些可信的语言学规则。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹可以编译成可重用的符号求解器，这些求解器能够直接解决许多任务、在困难情况下补充LLM推理，并提供可扩展的领域通用求解器归纳路线图。代码和数据已开源以支持复现。

Abstract: LLMs can solve program synthesis tasks but remain inefficient and unreliable on hard instances requiring large combinatorial search. Given a small set of reasoning traces, we use coding agents to compile them into reusable symbolic program synthesizers over constrained DSLs. The resulting solvers require no LLM calls at test time and are strong standalone systems: symbolic solver ensembles reach 91.3% accuracy on PBEBench-Lite and 84.7% on PBEBench-Hard, outperforming LLMs with test-time scaling for the latter by +16.3 percentage points at zero LLM inference cost. They also complement LLM search, improving PBEBench-Hard accuracy from 68.4% to 85.8% while reducing reported token usage by 78%, and raising SLR-Bench hard-tier accuracy from 34.4% to 58.0% in a neuro-symbolic hybrid setting. Compared to directly using coding agents as per-instance solvers, induced solvers are substantially more Pareto-efficient, amortizing a small one-time construction cost over many zero-token executions. Finally, most solvers transfer zero-shot to a real historical linguistics task - predicting sound changes in natural language data - reaching 80.1% accuracy under ensembling and recovering some plausible linguistic rules. Together, these results show that reasoning traces can be compiled into reusable symbolic solvers that solve many tasks directly, complement LLM inference on hard cases, and provide a scalable route to domain-general solver induction. We release code and data for reproducibility.

</details>


### [7] [Chainwash: Multi-Step Rewriting Attacks on Diffusion Language Model Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05503)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Akif Islam,Nadim Mahmud,Md. Ekramul Hamid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究扩散语言模型水印在多次改写攻击下的鲁棒性，发现五轮链式改写可将检测率从87.9%降至4.86%，表明重复改写是有效的水印攻击手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计水印方案假设自回归生成（从左到右逐token生成，上下文哈希定义清晰），但扩散语言模型通过任意顺序去噪生成文本，无法直接应用现有方案。研究旨在探索扩散语言模型水印在面对多次改写攻击时的鲁棒性。

Method: 研究使用1,605个约300词的水印完成文本，跨越五个WaterBench领域。由四个开源语言模型（1.5B至8B参数）对每个完成文本进行改写，采用五种风格（释义、人性化、简化、学术、总结扩展），每种风格链式执行最多五轮，共生成160,500个改写文本。所有重写模型均不知晓水印密钥。

Result: 原始水印在标准显著性阈值下检测率为87.9%。单次改写后检测率降至14%至41%（取决于重写器和风格）。五轮链式改写后检测率仅为4.86%，即94.76%的原始检测文本不再被标记。三轮改写后检测器得分已下降86%从水印基线趋近零假设分布。

Conclusion: 重复改写是对扩散语言模型水印的强攻击方式。经过5轮链式改写后，检测率从87.9%急剧下降至4.86%，意味着94.76%的原始标记文本不再被检测出。经过3轮改写后，检测器得分已下降86%趋近于零假设分布。该结论在所有四个测试的重写模型中均成立，表明即使不知道水印密钥的非授权重写也能有效破坏水印检测。

Abstract: Statistical watermarking is a common approach for verifying whether text was written by a language model. Most existing schemes assume autoregressive generation, where tokens are produced left to right and contextual hashing is well defined. Diffusion language models generate text by denoising tokens in arbitrary order, so these schemes cannot be applied directly. A recent watermark by Gloaguen et al. addresses this gap for LLaDA 8B Instruct and reports true positive detection above 99%. This paper studies what happens when watermarked text is rewritten not once but several times. Using the same watermark configuration, 1,605 watermarked completions of about 300 tokens each are produced across five WaterBench domains. Each completion is rewritten by four open weight language models, from 1.5B to 8B parameters, none of which know the watermark key. Five rewrite styles are tested: paraphrase, humanize, simplify, academic, and summarize expand. Each style is chained for up to five hops, producing 160,500 rewritten texts in total. The watermark is detected on 87.9% of the original outputs at the standard significance threshold. After a single rewrite, detection falls to between 14% and 41% depending on the rewriter and style. After five chained rewrites, detection falls to 4.86%, meaning 94.76% of the originally detected texts are no longer flagged. After three rewrites, the detector score has dropped 86% of the way from its watermarked baseline toward the null distribution. Repeated rewriting is therefore a much stronger attack than a single rewrite, and the result holds across all four rewriters tested.

</details>


### [8] [A Few Good Clauses: Comparing LLMs vs Domain-Trained Small Language Models on Structured Contract Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05532)
*Nicole Lincoln,Nick Whitehouse,Jaron Mar,Rivindu Perera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一项关于法律合同提取的研究表明，领域定制的Small Language Model（Olava Extract）能够以显著更低的成本（78-97%）超越多个前沿Large Language Models，同时保持更少的幻觉输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在验证在结构化合同提取任务上，经过领域训练的Small Language Model (SLM)是否能够以极低成本超越前沿Large Language Models (LLM)。研究挑战了商业化企业AI能力必须依赖最大模型和集中式托管提供商的传统假设。

Method: 研究人员测试了Olava Extract（一个自托管的法律领域专家混合模型）与五个前沿模型在结构化合同提取任务上的表现。通过计算宏F1和微F1分数、精度以及幻觉率等指标进行对比评估。

Result: Olava Extract在研究中取得最强综合性能，宏F1达到0.812，微F1达到0.842，同时相比测试的前沿模型将推理成本降低了78%至97%。该模型还实现了最高精度分数，产生的幻觉和不支持的提取更少，在法律工作流程中具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 研究表明高性能、人工可比的法律AI不再需要最大的外部托管模型。该研究挑战了商业上有价值的企业AI能力必须与越来越大模型、大规模基础设施投资和集中式托管提供商绑定的假设，预示着法律AI领域可能转向更经济高效的领域定制方案。

Abstract: This paper evaluates whether a domain trained Small Language Model (SLM) can outperform frontier Large Language Models on structured contract extraction at radically lower cost. We test Olava Extract, a self hosted legal domain Mixture of Experts model, against five frontier models.
  Olava Extract achieved the strongest aggregate performance in the study, with a macro F1 of 0.812 and a micro F1 of 0.842, while reducing inference cost by 78% to 97% compared with the frontier models tested. It also achieved the highest precision scores, producing fewer hallucinated and unsupported extractions, an important distinction in legal workflows where hallucinations create operational risk and downstream review burden.
  The findings shows that high performing, human comparable legal AI no longer requires the largest externally hosted models. More broadly, they challenge the assumption that commercially valuable enterprise AI capability must remain tied to ever larger models, massive infrastructure expenditure, and centrally hosted providers.

</details>


### [9] [The Cost of Context: Mitigating Textual Bias in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05594)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大模型RAG系统中普遍存在的recorruption现象——精确上下文反而导致模型放弃正确答案。通过注意力机制分析，发现根源在于视觉注意力崩溃和位置偏差。基于此提出的BAIR方法无需训练即可恢复模型视觉聚焦能力，在多个领域基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)与检索增强生成(RAG)结合时，外部文档的引入会掩盖严重的实例级失败模式。即使提供完全准确的"oracle"上下文，模型也可能放弃原本正确的预测，这一现象被称为recorruption，亟需系统性的诊断与干预。

Method: 通过注意力矩阵的机制诊断，发现recorruption源于双重注意力崩溃：(1)视觉 blindness——视觉注意力质量(M_vis)和锐度(S_vis)的系统性抑制；(2)结构性位置偏差——模型优先关注边界token而非语义相关性。BAIR通过恢复视觉显著性和对文本干扰项施加位置感知惩罚来解决这些漏洞。

Result: 揭示了"Illusion of Success"现象——许多看似正确的RAG结果仅是位置巧合（模型文本复制偏差与真值位置恰好对齐）。实验表明BAIR在医学事实性、社会公平性和地理空间基准上有效恢复多模态接地能力，提升诊断可靠性。

Conclusion: BAIR成功恢复多模态接地能力，提升医学诊断可靠性，全程无需重训练或微调。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly integrated with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations, the introduction of external documents can conceal severe failure modes at the instance level. We identify and formalize the phenomenon of recorruption, where the introduction of even perfectly accurate "oracle" context causes a capable model to abandon an initially correct prediction. Through a mechanistic diagnosis of internal attention matrices, we show that recorruption is driven by a two-fold attentional collapse: (1) visual blindness, characterized by the systemic suppression of visual attention mass ($M_{vis}$) and sharpness ($S_{vis}$), and (2) a structural positional bias that forces the model to prioritize boundary tokens over semantic relevance. Our analysis reveals an Illusion of Success, demonstrating that many seemingly correct RAG outcomes are merely positional coincidences where the model's textual copying bias happens to align with the ground-truth location. To address these vulnerabilities, we propose Bottleneck Attention Intervention for Recovery (BAIR), a parameter-free, inference-time framework that restores visual saliency and applies position-aware penalties to textual distractors. Across medical factuality, social fairness, and geospatial benchmarks, BAIR successfully restores multimodal grounding and improves diagnostic reliability without requiring model retraining or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [10] [When2Speak: A Dataset for Temporal Participation and Turn-Taking in Multi-Party Conversations for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05626)
*Vihaan Nama,Shreya Mendi,Zian Ye,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: When2Speak 数据集和 pipeline 用于训练 LLMs 的介入时机决策能力，涵盖 215,000+ 多方对话示例；SFT 可显著提升性能但存在过度保守问题，结合非对称奖励塑形的 RL 可有效缓解该缺陷，证明时间参与性是可训练的会话智能维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 在多方对话场景中存在严重的行为失准：它们倾向于在每个轮次都响应，导致过度打断和对话连贯性下降——在多参与者交互中，何时发言与说什么同等关键。

Method: 提出了 When2Speak 数据集（215,000+ 示例，来自 16,000 段 2-6 人对话）和四阶段生成 pipeline（真实世界 grounding、结构化增强、受控转录合成、微调就绪监督）；采用监督微调（SFT）和带非对称奖励塑形的强化学习（RL）两种训练策略。

Result: SFT 相比 zero-shot 基线显著提升（4B+ 参数模型 Macro F1 平均提升 60%，最大达 120%），但模型仍系统性过度保守（MIR~0.50，漏判近半数应介入时刻）；引入 RL 后 MIR 降至 0.186-0.218，召回率从 0.479 提升至 0.78-0.81。

Conclusion: 研究表明，时间参与性是会话智能的一个独特且可训练的维度， grounded synthetic data 为 enable LLMs 在多方对话中更自然、更恰当地参与提供了有效且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at generating contextually appropriate responses but remain poorly calibrated for multi-party conversations, where deciding when to speak is as critical as what to say. In such settings, naively responding at every turn leads to excessive interruptions and degraded conversational coherence. We introduce When2Speak, a grounded synthetic dataset and four-stage generation pipeline for learning intervention timing in group interactions. The dataset comprises over 215,000 examples derived from 16,000 conversations involving 2-6 speakers, spanning diverse conversational styles, tones, and participant dynamics, and explicitly modeling SPEAK vs. SILENT decisions at each turn. Our pipeline combines real-world grounding, structured augmentation, controlled transcript synthesis, and fine-tuning-ready supervision, and is fully open-sourced to support reproducibility and adaptation to domain-specific conversational norms. Across multiple model families, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on When2Speak significantly outperforms zero-shot baselines (e.g., the average Macro F1 increase across 4B+ parameter models was 60%, with the largest increase being 120%). However, SFT-trained models remain systematically over-conservative, missing nearly half of warranted interventions as seen through the Missed Intervention Rate (MIR), which was on average 0.50 and is noticed even at larger model sizes. To address this limitation, we apply reinforcement learning with asymmetric reward shaping, which reduces MIR to 0.186-0.218 and increases recall from 0.479 to 0.78-0.81. Our findings establish that temporal participation is a distinct and trainable dimension of conversational intelligence, and that grounded synthetic data provides an effective and scalable pathway for enabling LLMs to participate more naturally and appropriately in multi-party interactions.

</details>


### [11] [One Turn Too Late: Response-Aware Defense Against Hidden Malicious Intent in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05630)
*Xinjie Shen,Rongzhe Wei,Peizhi Niu,Haoyu Wang,Ruihan Wu,Eli Chien,Bo Li,Pin-Yu Chen,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Hidden malicious intent in multi-turn dialogue poses a growing threat to deployed large language models (LLMs). Rather than exposing a harmful objective in a single prompt, increasingly capable attackers can distribute their intent across multiple benign-looking turns. Recent studies show that even modern commercial models with advanced guardrails remain vulnerable to such attacks despite advances in safety alignment and external guardrails. In this work, we address this challenge by detecting the earliest turn at which delivering the candidate response would make the accumulated interaction sufficient to enable harmful action. This objective requires precise turn-level intervention that identifies the harm-enabling closure point while avoiding premature refusal of benign exploratory conversations. To further support training and evaluation, we construct the Multi-Turn Intent Dataset (MTID), which contains branching attack rollouts, matched benign hard negatives, and annotations of the earliest harm-enabling turns. We show that MTID helps enable a turn-level monitor TurnGate, which substantially outperforms existing baselines in harmful-intent detection while maintaining low over-refusal rates. TurnGate further generalizes across domains, attacker pipelines, and target models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/TurnGate.

</details>


### [12] [XL-SafetyBench: A Country-Grounded Cross-Cultural Benchmark for LLM Safety and Cultural Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05662)
*Dasol Choi,Eugenia Kim,Jaewon Noh,Sang Seo,Eunmi Kim,Myunggyo Oh,Yunjin Park,Brigitta Jesica Kartono,Josef Pichlmeier,Helena Berndt,Sai Krishna Mendu,Glenn Johannes Tungka,Özlem Gökçe,Suresh Gehlot,Katherine Pratt,Amanda Minnich,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XL-SafetyBench提出了一个包含10个国家-语言对的5500个测试用例的多语言安全评估套件，包含针对特定国家的对抗性提示 jailbreak 基准和文化基准，用于评估模型的文化敏感性；评估10个前沿模型和27个本地模型后发现，前沿模型的越狱鲁棒性和文化意识并无耦合关系，而本地模型的安全表现主要由生成失败而非真正的对齐导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: existing LLM safety benchmarks are English-centric and rely on translation, failing to capture country-specific harms; they rarely evaluate a model's ability to detect culturally embedded sensitivities as distinct from universal harms

Method: multi-stage pipeline combining LLM-assisted discovery, automated validation gates, and dual independent native-speaker annotators per country; evaluation using Attack Success Rate (ASR), Neutral-Safe Rate (NSR), and Cultural Sensitivity Rate (CSR)

Result: two key findings: (1) among frontier models, jailbreak robustness and cultural awareness are not coupled, so composite safety scores obscure per-axis variation; (2) local models exhibit a near-linear ASR-NSR trade-off (r = -0.81), indicating their apparent safety reflects generation failure rather than genuine alignment

Conclusion: local models often appear safe due to generation failures rather than genuine alignment; frontier models show that jailbreak robustness and cultural awareness are not coupled, so composite safety scores can obscure important per-axis variations; XL-SafetyBench facilitates more nuanced cross-cultural safety evaluation for the multilingual era

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks are predominantly English-centric and often rely on translation, failing to capture country-specific harms. Moreover, they rarely evaluate a model's ability to detect culturally embedded sensitivities as distinct from universal harms. We introduce XL-SafetyBench. a suite of 5,500 test cases across 10 country-language pairs, comprising a Jailbreak Benchmark of country-grounded adversarial prompts and a Cultural Benchmark where local sensitivities are embedded within innocuous requests. Each item is constructed via a multi-stage pipeline that combines LLM-assisted discovery, automated validation gates, and dual independent native-speaker annotators per country. To distinguish principled refusal from comprehension failure, we evaluate Attack Success Rate (ASR) alongside two complementary metrics we introduce: Neutral-Safe Rate (NSR) and Cultural Sensitivity Rate (CSR). Evaluating 10 frontier and 27 local LLMs reveals two key findings. First, jailbreak robustness and cultural awareness do not show a coupled relationship among frontier models, so a composite safety score obscures per-axis variation. Second, local models exhibit a near-linear ASR-NSR trade-off (r = -0.81), indicating that their apparent safety reflects generation failure rather than genuine alignment. XL-SafetyBench enables more nuanced, cross-cultural safety evaluation in the multilingual era.

</details>


### [13] [Decomposing the Basic Abilities of Large Language Models: Mitigating Cross-Task Interference in Multi-Task Instruct-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05676)
*Bing Wang,Ximing Li,Changchun Li,Jinjin Chi,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BADIT方法解决LLM多任务指令微调中的跨任务干扰问题。该方法将参数分解为正交的高奇异值LoRA专家表示基础能力，通过球面聚类动态强制正交性，在SuperNI基准的6个LLMs上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务指令微调的训练范式存在关键问题——跨任务干扰，源于不同任务对共享参数的梯度冲突。现有解决方案（如任务特定神经元选择和专家混合）仍存在跨任务干扰，因为许多参数仍被不同任务共享。

Method: BADIT将LLM参数分解为表示基础能力的正交高奇异值LoRA专家，并通过球面聚类对秩-1分量在训练过程中动态强制执行正交性。该方法基于发现某些参数持续共同激活并自然组织成基础组的经验观察。

Result: 在SuperNI基准测试中使用6个LLMs进行广泛实验，结果表明BADIT可以超越SOTA方法并缓解跨任务干扰程度。

Conclusion: BADIT通过将LLM参数分解为正交的高奇异值LoRA专家来有效解决跨任务干扰问题，在SuperNI基准测试中6个LLMs上超越了SOTA方法，显著缓解了跨任务干扰程度。

Abstract: Recently, the prominent performance of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by multi-task instruct-tuning. Unfortunately, this training paradigm suffers from a key issue, named cross-task interference, due to conflicting gradients over shared parameters among different tasks. Some previous methods mitigate this issue by isolating task-specific parameters, e.g., task-specific neuron selection and mixture-of-experts. In this paper, we empirically reveal that the cross-task interference still exists for the existing solutions because of many parameters also shared by different tasks, and accordingly, we propose a novel solution, namely Basic Abilities Decomposition for multi-task Instruct-Tuning (BADIT). Specifically, we empirically find that certain parameters are consistently co-activated, and that co-activated parameters naturally organize into base groups. This motivates us to analogize that LLMs encode several orthogonal basic abilities, and that any task can be represented as a linear combination of these abilities. Accordingly, we propose BADIT that decomposes LLM parameters into orthogonal high-singular-value LoRA experts representing basic abilities, and dynamically enforces their orthogonality during training via spherical clustering of rank-1 components. We conduct extensive experiments on the SuperNI benchmark with 6 LLMs, and empirical results demonstrate that BADIT can outperform SOTA methods and mitigate the degree of cross-task interference.

</details>


### [14] [Evaluation Awareness in Language Models Has Limited Effect on Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05835)
*Amelie Knecht,Lucas Florin,Thilo Hagendorff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型在思维链中表达"正在被评估"的意识（VEA）对实际行为影响很小。无论是否主动注入或移除VEA，模型行为变化均不显著，答案分布偏移不超过3.7个百分点。这表明VEA带来的安全风险可能低于当前研究界的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者担心LRM在思维链中意识到自己正在被评估时，会策略性地调整输出以优化符合感知到的评估标准，这可能导致模型表现得比实际更安全，造成安全隐患。

Method: 实验采用两种方法：on-policy方法对每个测试项采样多个思维链(CoT)，比较自发包含VEA与不包含VEA的样本行为差异；off-policy方法使用模型预填充技术主动注入或删除VEA句子后重新采样。测试覆盖安全、对齐、道德推理和政治观点等多个基准数据集。

Result: VEA对模型行为的影响有限：注入VEA几乎无效果（ω≤0.06）；移除VEA产生小幅偏移（ω≤0.12）；自发产生的VEA使答案分布偏移最多3.7个百分点（ω≤0.31）。

Conclusion: VEA poses a smaller safety risk than current literature assumes. High VEA rates should not be interpreted as strong evidence of strategic behavior or alignment tampering, as the actual behavioral impact is minimal.

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) sometimes note in their chain of thought (CoT) that they may be under evaluation. Researchers worry that this verbalised evaluation awareness (VEA) causes models to adapt their outputs strategically, optimising for perceived evaluation criteria, which, for instance, can make models appear safer than they actually are. However, whether VEA actually has this effect is largely unknown. We tested this across open-weight LRMs and benchmarks covering safety, alignment, moral reasoning, and political opinion. We tested this both on-policy, sampling multiple CoTs per item and comparing those that spontaneously contained VEA against those that did not, and off-policy, using model prefilling to inject evaluation-aware sentences where missing and remove them where present, with subsequent resampling. VEA has limited effect on model behaviour: injecting VEA into CoTs produces near-zero effects ($ω\leq 0.06$), removing it causes small shifts ($ω\leq 0.12$) and spontaneously occurring VEA shifts answer distributions by at most 3.7 percentage points ($ω\leq 0.31$). Our findings call for caution when interpreting high VEA rates as evidence of strategic behaviour or alignment tampering. Evaluation awareness may pose a smaller safety risk than the current literature assumes.

</details>


### [15] [BioTool: A Comprehensive Tool-Calling Dataset for Enhancing Biomedical Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05758)
*Xin Gao,Ruiyi Zhang,Meixi Du,Peijia Qin,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioTool is a comprehensive biomedical tool-calling dataset with 34 tools and 7,040 query-API pairs. Fine-tuning a 4B LLM on this dataset enables it to outperform GPT-5.1 and significantly improves downstream answer quality for biomedical applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLMs perform unsatisfactorily in specialized biomedical domains due to their inability to effectively leverage biomedical tools. Existing biomedical efforts rely on in-context learning and restrict models to small tool sets, limiting their practical utility for clinical experts and biomedical researchers.

Method: Introduced BioTool, a comprehensive biomedical tool-calling dataset comprising 34 tools from NCBI, Ensembl, and UniProt databases, along with 7,040 high-quality human-verified query-API call pairs. A 4-billion-parameter LLM was fine-tuned on this dataset for biomedical tool-calling.

Result: Fine-tuning on BioTool yields substantial improvements in biomedical tool-calling performance, outperforming cutting-edge commercial LLMs like GPT-5.1. Human expert evaluations confirm that integrating the BioTool-fine-tuned model significantly improves downstream answer quality compared to the same LLM without tool usage.

Conclusion: BioTool effectively enhances the biomedical capabilities of LLMs, providing a scalable solution for domain-specific tool integration. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available.

Abstract: Despite the success of large language models (LLMs) on general-purpose tasks, their performance in highly specialized domains such as biomedicine remains unsatisfactory. A key limitation is the inability of LLMs to effectively leverage biomedical tools, which clinical experts and biomedical researchers rely on extensively in daily workflows. While recent general-domain tool-calling datasets have substantially improved the capabilities of LLM agents, existing efforts in the biomedical domain largely rely on in-context learning and restrict models to a small set of tools. To address this gap, we introduce BioTool, a comprehensive biomedical tool-calling dataset designed for fine-tuning LLMs. BioTool comprises 34 frequently used tools collected from the NCBI, Ensembl, and UniProt databases, along with 7,040 high-quality, human-verified query-API call pairs spanning variation, genomics, proteomics, evolution, and general biology. Fine-tuning a 4-billion-parameter LLM on BioTool yields substantial improvements in biomedical tool-calling performance, outperforming cutting-edge commercial LLMs such as GPT-5.1. Furthermore, human expert evaluations demonstrate that integrating a BioTool-fine-tuned tool caller significantly improves downstream answer quality compared to the same LLM without tool usage, highlighting the effectiveness of BioTool in enhancing the biomedical capabilities of LLMs. The full dataset and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/gxx27/BioTool

</details>


### [16] [Who and What? Using Linguistic Features and Annotator Characteristics to Analyze Annotation Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06318)
*Maximilian Maurer,Maximilian Linde,Gabriella Lapesa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模分析了四个有害语言检测数据集中的标注者差异，综合考量标注者特征、文本语言学特征及其交互作用。研究发现交互效应至关重要，揭示了此前被忽视的交叉效应，且词汇线索和标注者态度起主要作用，但效应模式在不同数据集间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类标注差异是NLP中的核心现象，不同标注者对同一文本有不同视角。虽然有害语言数据集已积累了大量标注者人口统计学和态度信息，但标注内容本身（如文本的语言学特征）以及标注者特征与文本特征的交互作用尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 对四个有害语言检测参考数据集进行大规模分析，综合考察标注者特征、文本语言学特征及其交互作用，采用统计方法建模。

Result: 交互效应至关重要，揭示了此前工作中被忽视的交叉效应；词汇线索和标注者态度起关键作用；但效应模式在不同数据集间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果警示：对标注差异模式的泛化与迁移需谨慎，不同数据集可能呈现不同规律。

Abstract: Human label variation has been established as a central phenomenon in NLP: the perspectives different annotators have on the same item need to be embraced. Data collection practices thus shifted towards increasing the annotator numbers and releasing disaggregated datasets, harmful language being most resourced due to its high subjectivity. While this resulted in rich information about \textit{who} annotated (sociodemographics, attitudes, etc.), the \textit{what} (e.g., linguistic properties of items), and their interplay has received little attention. We present the first large-scale analysis of four reference datasets for harmful language detection, bringing together annotator characteristics, linguistic properties of the items, and their interactions in a statistically informed picture. We find that interactions are crucial, revealing intersectional effects ignored in previous work, and that a strong role is played by lexical cues and annotator attitudes. Effect patterns, however, vary considerably across datasets. This urges caution about generalization and transferability.

</details>


### [17] [Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06619)
*Jan Fillies,Ronald E. Robertson,Jeffrey Hancock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究正式化了Algospeak语言规避策略与检测器之间的共同进化动态，引入了"多数可理解调制"(MUM)概念来描述规避效果与可理解性之间的权衡。通过COVID-19虚假信息数据集和可复现框架，实验揭示了调制水平与理解度之间的特征关系，为内容审核和AI安全研究提供了重要工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型(LLM)日益介入内容生成和内容审核，被称为Algospeak的语言规避策略与检测器之间的共同进化不断加剧。研究需要正式化这种潜在动态关系，并理解在什么调制程度下，规避策略虽能有效躲避检测，但会丧失对大多数受众的可理解性。

Method: 研究采用可复现框架创建意义保持的Algospeak风格变体，基于现有分类法并具有可调调制水平。使用COVID-19虚假信息作为示例场景，构建了包含700个调制项的参考数据集（来自20个基础句子，涵盖5个调制水平和7种策略）。对7个不同语言模型进行两项关联评估：意义恢复测试（解释能力）和虚假信息检测测试（分类能力）。通过对调制水平进行曲线拟合来估计MUM阈值，并进行跨策略和模型的敏感性分析。

Result: 结果表明理解度与调制水平之间存在特征性关系。研究揭示了不同规避策略和模型对MUM阈值的影响差异，为理解Algospeak的动态机制提供了实证基础。

Conclusion: 研究为理解Algospeak的动态机制奠定了基础，提供了可复现的框架、数据集和实验设置。研究揭示了理解度与调制水平之间的特征关系，并展示了不同策略和模型间的敏感性差异。这一工作为未来的内容审核和AI安全研究提供了重要工具和洞察。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Steering Vector: Flow-based Activation Steering for Inference-Time Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05892)
*Zehao Jin,Ruixuan Deng,Junran Wang,Xinjie Shen,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FLAS通过学习概念条件的流场来控制语言模型行为，是首个在大规模评估中持续超越上下文提示的激活 steering 方法，揭示了激活空间几何的复杂动态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 steering 方法在大规模评估（如AxBench）中表现不如简单的上下文提示，且对新概念泛化能力差。研究假设这些局限源于先前方法共享的未验证简化假设——将 steering 干预限制为固定、单步、位置不变的变换。

Method: FLAS学习一个概念条件的速度场v_t(h,t,c)，通过流匹配将未控制的激活输送到控制状态。该方法放弃了先前方法中固定、单步、位置不变变换的假设约束，采用更通用的可学习变换。

Result: 在AxBench上，FLAS是首个持续超越提示的学习方法，在Gemma-2-2B-IT上达到1.015的held-out调和均值，在Gemma-2-9B-IT上达到1.113，且无需每概念调优。学习到的流呈现弯曲、多步、token变化的轨迹。

Conclusion: FLAS通过学习概念条件的速度场，实现了无需每概念调优即可超越提示的性能，并揭示了激活空间的曲率、多步和token变化特性，为激活空间几何学提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a promising alternative for controlling language-model behavior at inference time by modifying intermediate representations while keeping model parameters frozen. However, large-scale evaluations such as AxBench show that existing steering methods are often outperformed by simple in-context prompting and generalize poorly to unseen concepts. We hypothesize that these limitations arise from unvalidated simplifying assumptions shared across prior methods, which typically restrict steering interventions to fixed, single-step, position-invariant transforms. We propose FLAS (Flow-based Activation Steering), which learns a general, concept-conditioned velocity field $v_t(h,t,c)$ that transports unsteered activations to steered ones without relying on these assumptions. On AxBench, FLAS is the first learned method to consistently outperform prompting, reaching held-out harmonic means of $1.015$ on Gemma-2-2B-IT and $1.113$ on Gemma-2-9B-IT without per-concept tuning. Analysis of the learned flow shows curved, multi-step, token-varying trajectories, which suggests that previous hypotheses on activation space geometry might be incomplete.

</details>


### [19] [Logic-Regularized Verifier Elicits Reasoning from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05893)
*Xinyu Wang,Changzhi Sun,Lian Cheng,Yuanbin Wu,Dell Zhang,Xiaoling Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOVER是一种基于逻辑规则正则化的无监督验证器，通过内部激活和三类逻辑约束（否定一致性、组内/组间一致性）实现，无需标注数据即可显著提升LLM推理能力，性能接近监督验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统验证器依赖资源密集型的人工标注数据集构建，成本高昂且数据多样性受限，亟需无监督验证方案。

Method: 将验证器视为二元隐变量，利用内部激活信息，并在多条推理路径上强制执行三类逻辑约束：否定一致性、组内一致性和组间一致性（按最终答案分组）。通过将逻辑规则作为先验知识融入框架。

Result: 在10个数据集上，LOVER显著优于无监督基线方法，性能达到监督验证器的95%平均水平。

Conclusion: LOVER通过逻辑规则约束实现无需标注数据的LLM推理验证，为降低验证器构建成本、提升模型推理能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Verifiers are crucial components for enhancing modern LLMs' reasoning capability. Typicalverifiers require resource-intensive superviseddataset construction, which is costly and faceslimitations in data diversity. In this paper, wepropose LOVER, an unsupervised verifier regularized by logical rules. LOVER treats theverifier as a binary latent variable, utilizinginternal activations and enforcing three logical constraints on multiple reasoning paths:negation consistency, intra-group consistency,and inter-group consistency (grouped by thefinal answer). By incorporating logical rulesas priors, LOVER can leverage unlabeled examples and is directly compatible with any offthe-shelf LLMs. Experiments on 10 datasetsdemonstrate that LOVER significantly outperforms unsupervised baselines, achieving performance comparable to the supervised verifier(reaching its 95% level on average). The sourcecode is publicly available at https://github.com/wangxinyufighting/llm-lover.

</details>


### [20] [Lightweight Stylistic Consistency Profiling: Robust Detection of LLM-Generated Textual Content for Multimedia Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05950)
*Siyuan Li,Aodu Wulianghai,Xi Lin,Xibin Yuan,Qinghua Mao,Guangyan Li,Xiang Chen,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LiSCP is a lightweight stylistic consistency profiling method for robust LLM-generated text detection, combining discrete stylistic features with continuous semantic signals across multimodal-guided paraphrased variants, achieving up to 11.79% improvement in cross-domain settings and strong adversarial robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The widespread adoption of LLMs in content creation necessitates reliable detection methods. Existing approaches suffer from vulnerability to paraphrasing and adversarial manipulations due to their reliance on statistical cues or model-specific heuristics, resulting in limited robustness and interpretability.

Method: LiSCP constructs a consistency profile combining discrete stylistic features with continuous semantic signals, leveraging stylistic stability across multimodal-guided paraphrased text variants to enhance feature robustness under adversarial manipulation.

Result: LiSCP achieves superior in-domain detection performance and outperforms existing methods by up to 11.79% in cross-domain settings. It also demonstrates notable robustness under adversarial attacks and hybrid human-AI settings.

Conclusion: LiSCP provides an effective and robust solution for detecting LLM-generated textual content, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities across domains and resilience under adversarial conditions, offering a promising approach for multimedia moderation.

Abstract: The increasing prevalence of Large Language Models (LLMs) in content creation has made distinguishing human-written textual content from LLM-generated counterparts a critical task for multimedia moderation. Existing detectors often rely on statistical cues or model-specific heuristics, making them vulnerable to paraphrasing and adversarial manipulations, and consequently limiting their robustness and interpretability. In this work, we proposeLiSCP , a novel lightweight stylistic consistency profiling method for robust detection of LLM-generated textual content, focusing on feature stability under adversarial manipulation. Our approach constructs a consistency profile that combines discrete stylistic features with continuous semantic signals, leveraging stylistic stability across multimodal-guided paraphrased text variants. Experiments spanning real-world multimedia news and movie datasets and conventional text domains demonstrate that LiSCP achieves superior performance on in-domain detection and outperforms existing approaches by up to 11.79% in cross-domain settings. Additionally,it demonstrates notable robustness under adversarial scenarios, including adversarial attacks and hybrid human-AI settings.

</details>


### [21] [Hallucination as an Anomaly: Dynamic Intervention via Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05953)
*Erik Nielsen,Elia Cunegatti,Marcus Vukojevic,Giovanni Iacca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PCNET框架，通过概率电路在LLM残差流上进行可追踪密度估计，利用几何异常检测实现精准幻觉识别；并在此基础上提出PC-LDCD动态解码策略，仅在检测到幻觉时介入纠正。该方法在1B-8B规模模型上验证有效，无需采样、外部验证器或权重修改，即实现AUROC 99%的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉纠正方法存在核心缺陷：对所有token无差别应用纠正，导致原本正确的生成内容被破坏。需要一种能够精准区分正确与错误生成、仅在必要时进行修正的方法。

Method: 1) 使用概率电路(PC)作为LLM残差流的可追踪密度估计器；2) 通过精确的负对数似然(NLL)计算检测幻觉，将幻觉识别为事实流形上的几何异常；3) PC-LDCD在每个解码步骤中动态判断隐状态是否偏离事实区域，仅在检测到幻觉时触发对比解码。

Result: 1) 在CoQA、SQuAD v2.0、TriviaQA上实现接近完美的幻觉检测（AUROC高达99%）；2) PC-LDCD在TruthfulQA的True+Info、MC2、MC3指标上于3/4模型中达到最优；3) 将平均生成损坏率降至53.7%，保持率达79.3%；4) 涵盖对话推理、知识密集型问答、阅读理解、真实性评估四大任务。

Conclusion: PCNET通过几何流形分析实现了接近完美的幻觉检测性能（AUROC高达99%），PC-LDCD在TruthfulQA上取得最先进结果，同时将生成损坏率降至53.7%，保持率提升至79.3%，为LLM幻觉问题提供了高效且无需外部验证器的解决方案。

Abstract: One of the most critical challenges in Large Language Models is their tendency to hallucinate, i.e., produce factually incorrect responses. Existing approaches show promising results in terms of hallucination correction, but still suffer from a main limitation: they apply corrections indiscriminately to every token, corrupting also the originally correct generations. To overcome this drawback, we propose PCNET, a Probabilistic Circuit trained as a tractable density estimator over the LLM residual stream. The method detects hallucinations as geometric anomalies on the factual manifold, which is done via exact Negative Log-Likelihood computation, hence without the need for sampling, external verifiers, or weight modifications, as in existing techniques. To demonstrate its effectiveness, we exploit PCNET as a dynamic gate that distinguishes hallucinated from factual hidden states at each decoding step. This triggers our second main contribution, PC-LDCD (Probabilistic Circuit Latent Density Contrastive Decoding), only when the latent geometry deviates from factual regions, while leaving correct generations untouched. Across four LLMs, ranging from 1B to 8B models, and four benchmarks covering conversational reasoning, knowledge-intensive QA, reading comprehension, and truthfulness, PCNET achieves near-perfect hallucination detection across CoQA, SQuAD v2.0, and TriviaQA, with AUROC reaching up to 99%. Moreover, PC-LDCD obtains the highest True+Info, MC2, and MC3 scores on TruthfulQA in three out of four models, in comparison with state-of-the-art baselines, while reducing the mean corruption rate to 53.7% and achieving a preservation rate of 79.3%. Our proposed method is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [22] [TableVista: Benchmarking Multimodal Table Reasoning under Visual and Structural Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05955)
*Zheyuan Yang,Liqiang Shang,Junjie Chen,Xun Yang,Chenglong Xu,Bo Yuan,Chenyuan Jiao,Yaoru Sun,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TableVista是一个用于评估多模态基础模型在视觉与结构复杂性下表格推理能力的新基准，包含3,000个问题经10种视觉变体扩展为30,000个样本。评测29个模型后发现：模型对渲染风格鲁棒，但在复杂结构和纯视觉场景下表现退化，暴露了当前多模态表格理解能力的关键不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格推理评测缺乏对视觉复杂性与结构性复杂性的综合考量，无法全面评估多模态基础模型在真实场景下的推理鲁棒性与可靠性。

Method: 构建包含3,000个表格推理问题的高质量基准，每个问题经多风格渲染与变换管道扩展为10种视觉变体，最终生成30,000个多模态样本，对29个SOTA开源及闭源基础模型进行大规模评测。

Result: 模型在不同渲染风格下保持相对稳定，但在复杂结构布局和纯视觉配置下出现显著性能下降，表明当前模型难以在结构复杂度与视觉整合呈现结合时维持推理一致性。

Conclusion: TableVista揭示了当前多模态模型在处理结构复杂性与视觉整合时的关键能力缺口，为构建更鲁棒可靠的表格理解模型提供了重要方向。评测结果也为后续研究提供了明确的改进目标。

Abstract: We introduce TableVista, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating foundation models in multimodal table reasoning under visual and structural complexity. TableVista consists of 3,000 high-quality table reasoning problems, where each instance is expanded into 10 distinct visual variants through our multi-style rendering and transformation pipeline. This process encompasses diverse scenario styles, robustness perturbations, and vision-only configurations, culminating in 30,000 multimodal samples for a multi-dimensional evaluation. We conduct an extensive evaluation of 29 state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary foundation models on TableVista. Through comprehensive quantitative and qualitative analysis, we find that while evaluated models remain largely stable across diverse rendering styles, they exhibit pronounced performance degradation on complex structural layouts and vision-only settings, revealing that current models struggle to maintain reasoning consistency when structural complexity combines with visually integrated presentations. These findings highlight critical gaps in current multimodal capabilities, providing insights for advancing more robust and reliable table understanding models.

</details>


### [23] [GATHER: Convergence-Centric Hyper-Entity Retrieval for Zero-Shot Cell-Type Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06403)
*Zhonghui Zhang,Feng Jiang,Shaowei Qin,Jiahao Zhao,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GATHER，一种专为超实体查询设计的收敛中心检索器。通过全局多源图遍历识别拓扑收敛点作为高信息超实体，避免了局部实体探索的局限性，在单细胞细胞类型注释任务上实现了显著的性能提升和LLM调用次数的大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于知识图的RAG方法通过从源实体扩展和迭代LLM推理来检索证据。但在单细胞注释场景中，每个查询包含数十至数百个基因，没有单个基因具有决定性，标签只能从基因的集体共现中涌现。这种超实体查询从根本上挑战了基于单个基因的局部实体探索策略，导致可扩展性差和LLM成本高昂。

Method: GATHER执行全局多源图遍历，识别拓扑收敛点（可从多个输入基因共同到达的节点）；通过节点和路径重要性评分选择信息丰富的证据，整个检索过程无需LLM参与。在自建的细胞中心生物知识图谱(VCKG)上实例化。

Result: 在Immune和Lung两个数据集上，GATHER优于强基线方法(ToG, ToG-2, RoG, PoG)，实现了最高精确匹配准确率(27.45%和59.64%)，同时每个样本仅需1次LLM调用，而基线方法需要2-61次调用。

Conclusion: GATHER通过收敛节点压缩多实体信号为紧凑高信息证据，提供了比局部实体推理更高效的全局替代方案，在细胞类型注释任务上实现了显著的性能提升和成本降低。

Abstract: Zero-shot single-cell cell-type annotation aims to determine a cell's type from a given set of expressed genes without any training. Existing knowledge-graph-based RAG approaches retrieve evidence by expanding from source entities and relying on iterative LLM reasoning. However, in this setting each query contains tens to hundreds of genes, where no single gene is decisive and the label emerges only from their collective co-occurrence. Such hyper-entity queries fundamentally challenge local, entity-wise exploration strategies, which reason from individual genes, leading to poor scalability and substantial LLM cost. We propose GATHER (Graph-Aware Traversal with Hyper-Entity Retrieval), a convergence-centric retriever tailored to hyper-entity queries. It performs global multi-source graph traversal and identifies topological convergence points -- nodes jointly reachable from many input genes. These convergence nodes act as high-information hyper-entities that capture entity synergy. By incorporating node- and path-importance scoring, GATHER selects informative evidence entirely without LLM involvement during retrieval. Instantiated on a self-constructed cell-centric biological knowledge graph (VCKG), GATHER outperforms strong KG-RAG baselines (ToG, ToG-2, RoG, PoG) on two datasets (Immune and Lung), achieving the highest exact-match accuracy (27.45% and 59.64%) with only a single LLM call per sample, compared to 2--61 calls for KG-RAG baselines. Our results demonstrate that convergence nodes compress multi-entity signals into compact, high-information evidence that conveys more per item than multi-hop paths, providing an efficient global alternative to local entity-wise reasoning.

</details>


### [24] [Tatarstan Toponyms: A Bilingual Dataset and Hybrid RAG System for Geospatial Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05962)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多语言地名数据的自动地理空间问答系统，构建鞑靼斯坦双语地名数据集（9,688条记录，93.1%有坐标）和39,000条问答语料。混合检索器整合多语言语义索引与KD树空间过滤，Recall@1达0.988。XLM-RoBERTa-large在Reader评测中EM=0.992、F1=0.994，RuBERT后处理后也可达到100%准确率。所有资源已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间问答系统缺乏多语言地名（toponym）数据的支持，难以处理复杂的地名语义和空间关系查询。需要构建标准化的多语言地名知识库和配套的问答系统以服务地理编码和数字人文研究。

Method: 1) 构建鞑靼斯坦双语地名数据集（含语言、词源、行政区划、坐标信息，93.1%有地理标记）；2) 基于该数据集构造约39,000条问答三元组并保证答案本地化；3) 开发混合检索器：多语言e5-large密集语义索引 + KD树和Haversine距离的地理空间过滤；4) 评测三种Reader架构：RuBERT、XLM-RoBERTa-large、T5-RUS；5) 后处理消除数值差距。

Result: 混合检索在500条测试查询上：Recall@1=0.988, Recall@5=1.000, MRR=0.994，显著优于BM25和纯空间方法。Reader评测中XLM-RoBERTa-large最佳：EM=0.992, F1=0.994。原始输出RuBERT在坐标问题上F1=0，但后处理后恢复100%准确率，差异源于tokenization和预训练语料差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为多语言地区的地理空间QA服务、地理编码和数字人文应用提供了完整解决方案。所有资源（数据集、QA语料、模型权重、Web演示）已开源发布。消融实验表明，简单后处理即可消除RuBERT在数值问题上的缺陷，证明tokenization和预训练语料对模型性能影响显著。

Abstract: This paper addresses automatic geospatial question answering over multilingual toponymic data. An original bilingual dataset of toponyms of the Republic of Tatarstan is introduced, comprising 9,688 structured records with linguistic, etymological, administrative, and coordinate information (93.1% georeferenced). Based on this dataset, a question-answering corpus of approximately 39,000 question-context-answer triples is constructed with guaranteed answer localization. A hybrid retriever integrates dense semantic indexing (multilingual-e5-large) with geospatial filtering via KD-trees and haversine distance. On 500 test queries, the hybrid search achieves Recall@1=0.988, Recall@5=1.000, and MRR=0.994, significantly outperforming BM25 and purely spatial methods. Among tested reader architectures (RuBERT, XLM-RoBERTa-large, T5-RUS), XLM-RoBERTa-large attains the best quality: EM=0.992, F1=0.994. On raw outputs, RuBERT models fail on coordinate questions (F1=0) while XLM-RoBERTa-large reaches F1=0.984; however, simple post-processing eliminates numerical gaps and restores RuBERT accuracy to 100%. This discrepancy stems from tokenization differences and pre-training corpora composition. All resources (dataset, QA corpus, model weights, web demo) are openly published on Hugging Face. Results apply to geospatial QA services, geocoding, and digital humanities in multilingual regions.

</details>


### [25] [From Articles to Premises: Building PrimeFacts, an Extraction Methodology and Resource for Fact-Checking Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06006)
*Premtim Sahitaj,Jawan Kolanowski,Ariana Sahitaj,Veronika Solopova,Max Upravitelev,Daniel Röder,Iffat Maab,Junichi Yamagishi,Sebastian Möller,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PrimeFacts方法论和资源，从事实核查文章中提取细粒度证据。通过利用文章内超链接作为证据锚点，结合LLM将锚点句子去语境化为独立前提句，构建了包含13,106篇PolitiFact文章和49,718个证据锚点的大规模数据集。实验表明去语境化证据显著提升了检索效果（MRR相对提升30%）和判决预测性能（Macro-F1提升10-20点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实核查文章包含丰富的支持性证据和推理，但这些证据以非结构化方式呈现，难以被自动化验证系统访问和利用。

Method: 利用大型语言模型（LLM）将文章内超链接锚点句子改写为独立的、去语境化的前提句，并探索提取额外隐含证据的方法。在交叉文章证据检索和声明验证任务上评估提取的前提句效果。

Result: 去语境化证据检索率显著提升，Mean Reciprocal Rank相对提升达30%；用于判决预测时，Macro-F1提升10-20个百分点。提升在2类与5类判决粒度、不同模型架构下均保持一致。定性分析表明去语境化前提保持了与原始来源的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了从真实事实核查文章中复用证据用于自动化系统的可行性，并提供了一个大规模的结构化证据资源（13,106篇PolitiFact文章，49,718个证据锚点），为事实核查自动化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Fact-checking articles encode rich supporting evidence and reasoning, yet this evidence remains largely inaccessible to automated verification systems due to unstructured presentation. We introduce PrimeFacts, a methodology and resource for extracting fine-grained evidence from full fact-checking articles. We compile 13,106 PolitiFact articles with claims, verdicts, and all referenced sources, and we identify 49,718 in-article hyperlinks as natural anchors to pinpoint key evidence. Our framework leverages large language models (LLMs) to rewrite these anchor sentences into stand-alone, context-independent premises and investigates the extraction of additional implicit evidence. In evaluations on cross-article evidence retrieval and claim verification, the extracted premises substantially improve performance. Decontextualized evidence yields higher retrievability, achieving up to a 30 percent relative gain in Mean Reciprocal Rank over verbatim sentences, and using the evidence for verdict prediction raises Macro-F1 by 10-20 points over the baseline. These gains are consistent across different verdict granularities (2-class vs. 5-class) and model architectures. A qualitative analysis indicates that the decontextualized premises remain faithful to the original sources. Our work highlights the promise of reusing fact-checkers' evidence for automation and provides a large-scale resource of structured evidence from real-world fact-checks.

</details>


### [26] [More Aligned, Less Diverse? Analyzing the Grammar and Lexicon of Two Generations of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06030)
*Adrián Gude,Roi Santos-Ríos,Francis Bond,Dan Flickinger,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez,Olga Zamaraeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较了LLM生成文本与人类撰写文本（英语新闻）的句法特性，使用HPSG形式语法框架和生态学/信息论的多样性指标进行分析，发现较新的LLM比旧模型表现出更低的句法和词汇多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究趋势表明需要系统评估LLM生成文本与人类文本的差异，特别是在语法层面的变化尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 采用HPSG（中心词驱动的短语结构语法）形式框架，分析两代LLM与两个不同年份的人类撰写英语新闻数据集的句法结构和词汇类型分布，并使用生态学和信息论的多样性指标进行量化。

Result: 英语新闻文本在给定时间范围内变化甚微；较新的LLM表现出降低的句法多样性，尤其是词汇多样性，与早期非指令微调模型相比更为显著。

Conclusion: 指令微调虽增强了连贯性和指令遵循能力，但可能收窄了模型输出的表达范围，未来需进一步研究指令微调对语言表达能力的影响。

Abstract: This study contributes to a growing line of research in comparing LLM-generated texts with human-authored text, in this case, English news text. We focus in particular on the evaluation of syntactic properties through formal grammar frameworks. Our analysis compares two generations of LLMs in the context of two human-authored English news datasets from two different years. Employing the Head-Driven Phrase Structure Grammar (HPSG) formalism, we investigate the distributions of syntactic structures and lexical types of AI-generated texts and contrast them with the corresponding distributions in the human-authored New York Times (NYT) articles. We use diversity metrics from ecology and information theory to quantify variation in grammatical constructions and lexical types. We show that English news text has changed little in the given time frame, while newer LLMs display reduced syntactic and, especially, lexical diversity compared to older, non-instruction-tuned models. These findings point to future work in studying effects of instruction tuning, which, while enhancing coherence and adherence to prompts, may narrow the expressive range of model output.

</details>


### [27] [Navigating by Old Maps: The Pitfalls of Static Mechanistic Localization in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06076)
*Hang Chen,Jiaying Zhu,Hongyang Chen,Hongxu Liu,Xinyu Yang,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究验证了"Locate-then-Update"范式中一个未经验证的基本假设，通过系统性追踪Transformer电路在监督微调过程中的结构演化，发现电路存在"自由演化"特性，导致基于当前状态的静态机制无法有效指导未来参数更新，从而揭示了现有方法存在"有效性幻觉"，呼吁在机制定位中引入预见性视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Locate-then-Update"范式假设从当前静态参数推导的机制能可靠地引导未来动态参数更新，但这一基本假设尚未得到验证。

Method: 引入三个新指标分析电路演化：Circuit Distance（神经迁移）、Circuit Stability（语义稳定性）、Circuit Conflict（跨任务干扰）；系统追踪Transformer电路在SFT过程中的结构演化。

Result: 电路在参数更新过程中固有地表现出"自由演化"特性；静态机制因时间延迟而不足以指导未来状态。

Conclusion: 静态机制存在时间延迟问题，不足以指导未来状态更新；现有方法存在"有效性幻觉"；呼吁在机制定位中引入"预见性"，提出预测性框架。

Abstract: The "Locate-then-Update" paradigm has become a predominant approach in the post-training of large language models (LLMs), identifying critical components via mechanistic interpretability for targeted parameter updates. However, this paradigm rests on a fundamental yet unverified assumption: can mechanisms derived from current static parameters reliably guide future dynamic parameter updates? To investigate this, we systematically track the structural evolution of Transformer circuits throughout the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) process, revealing the underlying dynamics of task mechanisms. We introduce three novel metrics-Circuit Distance, Circuit Stability, and Circuit Conflict-to analyze circuit evolution across three dimensions: neural migration, semantic stability, and cross-task interference. Our empirical results reveal that circuits inherently exhibit "Free Evolution" during parameter updates. Consequently, static mechanisms extracted from current states inevitably suffer from temporal latency, making them fundamentally inadequate for guiding future states. Moreover, by deconstructing the "illusion of effectiveness" in existing methods, this work underscores the necessity of "foresight" in mechanistic localization and proposes a predictive framework for future research.

</details>


### [28] [Milestone-Guided Policy Learning for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06078)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuchen Yan,Hongxing Li,Teng Pan,Dingming Li,Ruiqing Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BEACON addresses reinforcement learning challenges in training long-horizon language agents through milestone-guided policy learning that ensures precise credit assignment by partitioning trajectories and shaping rewards at segment boundaries.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training long-horizon agentic language agents with RL is challenging due to two root causes: (1) credit misattribution where correct early actions are penalized due to terminal failures, and (2) sample inefficiency where scarce successful trajectories result in near-total loss of learning signal.

Method: BEACON partitions trajectories at milestone boundaries, applies temporal reward shaping within segments, and estimates advantages at dual scales to prevent distant failures from corrupting local action evaluation.

Result: On ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, BEACON consistently outperforms GRPO and GiGPO. On long-horizon ALFWorld tasks, BEACON achieves 92.9% success rate (vs GRPO's 53.5%) and improves effective sample utilization from 23.7% to 82.0%.

Conclusion: BEACON establishes milestone-anchored credit assignment as an effective paradigm for training long-horizon language agents, nearly doubling success rates while improving sample utilization efficiency.

Abstract: While long-horizon agentic tasks require language agents to perform dozens of sequential decisions, training such agents with reinforcement learning remains challenging. We identify two root causes: credit misattribution, where correct early actions are penalized due to terminal failures, and sample inefficiency, where scarce successful trajectories result in near-total loss of learning signal. We introduce a milestone-guided policy learning framework, BEACON, that leverages the compositional structure of long-horizon tasks to ensure precise credit assignment. BEACON partitions trajectories at milestone boundaries, applies temporal reward shaping within segments to credit partial progress, and estimates advantages at dual scales to prevent distant failures from corrupting the evaluation of local actions. On ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, BEACON consistently outperforms GRPO and GiGPO. Notably, on long-horizon ALFWorld tasks, BEACON achieves 92.9% success rate, nearly doubling GRPO's 53.5%, while improving effective sample utilization from 23.7% to 82.0%. These results establish milestone-anchored credit assignment as an effective paradigm for training long-horizon language agents. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/BEACON.

</details>


### [29] [Uncovering Entity Identity Confusion in Multimodal Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06096)
*Shu Wu,Xiaotian Ye,Xinyu Mou,Dongsheng Liu,Xiaohan Wang,Mengqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文首次识别并命名了多模态知识编辑中的"实体身份混淆"（EIC）失败模式，揭示其根源在于现有方法混淆了图像-实体绑定与实体-实体关系知识，并提出通过约束编辑至I-E处理阶段来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态知识编辑（MKE）旨在修正大视觉-语言模型部署后的内部知识，但编辑后模型的行为模式尚缺乏充分探索。

Method: 构建EC-Bench诊断基准，直接探测编辑前后图像-实体绑定的变化；分析EIC根源在于现有方法未能区分I-E绑定与E-E关系知识；探索将编辑约束至模型I-E处理阶段的缓解策略。

Result: EIC根源在于现有方法无法区分I-E绑定与E-E关系知识，模型将E-E关联作为捷径：图像仍被感知为原始实体，新实体名称仅作为虚假身份标签。将编辑约束至I-E处理阶段可显著减少EIC。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有的多模态知识编辑方法未能有效区分图像-实体绑定与实体-实体关系知识，导致模型过度依赖捷径。约束编辑作用于I-E处理阶段可显著减少EIC。该工作为 faithful MKE 提供了原则性需求和方法论指导。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge editing (MKE) aims to correct the internal knowledge of large vision-language models after deployment, yet the behavioral patterns of post-edit models remain underexplored. In this paper, we identify a systemic failure mode in edited models, termed Entity Identity Confusion (EIC): edited models exhibit an absurd behavior where text-only queries about the original entity's identity unexpectedly return information about the new entity. To rigorously investigate EIC, we construct EC-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that directly probes how image-entity bindings shift before and after editing. Our analysis reveals that EIC stems from existing methods failing to distinguish between Image-Entity (I-E) binding and Entity-Entity (E-E) relational knowledge in the model, causing models to overfit E-E associations as a shortcut: the image is still perceived as the original entity, with the new entity's name serving only as a spurious identity label. We further explore potential mitigation strategies, showing that constraining edits to the model's I-E processing stage encourages edits to act more faithfully on I-E binding, thereby substantially reducing EIC. Based on these findings, we discuss principled desiderata for faithful MKE and provide methodological guidance for future research.

</details>


### [30] [MemReranker: Reasoning-Aware Reranking for Agent Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06132)
*Chunyu Li,Jingyi Kang,Ding Chen,Mengyuan Zhang,Jiajun Shen,Bo Tang,Xuanhe Zhou,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemReranker是一个针对Agent记忆系统设计的重排模型家族(0.6B/4B)，通过多阶段LLM知识蒸馏解决语义匹配与推理能力脱节问题，在内存检索基准上以小参数量实现与GPT-4o-mini和Gemini-3-Flash相当性能，同时保持低延迟和领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索-重排系统依赖语义相似度匹配，缺乏真正推理能力，导致召回结果语义相关但无法提供回答问题的关键信息。具体表现为：相关度分数校准不当、复杂查询（时序约束、因果推理）下排序退化、无法利用对话上下文消歧。

Method: 基于Qwen3-Reranker的多阶段LLM知识蒸馏框架，包含三个核心技术：(1)多教师成对比较生成校准软标签；(2)BCE逐点蒸馏建立均匀分布分数；(3)InfoNCE对比学习增强难例区分能力。训练数据融合通用语料与记忆专用多轮对话数据。

Result: MemReranker-0.6B大幅超越BGE-Reranker，关键指标持平4B/8B模型及GPT-4o-mini；4B模型达0.737 MAP，多项指标与Gemini-3-Flash相当，推理延迟仅大模型的10-20%；金融医疗垂直领域保持主流大模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: MemReranker通过多阶段LLM知识蒸馏成功解决了记忆系统中检索-重排范式的核心痛点。0.6B模型在内存检索基准上显著超越基线，4B模型达到0.737 MAP且推理延迟仅为大模型的10-20%，同时保持金融医疗领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: In agent memory systems, the reranking model serves as the critical bridge connecting user queries with long-term memory. Most systems adopt the "retrieve-then-rerank" two-stage paradigm, but generic reranking models rely on semantic similarity matching and lack genuine reasoning capabilities, leading to a problem where recalled results are semantically highly relevant yet do not contain the key information needed to answer the question. This deficiency manifests in memory scenarios as three specific problems. First, relevance scores are miscalibrated, making threshold-based filtering difficult. Second, ranking degrades when facing temporal constraints, causal reasoning, and other complex queries. Third, the model cannot leverage dialogue context for semantic disambiguation. This report introduces MemReranker, a reranking model family (0.6B/4B) built on Qwen3-Reranker through multi-stage LLM knowledge distillation. Multi-teacher pairwise comparisons generate calibrated soft labels, BCE pointwise distillation establishes well-distributed scores, and InfoNCE contrastive learning enhances hard-sample discrimination. Training data combines general corpora with memory-specific multi-turn dialogue data covering temporal constraints, causal reasoning, and coreference resolution. On the memory retrieval benchmark, MemReranker-0.6B substantially outperforms BGE-Reranker and matches open-source 4B/8B models as well as GPT-4o-mini on key metrics. MemReranker-4B further achieves 0.737 MAP, with several metrics on par with Gemini-3-Flash, while maintaining inference latency at only 10--20\% of large models. On finance and healthcare vertical-domain benchmarks, the models preserve generalization capabilities on par with mainstream large-parameter rerankers.

</details>


### [31] [IRC-Bench: Recognizing Entities from Contextual Cues in First-Person Reminiscences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06142)
*Yehudit Aperstein,Eden Moran,Alexander Apartsin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IRC-Bench是首个针对回忆录转录本中隐式实体识别的基准数据集，通过Entity-Grounded与Entity-Elided叙事配对解决非局部性挑战，评估显示QLoRA Llama 3.1 8B和微调DPR分别为开放世界和封闭世界最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回忆录叙述中人们常通过语境线索间接提及人物、地点和事件，而非显式名称。这种隐式引用构成计算难题：目标实体需从分散的叙述证据中推断，而非局部提及。现有NLP任务（命名实体识别、实体链接、共指消解）无法有效处理此类"非局部性"问题。

Method: IRC-Bench从1,994份回忆录转录本中构建了25,136个样本，涵盖11个主题领域，包含12,337个Wiki-data链接实体。每个样本由Entity-Grounded Narrative（含显式实体提及）和Entity-Elided Narrative（移除直接提及）配对组成。评估了19种配置，涵盖LLM生成、密集检索、RAG和微调方法。

Result: 开放世界场景中，QLoRA适配的Llama 3.1 8B表现最佳（38.94%精确匹配；51.59% Jaccard）；封闭世界检索中，微调DPR领先（35.38% Hit@1；71.49% Hit@10）。

Conclusion: 发布IRC-Bench基准数据集，包含数据、代码和评估工具，支持开源复现。该基准针对非局部性这一区别于传统NER、实体链接和共指消解的核心挑战，为回忆录转录本中的隐式实体识别提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: When people recount personal memories, they often refer to people, places, and events indirectly, relying on contextual cues rather than explicit names. Such implicit references are central to reminiscence narratives: first-person accounts of lived experience used in therapeutic, archival, and social settings. They pose a difficult computational problem because the intended entity must be inferred from dispersed narrative evidence rather than from a local mention. We introduce IRC-Bench, the Implicit Reminiscence Context Benchmark, for evaluating implicit entity recognition in reminiscence transcripts. The benchmark targets non-locality: entity-identifying cues are distributed across multiple, non-contiguous clauses, unlike named entity recognition, entity linking, or coreference resolution. IRC-Bench comprises 25,136 samples constructed from 12,337 Wiki-data-linked entities across 1,994 transcripts spanning 11 thematic domains. Each sample pairs an Entity-Grounded Narrative, in which the target entity is explicitly mentioned, with an Entity-Elided Narrative, in which direct mentions are removed. We evaluate 19 configurations across LLM generation, dense retrieval, RAG, and fine-tuning. QLoRA-adapted Llama 3.1 8B performs best in the open-world setting (38.94% exact match; 51.59% Jaccard), while fine-tuned DPR leads closed-world retrieval (35.38% Hit@1; 71.49% Hit@10). We release IRC-Bench with data, code, and evaluation tools.

</details>


### [32] [A$^2$TGPO: Agentic Turn-Group Policy Optimization with Adaptive Turn-level Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06200)
*Dingwei Chen,Zefang Zong,Zhipeng Ma,Leo Luo,Yang Li,Chengming Li,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出A²TGPO方法，通过回合组归一化、方差重缩放折扣累积和自适应回合级裁剪三个机制，解决了信息增益信号在强化学习训练中的三个系统性问题，提升了agentic大型语言模型的多轮交互中个别工具调用的贡献评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于信息增益(IG)的强化学习训练存在三个系统性问题：在不同位置上下文中归一化会扭曲单个回合的相对地位；可变数量的累积项导致优势量级随轨迹深度漂移；固定裁剪范围对具有截然不同IG信号的回合采用相同的策略更新规则。

Method: 提出A²TGPO方法，包含三个核心组件：1）回合组归一化：在每个(提示词, 回合索引)组内对IG进行归一化，确保同一交互深度的回合仅与同层级的样本比较；2）方差重缩放的折扣累积：用累积归一化IG除以累积项数的平方根，保持不同回合位置的优势量级可比；3）自适应回合级裁剪：根据归一化IG动态调整每个回合的裁剪范围，为信息丰富的回合拓宽更新区域，为信息匮乏的回合缩小更新范围。

Result: A²TGPO保留了IG作为内在过程信号的优势，同时通过重新设计归一化、累积和消费机制来解决上述三个挑战。

Conclusion: A²TGPO通过重新设计IG信号的归一化、累积和消费方式，有效解决了传统方法中的三个系统性挑战，使每个交互深度下的回合都能获得公平的比较，优势量级在不同回合位置保持可比性，并且每个回合的更新范围能根据其信息量自适应调整，从而提升agentic LLM的强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for agentic large language models (LLMs) typically relies on a sparse, trajectory-level outcome reward, making it difficult to evaluate the contribution of individual tool-calls within multi-turn interactions. Existing approaches to such process credit assignment either depend on separate external process reward models that introduce additional consumption, or tree-based structural rollout that merely redistributes the outcome signal while constraining trajectory diversity. A promising alternative leverages the per-turn change in the policy's predicted probability of the ground-truth, termed Information Gain (IG), as an intrinsic process signal without an external evaluator. However, prior work on leveraging IG signals within the RL training loop faces three systematic challenges: normalizing across turns that face heterogeneous positional contexts can distort the relative standing of individual turns, accumulating a variable number of terms causes advantage magnitudes to drift with trajectory depth, and a fixed clipping range governs policy updates identically for turns with vastly different IG signals. In this paper, we propose A$^2$TGPO (Agentic Turn-Group Policy Optimization with Adaptive Turn-level Clipping), which retains IG as the intrinsic signal but re-designs how it is normalized, accumulated, and consumed: (i) turn-group normalization: normalizes IG within each (prompt, turn-index) group so that each turn is compared only against peers at the same interaction depth; (ii) variance-rescaled discounted accumulation: divides cumulative normalized IG by square root of accumulated terms to keep advantage magnitudes comparable across turn positions; and (iii) adaptive turn-level clipping: modulates each turn's clipping range based on its normalized IG, widening the update region for informative turns and narrowing it for uninformative ones.

</details>


### [33] [TIDE: Every Layer Knows the Token Beneath the Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06216)
*Ajay Jaiswal,Lauren Hannah,Han-Byul Kim,Duc Hoang,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Minsik Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We revisit a universally accepted but under-examined design choice in every modern LLM: a token index is looked up once at the input embedding layer and then permanently discarded. This single-injection assumption induces two structural failures: (i) the Rare Token Problem, where a Zipf-type distribution of vocabulary causes rare-token embeddings are chronically under-trained due to receiving a fraction of the cumulative gradient signal compared to common tokens; and (ii) the Contextual Collapse Problem, where limited parameters models map distributionally similar tokens to indistinguishable hidden states. As an attempt to address both, we propose TIDE, which augments the standard transformer with EmbeddingMemory: an ensemble of K independent MemoryBlocks that map token indices to context-free semantic vectors, computed once and injected into every layer through a depth-conditioned softmax router with a learnable null bank. We theoretically and empirically establish the benefits of TIDE in addressing the issues associated with single-token identity injection as well as improve performance across multiple language modeling and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [34] [UniPrefill: Universal Long-Context Prefill Acceleration via Block-wise Dynamic Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06221)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniPrefill提出了一种通用预填充加速框架，通过token级别计算加速实现对几乎任意模型架构的支持。该框架以continuous batching算子的形式实现，支持prefill-decode协同处理和tensor并行，可无缝集成到vLLM中，在TTFT上实现最高2.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预填充加速方法主要基于稀疏注意力机制，仅在全注意力模型上能达到最大加速效果。当应用于线性注意力/滑动窗口注意力等新型混合架构时，这些方法性能显著下降。此外，这些方法通常与continuous batching不兼容，难以集成到vLLM等现代推理引擎中。

Method: 提出UniPrefill框架，通过直接在token级别加速模型计算来实现预填充加速。该框架被实现为continuous batching算子，并扩展了vLLM的调度策略以原生支持prefill-decode协同处理和tensor并行，使UniPrefill能够无缝集成到vLLM中。

Result: UniPrefill在Time-To-First-Token (TTFT)指标上实现最高2.1倍加速，且随着并发请求数量增加，加速效果愈发显著。
