TL;DR: 本文针对低资源口语方言（尤其是阿拉伯语方言）的语义切分问题，创建了一个多类型基准数据集（1000+样本），涵盖电话对话、播客、新闻广播和小说对话四种体裁。研究表明现有模型在标准MSA新闻体裁上表现良好，但在方言转录语音上性能下降。作者提出了一种针对局部语义连贯性和话语不连续性鲁棒性的切分模型，在方言非新闻体裁上显著优于基线模型，且该方法可泛化至其他低资源口语语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义切分模型主要针对高资源书面文本开发和评估，对低资源口语变体的有效性受限。阿拉伯语方言具有非正式句法、代码切换和弱标记话语结构等特征，传统切分方法面临严峻挑战。

Method: 构建了一个包含4种体裁（电话对话、播客、新闻、文学对话）、由母语者标注验证的多类型基准数据集；提出一种针对局部语义连贯性和话语不连续性鲁棒性的语义切分模型。

Result: 实验表明，在MSA新闻体裁上表现良好的切分模型在方言转录语音上性能显著下降；提出的模型在方言非新闻体裁上持续优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 该基准数据集和切分方法具有良好的可扩展性，可泛化至其他低资源口语语言，为低资源话语分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is a core component of discourse analysis, yet existing models are primarily developed and evaluated on high-resource written text, limiting their effectiveness on low-resource spoken varieties. In particular, dialectal Arabic exhibits informal syntax, code-switching, and weakly marked discourse structure that challenge standard segmentation approaches. In this paper, we introduce a new multi-genre benchmark (more than 1000 samples) for semantic segmentation in conversational Arabic, focusing on dialectal discourse. The benchmark covers transcribed casual telephone conversations, code-switched podcasts, broadcast news, and expressive dialogue from novels, and was annotated and validated by native Arabic annotators. Using this benchmark, we show that segmentation models performing well on MSA news genres degrade on dialectal transcribed speech. We further propose a segmentation model that targets local semantic coherence and robustness to discourse discontinuities, consistently outperforming strong baselines on dialectal non-news genres. The benchmark and approach generalize to other low-resource spoken languages.

</details>


### [38] [Quantifying the Statistical Effect of Rubric Modifications on Human-Autorater Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06283)
*Jessica Huynh,Alfredo Gomez,Athiya Deviyani,Renee Shelby,Jeffrey P. Bigham,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究 rubric 设计（整体式 vs 分析式评分）对人机评分一致性的影响，发现示例丰富、减少位置偏差可提高一致性，而复杂度增加和保守聚合方法会降低一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 作为评判者（autorater）在评估和内容审核中的广泛应用，如何设计 rubric 以提高人类评分者与自动评分者之间的一致性成为关键问题。

Method: 在自动作文评分（AES）和指令遵循评估两个领域，对比分析 holistic judgments（整体判断）与 analytic judgments（分析判断）两类 rubric 设计对人机评分一致性的影响。

Result: 提供代表性示例和额外上下文、减少位置偏差的 rubric 修改可提高人机一致性；而 rubric 复杂度增加和保守聚合方法则会降低一致性。

Conclusion: 实践者应仔细分析特定领域和 rubric 的表现，审慎设计评分标准以提升人类评分者与自动评分者之间的一致性。

Abstract: Autoraters, also referred to as LLM-as-judges, are increasingly used for evaluation and automated content moderation. However, there is limited statistical analysis of how modifications in a rubric presented to both humans and autoraters affect their score agreement. Rubrics that ask for an overall or \emph{holistic} judgment - for example, rating the ``quality'' of an essay - may be inconsistently interpreted due to the complexity or subjectivity of the criteria. Conversely, rubrics can ask for \emph{analytic} judgments, which decompose assessment criteria - for example, ``quality'' into ``fluency'' and ``organization''. While these rubrics can be edited to improve the individual accuracy of both human and automated scoring, this approach may result in disagreement between the two scores, or with the associated holistic judgment. Designing and deploying reliable autoraters requires understanding not just the relationship between human and autorater annotations but how that relationship changes as holistic or analytic judgments are elicited. The results indicate that rubric edits providing representative examples and additional context, and reducing positional bias in the rubric increased human-autorater agreement, while higher rubric complexity and conservative aggregation methods tended to decrease it. The findings from the automatic essay scoring and instruction-following evaluation domains suggest that practitioners should carefully analyze domain- and rubric-specific performance to move towards higher human-autorater agreement.

</details>


### [39] [LatentRAG: Latent Reasoning and Retrieval for Efficient Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06285)
*Yijia Zheng,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRAG是一种新颖的检索增强生成框架，通过将推理和检索从离散语言空间转移到连续潜在空间，在单次前向传播中直接生成潜在思维和子查询标记。该方法避免了自回归生成的高延迟开销，同时通过与密集检索模型的对齐实现高效检索。实验证明其性能可比肩显式Agentic RAG，但推理速度提升约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单步RAG在处理复杂问题时表现不足，而Agentic RAG虽然通过多步迭代检索提升了效果，但存在高延迟问题。这是因为Agentic RAG需要自回归生成大量自然语言形式的中间思维和子查询，导致推理效率低下。

Method: LatentRAG将推理和检索从离散语言空间转移到连续潜在空间。该框架利用LLM的隐藏状态直接在单次前向传播中生成潜在思维和子查询标记，无需自回归生成冗长的自然语言文本。同时，通过在潜在空间中对齐LLM与密集检索模型，支持对潜在子查询标记进行检索，并实现端到端联合优化。此外，引入并行潜在解码机制将潜在标记还原为自然语言，提升可解释性。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的实验表明，LatentRAG在性能上与显式Agentic RAG方法相当，同时将推理延迟降低约90%，显著缩小了与单步RAG的延迟差距。

Conclusion: 实验表明，LatentRAG在保持与显式Agentic RAG相当性能的同时，将推理延迟降低约90%，大幅缩小了与单步RAG之间的延迟差距，为RAG系统的高效化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Single-step retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides an efficient way to incorporate external information for simple question answering tasks but struggles with complex questions. Agentic RAG extends this paradigm by replacing single-step retrieval with a multi-step process, in which the large language model (LLM) acts as a search agent that generates intermediate thoughts and subqueries to iteratively interact with the retrieval system. This iterative process incurs substantial latency due to the autoregressive generation of lengthy thoughts and subqueries. To address this limitation, we propose LatentRAG, a novel framework that shifts both reasoning and retrieval from discrete language space to continuous latent space. Unlike existing explicit methods that generate natural language thoughts or subqueries token-by-token, LatentRAG produces latent tokens for thoughts and subqueries directly from the hidden states in a single forward pass. We align LLMs with dense retrieval models in the latent space, enabling retrieval over latent subquery tokens and supporting end-to-end joint optimization. To improve transparency and encourage semantically meaningful latent representations, we incorporate a parallel latent decoding mechanism that translates latent tokens back into natural language. Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets show that LatentRAG achieves performance comparable to explicit agentic RAG methods while reducing inference latency by approximately 90%, substantially narrowing the latency gap with traditional single-step RAG.

</details>


### [40] [Log-Likelihood, Simpson's Paradox, and the Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06294)
*Tom Kempton,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对当前AI生成文本检测器性能欠佳的问题，本文诊断出根本原因是token分数跨隐藏空间不同统计结构区域的错误聚合导致了辛普森悖论，并提出基于贝叶斯决策理论的局部校准方法作为原则性解决方案，可显著提升各类检测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI生成文本检测方法基于"似然假设"——机器生成文本对检测模型应该显得更概率。然而，作者发现区分人类和机器文本的token级信号在检测模型的隐藏空间中是非均匀分布的。

Method: 提出了一种局部校准方法：首先学习轻量级预测器来预测基于隐藏空间位置的分数分布，然后聚合校准后的对数似然比而非原始token分数。该方法可与任何token平均管道兼容，是一个模块化且原则性的修正方案。

Result: 单次干预即可显著且一致地提升所有基线检测器在所有数据集上的性能。例如，校准后的Fast-DetectGPT在GPT-5.4文本上的AUROC从0.63提升至0.85；作者提出的局部校准DMAP检测器在所有测试中达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯决策理论的局部校准方法，兼容任何token平均管道，可作为社区进一步改进AI生成文本检测的基础。后续可通过更丰富的分布模型、改进的校准策略、以及通过完整贝叶斯最优决策规则与隐藏空间几何信号的原则性集成来进一步提升性能。

Abstract: The ability to reliably distinguish human-written text from that generated by large language models is of profound societal importance. The dominant approach to this problem exploits the likelihood hypothesis: that machine-generated text should appear more probable to a detector language model than human-written text. However, we demonstrate that the token-level signal distinguishing human and machine text is non-uniform across the hidden space of the detector model, and naively averaging likelihood-based token scores across regions with fundamentally different statistical structure, as most detectors do, causes a form of Simpson's paradox: a strong local signal is destroyed by inappropriate aggregation. To correct for this, we introduce a learned local calibration step grounded in Bayesian decision theory. Rather than aggregating raw token scores, we first learn lightweight predictors of the score distributions conditioned on position in hidden space, and aggregate calibrated log-likelihood ratios instead. This single intervention dramatically and consistently improves detection performance across all baseline detectors and all datasets we consider. For example, our calibrated variant of Fast-DetectGPT improves AUROC from $0.63$ to $0.85$ on GPT-5.4 text, and a locally-calibrated DMAP detector we introduce achieves state-of-the-art performance across the board. That said, our central contribution is not a new detector, but a precise diagnosis of a significant cause of under-performance of existing detectors and a principled, modular remedy compatible with any token-averaging pipeline. This will serve as a foundation for the community to build upon, with natural avenues including richer distributional models, improved calibration strategies, and principled ensembling with hidden-space geometry signals via the full Bayes-optimal decision rule.

</details>


### [41] [MultiLinguahah : A New Unsupervised Multilingual Acoustic Laughter Segmentation Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06309)
*Callejas Sofia,Gomez Nahuel,Pelachaud Catherine,Ravenet Brian,Barriere Valentin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的多语言笑声分割方法，通过将笑声检测问题建模为基于能量分割音频序列的异常检测任务，利用BYOL-A编码器学习音频表征并结合Isolation Forest进行分类。实验证明该方法在非英语场景中优于现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前笑声检测和分割技术面临两个主要瓶颈：一是依赖昂贵的人工标注，二是现有数据集和模型主要基于英语语境，缺乏多语言泛化能力。

Method: 采用能量分割对音频序列进行预处理，通过BYOL-A编码器学习音频表征，然后使用Isolation Forest进行异常检测以实现笑声分割。

Result: 在四个数据集（单口喜剧、电视情景剧和AudioSet通用音频）上的实验表明，传统最先进方法在非英语环境下性能显著下降，而该方法在多语言场景下表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于无监督异常检测的多语言笑声分割方法在非英语场景中表现优于当前最先进的笑声检测算法，证明了该方法在跨语言泛化方面的有效性。

Abstract: Laughter is a social non-vocalization that is universal across cultures and languages, and is crucial for human communication, including social bonding and communication signaling. However, detecting laughter in audio is a challenging task, and segmenting is even more difficult. Currently, Machine Learning methods generally rely on costly manual annotation, and their datasets are mostly based on English contexts. Thus, we propose an unsupervised multilingual method that sets up the laughter segmentation task as an anomaly detection of energy-based segmented audio sequences. Our method applies an Isolation Forest on audio representations learned from BYOL-A encoder. We compare our method with several state-of-the-art laughter detection algorithms on four datasets, including stand-up comedy, sitcoms, and general short audio from AudioSet. Our results show that state-of-the-art methods are not optimized for multilingual contexts, while our method outperforms them in non-English settings.

</details>


### [42] [Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools: A Full-Pipeline Recipe for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326)
*Qianjia Cheng,Yuchen Zhang,Zhilin Wang,Yuxin Zuo,Shunkai Zhang,Yuchen Fan,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种综合性的TIR（工具集成推理）训练方案，通过精心设计的SFT和RLVR阶段，在保持模型文本推理能力的同时有效注入工具使用行为，最终在Qwen3 4B/30B模型上实现开源SOTA性能，AIME 2025分别达到96.7%和99.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到工具启用评估可能降低推理性能这一悖论现象，即使强思考模型几乎不进行实际工具调用；同时需解决如何在注入工具使用行为的同时不牺牲无工具推理能力的问题。

Method: 提出四项关键技术：（i）选择适合工具增强的教师轨迹问题；（ii）控制工具使用轨迹比例防止灾难性遗忘；（iii）优化pass@k和响应长度而非训练损失；（iv）基于适当SFT初始化的稳定RLVR阶段。

Result: 在Qwen3思考模型4B和30B规模上实现开源SOTA，AIME 2025分别达到96.7%和99.2%。

Conclusion: 综合SFT和RLVR的TIR训练配方是有效方案，关键在于教师轨迹可学习性、工具轨迹比例控制、训练目标选择以及稳定的RLVR阶段。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) offers a direct way to extend thinking models beyond the limits of text-only reasoning. Paradoxically, we observe that tool-enabled evaluation can degrade reasoning performance even when the strong thinking models make almost no actual tool calls. In this paper, we investigate how to inject natural tool-use behavior into a strong thinking model without sacrificing its no-tool reasoning ability, and present a comprehensive TIR recipe. We highlight that (i) the effectiveness of TIR supervised fine-tuning (SFT) hinges on the learnability of teacher trajectories, which should prioritize problems inherently suited for tool-augmented solutions; (ii) controlling the proportion of tool-use trajectories could mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of text-only reasoning capacity; (iii) optimizing for pass@k and response length instead of training loss could maximize TIR SFT gains while preserving headroom for reinforcement learning (RL) exploration; (iv) a stable RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) stage, built upon suitable SFT initialization and explicit safeguards against mode collapse, provides a simple yet remarkably effective solution. When applied to Qwen3 thinking models at 4B and 30B scales, our recipe yields models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in a wide range of benchmarks among open-source models, such as 96.7% and 99.2% on AIME 2025 for 4B and 30B, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了语言模型安全行为存在"评估-部署上下文分歧"现象：不同模型对评估框架的反应截然不同（有的在评估时更谨慎，有的在部署时更谨慎），且这种异质性依赖于评判者的选择。这表明安全基准可能无法可靠预测模型在真实部署场景中的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准常被用于推断语言模型在部署后的行为，但这种推断是脆弱的，因为模型行为可能取决于提示是否看起来像评估。现有基准缺乏对"评估-部署行为差异"的系统测量，需要控制多个混淆变量。

Method: 采用配对提示协议（paired-prompt protocol），控制释义变异、基准熟悉度和评判者框架敏感性。跨越五个指令微调检查点（来自四个开源权重家族）加上OLMo-3 base/instruct匹配的消融实验，每个检查点生成840个样本（20个配对项）。使用两种评判者进行评估：一种是内部安全分类器，另一种是跨家族分类器（Llama-Guard-3-8B）。

Result: 发现显著异质性：OLMo-3-Instruct是eval-cautious（评估谨慎型），评估框架使拒绝率比中性条件提升11.8pp（p=0.007），有害遵从率比部署条件降低3.6pp（p=0.024）；而Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini和Llama-3.1-8B是deployment-cautious（部署谨慎型），评估vs部署的拒绝效应为-9至-20pp。OLMo-3 base也表现部署谨慎模式，识别对齐为反转阶段；Llama-3.1-70B保留方向但幅度减弱。跨家族异质性是评判者依赖的。

Conclusion: 跨家族异质性取决于评判者；不同安全分类器（Llama-Guard-3-8B）会保留OLMo内部eval-cautious方向但消除跨家族对比，表明两种评判者操作化了不同的构念。这项研究揭示了安全基准推断部署行为的脆弱性，并强调了评估设计中对框架效应控制的重要性。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks are routinely treated as evidence about how a language model will behave once deployed, but this inference is fragile if behavior depends on whether a prompt looks like an evaluation. We define evaluation-context divergence as an observable within-item change in behavior induced by framing a fixed task as an evaluation, a live deployment interaction, or a neutral request, and present a paired-prompt protocol that measures it in open-weight LLMs while controlling for paraphrase variation, benchmark familiarity, and judge framing-sensitivity.
  Across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four open-weight families plus a matched OLMo-3 base/instruct ablation ($20$ paired items, $840$ generations per checkpoint), we find striking heterogeneity. OLMo-3-Instruct alone is eval-cautious -- evaluation framing raises refusal vs. neutral by $11.8$pp ($p=0.007$) and reduces harmful compliance vs. deployment by $3.6$pp ($p=0.024$, $0/20$ items inverted) -- while Mistral-Small-3.2, Phi-3.5-mini, and Llama-3.1-8B are deployment-cautious}, with marginal eval-vs-deployment refusal effects of $-9$ to $-20$pp. The matched OLMo-3 base also exhibits the deployment-cautious pattern, identifying alignment as the inversion stage; within Llama-3.1, the $70$B model preserves direction with attenuated magnitude, ruling out a simple ``small-model effect that reverses at scale.'' One caveat: the cross-family heterogeneity is judge-dependent. Re-judging with a different-family safety classifier (Llama-Guard-3-8B) preserves the within-OLMo eval-cautious direction but flattens the cross-family contrast, indicating that the two judges operationalize distinct constructs.

</details>


### [44] [Don't Lose Focus: Activation Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06342)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKOP (Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections) solves the attention rerouting problem in LLM activation steering by preserving attention patterns on critical tokens, achieving a 5-7x reduction in utility degradation while maintaining >95% steering efficacy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing activation steering methods for controlling LLM behavior suffer from a fundamental trade-off: they effectively steer model behavior but simultaneously degrade reasoning and retrieval performance. This trade-off is primarily caused by "attention rerouting" where steering vectors alter query-key matching patterns.

Method: SKOP operates by projecting steering vectors orthogonally to preserve attention patterns on focus tokens while allowing redistribution among less critical tokens. The method constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy.

Result: SKOP reduces utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Additionally, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP maintains robust performance.

Conclusion: SKOP provides a principled solution to the attention rerouting problem in LLM steering, achieving a favorable trade-off between steering efficacy and utility preservation, with particular advantages in long-context retrieval scenarios where vanilla steering fails.

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behaviour towards target behaviour by intervening in internal representations, yet it often degrades reasoning and retrieval performance. We argue that a primary cause of this trade-off is attention rerouting: steering vectors alter query-key matching, shifting attention away from contextually important tokens toward less informative ones. To address this, we propose Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections (SKOP), a steering method that constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy. SKOP achieves this by preserving attention patterns on a small set of focus tokens the model relies on for reasoning and retrieval, while allowing redistribution among less critical tail tokens. Across multiple steering benchmarks, we show that SKOP achieves the best joint steering-utility trade-off, reducing utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Our results further suggest that, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP can maintain robust performance by avoiding attention rerouting.

</details>


### [45] [SEQUOR: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Realistic Constraint Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06353)
*Beatriz Canaverde,Duarte M. Alves,José Pombal,Giuseppe Attanasio,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEQUOR是一个用于评估长时多轮对话中约束遵守能力的自动基准，发现当前模型在对话轮次增加、多约束叠加及约束动态变化时指令遵循准确率显著下降，揭示了现有模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准多聚焦单轮或短多轮场景，缺乏对模型在长时对话中指令遵循能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建了基于真实世界对话约束提取的模拟人物驱动交互，自动评估多轮对话中的约束遵守情况。

Result: 单约束下对话越长准确率下降超11%；多约束同时遵循时准确率下降超40%；约束动态添加或替换时准确率下降超9%。

Conclusion: 当前模型在多轮对话场景中仍难以可靠地遵循用户指令，SEQUOR基准为更好地评估助手指令遵循能力提供了有效方式。

Abstract: In a conversation, a helpful assistant must reliably follow user directives, even as they refine, modify, or contradict earlier requests. Yet most instruction-following benchmarks focus on single-turn or short multi-turn scenarios, leaving open how well models handle long-horizon instruction-following tasks. To bridge this gap, we present SEQUOR, an automatic benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in long multi-turn conversations. SEQUOR consists of simulated persona-driven interactions built with constraints extracted from real-world conversations. Our results show that even when following a single constraint, instruction-following accuracy consistently decreases as the conversation grows longer, with drops exceeding 11%. This decline becomes larger when models have to follow multiple constraints simultaneously, reducing their accuracy by over 40%. In scenarios where constraints are added or replaced at arbitrary points of the conversation, model accuracy decreases by more than 9%. Taken together, our results reveal that current models still struggle to follow user instructions in multi-turn conversations, and provide a way for better measuring instruction-following capabilities in assistants.

</details>


### [46] [MiA-Signature: Approximating Global Activation for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06416)
*Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Mo Yu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MiA-Signature（心灵景观激活签名），一种基于认知科学启发的压缩表示方法，用于近似LLM中全局激活模式。通过子模选择高级概念构建签名，可选地利用工作记忆迭代更新，集成到RAG和智能体系统中，在长上下文理解任务上获得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学表明可报告的意识访问与分布式记忆系统上的全局点燃相关，但个体只能部分访问激活内容，无法直接获取或枚举所有激活内容。这表明认知可能依赖紧凑表示来近似激活的全局影响。

Method: 通过子模优化选择覆盖激活上下文空间的高级概念构建MiA-Signature，并可选择性地使用工作记忆进行轻量级迭代更新。该签名作为条件信号集成到RAG和智能体系统中。

Result: 在多个长上下文理解任务中，将MiA-Signature集成到RAG和智能体系统后均获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MiA-Signature作为一种压缩表示，能够有效近似完整激活状态对下游处理的影响，同时保持计算可处理性，为LLM系统的长上下文理解提供了一种认知启发的解决方案。

Abstract: A growing body of work in cognitive science suggests that reportable conscious access is associated with \emph{global ignition} over distributed memory systems, while such activation is only partially accessible as individuals cannot directly access or enumerate all activated contents. This tension suggests a plausible mechanism that cognition may rely on a compact representation that approximates the global influence of activation on downstream processing. Inspired by this idea, we introduce the concept of \textbf{Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)}, a compressed representation of the global activation pattern induced by a query. In LLM systems, this is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, optionally refined through lightweight iterative updates using working memory. The resulting MiA-Signature serves as a conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks.

</details>


### [47] [From 124 Million Tokens to 1,021 Neologisms: A Large-Scale Pipeline for Automatic Neologism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06426)
*Diego Rossini,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个结合形态学理论与LLM的大规模英语新词检测pipeline，在5.27亿Reddit帖子上识别出1,021个候选词，其中599个经验证为真实新词。开源了完整代码和标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新词（neologism）检测对于语言演变追踪、词库建设和NLP系统更新至关重要，但大规模自动化检测面临挑战：如何界定"新词"边界、过滤噪音、验证结果。

Method: 采用规则过滤与大语言模型分类相结合的双阶段pipeline。以grammatical和extra-grammatical形态学框架定义新词边界，实现四分类（neologism/entity/foreign/none）。在5.27亿条Reddit帖子上实例化，LLM通过多数投票独立分类，最终人工验证全部1,021个候选词。

Result: 从124.6M唯一token中筛选出1,021个候选词（过滤率>99.99%），人工标注确认599个（58.7%）为真正词汇创新。多个LLM分类结果存在显著跨模型分歧。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个可复用的模块化框架，将形态学理论与大语言模型相结合，实现了大规模新词检测。58.7%的人工验证准确率表明pipeline具有实用性，但LLM间的显著分歧揭示了"新词"概念操作化的内在挑战。开源代码和数据为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a scalable, modular pipeline for automatic neologism detection that combines rule-based filtering with LLM classification. The pipeline is grounded in two complementary word-formation frameworks, grammatical and extra-grammatical morphology, which jointly define the scope of what counts as a neologism and inform a four-class classification scheme (neologism, entity, foreign, none). While designed to be modular and transferable at the architectural level, the pipeline is instantiated on 527 million English-language Reddit posts spanning 2005-2024. From this corpus, we extract 124.6 million unique tokens and reduce them by over 99.99% to yield 1,021 neologism candidates, a set small enough for manual expert verification. Multiple LLMs independently classify each candidate via majority vote, with a final verification step, revealing substantial cross-model disagreement and highlighting the challenge of operationalizing neologism detection at scale. Manual annotation of all 1,021 candidates confirms that 599 (58.7%) are genuine lexical innovations. The pipeline code, vocabulary compilation scripts, and the annotated candidate list are available at https://github.com/DiegoRossini/neologism-pipeline.

</details>


### [48] [COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435)
*Vimala Balakrishnan,Lee Zing Hii,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了文本和语言特征在COVID-19虚假新闻检测中的应用，证明传统机器学习方法（如随机森林）在此任务上有效，但特征融合并未带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容特征（尤其是文本和语言特征）在虚假新闻检测中的研究不足，尽管经验证据表明这些特征有助于区分真假新闻。

Method: 在COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集上，使用内容特征（词二元组、词性分布等），并采用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林五种机器学习算法进行实验。

Result: 随机森林表现最佳，支持向量机次之；文本和语言特征单独使用时均能改善检测效果，但合并使用并未显著提升；词二元组和词性标签的效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 文本和语言特征可以成功地用于使用传统机器学习方法（而非深度学习）检测虚假新闻。

Abstract: The use of content features, particularly textual and linguistic for fake news detection is under-researched, despite empirical evidence showing the features could contribute to differentiating real and fake news. To this end, this study investigates a selection of content features such as word bigrams, part of speech distribution etc. to improve fake news detection. We performed a series of experiments on a new dataset gathered during the COVID-19 pandemic and using Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbor, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. Random Forest yielded the best results, followed closely by Support Vector Machine, across all setups. In general, both the textual and linguistic features were found to improve fake news detection when used separately, however, combining them into a single model did not improve the detection significantly. Differences were also noted between the use of bigrams and part of speech tags. The study shows that textual and linguistic features can be used successfully in detecting fake news using the traditional machine learning approach as opposed to deep learning.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Emotion Consistency Analysis of Large Language Models in Emotional Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06476)
*Sneha Oram,Ojaswita Bhushan,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究检验了大语言模型在情绪驱动对话场景中对其自身生成响应的一致性评估能力。研究通过让模型重新评估自身生成的包含虚假预设的文本，测试了三个模型在不同情绪强度下的表现。结果表明模型普遍存在易受虚假信息影响的缺陷，尤其在适度情绪语境下更为严重，提示当前LLM在高风险情绪敏感应用中面临重大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在情绪敏感场景中的应用日益广泛，但其是否能够一致地评估自身生成的内容尚不明确。特别是在包含虚假预设的查询面前，模型的表现和脆弱性需要系统评估。

Method: 将LLM生成的文本作为查询重新输入同一模型进行评估，采用三个包含递增强度虚假预设的假声明问题，在极端情绪和适度情绪两个维度上进行测试。选用三个模型：Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT4o-mini和Mistral-7B，并结合注意力分数分析模型优先级从评估到生成的转变。

Result: LLM表现低于平均水平，容易受到查询中嵌入的虚假信念影响，在适度情绪内容上尤为突出。注意力分数分析揭示了模型优先级从评估向生成转移。

Conclusion: 研究结果对LLM在高风险、情绪敏感场景中的部署具有重要启示意义，表明当前模型在面对嵌入虚假信念的查询时存在明显漏洞。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct an analysis to examine the consistency of Large Language Models (LLMs) with respect to their own generated responses in an emotionally-driven conversational context. Specifically, the text generated by LLM is framed as a query to the same model, and its responses are subsequently assessed. This is performed with three queries across two dimensions of extreme and moderate emotions. The three queries are, in particular, false claim queries that contain inherently wrong assumptions (false presuppositions) in increasing order of intensity. Two commercial models, Claude-3.5-haiku, GPT4o-mini, and a medium-sized model, Mistral-7B, are considered in the study. Our findings indicate that LLMs exhibit below-average performance and remain vulnerable to false beliefs embedded within queries. This susceptibility is especially pronounced for moderate emotional content. Furthermore, an extended attention-score-based analysis highlights a shift in models' priority from evaluative to generative. The results raise important considerations for LLMs' deployment in high-stakes, emotionally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [50] [Litespark Inference on Consumer CPUs: Custom SIMD Kernels for Ternary Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06485)
*Nii Osae Osae Dade,Tony Morri,Moinul Hossain Rahat,Sayandip Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Litespark-Inference，一个利用定制 SIMD 内核在消费级 CPU 上高效运行三值权重 LLM 的推理框架。该方法将矩阵乘法替换为加法/减法操作，在 Apple Silicon、Intel 和 AMD 平台上均实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高计算需求限制了其普及，目前依赖昂贵的 datacenter GPU 或云端API访问。全球超过十亿台个人电脑的AI计算能力未被充分利用。三值模型理论上可通过消除浮点乘法来解决这一问题，但现有框架未充分利用这一特性。

Method: 开发了定制SIMD内核，利用整数点积指令替代矩阵乘法，将三值权重运算简化为加法和减法操作。该实现以pip安装包形式发布，可直接集成Hugging-Face生态。

Result: 在 Apple Silicon 上实现：首 token 延迟加速 9.2 倍、吞吐量提升 52 倍、内存占用降低 14 倍；在 Intel 和 AMD 处理器上也获得了类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: Litespark-Inference成功实现了在消费级硬件上高效运行三值模型的大语言模型推理框架，使超过十亿台个人电脑能够参与AI工作负载，为边缘设备上的LLM部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed artificial intelligence, but their computational requirements remain prohibitive for most users. Standard inference demands expensive datacenter GPUs or cloud API access, leaving over one billion personal computers underutilized for AI workloads. Ternary models offer a path forward: their weights are constrained to {-1, 0, +1}, theoretically eliminating the need for floating-point multiplication. However, existing frameworks fail to exploit this structure, treating ternary models as dense floating-point networks. We address this gap with custom SIMD kernels that replace matrix multiplication with simple addition and subtraction operations, targeting the integer dot product instructions available on modern CPUs. Our implementation, Litespark-Inference, is pip-installable and integrates directly with Hugging-Face, achieving 9.2x faster time-to-first-token, 52x higher throughput, and 14x memory reduction compared to standard PyTorch inference on Apple Silicon, with similar speedups on Intel and AMD processors.

</details>


### [51] [The Frequency Confound in Language-Model Surprisal and Metaphor Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06506)
*Omar Momen,Sina Zarrieß*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper questions the validity of using LM surprisal as an indicator of contextual predictability in metaphor processing. By systematically controlling for word frequency across multiple model scales and training stages, the authors show that word frequency may be the true underlying driver of metaphor novelty effects rather than contextual predictability per se.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Language model surprisal is frequently used as a proxy for contextual predictability and has been linked to metaphor novelty. However, surprisal and lexical frequency are inherently correlated, making it difficult to disentangle their independent contributions to metaphor novelty judgments.

Method: Controlled analysis of Pythia models across 8 model sizes and 154 training checkpoints, using two distinct word frequency measures alongside surprisal estimates.

Result: Word frequency consistently outperforms surprisal as a predictor of metaphor novelty. The surprisal-novelty correlation is strongest at early training stages but diminishes over continued training, coinciding with an increase in the surprisal-frequency correlation.

Conclusion: Word frequency, not surprisal, may be the primary driver of metaphor novelty ratings; reported optimal LM surprisal settings may conflate contextual predictability effects with lexical frequency effects.

Abstract: Language-model (LM) surprisal is widely used as a proxy for contextual predictability and has been reported to correlate with metaphor novelty judgments. However, surprisal is tightly intertwined with lexical frequency. We explore this interaction on metaphor novelty ratings using two different word frequency measures. We analyse surprisal estimates from eight Pythia model sizes and 154 training checkpoints. Across settings, word frequency is a stronger predictor of metaphor novelty than surprisal. Across training stages, the surprisal--novelty association peaks at an early stage and then falls again, mirroring a similarly timed increase in the surprisal--frequency association. These results suggest that the often-reported optimal LM surprisal settings may incorrectly associate contextual predictability with metaphor novelty and processing difficulty, whereas lexical frequency may be the major underlying factor.

</details>


### [52] [STALE: Can LLM Agents Know When Their Memories Are No Longer Valid?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06527)
*Hanxiang Chao,Yihan Bai,Rui Sheng,Tianle Li,Yushi Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了"隐式冲突"问题，即LLM智能体在后续观察无明确否定的情况下难以检测到先验记忆已过时。为此构建了STALE基准（含400个冲突场景、1200个评估查询），从状态解析、前提抵抗、隐式策略适应三个维度评估模型，发现当前最先进模型准确率仅55.2%，表明需要显式状态裁决机制来提升记忆鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM agent benchmarks only measure static fact retrieval, overlooking the critical ability to revise stored beliefs when new evidence emerges. The paper identifies "Implicit Conflict" as a key underexplored failure mode where later observations invalidate earlier memories without explicit negation, requiring contextual inference and commonsense reasoning to detect.

Method: Proposes a three-dimensional probing framework (State Resolution, Premise Resistance, Implicit Policy Adaptation) to evaluate LLM agents on STALE benchmark with 400 expert-validated conflict scenarios (1,200 queries across 100+ topics, contexts up to 150K tokens). Also presents CUPMem prototype that strengthens write-time revision through structured state consolidation and propagation-aware search.

Result: Frontier LLMs and specialized memory frameworks show a pervasive gap between retrieving updated evidence and acting on it. The best model achieves only 55.2% overall accuracy. Models frequently accept outdated assumptions embedded in user queries and struggle to recognize cross-domain invalidation of related memories.

Conclusion: Explicit state adjudication via structured state consolidation and propagation-aware search represents a promising direction for robust agentic memory systems.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to maintain coherent, long-term personalized memory, yet current benchmarks primarily measure static fact retrieval, overlooking the ability to revise stored beliefs when new evidence emerges. We identify a critical and underexplored failure mode, Implicit Conflict: a later observation invalidates an earlier memory without explicit negation, requiring contextual inference and commonsense reasoning to detect. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we introduce STALE, a benchmark of 400 expert-validated conflict scenarios (1,200 evaluation queries across three probing dimensions) spanning over 100 everyday topics with contexts up to 150K tokens. We propose a three-dimensional probing framework that tests State Resolution (detecting that a prior belief is outdated), Premise Resistance (rejecting queries that falsely presuppose a stale state), and Implicit Policy Adaptation (proactively applying updated states in downstream behavior). A systematic evaluation of frontier LLMs and specialized memory frameworks reveals a pervasive gap between retrieving updated evidence and acting on it, with even the best evaluated model achieving only 55.2% overall accuracy. Models often accept outdated assumptions embedded in a user's query, and they struggle to recognize when a change in one aspect of the user's state should invalidate related memories. To establish an initial baseline for state-aware memory, we further present CUPMem, a prototype that strengthens write-time revision through structured state consolidation and propagation-aware search, suggesting that explicit state adjudication is a promising direction for robust agentic memory.

</details>


### [53] [Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06546)
*Bowen Peng,Théo Gigant,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Token-Superposition Training（TST），一种两阶段drop-in预训练方法，通过多热交叉熵目标将多个连续token打包训练以提升数据吞吐量，无需修改模型架构即可在10B规模实现2.5倍预训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的预训练在规模化时计算成本极高且效率低下，传统的改进方式需要对并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构进行复杂且具有侵入性的修改，缺乏一种简单高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方法：1）叠加阶段（Superposition Phase）：将多个连续token打包为"袋"（bag），使用多热交叉熵（MCE）目标进行高效训练；2）恢复阶段（Recovery Phase）：恢复标准训练方式。同时评估了270M、600M参数模型及3B和10B规模的A1B MoE模型。

Result: TST在多种规模下均表现出高鲁棒性，始终优于基线损失和下游评估；在相同损失条件下，10B A1B规模下实现最多2.5倍的总预训练时间减少。

Conclusion: TST作为drop-in方法，无需修改并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构，即可显著提升预训练的数据吞吐量。在10B A1B规模下，最多可实现2.5倍的总预训练时间缩减，且在损失函数和下游任务评估中均优于基线。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [54] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cola DLM是一种层次化潜空间扩散语言模型，通过将文本生成重构为层次化信息分解过程（在连续潜空间中建模全局语义先验），实现生成效率、表示学习和全局语义建模的联合优化，证明了连续潜空间先验建模可作为token级语言建模的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型虽在自回归范式下取得成功，但高质量文本生成不必局限于固定的从左到右顺序。当前的替代方案仍难以同时实现生成效率、可扩展的表示学习和有效的全局语义建模。

Method: 提出层次化信息分解框架：1) Text VAE学习稳定的text-to-latent映射；2) block-causal DiT在连续潜空间中建模全局语义先验；3) 通过条件解码生成文本。从马尔可夫路径视角，其扩散过程执行潜空间先验传输而非token级观测恢复，实现全局语义组织与局部文本实现的解耦。

Result: 通过4个研究问题、8个基准数据集、与约2B参数自回归和LLaDA基线的严格匹配、以及高达约2000 EFLOPs的扩展曲线实验，验证了Cola DLM的有效配置和强扩展行为。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了层次化连续潜空间先验建模作为token级语言建模的原则性替代方案的有效性。生成质量和扩展行为可能比似然值更能反映模型能力，同时为离散文本和连续模态的统一建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [55] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lighthouse Attention 是一种训练阶段的层次化选择注意力算法，通过对称压缩 Q、K、V 序列来加速因果 Transformer 的训练，可在线训练结束后移除恢复完整注意力。在小规模 LLM 预训练实验中，该方法实现了更快的训练速度和更低的最终损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极长序列上训练因果 Transformer 时，缩放点积注意力（SDPA）的二次方时间和内存开销成为主要瓶颈，限制了模型扩展。

Method: (i) 子二次方层次化预处理/后处理步骤，实现自适应压缩和解压缩；(ii) 对称压缩策略，同时对查询、键、值进行池化，保持从左到右的因果性；(iii) 两阶段训练——先用 Lighthouse Attention 预训练大部分时间，再用短训练恢复完整注意力模型。

Result: 小规模 LLM 预训练实验中，在其他设置相同的情况下，Lighthouse Attention 实现了更快的总训练时间和恢复阶段后更低的最终损失。

Conclusion: Lighthouse Attention 作为训练辅助技术，可有效加速长序列 Transformer 训练，且训练完成后可移除，保留完整模型能力。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>


### [56] [Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06594)
*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了两种自动生成认知康复治疗临床报告的方法：基于规则的模板系统与GPT-4零样本LLM方法。评估由8名言语治疗师完成，结果显示模板系统在流畅性、一致性和结果呈现上更优，GPT-4则生成更简洁的报告。研究为低资源环境下的临床NLP系统提供了8条设计建议，并展示了如何通过对照比较来指导生成式AI在医疗领域的负责任应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知康复治疗需求增长与言语治疗师短缺的矛盾日益突出，远程康复工具产生的海量交互数据难以被临床医生高效审阅。该研究旨在为低资源环境下无参考报告的Avatar引导式居家认知康复会话自动生成临床报告。

Method: 研究采用对照实验设计，固定使用专家验证的相同结构化变量，对比两种方法：(1)编码言语治疗领域知识的规则模板系统，强调临床可靠性与可追溯性；(2)GPT-4零样本LLM方法，追求更流畅简洁的输出。由8名言语治疗师及高年级学生使用9项标准问卷进行评估。

Result: 两种方法呈现明显的权衡：模板系统在流畅性、一致性和结果呈现方面得分更高，GPT-4则输出更简洁。各评估维度的方向性差异一致，但经校正后未达统计显著水平，反映了专家临床评估的规模限制。

Conclusion: 研究提出了远程康复场景下临床报告系统的8条设计建议，并贡献了一套可复现的方法论——结合专家知识提取、分类学驱动生成与多维度人工评估，适用于低资源环境下的临床NLP。更广泛地，该研究为生成式AI在医疗领域的负责任采纳提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [57] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSD统一框架，系统研究LLM自蒸馏问题，通过整合多教师一致性、EMA稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪五大机制，在6个基准6个模型上验证了各组件的有效性及其交互规律，构建的UniSDfull集成流程实现最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法主要考察孤立的设计选择，其有效性、作用机制及相互作用尚不清晰；自回归LLM中的自蒸馏面临自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性任务依赖、合理Rationale可能提供不稳定或不可靠监督等挑战

Method: 提出了UniSD统一框架，系统整合了多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪五大机制，分别解决监督可靠性、表示对齐和训练稳定性问题

Result: UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动性能提升、以及这些组件如何在任务间相互作用

Conclusion: 构建了UniSDfull统一流程，在6个基准、6个模型上取得最佳整体性能，超越基线模型+5.4点、超越最强基线+2.8点，验证了自蒸馏作为高效LLM适应方法的可行性与可操控性

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [58] [Parser agreement and disagreement in L2 Korean UD: Implications for human-in-the-loop annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06625)
*Hakyung Sung,Gyu-Ho Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: We propose a human-in-the-loop workflow for L2 Korean morphosyntactic annotation using agreement between two domain-adapted parsers as a proxy for correctness. Evaluation confirms strong correspondence between parser and human judgments, validating semi-automatic annotation as feasible. Disagreements are linguistically predictable but present both tractable and deeper representational challenges.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L2 Korean morphosyntactic annotation is challenging and resource-intensive; need to assess whether parser agreement can serve as a reliable proxy for annotation quality in semi-automatic workflows.

Method: Simplified human-in-the-loop workflow leveraging agreement between two domain-adapted parsers; evaluation via comparison with independent human judgments on annotation correctness.

Result: Strong correspondence between parser and human judgments on annotation correctness; parser disagreements cluster in linguistically predictable domains (grammatical-relation distinctions, clause-boundary ambiguity).

Conclusion: Parser disagreements are predictable (grammatical relations, clause boundaries) but include deeper representational challenges; semi-automatic L2-Korean UD annotation is feasible but requires iterative model refinement.

Abstract: We propose a simplified human-in-the-loop workflow for second language (L2) Korean morphosyntactic annotation by leveraging agreement between two domain-adapted parsers. We first evaluate whether parser agreement can serve as a proxy for annotation correctness by comparing it with independent human judgments. The results show strong correspondence between parser and human judgments, supporting the feasibility of semi-automatic L2-Korean UD annotation. Further analysis demonstrates that parser disagreements cluster in linguistically predictable domains such as grammatical-relation distinctions and clause-boundary ambiguity. While many disagreement cases are tractable for iterative model refinement, others reflect deeper representational challenges inherent in parsing and tagging L2-Korean corpora.

</details>


### [59] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个可复现的源代码归属评估框架，通过AST解析器提取并评估LLM生成的Markdown报告中的内联引用，从链接有效性、内容相关性、事实准确性三个维度进行评估。14个模型的基准测试表明，即使是最强模型也只能达到39-77%的事实准确性，揭示了表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间的严重脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM研究智能体生成的引用报告存在可信度问题。现有方法要么依赖模型自我引用（存在偏见风险），要么使用RAG但不验证来源可访问性、相关性或事实一致性。缺乏系统性的引用评估基础设施。

Method: 提出首个源代码归属评估框架：1) 使用AST解析器提取内联引用；2) 从三个方面评估引用：Link Works（URL可访问性）、Relevant Content（主题相关性）、Fact Check（事实准确性）；3) 使用基于评分标准的LLM评判器进行基准测试，并通过人工审核校准。

Result: 测试14个闭源和开源LLM后发现：最强模型链接有效性和相关性均超过80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；不到一半的开源模型能在单次设置中成功生成引用报告；消融研究表明，当工具调用从2增加到150时，Fact Check准确率平均下降约42%。

Conclusion: 检索增加并不带来更准确的引用，揭示了表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间的严重脱节。该框架提供了必要的评估基础设施来量化这一问题，推动LLM研究智能体的可信度建设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [60] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StraTA通过引入轨迹级策略显式建模来解决LLM智能体在长时序决策中的探索和信用分配问题，结合层次化GRPO rollout、多样化策略采样和关键自我判断机制，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld三个基准上取得SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的强化学习方法大多属于纯反应式(purely reactive)范式，在长时序决策任务中存在两个核心缺陷：1) 探索能力不足(exploration weakness)；2) 信用分配困难(credit assignment weakness)。这两个问题严重制约了LLM在复杂多步任务中的表现。

Method: StraTA框架包含三个核心组件：1) 策略抽象机制：从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略(compact strategy)，并用该策略条件化后续动作生成；2) 层次化GRPO rollout设计：联合训练策略生成和动作执行模块；3) 增强机制：多样化策略 rollout + 关键自我判断(critical self-judgment)模块。

Result: ALFWorld达93.1%成功率，WebShop达84.2%成功率，SciWorld整体得分63.5%，超越前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: 显式轨迹级策略建模能有效解决长时序决策中的探索和信用分配问题，层次化框架和多样化采样策略是提升样本效率和最终性能的关键设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [61] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO是一种模块化MoE架构，通过文档级约束使专家自动按语义领域专业化，允许在内存受限环境下选择性使用部分专家子集而保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型在限制使用部分专家时性能严重下降，限制了稀疏大模型在内存受限场景下的实际应用。

Method: 通过限制同一文档内token共享专家池，同时允许不同文档使用不同池，使相似领域token自发依赖相似专家，在预训练中形成语义级专家分组（如数学、代码领域）。

Result: 1B活跃/14B总量参数的EMO-1T在大模型设定下匹配标准MoE性能；保留25%（12.5%）专家仅导致1%（3%）性能下降，而标准MoE崩溃；专家子集按语义级（数学、代码等）专业化。

Conclusion: EMO通过文档边界约束实现专家模块化，无需人工先验即可支持独立使用和组合专家子集，为大模型的高效模块化部署和可组合架构开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Are Flat Minima an Illusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05209)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 挑战"平坦极小值导致泛化"假说：证明重参数化可人为膨胀Hessian，表明平坦度并非泛化的真正原因。提出"weakness"（学习器语言中兼容学习函数的补空间体积）作为重参数化不变量的新解释。实验证明weakness与泛化正相关（MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上均显著），而sharpness负相关，simplicity仅在特定数据集有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Challenge the flat minima hypothesis for neural network generalization, motivated by the observation that function-preserving reparameterization can inflate the Hessian by two orders of magnitude without changing any prediction, proving that flatness cannot be the causal mechanism.

Method: Proposes "weakness" — the volume of completions compatible with the learned function in the learner's embodied language — as a reparameterization-invariant alternative to flatness. Conducts controlled head-to-head experiments on 100 networks with identical architecture and training on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST.

Result: On MNIST: weakness predicts generalization (ρ=+0.374, p=0.00012), sharpness anticorrelates (ρ=-0.226), simplicity predicts nothing (p=0.848). Large-batch advantage vanishes as data grows: +1.6% at n=2,000 → +0.02% at n=60,000. On Fashion-MNIST: weakness (ρ=+0.384, p=8.15×10⁻⁵) also outperforms.

Conclusion: Flat minima were never the answer. Weakness (reparameterization-invariant) is the correct driver of generalization, not flatness. PAC-Bayes bounds work because they correlate with weakness. Weakness is minimax-optimal under exchangeable demands.

Abstract: Neural networks that land in flat regions of the loss landscape tend to generalise better than those in sharp regions. Sharpness-Aware Minimisation exploits this to improve generalisation. But function-preserving reparameterisation can inflate the Hessian of any minimum by two orders of magnitude without changing a single prediction. If the geometry of weight space can be manufactured from nothing, it cannot be the cause of anything. In other words, flat is simple and simplicity depends on encoding. Here I show that the actual driver is weakness, the volume of completions compatible with the learned function in the learner's embodied language. Weakness is reparameterisation-invariant because it is defined over what the network \emph{does}, not how it is parameterised. I prove weakness is minimax-optimal under exchangeable demands, and that PAC-Bayes bounds work because they correlate with it. On MNIST, the large-batch generalisation advantage \emph{vanishes} as training data grows, from $+1.6\%$ at $n = 2{,}000$ to $+0.02\%$ at $n = 60{,}000$. A quantity whose predictive power depends on how much data you have is not a cause but a confounder. I run head-to-heads on 100 networks with identical architecture and training. For MNIST weakness predicts generalisation ($ρ= +0.374$, $p = 0.00012$), sharpness anticorrelates ($ρ= -0.226$) and simplicity predicts nothing ($p = 0.848$). For Fashion-MNIST ($ρ= +0.384$, $p = 8.15 \times 10^{-5}$), though simplicity is at least somewhat predictive there. Simplicity is dataset dependent, whereas weakness is invariant. Flat minima were never the answer.

</details>


### [63] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用 All of Us 全国性电子健康档案（EHR）数据，通过混合特征选择和人口统计学分层建模，实现慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）诊断预测，AUC 达 0.8461，展示了 EHR 数据在人群代表性风险分层中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见异质性炎症性疾病，疾病负担重、医疗成本高。早期识别困难，原因包括：症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见病重叠、表型异质性复杂、既往预测研究多依赖单一机构数据导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 1. 数据来源：All of Us 全国性纵向 EHR 数据，利用诊断前2年历史数据；2. 特征工程：混合特征选择流程——先进行基于流行率的统计筛选，再结合模型重要性排名，将约110,000个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；3. 建模策略：按6个成人性别与生命阶段亚组进行人口统计学分层建模，各亚组独立超参数调优。

Result: 模型整体 AUC 达 0.8461，较最佳基线提升 0.0168，显示出良好的区分能力。

Conclusion: 常规收集的 EHR 数据可支持人群代表性的 CRS 风险分层，为初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级决策提供信息。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [64] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出顺序智能体调优（SAT），一种无需中心控制器的多智能体协作训练范式。通过因子化策略表示、块坐标更新、序列感知优势估计和KL信任域，实现去中心化可扩展训练，理论上保证单调改进和即插即用不变性。实验表明12B参数的3个智能体团队即可超越32B大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）参数量大虽性能强，但部署成本高昂。虽然多个小型高效LLM协作可能匹配甚至超越单一大型模型，但联合更新多个智能体会导致复合分布偏移，使训练中的协调和稳定性变得困难。

Method: 提出顺序智能体调优（SAT）框架：1）将团队表示为因子化策略；2）采用智能体间块坐标更新，实现去中心化可扩展训练，无需中心控制器；3）开发序列感知的on-policy优势估计器，条件化于演化中的团队策略；4）结合每智能体KL信任域以隔离策略漂移。

Result: 理论证明：1）单调改进保证，稳定性训练过程；2）即插即用不变性：任何智能体可升级为更强模型而无需重训练其他智能体，性能下界保证提升。实验：3个4B智能体（总计12B）在AIME24/25上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；替换为2个8B智能体后，组合分数提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT通过去中心化训练范式成功解决了多智能体协作训练的稳定性问题，证明了小型模型团队可超越大型模型，且具备理论保证的即插即用特性，为高效部署LLM提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [65] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper bridges predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets—a framework characterizing how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon. The key finding is that chaos causes the effective Rashomon set to contract exponentially with lead time, with contraction rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent. The paper develops Lyapunov-weighted metrics for tighter predictive disagreement bounds and decision-aligned selection algorithms. Experiments on synthetic (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world systems (wind power, traffic, weather) show 18-34% improvement in decision quality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics have evolved as two independent research challenges in machine learning despite their conceptual connections. In chaotic systems, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence—unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed. This creates a gap in understanding how model multiplicity behaves in chaotic domains.

Method: The paper introduces horizon-constrained Rashomon sets as a theoretical framework to characterize model multiplicity evolution with prediction horizon. Key methods include: (1) proving exponential contraction of effective Rashomon set with lead time at rate determined by maximum Lyapunov exponent, (2) developing Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement compared to standard metrics, and (3) designing decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone.

Result: Empirical validation on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrates that the proposed framework improves decision quality by 18-34% while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

Conclusion: The framework establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains. Key contributions include: (1) horizon-constrained Rashomon sets for characterizing model multiplicity evolution, (2) proof that effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by Lyapunov exponent, (3) Lyapunov-weighted metrics for tighter bounds on predictive disagreement, (4) decision-aligned selection algorithms, and (5) empirical validation showing 18-34% improvement in decision quality across diverse domains.

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [66] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为概念导向理论建立形式化框架，证明标准行为去除方法是LEACE的特例，提出LEACE-Switch和MidSteer，在多种生成模型上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间表征导向已成为控制生成模型的重要策略，但目前缺乏全面的理论框架支撑。

Method: 1）建立导向与仿射概念擦除的关联，证明标准去除方法为LEACE（仿射擦除的闭合形式方法）的特例；2）提出LEACE-Switch原则性框架，刻画其作为最优仿射解的条件；3）提出MidSteer（最小扰动概念导向），放宽假设条件，支持定向最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大型语言模型）上表现优异。

Conclusion: MidSteer作为一个通用框架，在视觉扩散模型和大型语言模型等多种任务、模态和架构中表现优异。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [67] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSA introduces learnable per-token gates that adaptively skip transformer layers based on token difficulty, reducing computational cost by 14-23% with negligible quality loss. The approach is parameter-efficient (1.7% overhead), differentiable, and transfers to inference for real speedups.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard transformers apply uniform layer depth to all tokens despite varying contextual difficulty. This inefficiency creates computational overhead for tokens requiring fewer processing steps.

Method: Token-Selective Attention (TSA) uses lightweight two-layer MLP routers to generate continuous halting probabilities for each token at each layer, enabling differentiable adaptive skip of token-layer operations. No architectural changes or explicit depth pressure required.

Result: Learns difficulty-proportional routing (20% skip at λ=0); achieves 14-23% TLOp reduction on character-level LM with <0.5% quality loss; outperforms early exit by 0.7% lower validation loss at matched efficiency; enables inference speedup via sparse execution.

Conclusion: TSA provides an efficient mechanism for adaptive computation in transformers, achieving 14-23% TLOp reduction with minimal quality degradation. The learned routing transfers to inference, enabling real wall-clock speedups, and outperforms early exit strategies at matched efficiency.

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>

*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [69] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角分析了稀疏自编码器中特征联合激活的不稳定性问题，揭示了过完备字典场景下组合 steering 的理论极限，为设计更鲁棒的组合控制机制提供了数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于线性表示假说，忽略了过完备字典中非线性干扰效应的影响；组合 steering（即同时激活多个语义潜变量）的理论根基尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 采用几何框架建模激活空间为高维稀疏锥流形；在球面字典模型下推导渐近组合崩溃阈值（由信号锥的高斯均值宽度/统计维度刻画）；分析ReLU非线性在关联特征空间中如何将微观方差波动转化为系统性漂移。

Result: 推导出稀疏锥流形在球面字典模型下的渐近组合崩溃阈值；发现在高偏差区域，ReLU整流导致微观关联方差波动累积为系统性漂移，形成类似棘轮效应的干扰增长；CLEVR实验验证了层级相关特征相比随机基线更快触发相变。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了基于联合激活的控制在过完备字典场景下的几何约束，指出简单线性叠加在组合场景中不可扩展，并呼吁设计能显式管理干扰的组合机制。该工作为特征空间的组合操作提供了理论基础，可能对可解释性研究和安全应用产生重要影响。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [70] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了在预训练-微调范式下不可学习示例的行为，发现预训练权重会削弱UE有效性，并提出SSC方法通过语义引导的扰动生成来对抗这种语义过滤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可学习示例(UE)研究主要在从零训练(from-scratch)设置下评估，而忽视了广泛采用的预训练-微调(PF)范式，导致对UE在真实应用场景中的有效性缺乏系统认知。

Method: 提出层级欺骗策略Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC)，将不可学习扰动的生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。该方法通过语义引导的扰动生成，使嵌入的噪声更难被浅层特征的语义过滤机制识别和消除。

Result: 加载和冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UE方法的有效性。在预训练-微调范式下，冻结的浅层保留数据语义，能够有效过滤掉不可学习噪声等干扰信息。

Conclusion: 提出的SSC方法通过将扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间中，能够在预训练-微调范式下保持数据的不可学习性，为基于预训练的不可学习学习架起了关键桥梁。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>
