### [71] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper proves that composing two randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs) asymptotically matches uniform random rotations for modern compression schemes, three RHTs are needed for vector quantization, and a linear-time adaptive check enables runtime optimization based on input moments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are computationally expensive in high dimensions, so randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs) are used as practical substitutes. However, a single RHT fails worst-case input guarantees, creating a gap between theory and practice in gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, and approximate nearest-neighbor search.

Method: Theoretical analysis using Kolmogorov and 1-Wasserstein distance metrics; composing multiple randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs); covariance decay analysis; moment-based adaptive check.

Result: Two RHTs achieve O(d^(-1/2)) convergence to Gaussian in both Kolmogorov and 1-Wasserstein distance for each coordinate. Three RHTs ensure decaying coordinate covariance for VQ compatibility. A linear-time O(d) moment check enables adaptive runtime selection of RHT count.

Conclusion: Two RHTs suffice for modern compression schemes (DRIVE, QUIC-FL), three RHTs needed for VQ due to block correlation requirements, and an adaptive runtime approach is proposed.

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [72] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中因"落后者效应"导致的效率瓶颈问题，提出了一种无需训练的推理优化框架MACS，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制分别解决信息异质性和模态动态性两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理时存在严重的落后者效应，现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：信息异质性（冗余视觉token与关键视觉token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉-文本比例变化导致资源错配）。

Method: MACS框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制，通过量化视觉token的语义价值来区分重要与冗余信息；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制，根据输入的实时模态组成动态分配专家资源。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上的实验表明，MACS显著优于现有方法，有效提升了MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理场景下的效率。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了一个新颖且鲁棒的解决方案，且作为训练无关的推理框架具有较好的通用性和易部署性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [73] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开创性地研究对比极限识别与生成：学习者观察无序对比对而非正例标签。在无噪声和有限对抗腐败两种设置下建立了完整理论，核心发现是某些类在对比学习下具有对抗腐败免疫力，但传统正例学习甚至无法容忍单一腐败。这打破了"正例学习优于对比学习"的直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极限辨识(Gold 1967)和极限生成(Kleinberg and Mullainathan 2024)均依赖正例或完全标注数据；然而许多自然监督信号本质上是关系型的而非单例的，编码的是示例间的关系而非单独标签；因此需要研究从对比数据流中学习，其中观察的是无序对{x,y}满足h(x)≠h(y)，但哪个是正例对学习者隐藏

Method: 提出对比识别与极限生成框架；核心技术工具为共同交叉图(common crossing graph)，以覆盖与关联语言统一编码学习问题；建立了几何精化的可辨识性充要条件

Result: 1) 无噪声设置：精确刻画对比可辨识类(对Angluin 1980 tell-tale条件的几何细化)；引入对比闭包维度；完全刻画均匀对比生成的样本复杂度；证明对比生成与文本辨识之间严格分层——二者互不可比较
2) 有限对抗腐败下呈现sharp reversal：存在某些类在任意有限腐败预算下可通过单一预算无关算法从对比对中辨识，但从正例中即使只有一个腐败观测也无法辨识

Conclusion: 提出了共同交叉图(common crossing graph)作为统一技术工具，编码成对歧义、家族级生成障碍和腐败缺陷；揭示了对比学习相比传统正例学习的理论优势——在对抗性腐败下具有更强的鲁棒性

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [74] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper presents LLMSpace, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites, which jointly models operational and embodied carbon to reveal trade-offs between carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The growing energy demands of LLMs create an energy and carbon crisis, and while solar-powered AI-enabled LEO satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements.

Method: LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation, using realistic satellite and GPU configurations.

Result: LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference.

Conclusion: LLMSpace enables systematic exploration of sustainable space-based LLM inference by revealing trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for AI-enabled LEO satellites.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [75] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将强化学习推理中的结果监督内化为过程监督的新视角，通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹来自动提取过程级学习信号，实现仅在结果监督下的细粒度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推理方法面临两大问题：基于结果级奖励的序列级优化难以精确分配信用，而依赖外部构建的过程监督成本高且难以扩展。核心挑战是如何将仅在序列末端提供的反馈转化为能够指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号。进一步抽象为新训练范式：模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并精炼自身的内部过程监督。

Result: 在仅使用结果监督的条件下实现细粒度策略优化，开辟了不同于外部提供过程监督的信用分配新路径。

Conclusion: 该研究将强化学习推理理解为一个将结果监督内化为过程监督的过程，为推理任务中的细粒度信用分配提供了新方向。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [76] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种动态在线数据重加权框架，通过相似性质量信号自适应调整样本学习率进行训练，避免离线方法的数据筛选/重采样问题，以隐式课程学习方式提升LLM跨基准泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择与混合方法以离线范式运作，与训练过程脱节，导致工程开销大、在模型/任务迁移下脆弱；且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，牺牲数据多样性、损害泛化能力。

Method: ADAPT在训练过程中根据相似性质量信号动态调整每个样本的学习率权重，而非静态预处理数据，保持样本数量不变。其隐式课程学习机制使模型随训练进展逐步从粗粒度模式转向细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大模型预训练中，ADAPT一致优于离线选择/混合方法及先前在线方法，在相同FLOPs下实现更强的跨基准泛化性能。

Conclusion: ADAPT为LLM训练中的数据管理提供了统一且高效的新范式，通过在线动态调整克服了离线方法的核心局限，展示了在平等计算预算下跨基准的泛化优势。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [77] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [78] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes using an evolution strategy to optimize the quantization of deep learning models, moving beyond traditional nearest neighbor rounding. By iteratively adjusting small percentages of weights to different quantization states, the method can efficiently improve accuracy of quantized models for deployment on resource-constrained devices.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep learning models, while highly effective, often suffer from excessive complexity and memory requirements, making them unsuitable for deployment on IoT devices, mobile platforms, autonomous systems, and real-time applications. While quantization is a popular model compression technique, most existing approaches rely on nearest neighbor rounding, which does not guarantee optimal accuracy outcomes.

Method: The proposed method uses an evolution strategy to optimize quantization states. In each iteration, a small percentage of weights are selected and their values are shifted to different quantization states, iteratively searching for better quantization configurations that improve model accuracy.

Result: The experiments demonstrate that the proposed evolutionary quantization approach, with proper selection of operators and parameters, can rapidly improve the accuracy of quantized models. Validated on VGG and ResNet for image classification/detection tasks, as well as autoencoder architectures.

Conclusion: The evolution strategy with appropriate operators and parameters can effectively and efficiently improve the accuracy of quantized models across various architectures including VGG, ResNet, and autoencoders for image classification, detection, and other tasks.

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [79] [Forecasting Green Skill Demand in the Automotive Industry: Evidence from Online Job Postings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05280)
*Sabur Butt,Joshua N. Arrazola E.,Hector G. Ceballos,Patricia Caratozzolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A computational framework using multilingual embeddings and ESCO validation identifies green skills from 204,373 job postings in Mexico's automotive industry. FEDformer and other Transformer models best forecast demand trends, revealing 274 green skills with fastest growth in renewable energy, recycling, and hydrogen sectors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Systematic methods for identifying and forecasting green skills remain limited despite the global transition toward sustainable economies reshaping labor markets.

Method: Two-stage computational pipeline combining multilingual embeddings and ESCO validation, benchmarked with 15 time series forecasting models using rolling origin evaluation.

Result: Identified 274 unique green skills (4.22% of all skills); Transformer-based models (FEDformer, Reformer, Informer) achieved best performance with MAE ~2.5e-5 and relative RMSE <15%; current demand concentrated in operational sustainability while fastest growth in renewable energy, recycling, and hydrogen technologies.

Conclusion: The proposed pipeline effectively identifies and forecasts green skill demand, supporting data-driven workforce planning in the green transition.

Abstract: The global transition toward sustainable economies is reshaping labor markets, yet systematic methods for identifying and forecasting green skills remain limited. This study presents a computational framework to measure and predict green skill demand using online job postings from Mexico's automotive industry, which contributes about 4% of national GDP. We compile a dataset of job advertisements from Indeed Mexico, OCC Mundial, and LinkedIn (July 2024 to July 2025), yielding 204,373 skill records. A two-stage pipeline combining multilingual embeddings and ESCO validation identifies 274 unique green skills across 8,576 occurrences (4.22% of all skills). We benchmark 15 time series forecasting models using a rolling origin evaluation. Transformer-based models, especially FEDformer, Reformer, and Informer, achieve the best performance, with MAE around 2.5e-5 and relative RMSE below 15. We further propose a framework to classify skills by absolute and relative growth, identifying stable, emerging, and high-impact competencies. Results show current demand is concentrated in operational sustainability practices, while the fastest-growing skills relate to renewable energy, recycling, and hydrogen technologies. This pipeline supports data-driven workforce planning in the green transition.

</details>


### [80] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计LLM各Transformer层中任务特定的参数重要性分数来调制梯度，从而在保留旧任务知识的同时学习新任务，有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在持续学习中常遭受灾难性遗忘问题：按顺序学习新任务后，在早期任务上的表现变差。现有方法（数据回放、参数冻结或正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知，无法区分哪些参数应保留或更新。

Method: 该方法通过估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的、逐元素的参数重要性分数，并利用这些分数来调制梯度更新。具体而言，对先前任务重要的参数获得较小的更新，而不太相关的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法始终优于基线方法，能够更好地保留旧任务，同时在新任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，该方法在持续学习基准测试中始终优于基线方法，能够在保持新任务性能的同时更好地保留旧任务知识，为大语言模型的持续学习提供了一种有效且语义感知的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [81] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAD框架，结合聚类联邦学习与双模微架构(DM²A)，通过共享编码器+双分支结构同时实现无监督异常检测与监督攻击分类，并利用聚类策略处理设备异质性问题。实验表明该方法在80%未标记客户端场景下检测性能提升30%，通信成本降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT/IIoT快速扩展导致攻击面巨大且异质，传统的集中式IDS难以应对；现有联邦学习方法难以跨设备泛化，且无法有效利用边缘环境中大量未标记数据。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，核心是DM²A双模微架构（共享编码器+双分支： unsupervised异常检测分支 + supervised攻击分类分支），结合聚类联邦学习（CFL）动态分组具有相似流量模式的设备，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%未标记客户端的场景下，检测性能相对提升30%，通信开销减半。

Conclusion: CLAD通过整合聚类联邦学习与双模微架构，在标签稀缺和高设备异质性的IoT安全场景中实现了优越的性能，证明联合无监督异常检测与监督攻击分类的可行性和聚类策略的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [82] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [83] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种用于药物发现的主动学习算法，平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法更高效（样本效率提升7%-32%），且比最接近的竞品快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体的通过率不足5%，且需要针对新蛋白（无先验数据）进行从头筛选。如何在尽可能少的测试次数内找到足够多的高质量配体是关键挑战。

Method: 提出名为SPADE的配体选择算法，引入新颖的配体选择方法，采用主动学习策略，仅需稀疏数据即可驱动候选配体的迭代选择与测试。

Result: SPADE平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体。在一对一比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%，评分速度比最接近的竞品快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE为快速药物发现提供了一种高效的稀疏数据学习方法，在样本效率和计算速度方面均展现出显著优势，为缺乏先验数据的新靶点药物发现提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [84] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究预训练扩散模型在零样本条件采样问题（如图像补全、超分辨率）中的应用。针对现有投影方法仅保证测量一致性而忽视概率分布准确性的问题，提出基于法向-切向分解的理论框架，将误差分解为初始化误差和路径得分不匹配误差，并通过投影朗之万初始化与引导逆向去噪的结合方法提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing projection-based samplers for diffusion models only enforce measurement consistency but fail to properly distribute probability mass along the feasible set, leading to biased conditional samples in zero-shot conditional sampling tasks like inpainting and super-resolution.

Method: Normal-tangent decomposition of score function; projected-Langevin initialization combined with guided reverse denoising. The theory proves error bounds using conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components.

Result: Error decomposition into initialization error and pathwise score-mismatch error, with the latter bounded by dimension-free conditional mutual information. Empirical results show the proposed method outperforms projection-based baselines.

Conclusion: Proposed method outperforms existing projection-based baseline methods in both inpainting and super-resolution tasks.

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [85] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多图VRP的可扩展性问题，提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，通过预编码边聚合和非自回归架构实现高效路由，在保证解质量的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法在车辆路径问题（VRP）中仅限于欧几里得设置或简单图；多图场景（平行边代表不同权衡的旅行选项）缺乏有效方法；现有方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构实现分解；采用分层强化学习方法联合训练两阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上达到或超越现有最优方法，同时显著加快了训练和推理速度。

Conclusion: NEPF通过将路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，有效解决了多图VRP的扩展性问题，在保证解质量的同时显著提升了训练和推理效率。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [86] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含状态依赖事件的半显式微分代数方程(DAE)的可微参数优化问题。针对代数变量隐式定义、事件时间参数依赖和重置不连续性三大挑战，提出了自动微分穿越仿真和显式离散伴随两种互补的梯度计算策略，并从多维度比较了两种方法的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度学习方法难以处理状态依赖事件DAE系统的三个核心挑战：代数变量的隐式定义、事件时间对参数的依赖，以及重置映射引入的不连续性。

Method: 提出两种互补的梯度计算策略：(1) 自动微分穿越仿真方法——在向量场内求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理微分，并采用分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随方法——将前向仿真表示为事件分裂残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 建立了统一的学习问题框架（约束最小二乘问题），并在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性等维度上对两种方法进行了系统性比较。

Conclusion: 两种方法均基于固定事件顺序和横向门限穿越的局部有效性条件，为半显式DAE参数学习提供了梯度计算的理论框架。残余项被明确解释为等式约束而非启发式惩罚，澄清了此前方法中的模糊之处。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [87] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN是一种面向大语言模型的分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过f散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，在KL散度下的凸对偶形式将目标转化为log-sum-exp形式，在保持模型效用的同时显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练方法（如CAT、CAPO）虽利用嵌入空间的梯度扰动提高效率，但缺乏对对抗样本难度的自适应区分机制，无法针对性地强化难例学习，导致鲁棒性优化不够充分。

Method: WARDEN在经验数据分布的散度球内优化最坏情况对抗损失；通过f散度模糊集动态调整样本权重，使模型自动关注更难的对抗样本；采用KL散度下的凸对偶推导，将目标函数转化为log-sum-exp形式。

Result: 在多个LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，且计算成本和模型效用损失与CAT、CAPO、MixAT基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 该研究开辟了信息论目标函数的新类别，提供了可扩展的鲁棒对齐实用方案，在保持模型效用的前提下有效提升了LLM的对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [88] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes a conditional generative compressed sensing framework that separates the roles of conditioning in sampling and recovery. Provides theoretical recovery guarantees and demonstrates with Stable Diffusion that prompts meaningfully influence both sensing and reconstruction, suggesting prompts should be treated as tunable design variables rather than fixed parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard generative compressed sensing treats conditioning uniformly, but in practice conditioning serves dual roles: designing the sampling distribution and defining the recovery model. This work aims to understand and leverage these distinct roles explicitly.

Method: Theoretical analysis with stable recovery bounds for ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, combined with prompt-matched Christoffel sampling methodology and empirical validation using Stable Diffusion.

Result: Prompt-matched Christoffel sampling achieves near-optimal complexity bounds comparable to existing generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch introduces an explicit compatibility penalty degrading recovery performance. Experiments confirm prompts reshape sampling distributions and affect image recovery quality.

Conclusion: Prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery. The framework enables meaningful control over both sampling strategies and recovery quality through prompt engineering.

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [89] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理任务会导致灾难性模型崩溃，模型学习到总是预测"Yes"或"No"的trivial解。提出语义损失函数（结合图约束和动态λ调度）可有效防止崩溃，精度提升42.7%，验证了语义损失对Transformer因果推理的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理任务（如传递性、d-分离）上，标准微调导致模型陷入"总是预测Yes/No"的捷径解，无法学习真实因果结构。需解决灾难性崩溃问题并确保模型学习有意义推理。

Method: 提出语义损失函数：(1)基于图的逻辑约束编码因果结构规则；(2)动态λ调度平衡语义损失与任务损失。模型：Gemma 270M等5种变体。评估：200,000+样本，包含对抗性结构推理测试。

Result: 语义损失方法达70.4% (传递性)和68.6% (d-分离)准确率，比崩溃基线提升42.7%。对抗评估中语义模型保持67-70%准确率，而崩溃模型骤降至43-71%。

Conclusion: 语义损失函数是Transformer稳定因果推理的必要条件而非可选项，可有效防止崩溃并实现上下文依赖的稳定预测。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [90] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个全面评估GSSL在文本驱动噪声图上性能的研究，提出统一框架NATD-GSSL，揭示不同pretext task和GNN架构在噪声环境下的鲁棒性差异，为实际应用提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习(GSSL)方法假设输入图为人工整理的干净图，但实际中知识图谱多为从文本自动提取，包含大量噪声。已有鲁棒性研究仅使用合成噪声，缺乏对真实噪声场景的探索。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL统一框架，整合自动图构建、图refinement和GSSL。采用双图评估协议：对比从MedMentions提取的噪声图与UMLS干净参考图（通过共同标准对齐）。评估多种pretext task和GNN架构。

Result: 1. 不同pretext task鲁棒性差异显著：关系重建对噪声高度敏感（受益于定义良好的schema），特征重建具有较强鲁棒性（性能接近干净图），对比学习受噪声影响较小但依赖与下游任务的对齐；2. GNN架构影响关键：双向关系消息传递设计更适合噪声文本驱动图，单向关系设计在干净图上表现更佳

Conclusion: NATD-GSSL为实际应用中处理噪声图提供实践指导，在预训练语言模型基线上提升至7%。代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [91] [A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Graph Construction Methods and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05476)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个双用途基准测试，用于联合评估 GNN 在噪声文本图上的性能以及图构建方法的有效性，通过生物医学领域语料库中的两种自动构建图与专家参考图的对比，实现对 GNN 鲁棒性的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本自动构建的知识图谱存在噪声、碎片化和语义不一致性，严重影响 GNN 在下游任务中的性能，且难以区分性能差异源于模型还是图谱本身的质量。

Method: 在生物医学领域从单一语料库构建基准测试，包含两种使用不同抽取方法自动构建的图，以及由专家策划的高质量参考图（作为性能上界），通过半监督节点分类任务进行对照实验和系统评估。

Result: 文章主要介绍了基准测试的设计框架，未见具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 该基准测试提供了标准化、可复现且可扩展的评估框架，便于集成新的图抽取方法和学习模型，为研究图构建方法与 GNN 鲁棒性提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs automatically constructed from text are increasingly used in real-world applications. However, their inherent noise, fragmentation, and semantic inconsistencies significantly affect the performance of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) on downstream tasks. Assessing their performance and robustness remains difficult, as it is often unclear whether observed results stem from the learning model or from the quality of the constructed graph itself. In this work, we introduce a dual-purpose benchmark designed to jointly evaluate (i) the performance of GNNs on noisy, text-derived graphs and (ii) the effectiveness of graph construction methods on a downstream task. The benchmark is built in the biomedical domain from a single textual corpus and includes two automatically constructed graphs generated using different extraction methods, alongside a high-quality reference graph curated by experts that serves as an upper performance bound. This design enables controlled comparison of construction methods and systematic evaluation of GNN robustness through semi-supervised node classification. We further provide a standardized, reproducible, and extensible evaluation framework, facilitating the integration of new graph extraction methods and learning models.

</details>


### [92] [GRALIS: A Unified Canonical Framework for Linear Attribution Methods via Riesz Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05480)
*Raimondo Fanale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出GRALIS统一框架，基于Riesz表示定理为加性、线性、连续的归因方法建立规范表示理论。该框架通过7个形式化定理同时保证完备性、收敛性、Shapley交互值等属性，在13.5/14公理性质量上显著超越单一方法。初步验证表明其在医学图像分类任务上具有竞争力，但计划中的大规模基准对比尚未完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的可解释人工智能(XAI)归因方法——GradCAM、SHAP、LIME、Integrated Gradients——分别在独立的理论基础下运作，彼此之间缺乏正式的可比性框架。每种方法仅能满足部分公理性质，无法在统一理论下同时保证多重属性。这种分裂状态限制了方法的系统性评估与改进，亟需建立一个能统一表达和比较这些方法的基础理论框架。

Method: 该研究提出GRALIS（梯度-Riesz平均局部积分Shapley）数学框架，基于Riesz表示定理建立归因表示理论。该理论证明：L²(Q,μ)上每个加性、线性、连续的归因泛函都存在唯一的规范表示(Q,w,Δ)。该框架涵盖SHAP、IG、LIME和线性化GradCAM，但不包含非线性泛函（如标准GradCAM或注意力图）。通过7个形式化定理提供统一保证，包括：规范形式必要性、精确完备性、蒙特卡洛收敛性(O(1/√m)+O(1/k))、精确Shapley交互值、Hoeffding方差分解、Sobol敏感度推广及多尺度扩展(MS-GRALIS)。附录通过Mobius变换建立GRALIS-SIV对应关系，避免循环论证。

Result: GRALIS在公理满足度上显著优于现有方法：达到13.5/14项公理性质（包括完备性、敏感性、局部性、k阶交互及最优多尺度聚合），而各单一方法仅能满足2.5-6项。在BreaKHis数据集（1,187张组织学图像，DenseNet-121）上的初步验证显示：恶性分类Deletion faithfulness AUC提升+0.015，类条件一致性达96%，SAL值为0.762±0.109，稀疏度指数为0.39。

Conclusion: GRALIS在BreaKHis数据集上取得显著验证结果：Deletion faithfulness AUC提升+0.015（恶性分类），96%类条件一致性，SAL=0.762±0.109，稀疏度指数0.39。该框架统一了多种归因方法，解决了方法间不可比较的问题，并在13.5/14个公理性质上满足条件，显著优于单一方法的2.5-6/14表现。后续工作计划在配套论文中进行更全面的基准方法对比。

Abstract: The main XAI attribution methods for deep neural networks -- GradCAM, SHAP, LIME, Integrated Gradients -- operate on separate theoretical foundations and are not formally comparable. We present GRALIS (Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley), a mathematical framework establishing a representation theory for attributions: every additive, linear, and continuous attribution functional on L^2(Q,mu) admits a unique canonical representation (Q, w, Delta), proved necessary by the Riesz Representation Theorem. This class encompasses SHAP, IG, LIME and linearized GradCAM, but excludes nonlinear functionals such as standard GradCAM or attention maps. Seven formal theorems provide simultaneous guarantees absent in any individual method: (T1) necessary canonical form; (T2) exact completeness; (T3) Monte Carlo convergence O(1/sqrt(m))+O(1/k); (T4) exact Shapley Interaction Values; (T5) Hoeffding ANOVA decomposition; (T6) Sobol sensitivity generalization; (T7) multi-scale extension (MS-GRALIS) with minimum-variance weights. An algebraic appendix justifies the GRALIS-SIV correspondence via the Mobius transform without circularity. GRALIS satisfies 13.5/14 axiomatic properties vs. 2.5-6/14 for individual methods, including completeness, sensitivity, locality, order-k interactions and optimal multi-scale aggregation simultaneously. Preliminary validation on BreaKHis (1,187 histology images, DenseNet-121) reports deletion faithfulness AUC +0.015 (malignant), 96% class-conditional consistency, SAL = 0.762+/-0.109 and sparsity index 0.39. Extended comparison with baseline XAI methods is planned for a companion paper.

</details>


### [93] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ANPS方法解决强化学习中价值函数训练与策略更新相互依赖的经典难题，通过修改训练分布而非约束策略更新来保证安全性。SV-API通过固定目标策略、迭代更新行为策略收集经验，仅在满足稳定准则时提交更新。实验表明SV-PPO可执行更大策略更新同时保持或提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中的经典"鸡与蛋"困境：安全提升策略需要价值函数在更新后策略的状态访问分布上准确，但该分布未知且无法采样用于训练价值函数。现有保守更新方法以缩小策略更新为代价换取安全性。

Method: 提出近似下一策略采样(ANPS)方法，通过修改训练数据分布而非约束策略更新来解决"鸡与蛋"问题；设计稳定价值近似策略迭代(SV-API)算法，固定目标策略同时让迭代更新的行为策略收集经验，仅在满足收敛准则时才提交新策略；将SV-API应用于PPO得到SV-PPO。

Result: SV-PPO在Atari高维离散任务和连续控制基准上匹配或超越基线性能，且能执行更大规模的目标策略更新。

Conclusion: SV-PPO在Atari高维离散任务和连续控制基准上匹配或超越性能，同时执行更大规模的目标策略更新，验证了ANPS作为解决该经典RL难题的可行方案。

Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

</details>


### [94] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper addresses scalability issues in federated learning by proposing a decentralized agentic strategy that uses only local state and mean-field summaries. The optimal policy is derived in closed form using a minimax criterion targeting the weakest agent, and provably converges to the Nash-optimal centralized policy in the large-population limit. An online weighting mechanism further improves predictions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Federated learning faces scalability challenges with computational and communication costs that fail to scale with the number of agents. There is a need for decentralized agentic strategies that allow each agent to act autonomously using only its own state and minimal ensemble summaries.

Method: The authors derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form, characterized by a worst-client/minimax criterion that minimizes the under-performer regret (maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent). An online weighting mechanism optimizes the server-computed mixture of client predictions.

Result: The proposed decentralized policy achieves theoretical convergence guarantees and typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines in numerical experiments.

Conclusion: The decentralized policy asymptotically converges to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, and numerical experiments validate the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate performance improvements over greedy baselines.

Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [95] [Shortcut Solutions Learned by Transformers Impair Continual Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05495)
*William T. Redman,Erik C. Johnson,Brian Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究扩展LEGO框架到持续学习场景，对比BERT和ALBERT模型发现：前馈Transformer倾向于学习捷径解决方案导致灾难性遗忘，而循环架构的ALBERT因具有更好的归纳偏差可通过特定训练策略挽救性能，但两类模型在组合性任务上均表现失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别跨领域的共同特征是人类类比能力的基础，对持续学习至关重要，但目前缺乏对Transformer模型如何利用表示学习新相关经验的系统性理解。

Method: 将LEGO框架扩展到持续学习设置（continual LEGO），系统研究前馈和循环Transformer模型在CL任务上的表现。

Result: BERT学习捷径解决方案，限制泛化能力并阻止向新经验的正向迁移；ALBERT学习类似For循环的解决方案，表现更好。两类模型在需要跨经验组合的任务上都失败，但ALBERT可通过结合跨经验数据的训练策略挽救性能，而BERT则因初始训练中形成的捷径解决方案无法挽救。

Conclusion: ALBERT的循环架构可能具有更适合持续学习的归纳偏差；Transformer架构与计算解决方案之间的相互作用值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Identifying and exploiting common features across domains is at the heart of the human ability to make analogies, and is believed to be crucial for the ability to continually learn. To do this successfully, general and flexible computational strategies must be developed. While the extent to which Transformer neural network models can perform compositional reasoning has been the subject of intensive recent investigation, little work has been done to systematically understand how well these models can leverage their representations to learn new, related experiences. To address this gap, we expand the previously developed Learning Equality and Group Operations (LEGO) framework to a continual learning (CL) setting ("continual LEGO"). Using this continual LEGO experimental paradigm, we study the capability of feedforward and recurrent Transformer models to perform CL. We find that BERT, a canonical feedforward Transformer model, learns shortcut solutions that limits its ability to generalize and prevents strong forward transfer to new experiences. In contrast, we find evidence supporting the hypothesis that ALBERT, a recurrent version of BERT, learns a For loop-esque solution, which leads to better CL performance. When applying BERT and ALBERT models to a CL setting that requires composition across experiences, we find that both model families fail. Our investigation suggests that ALBERT models can have their performance drop rescued by use of training strategies that combine data across experiences, but this is not true for BERT models, where a detrimental shortcut solution becomes entrenched with initial training. Our results demonstrate that the recurrent ALBERT model may have an inductive bias better suited for CL and motivate future investigation of the interplay between Transformer architecture and computational solutions that emerge in modern models and tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对在线学习和时间序列场景下保角预测的协变量异质性问题，提出结合在线适应与协变量局部化校准的OLCP方法，并进一步通过在线专家聚合框架实现带宽自适应的OLCP-Hedge，提供了理论覆盖率保证，实验验证其能生成更窄的有效预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线保角方法（如ACI）虽能实现长期有效性，但在协变量异质性条件下效率较低，原因是依赖全局校准，无法很好地反映数据的异质性特征。

Method: 提出了Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)方法，结合在线适应性与协变量依赖的局部化校准；进一步开发了OLCP-Hedge，将带宽选择问题转化为约束在线凸优化框架下的在线专家聚合问题。

Result: 仿真实验和真实数据实验表明，所提方法在保持有效长期覆盖率的同时，生成的预测集比现有基线方法更窄。

Conclusion: 作者提出了两个算法（OLCP和OLCP-Hedge），提供了覆盖率保证，并通过实验验证了方法在保持有效长期覆盖率的同时，能生成比基线方法更窄的预测集，从而提高效率。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出NM-PPG方法解决主动特征获取问题，通过连续松弛实现路径梯度优化，配合直通rollout、熵正则化和温度锐化等技术，在保持非短视决策能力的同时有效降低了梯度方差，并在实验中验证了其相对于现有最佳方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取(AFA)将特征获取视为代价高昂的序列决策问题，传统方法将其建模为POMDP，但现有方法存在策略梯度方差高或仅能优化短视策略的不足。

Method: 提出非短视路径策略梯度(NM-PPG)方法，主要创新包括：(1) 对获取过程进行连续松弛，使得梯度可沿完整获取轨迹路径传播；(2) 直通 rollout 机制：前向传播采用硬获取，后向传播通过对应软松弛反向传播；(3) 熵正则化稳定优化；(4) 分阶段温度锐化技术。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，NM-PPG相比现有最优基线方法获得了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了NM-PPG方法，通过连续松弛、路径梯度、直通 rollout、熵正则化和温度锐化等技术，实现了 AFA 问题的非短视策略优化，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [98] [Bayesian Rain Field Reconstruction using Commercial Microwave Links and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05520)
*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用扩散模型作为空间先验，将商业微波链路（CML）的降雨场重建建模为贝叶斯逆问题，无需训练即可通过多种后验采样方法实现高精度重建，在合成和真实数据上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将CML简化为点传感器，忽略了路径积分与信号衰减之间的物理关系，导致在非均匀降水条件下性能下降。

Method: 方法上，将雨场重建建模为贝叶斯逆问题，以扩散模型（DM）作为高保真空间先验分布。该方法无需额外训练即可通过 Plug-and-Play、序列蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）和副本交换（Replica Exchange）等多种方法进行后验采样。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有CML重建基线方法取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将扩散模型作为高保真空间先验，成功将降雨场重建问题框架化为贝叶斯逆问题，绕过了传统方法依赖训练数据的局限，并为后验采样提供了多种灵活的求解方法家族。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [99] [MOSAIC: Module Discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal Learning for Scientific Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05524)
*Shicheng Fan,Nour Elhendawy,Jianle Sun,Ke Fang,Kun Zhang,Yihang Wang,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC将可解释观测的语义迁移到潜在空间，通过稀疏加性解码器实现模块级可解释性。在RNA分子动力学、太阳风、ENSO气候、田纳西伊士曼过程和托卡马克基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing CRL methods achieve identifiability (up to permutation and reparameterization) but lack interpretability—latent semantics require post hoc alignment with ground truth. This is critical for scientific time series where discovering interpretable structures is the primary goal and underlying mechanisms are unknown.

Method: MOSAIC integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery via a sparse additive decoder. The method identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation and recovers sparse associated observation sets per latent. Theoretical analysis shows ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions with finite-sample recovery guarantees for sparse-additive variants.

Result: MOSAIC enables interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series by recovering domain-consistent variable groups.

Conclusion: MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, Tennessee Eastman process, and synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to recover latent variables with identifiability guarantees, typically up to permutation and component-wise reparameterization under appropriate assumptions. However, identifiability does not imply interpretability: latent semantics are typically assigned post hoc by alignment with known ground-truth factors. This limitation is particularly acute in scientific time series, where underlying mechanisms are unknown and discovering interpretable structure is a primary goal. In contrast, scientific observations (such as residue-pair distances, climate indices, or process sensors) are inherently semantic, as they correspond to named physical quantities. This raises a key question: can the interpretability of observations be transferred to the identifiable latent space? We propose MOSAIC (Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning), a sparse temporal VAE that integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery over observed variables. MOSAIC identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation, and recovers for each latent a sparse set of associated observations through an additive decoder, yielding module-level interpretability. We show that ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions, and provide finite-sample recovery guarantees for a tractable sparse-additive variant. Empirically, MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, the Tennessee Eastman process, and a synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

</details>


### [100] [Energy Generative Modeling: A Lyapunov-based Energy Matching Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05530)
*Yixuan Wang,Wenqian Xue,Warren E. Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将静态标量能量生成模型的训练与采样阶段统一为Wasserstein空间上的非线性控制问题，以KL散度为Lyapunov函数。在此框架下，证明了Langevin采样可获得有限步停止准则，并建立了能量函数加性组合保持不变测度和Lyapunov证书的理论基础，为约束生成和加速采样提供了控制理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态标量能量生成模型中，训练和采样被视为分离的两个阶段，研究旨在统一这两个过程，并引入非线性控制理论工具。

Method: 通过将密度传输建模为Wasserstein空间上的非线性控制问题，以KL散度作为Lyapunov函数，实现训练与采样的统一框架化。

Result: (1) 推导出Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，证明确定性梯度流在同一能量景观下不存在Lyapunov证书；(2) 证明训练得到的标量能量的加性组合保持显式Gibbs不变测度并继承闭环Lyapunov证书。实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该研究将静态标量能量生成模型与非线性控制理论统一框架，建立了采样停止准则，并证明了能量函数的加性组合仍保持Lyapunov证书和Gibbs不变测度，为约束生成和加速采样奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Generative models based on static scalar energy functions represent an emerging paradigm in which a single time independent potential drives sample generation through its gradient field, eliminating the need for time conditioning entirely. We unify the training and sampling phases of this paradigm, conventionally treated as separate procedures, within a single framework: density transport on the Wasserstein space, cast as a nonlinear control problem in which the Kullback Leibler (KL) divergence serves as a Lyapunov function. Training and sampling are then two instances of this same master dynamics, differing only in initial condition. Within this autonomous framework we develop two analytic results. First, since the Lyapunov certificate is asymptotic, we derive a finite step stopping criterion for Langevin sampling and prove that no Lyapunov certificate exists for the deterministic gradient flow on the same energy landscape. Second, the reformulation brings the toolkit of nonlinear control theory to bear on static scalar energy generative modeling, that is, we show that additive composition of trained scalar energies retains an explicit Gibbs invariant measure and inherits the closed-loop Lyapunov certificate. Beyond these immediate results, this reformulation bridges static scalar energy generative models with the full toolkit of nonlinear control theory, opening the door to barrier functions for constrained generation and contraction metrics for accelerated sampling. Experiments on synthetic distributions validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Scalable One-Step Generative Modeling for Autoregressive Dynamical System Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05540)
*Tianyue Yang,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MeLISA是一种无潜在变量的像素空间自回归生成替代模型，通过块级单次评估生成和时窗/时增量一致性约束，在高分辨率物理动力学中实现了高效的短时预测和保真的长时统计展开，兼具神经算子的推理速度和扩散模型的多模态保真度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子虽能廉价进行自回归预测，但在湍流状态下易漂移失效；扩散模型和潜在生成替代模型虽能表达随机过渡，却依赖多步去噪、噪声调度设计或辅助压缩模型，增加了推理开销。因此需要一种兼具高效 rollout 与长期统计保真度的无潜在变量自回归生成替代方案。

Method: MeLISA基于像素空间MeanFlow构建，采用块级随机过渡核，每个预测块仅需单次模型评估即可生成，完全规避了潜在编码器和迭代扩散解码器。同时引入Window-Consistency MeanFlow目标函数以从部分观测时窗中学习条件时空生成，并结合Time Increment Consistency损失约束多滞后有限增量、瞄准时序相关性结构，从而稳定长时序展开。

Result: 在256×256 Kolmogorov湍流流和192×192湍流通道流切片两个高分辨率基准上，MeLISA在短时预测精度和长时统计指标（能谱、湍动能、混合率相关动力学）上均优于神经算子基线，推理速度与神经算子相当甚至更快。3.7–5.7M参数紧凑模型即展现强参数效率，DiT变体可扩展至150M参数规模。

Conclusion: MeLISA通过Window-Consistency MeanFlow和Time Increment Consistency损失实现了高效且统计准确的长时预测，兼具推理速度和参数效率优势，为高分辨率物理动力学提供了一条可行的无潜在变量自回归生成替代方案。

Abstract: Fast surrogate modeling for high-dimensional physical dynamics requires more than low short-term error: useful models must roll out efficiently while preserving the statistical structure of long trajectories. Neural operators provide inexpensive autoregressive forecasts but can drift in turbulent regimes, whereas rolling diffusion and latent generative surrogates can represent stochastic transitions at the cost of multi-step denoising, noise-schedule design, or auxiliary compression models. We propose MeanFlow Long-term Invariant Spatiotemporal Consistency Autoregressive Models (MeLISA), a latent-free autoregressive generative surrogate built on pixel-space MeanFlow. MeLISA defines a blockwise stochastic transition kernel that generates each forecast block with a single model evaluation, avoiding latent encoders and iterative diffusion solvers at inference time. To stabilize long-horizon rollouts, MeLISA combines a Window-Consistency MeanFlow objective that learns conditional spatiotemporal generation from partially observed temporal windows with a Time Increment Consistency loss that constrains multi-lag finite increments and targets temporal-correlation structure. We evaluate MeLISA with compact UNet and scalable DiT backbones on two high-resolution benchmarks, extended 2D Kolmogorov flow at $256 \times 256$ and turbulent channel-flow slice at $192 \times 192$. MeLISA outperforms neural-operator baselines on short-term forecasting accuracy and long-horizon statistical metrics, including energy spectra, turbulent kinetic energy, and mixing-rate-related dynamics, while achieving inference speeds comparable to, and in some cases faster than, neural operators. Compact 3.7-5.7M-parameter variants already deliver strong parameter efficiency, and DiT variants provide a scalable path up to 150M parameters. Overall, MeLISA benefits both rollout efficiency and long-horizon statistical accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Q-Chunking for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05544)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Q分块(AQC)方法，解决离线到在线强化学习中动作分块大小固定的问题。通过比较不同分块大小相对于每 horizon 基线的优势，并按折扣因子归一化，动态选择最优分块大小。理论证明该方法具有噪声免疫性，自适应分块优于任何固定分块大小。在OGBench、Robomimic和RoboCasa-GR1任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线到在线强化学习方法使用固定的动作分块大小，但这并非最优：在接触事件附近需要短分块进行反应控制，而在自由空间运动时需要长分块进行更好的信用分配。简单比较学习到的Q值会因为折扣因子不匹配而系统性地崩溃到最短分块，在低价值状态下退化为噪声。

Method: 提出自适应Q分块(AQC)方法，通过比较每个分块大小相对于每 horizon 基线的优势，并用折扣因子归一化。这个准则将偏置的错误答案转换为无偏的近似随机选择（当没有真实信号时），并在特定尺度能实现更好规划时变得有区分力。

Result: AQC在OGBench和Robomimic上实现了最先进的离线在线成功率，并可应用于增强大规模VLA模型的性能，在RoboCasa-GR1任务上显著提升表现。

Conclusion: 理论证明了优势选择器的噪声免疫性边界和自适应分块相对于任何固定分块大小的价值优势。该方法为离线到在线强化学习中的时序探索提供了自适应解决方案。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning with action chunking eliminates multi-step off-policy bias and enables temporally coherent exploration, but all existing methods use a fixed chunk size across every state. This is suboptimal: near contact events the agent needs short chunks for reactive control, while during free-space motion long chunks provide better credit assignment. The natural solution is to train critics for several chunk sizes and select the best one at each state, but naive comparison of learned critic values systematically collapses to the shortest chunk due to discount-scale mismatch, and degrades to noise in low-value states. We propose Adaptive Q-Chunking (AQC), which resolves both failures by comparing the advantage of each chunk size relative to a per-horizon baseline, normalized by the discount factor. This criterion converts biased wrong answers into unbiased near-random choices when no genuine signal exists, and becomes discriminative when a particular scale enables better planning. We prove theoretical bounds on the advantage selector's noise immunity and on the value dominance of adaptive chunking over any fixed chunk size. We demonstrate that AQC achieves state-of-the-art offline and online success rates on OGBench and Robomimic, and can be applied to enhance the performance of large-scale VLA models that predict action sequences, significantly boosting performance on RoboCasa-GR1 tasks.

</details>


### [103] [Accelerating LMO-Based Optimization via Implicit Gradient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05577)
*Won-Jun Jang,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LMO-IGT提出了一种结合隐式梯度传输的随机LMO优化新方法，在单次梯度计算下实现O(ε^-3.5)复杂度，并通过Muon-IGT等实例验证了其在现代优化中的实用加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMO方法的理论分散在无约束和约束形式中，方差缩减技术虽能加速但带来额外梯度评估开销，且对LMO方法的理论理解不够统一。

Method: 提出LMO-IGT方法，利用隐式梯度传输在运输点评估随机梯度；引入正则化支持函数(RSF)作为新的收敛准则，统一了梯度范数和Frank-Wolfe间隙概念；建立了LMO方法的统一框架。

Result: 随机LMO达到O(ε^-4)，方差缩减LMO达到O(ε^-3)但需额外梯度评估，LMO-IGT仅用单次随机梯度达到O(ε^-3.5)。

Conclusion: LMO-IGT通过隐式梯度传输实现了理论与实践的有效加速，其中Muon-IGT在各种场景下表现最优，证明了IGT作为现代LMO优化加速机制的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent optimizers such as Lion and Muon have demonstrated strong empirical performance by normalizing gradient momentum via linear minimization oracles (LMOs). While variance reduction has been explored to accelerate LMO-based methods, it typically incurs substantial computational overhead due to additional gradient evaluations. At the same time, the theoretical understanding of LMO-based methods remains fragmented across unconstrained and constrained formulations. Motivated by these limitations, we propose \emph{LMO-IGT}, a new class of stochastic LMO-based methods leveraging implicit gradient transport (IGT). We further introduce a unified framework for stochastic LMO-based optimization together with a new stationarity measure, the \emph{regularized support function} (RSF), which bridges gradient-norm and Frank--Wolfe-gap notions within a common framework. By evaluating stochastic gradients at transported points, LMO-IGT accelerates convergence while retaining the single-gradient-per-iteration structure of standard stochastic LMO. Our analysis establishes that stochastic LMO achieves an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, variance-reduced LMO achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ at the cost of additional gradient evaluations, and LMO-IGT achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3.5})$ using only a single stochastic gradient per iteration. Empirically, LMO-IGT consistently improves over stochastic LMO counterparts with negligible overhead. Among its instantiations, Muon-IGT achieves the strongest overall performance across evaluated settings, demonstrating that IGT provides an effective and practical acceleration mechanism for modern LMO-based optimization.

</details>


### [104] [When Can Voting Help, Hurt, or Change Course? Exact Structure of Binary Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05592)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多数投票改善随机预测器的传统认知——经典理论认为投票是单调的（多数阈值以上越多投票越好，以下则反之），但作者证明这一图景不完整。在de Finetti表示框架下，投票行为由潜在的正确率分布决定，可产生非单调甚至无限次趋势变化的复杂投票曲线。核心贡献是建立了完整的奇数预算曲线（odd-budget curve）与"符号投票签名"（signed voting signature）之间的等价性——曲线增量是带符号的Hausdorff矩，且完整曲线可唯一恢复该签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定能力理论将多数投票视为单调干预——在多数阈值以上投票越多越好，以下则反之。本文指出这一图景根本上是是不完整的，需要更深入的理论来解释投票曲线可能呈现的复杂形状现象。

Method: 采用de Finetti表示框架刻画可交换重复正确性的潜在分布；构造了简单的潜在混合分布来产生非单调投票曲线和无限次趋势变化的显式构造；建立投票签名概念——记录每个二项方差尺度下阈值以上与以下的超额潜在质量；证明完整的奇数预算曲线与投票签名等价，曲线增量是带符号的Hausdorff矩。

Result: 发现了投票曲线的多种反直觉形态：非单调行为、无限次趋势变化；建立了曲线与潜在分布之间的完整对应关系；证明了签名分支对称不可辨识性；区分了两种估计机制——直接获取样本正确率信息可恢复完整签名，而固定深度的分组标签只能揭示有限前缀。

Conclusion: 本文提供了多数投票的完整理论框架，解释了形状现象、不可辨识性、可实现性、变异性和端点速率，同时明确了估计问题的本质差异——信息获取方式决定了能恢复的签名长度。

Abstract: Majority voting is one of the few black-box interventions that can improve a fixed stochastic predictor: repeated access can be cheaper than changing a high-capability model. Classical fixed-competence theory makes this intervention look monotone -- more votes help above the majority threshold and hurt below it. We show that this picture is fundamentally incomplete. Under the de Finetti representation for exchangeable repeated correctness, voting is governed by a latent distribution of per-example correctness probabilities.
