We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [174] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇技术说明文章，旨在澄清TurboQuant与早期DRIVE和EDEN量化方案之间的关系。研究表明，TurboQuant_mse是EDEN的一个特例（固定标度参数S=1），而TurboQuant_prod通过次优方式组合了EDEN和QJL。实验证明，优化参数后的EDEN在所有设置下都优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期TurboQuant工作与早期DRIVE（NeurIPS 2021）和EDEN（ICML 2022）方案之间的关系，解决TurboQuant工作中对EDEN的误解，并证明优化后的EDEN方案始终优于TurboQuant。

Method: 通过理论分析证明TurboQuant是EDEN的特例或次优组合：1）分析TurboQuant_mse与EDEN的关系，表明固定S=1通常次优但在大维度下趋近最优；2）分析TurboQuant_prod的三个次优来源；3）在TurboQuant论文的所有精度实验设置下重复实验进行比较。

Result: 理论分析表明：（1）TurboQuant_mse是S=1的EDEN特例；（2）TurboQuant_prod在三个方面次优：使用S=1、1-bit EDEN优于QJL的1-bit残差量化、直接用b-bit EDEN优于链式量化。实验结果证实：偏差EDEN（优化S）优于TurboQuant_mse，无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod（常超过1 bit，如2-bit EDEN超越3-bit TurboQuant_prod）。

Conclusion: EDEN统一了DRIVE和TurboQuant，且通过优化参数S和避免链式量化，在所有测试设置中始终优于TurboQuant，表明TurboQuant的贡献主要是验证了EDEN框架的有效性，而非提出新的方法。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [175] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出拉格朗日高斯过程(LGPs)，通过离散强制欧拉-拉格朗日方程和变分离散化方案实现数据高效的动力学学习。该方法保留几何结构，可在无速度/动量数据下从位置快照学习物理一致的动态模型，提供不确定性量化，实现稳定的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current methods suffer from erroneous energy drift during long-term predictions, and most approaches require access to velocities or momenta which are often unavailable in practical applications (e.g., motion capture, visual servoing).

Method: Proposes Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) using linear operators for GP conditioning constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Preserves geometric structure via Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, enabling learning directly from discrete position snapshots.

Result: LGPs successfully learn physically consistent dynamics from sparse positional data with uncertainty quantification, enabling stable long-term predictions across synthetic and real-world scenarios including a soft robot with hysteresis.

Conclusion: LGPs enable data-efficient learning of physically consistent dynamics from sparse position data with uncertainty quantification, supporting stable long-term predictions in practical scenarios like soft robotics with hysteresis.

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [176] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [177] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在f-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌的差分隐私随机梯度下降(DP-SGD)进行了紧致的Trade-off函数分析。与Poisson子采样不同，随机洗牌方法可获得透明可解释的闭式界。通过Berry-Esseen定理给出具体参数设置（σ=1时需M≈1.14×10^6轮，N≈1.14×10^7样本），并引入新的极限理论技术使δ仅呈O(√E)依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Poisson子采样的f-DP分析仅能通过机器计算获得隐式公式，缺乏透明性；本文旨在为随机洗牌方法建立紧致且可解释的闭式Trade-off函数界，并覆盖σ≥√(3/ln M)这一重要参数 regime。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 在f-DP框架下分析随机洗牌机制；(2) 应用Berry-Esseen定理推导闭式界；(3) 提出基于广义大数定律的新证明技术，获得渐近极限结果；(4) 多epoch组合分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：① 具体参数设置（δ=1/100, σ=1）需M≈1.14×10^6轮和N≈1.14×10^7样本；② 组合后δ与epoch数E呈线性依赖，限制E=O(√M)；③ 渐近结果：当E=c_M²M (c_M→0)时，E-fold组合Trade-off函数一致收敛到理想对角线1-a，δ仅呈O(√E)依赖。

Conclusion: 本文在随机洗牌DP-SGD的f-DP分析上取得突破，建立了透明紧致的闭式界，并揭示了σ≥√(3/ln M)与σ≤1/√(2ln M)两个regime的本质差异。开放问题：获得显式收敛速率的精确刻画。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedGMC是一种新型图联邦学习方法，通过双流形校准机制而非刚性参数对齐来同时处理语义和结构异构性，在保留全局共性的同时最大化本地客户的个性化表示空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来应对异构性，但这依赖于限制性的全局线性假设——即用单一全局一致的表示空间汇总局部数据分布。这严重压缩了客户的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样的本地图分布。

Method: FedGMC提出双流形校准机制：(1)语义层面，通过等距语义锚点构建几何最优的全局语义流形，指导局部语义流形校准；(2)结构层面，通过构建全局结构模板建立全局结构流形，指导局部结构流形校准。服务器端动态聚合本地流形以迭代优化全局流形。

Result: 在11个同配性和异配性图数据集上的实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局一致性和局部个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC通过统一流形视角有效解决了图联邦学习中的异构性问题，在11个同配性和异配性图数据集上显著优于现有方法，证明了该方法在平衡全局一致性和局部个性化方面的有效性。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [179] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TARDIS is an inference-time refinement framework for tabular data synthesis that operates on a frozen pre-trained diffusion backbone (TabDiff). It uses TPE-based search over score-level guidance to optimize Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR) — a pattern that minimizes symmetric distances between synthetic and real samples both continuously and discretely. Across 15 benchmarks, TARDIS achieves +8.6% median improvement over real data and +12.9% mean improvement over its backbone, completing in 1-80 minutes on a single GPU.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current diffusion-based tabular generators approach but rarely exceed real-data utility. Closing this gap has been pursued exclusively via training-time approaches (architectural advances, scaling, retraining). The inference-time alternative — refining pre-trained generator outputs without modifying parameters — remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis.

Method: TARDIS uses Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) to search over score-level guidance in the reverse diffusion process. The search optimizes Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR) — a symmetric Chamfer functional minimizing distances between synthetic and real samples continuously via score gradients and discretely via batch-ranking post-generation. Per-dataset configuration includes post-hoc sample selectors and optional soft-label distillation. The frozen pre-trained backbone (TabDiff) remains untouched during inference.

Result: Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks, TARDIS achieves: (1) median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins); (2) improvement over TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4); (3) matching backbone performance on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy; (4) inference-time refinement completes in 1-80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

Conclusion: TARDIS achieved a median +8.6% improvement over real data across 15 benchmarks and +12.9% over the backbone TabDiff model (p<10^-4). Inference-time refinement completes in 1-80 minutes on a single GPU while maintaining manifold fidelity, diversity, and privacy. BCR is validated as the dominant refinement pattern for tabular synthesis.

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [180] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的层级归因框架，通过粗到细区域搜索策略生成归因图，并提取归因熵、相机内空间方差、跨相机基尼系数三个统计量作为风险预测信号，在多个数据集上验证了这些信号与规划风险的关联性，可用于规划安全监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成的视觉证据；规划任务与图像分类不同，需要从六视角摄像头输入预测连续多步轨迹，归因需同时捕获关键视角和区域及其对输出的影响。

Method: 采用粗到细的区域归因策略，在全视角输入中搜索候选区域并进行归因细化；提取三个归因统计量作为规划风险的预测信号：归因熵、相机内空间方差、跨相机基尼系数。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD、GenAD上实验验证，归因统计量与规划风险相关：与轨迹误差的Spearman相关性达0.30±0.07，碰撞检测AUROC达0.77±0.04；在未见场景上泛化良好，不同归因方法下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 提出的层级归因框架能够有效识别端到端自动驾驶规划中的风险，可作为规划安全监控的辅助信号，在不同场景和归因方法间具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [181] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP-FM是一种用于流匹配的条件生成算法，通过学习基函数来表示多个训练分布的速度场，并利用最小二乘投影快速适应新分布，无需额外训练即可生成目标分布样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型在自然语言条件图像生成方面已取得显著成功，但如何从少量示例数据点实现模型自适应仍是一个未被充分探索且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: FP-FM学习基函数来跨越一组训练分布对应的速度场，通过简单的最小二乘投影计算来适应新分布。此外还引入了多种变体，通过丰富系数计算（如让系数依赖时间）来在表达力和计算成本之间提供权衡。

Result: FP-FM在合成数据集和图像数据集上均实现了显著提升的精确率和召回率，特别是在未见过的分布上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 该方法能够高效地从多样化目标分布生成样本，且在推理时无需额外训练，为基于示例的生成式适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [182] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE)损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先进似标签平滑两个机制将类层次结构信息融入交叉熵损失。在CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft和NABirds三个数据集上，相比标准交叉熵和多层次损失方法均有显著提升，且可作为直接替代方案零成本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵对所有错误分类一视同仁，忽略了类层次结构编码的语义距离信息，导致层次化分类任务中对语义相近错误的惩罚不足。

Method: 提出HACE，包含两个核心组件：(1) 预测聚合——将模型的概率质量沿类层次结构向上传播，确保父节点累积其子节点的置信度；(2) 祖先进似标签平滑——将真实标签的监督信号沿从真类到根节点的路径进行分布式传播。

Result: 端到端训练：18个架构-数据集组合中15个实现精度提升，平均增益4.66%；DINOv2-Large冻结特征线性探测：三个数据集上均超越所有对比方法，平均领先最优基线2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的直接替代方案，在层次化分类任务中表现出显著优势，可有效提升模型性能而不增加推理复杂度。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [183] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一种集成模型压缩框架，通过交替执行压缩生成和剪枝操作，在提升模型效率的同时保持对原始集成的高保真度，实验验证其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽性能卓越，但聚合大量弱学习器会带来部署困难、可解释性差及鲁棒性验证复杂等问题。现有的压缩方法（剪枝或压缩）各有利弊，需寻找更优平衡。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论支撑的主动生成程序创建新学习器以增强集成多样性；第二阶段对丰富后的集成执行剪枝操作。两阶段均支持对原模型保真度的精细控制。

Result: 实验表明PACE在性能上优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供可量化的保真度保证。

Conclusion: PACE框架通过两阶段策略（先生成后剪枝）实现对集成模型的有效压缩，同时提供对原始模型保真度的精细控制。这种交替结合压缩和剪枝的方法能够平衡模型性能和部署效率。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [184] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"元游戏"(metagame)框架，通过将归因方法本身建模为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，来量化模型解释中的二阶交互效应。理论上证明了归因可层次分解为元归因；实践中应用于指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和扩散Transformer的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing attribution methods only provide first-order explanations but lack a framework for quantifying directional second-order interaction effects between features in explanations.

Method: Treat the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and compute its Shapley value to measure the directional influence (meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$) of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$.

Result: Meta-attributions successfully quantify token interactions in instruction-tuned LMs, explain cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and interpret text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

Conclusion: Meta-attributions hierarchically decompose first-order attributions and serve as directional extensions of existing interaction indices, with practical applications in analyzing language models, vision-language encoders, and diffusion transformers.

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [185] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分子生成模型潜在空间中属性控制的有效性，发现SELFIES表示中的特定伪影会严重影响潜在空间的化学解释。通过引入混杂感知评估方法，证明了在控制表示层面的混杂因素后，化学有意义的属性控制是可行的，但必须通过解码分子进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型通常假设潜在空间具有有意义的几何结构，但可观察的属性可预测性可能反映的是序列级捷径而非化学组织。SELFIES表示中的特定伪影（长度、分支令牌、环令牌、令牌熵）会强烈编码到潜在空间中，需要开发新的评估方法来正确区分化学信号与表示层面的混杂因素。

Method: 使用无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES分子表示上进行训练。通过拟合线性探针到RDKit描述符来识别全局控制方向，并引入基于残差化、混杂方向对齐分析和分子解码遍历的混杂感知评估框架，以区分化学信号与SELFIES伪影。

Result: 在混杂感知评估下，发现cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA等属性具有稳健的单调控制能力。非线性探针进一步表明，某些属性（如HBA）可获得稳定的全局控制方向，而其他属性更适合用局部潜在梯度来描述。

Conclusion: 在纠缠的分子潜在空间中，化学有意义的控制是可行的，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表示层面的混杂因素。研究还发现某些属性（如cLogP）适合全局方向控制，而其他属性可能更适合通过局部潜在梯度来描述。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [186] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR（动态模式重校准）是一种用于时间序列预测的即插即用机制，通过"感知-路由-调制"管道在token级别进行动态调整，解决现有深度预测模型使用固定权重矩阵导致的静态模式响应问题。DPRNet作为独立模型在12个基准数据集上表现具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度预测模型依赖全局共享的固定权重矩阵，无法适应时间序列中不断变化的局部时间模式。模型被迫收敛到"折中平均值"，缺乏对动态局部特征的适应性，导致预测性能受限。

Method: DPR采用轻量级的"感知-路由-调制"管道：首先感知当前上下文，计算在自适应响应模式学习基上的软路由分布，生成时间感知的调制向量，最后通过残差Hadamard乘积对隐藏状态进行重校准。该方法支持两种部署模式：(1)作为骨干无关的适配器增强现有模型；(2)作为独立模型DPRNet。

Result: 作为适配器使用时，DPR以最小开销增强了多种架构的预测性能，验证了其解决通用瓶颈的能力。作为独立模型，DPRNet在12个基准数据集上达到具有竞争力的表现，证明了动态重校准机制相对于宏观参数缩放的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态token级重校准是时间序列预测的重要改进方向。DPR证明了通过轻量级机制实现动态模式适应比单纯增加模型参数量更具效率，为未来时间序列预测模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [187] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究连续时间ℓ2-Boosting在ℓ1隐式偏差下的良性过拟合现象，证明了纯噪声模型中贪婪选择导致对数速率的过剩方差衰减（而非ℓ2几何下的线性衰减），并提出一种无需调参的早停规则在L1有界信号下达到最优预测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有良性过拟合理论在ℓ2几何下已较完善，但ℓ1隐式偏差（即贪婪集成）下的行为缺乏分析工具，原因在于坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合破坏了标准谱预解式方法。

Method: 采用连续时间ℓ2-Boosting在高维 setting 下进行分析；核心技术工具为凸高斯 minimax 定理（CGMT）结合双侧截断高斯矩的精细渐近展开，以解析求解非光滑ℓ1插值器。

Result: 在纯噪声模型下，良性过拟合以线性速率失效——贪婪选择将噪声局部化到稀疏活跃集，过剩方差以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(p/n))衰减；在尖峰-各向同性设计下，当r₂≫n时风险趋于零，但仅以对数速率而非ℓ2几何下的线性衰减；早停规则在L1有界信号下可恢复Lasso基本不等式并达到最小最大最优率。

Conclusion: 提出关键发现：贪婪选择以对数速率将噪声局部化到稀疏活跃集；L1几何下的良性过拟合以Θ(σ²/log(p/n))速率失败，比L2几何的线性衰减慢；通过无调参早停规则可在L1有界信号下恢复Lasso基本不等式并达到最小最大最优经验预测率。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [188] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析KL-Shampoo中Kronecker预条件子的特征值谱呈现"尖峰-平坦"形状这一结构性质，创新性地将正交化方法与Kronecker因子预条件相结合，提出Pro-KLShampoo算法。该方法在r维子空间上保留完整谱结构，在其余方向上应用正交化，从而融合两种优化范式的优势，在多个预训练规模上实现了更好的性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM预训练中存在两条优化器前沿：基于KL散度最小化的显式Kronecker因子预条件（如KL-Shampoo）和基于梯度动量正交化的方法（如Muon/谱范数下的最速下降）。这两条路线通常独立发展，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 本文提出Pro-KLShampoo算法，核心思想是将KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子限制为与尖峰-平坦特征相符的参数族：在r维子空间上保留完整谱结构，在剩余n-r方向上使用单一共享特征值，并在这些方向上应用正交化。论文给出了一个恒等式，证明该正交化可恢复完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M和LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在验证损失、峰值每GPU内存和达到特定损失水平的墙上时间三个维度上均一致性地超越KL-Shampoo，且该优势在所有测试的子空间秩设置下均成立。

Conclusion: Pro-KLShampoo通过在四个预训练规模上的实验验证了其有效性，在验证损失、峰值GPU内存和达到特定损失水平的墙上时间三个指标上均优于原始KL-Shampoo，且性能提升在所测试的所有子空间秩设置下保持一致。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [189] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索从LLM训练数据中恢复患者特征间相关性和因果关系的方法，通过患者比较三联问题和提示级环境变换技术，结合不变因果预测(ICP)框架，在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了间接诱导方法能够恢复有意义且稳定的关联结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型训练数据包含大量生物医学文献和不同患者群体信息，但直接询问可能导致误导性结果，需要探索从LLM中恢复患者特征间相关性和因果链接信息的方法。

Method: 提出基于结构化比较问题的患者比较三联问题方法，结合LLM表示的统计模型，通过提示级环境变换获取不同亚群体的相关性估计，并应用不变因果预测(ICP)方法获得候选不变父链接。

Result: 在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了方法可行性，诱导的相关性在不同提示环境中平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，同时在统计上存在显著差异，支持下游不变性检验，ICP方法能够提供一组候选不变父链接。

Conclusion: 间接通过三联比较问题的诱导方法能够从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，为从隐式相关性到符合LLM回答模式的因果陈述提供了一条谨慎的路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [190] [Topological Signatures of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06352)
*Yifan Tang,Qiquan Wang,Inés García-Redondo,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper uses persistent homology to analyze the grokking phenomenon in neural networks trained on modular arithmetic. The key finding is that grokking exhibits a distinctive topological signature — specifically, a sharp increase in H1 persistence — reflecting the cyclic structure of the underlying task. Unlike existing spectral or geometric methods, persistent homology provides a unified characterization capturing multi-scale structure. These topological transitions are shown to correlate with generalization rather than memorization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the need to understand the underlying mechanisms of grokking — the phenomenon where neural networks initially overfit then suddenly generalize. Existing diagnostic tools (spectral and geometric) provide fragmented insights, and there is a gap in unified frameworks that capture both local and global structure during this learning process.

Method: The authors apply persistent homology analysis to point clouds derived from embedding matrices of models trained on modular arithmetic tasks with varying primes. They compare this approach against existing spectral methods (Fourier analysis) and geometric diagnostics (local intrinsic dimension), and conduct ablation studies across different data regimes and control settings.

Result: A clear topological signature of grokking is identified: a sharp increase in both maximum and total persistence of first homology (H1). Persistence diagrams reveal the emergence of a dominant long-lived topological feature accompanied by increasingly structured secondary features, reflecting the underlying cyclic structure of the task.

Conclusion: Persistent homology provides a unified framework that captures both local and global multi-scale structure, offering principled and interpretable insights into how neural networks internalize latent structure during training. These topological transitions are specifically tied to generalization rather than memorization.

Abstract: We study the grokking phenomenon through the lens of topology. Using persistent homology on point clouds derived from the embedding matrices of a range of models trained on modular arithmetic with varying primes, we identify a clear and consistent topological signature of grokking: a sharp increase in both the maximum and total persistence of first homology ($H_1$). Persistence diagrams reveal the emergence of a dominant long-lived topological feature together with increasingly structured secondary features, reflecting the underlying cyclic structure of the task. Compared to existing spectral and geometric diagnostics -- specifically, Fourier analysis and local intrinsic dimension -- persistent homology provides a unified geometric and topological characterization of representation learning, capturing both local and global multi-scale structure. Ablations across data regimes and control settings show that these topological transitions are tied to generalization rather than memorization. Our results suggest that persistent homology offers a principled and interpretable framework for analyzing how neural networks internalize latent structure during training.

</details>


### [191] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM)，首次将顺序无关自回归模型应用于不完整数据的系统性框架，通过隐式插补和主动信息获取机制，在多种真实基准上优于现有插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 从缺失数据角度重新解释模型；2) 证明标准训练过程隐式执行MCAR机制下的插补；3) 提出在一般缺失机制下直接训练不完整数据集的框架；4) 利用摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，序贯选择最具信息量的缺失变量用于下游预测或推断。

Result: MO-ARM在多种真实世界基准测试中一致性地优于现有插补基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立了顺序无关自回归模型与缺失数据处理的系统性联系，证明了其在高缺失率场景下的鲁棒样本外插补性能，并开辟了主动信息获取的新应用方向。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [192] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作通过梯度检查点技术实现精确全梯度计算，解决了迭代随机净化防御评估中的内存瓶颈问题，揭示了近似梯度评估高估鲁棒性的风险，并提供了更可靠的防御基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代随机净化防御的评估常因内存限制而采用近似反向传播，导致攻击信号弱化，可能高估防御鲁棒性；同时，迭代净化中的随机性控制不足，不同净化轨迹会显著改变鲁棒性评估结果。

Method: 结合梯度检查点技术与受控随机性评估协议，实现低内存占用的精确端到端梯度计算，从而支持对扩散模型和基于能量的模型进行全梯度白盒对抗攻击评估。

Result: 全梯度攻击揭示了近似梯度评估遗漏的防御漏洞，在ℓ∞和ℓ2范数下实现了更强的白盒攻击效果，并可探测分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试提供了重要工具，并通过精确梯度评估揭示了现有近似梯度方法可能高估防御鲁棒性的风险。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [193] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了Chronos基础模型处理和内部表征频域信息的能力。通过对简单离散正弦信号进行受控测试，发现模型能够捕获频率信息，但存在特定频率区域的表征质量退化问题。研究为信号处理和信息融合应用提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型虽应用广泛，但对其编码基本信号特性的程度研究不足，存在认知缺口。

Method: 在受控条件下使用轻量级在线最小描述长度（MDL）探针对Chronos的解码器架构进行测试，检验频率信息在其内部表征中的存在性和可分离性。

Result: 揭示了频率内容在频谱中的捕获方式，并识别出模型表征质量可能退化或需要特别关注的频域区域。

Conclusion: 本研究为Chronos用户提供了信号处理和信息融合场景下的实践指导，并推进了时序基础模型的可解释性评估工作。研究表明需要在特定频率区域特别注意模型表征质量的潜在退化问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [194] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuxPath-FM框架，将辅助变量的分布从高斯噪声推广至任意分布，允许构建形式为X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η的多样化轨迹。该方法在保持理论一致性的同时，为生成模型提供了更灵活的概率路径设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配方法将辅助变量限制为高斯噪声，缺乏灵活性，无法充分利用不同先验分布的独特几何特性来设计多样化的概率路径。

Method: 将来自任意分布的辅助变量η引入概率路径，构建形式为X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η的轨迹，证明了该构造保持连续性方程并与边际公式化的训练目标一致。

Result: 该框架支持多种先验分布（高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher等），每种分布提供不同的几何特性；可编码结构化语义信息实现标签引导生成。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了兼具理论严谨性和实践灵活性的通用框架，可应用于生成建模、标签引导生成等多种任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [195] [MinMax Recurrent Neural Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06384)
*Alessandro Ronca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MinMax algebra enables RNNs that avoid vanishing/exploding gradients, achieve full regular language expressivity, and scale efficiently with bounded states—validated by strong synthetic and real-world task performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address vanishing and exploding gradient problems in traditional RNNs while maintaining expressive power and efficient implementation.

Method: Cascade multiple layers of neurons using MinMax algebra-based recurrence to create MinMax Recurrent Neural Cascades (RNCs); evaluate both theoretically and empirically.

Result: Theoretically: (1) Expressivity includes all regular languages; (2) Parallel evaluation with logarithmic runtime; (3) Uniformly bounded states and activations; (4) Gradient exists and bounded; (5) No vanishing state gradient (constant gradient regardless of time distance). Empirically: (1) Perfect performance on synthetic tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art RNNs; (2) 127M parameter model achieves competitive performance on next-token prediction.

Conclusion: MinMax RNCs demonstrate favorable theoretical properties (bounded states, non-vanishing gradients) that translate to practical capabilities, achieving perfect performance on synthetic tasks and competitive results on real-world next-token prediction with 127M parameters.

Abstract: We show that the MinMax algebra provides a form of recurrence that is expressively powerful, efficiently implementable, and most importantly it is not affected by vanishing or exploding gradient. We call MinMax Recurrent Neural Cascades (RNCs) the models obtained by cascading several layers of neurons that employ such recurrence. We show that MinMax RNCs enjoy many favourable theoretical properties. First, their formal expressivity includes all regular languages, arguably the maximal expressivity for a finite-memory system. Second, they can be evaluated in parallel with a runtime that is logarithmic in the input length given enough processors; and they can also be evaluated sequentially. Third, their state and activations are bounded uniformly for all input lengths. Fourth, at almost all points, their loss gradient exists and it is bounded. Fifth, they do not exhibit a vanishing state gradient: the gradient of a state w.r.t. a past state can have constant value one regardless of the time distance between the two states. Finally, we find empirical evidence that the favourable theoretical properties of MinMax RNCs are matched by their practical capabilities: they are able to perfectly solve a number of synthetic tasks, showing superior performance compared to the considered state-of-the-art recurrent neural networks; also, we train a MinMax RNC of 127M parameters on next-token prediction, and the obtained model shows competitive performance for its size, providing evidence of the potential of MinMax RNCs on real-world tasks.

</details>


### [196] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于条件独立信息的局部协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样有效，建立了统一的无环性无关协变量选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据估计因果效应需要识别有效的调整集，但在存在潜在混杂和反馈回路的现实场景中，这一任务极具挑战性。现有方法通常假设因果图无环或依赖全局因果结构学习，限制了适用性和计算效率。

Method: 利用条件独立信息进行局部数据驱动的协变量选择方法。该方法的核心理论依据是条件独立断言在σ-非循环化下的不变性性质。

Result: 证明了该方法在循环因果模型中同样具备可靠性和完备性保证，其理论保证可从无环模型扩展到有环模型。

Conclusion: 该方法在循环和非循环因果模型中均可适用，无需修改，为协变量选择和因果效应估计提供了统一的无环性无关视角。通过广泛的合成数据实验验证了该方法在循环因果模型中的可靠性能。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [197] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标准on-policy蒸馏(OPD)的优势加权策略梯度方法的三个结构性问题（高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失、探索瓶颈），提出非对称on-policy蒸馏(AOPD)，通过在非正优势区域用局部散度最小化替代无效负强化，同时保留正强化学习。实验表明AOPD在数学推理基准上显著优于标准OPD，尤其在弱初始化条件下增益达8.34。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) with advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses: high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.

Method: AOPD replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning, targeting high variance updates, vanishing gradients, and exploration bottlenecks.

Result: AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with average gains of 4.09/8.34 under strong/weak initialization. It maintains higher policy entropy during training and demonstrates better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

Conclusion: AOPD effectively addresses OPD's structural weaknesses, achieving 4.09/8.34 gains on mathematical reasoning under strong/weak initialization, with higher policy entropy and better sequential tool-use adaptation capability retention.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [198] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge是一个后训练剪枝框架，通过Hessian感知的重要性估计和软掩码渐进退火策略，直接优化稀疏掩码而非增加训练数据量。在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏度下，仅用5B tokens即达到57.27%准确率，超越密集模型并接近使用40B tokens的SOTA方法，显著改善了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练半结构化剪枝方法面临严重的质量退化问题，主要源于权重间的结构耦合效应。传统解决方案依赖大规模稀疏重训练（如40B tokens）来恢复精度，导致计算成本极高。作者认为直接在稀疏掩码层面进行优化可能比单纯增加训练数据量更为高效。

Method: SparseForge采用Hessian感知的重要性估计来评估权重重要性，并结合软掩码的渐进退火（progressive annealing）策略将软稀疏模式逐步转化为硬件可执行的2:4结构化稀疏。该方法跳过了传统方法依赖大规模稀疏重训练恢复精度的范式，直接在稀疏掩码层面进行优化。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B上应用2:4稀疏度，SparseForge仅使用5B重训练tokens即达到57.27%的平均零样本准确率，显著超越密集模型基线（56.43%），逼近使用40B tokens的SOTA方法（57.52%）。该精度-效率权衡的优势在其他模型家族中也得到验证。

Conclusion: SparseForge在极少的5B tokens条件下即可超越密集模型基线（56.43%），接近使用40B tokens的SOTA方法（57.52%），证明了直接优化稀疏掩码而非增加训练数据量是一种更高效的稀疏恢复路径。该方法在多个模型家族中表现一致，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [199] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRInGe proposes a new integrated gradients method that operates in predictive distribution space using Fisher-Rao geodesic interpolation instead of traditional input-space baselines, addressing brittleness issues through pullback Fisher metric and trust region stabilization, demonstrating improved performance on calibration metrics across multiple architectures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Integrated Gradients (IG) suffers from brittleness due to reliance on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation effects. The authors seek a more principled approach that operates in predictive distribution space rather than input space.

Method: FRInGe uses a maximum-entropy predictive reference instead of input baselines and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The input-space trajectory is realized through pullback Fisher metric, stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions. Attribution is obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory.

Result: Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe shows clear improvements in calibration-oriented attribution metrics (especially MAS scores) while maintaining competitive performance on perturbation AUC and infidelity metrics.

Conclusion: FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics (especially MAS scores), while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity. The method successfully addresses the brittleness issues of standard IG through distribution-space interpolation and pullback metric stabilization.

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [200] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究分布式图（跨多个客户端）中子图模式检测的问题，提出层间嵌入同步框架解决跨分区子图模式不可识别的问题，实验表明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合具有互补性，且需按步交换而非按轮交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN方法假设可访问完整图，但在分布式场景下各客户端只能访问本地子图，导致客户端本地计算与集中式模型的表示不一致，形成"表示等价性差距"。跨分区边界的子图模式在本地变得不可识别。

Method: 提出逐层嵌入交换框架：在前向传播的每一层同步节点嵌入表示，无需暴露原始特征或标签。在扩展子图假设和共享模型参数条件下，该框架可恢复与集中式GNN相同的节点表示。

Result: 在含环、二分团和scatter-gather模式的合成有向多重图上，嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合具有互补而非可替代关系。二者结合可恢复大部分表示差距。

Conclusion: 交换嵌入的时效性至关重要：按步交换（fresh）比按轮交换（stale）效果更好。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [201] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [202] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题，提出基于Transformer架构的FedFrozen两阶段优化框架。该方法通过在预热阶段训练完整模型后，冻结query/key块而仅优化value块来实现稳定训练。理论上建立了线性注意力下的优化解释框架并揭示了预热长度的权衡机制，实验验证了该方法能有效提升异构联邦学习中Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在异构客户端场景下面临严重挑战，主要源于不一致的本地更新导致的客户端漂移问题。现有方法主要通过目标级正则化或更新校正机制解决，但最新研究表明Transformer架构可能天然对异构联邦训练具有更强的鲁棒性，这启发了作者深入探索注意力机制中不同参数组件的作用。

Method: 提出FedFrozen两阶段优化框架：第一阶段进行全模型预热训练，第二阶段冻结query/key块，仅继续优化value块。理论上证明：在线性注意力形式下，预热阶段可解释为正则化核轮廓目标的非精确下降过程，冻结阶段退化为固定注意力核下的受限value块优化问题，并揭示了预热长度选择的显式权衡关系。

Result: 仿真实验验证了预测的偏置漂移行为，真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异构联邦学习中显著提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: FedFrozen通过两阶段框架有效平衡了模型稳定性和优化效率，在异构联邦学习中显著提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性，且存在可调节的预热长度权衡机制。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [203] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CSI人体活动识别(HAR)在跨场景域迁移时性能下降的问题，提出SAMoE-C方法，通过混合专家系统和注意力路由机制实现场景自适应，在仅需极小重放缓冲区的情况下实现高效跨域学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI-HAR的持续学习方法存在以下问题：随领域累积扩展性差、依赖大重放缓冲区、推理成本线性增长。这促使研究者寻求一种可扩展且高效的跨域CSI-HAR解决方案。

Method: 提出SAMoE-C框架，核心包括：(1)混合专家(MoE)系统，通过模块化专家组合实现知识解耦；(2)基于注意力的语义路由器，仅激活输入相关的专家；(3)轻量级训练协议，仅需极小重放缓冲区即可稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上的实验表明，SAMoE-C接近最优准确率，同时显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: SAMoE-C通过融合模块化专家、选择性激活和轻量训练协议，实现了可扩展的跨域CSI-HAR部署，在真实场景中具备低训练开销和高计算效率。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [204] [ORTHOBO: Orthogonal Bayesian Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06454)
*Maresa Schröder,Pascal Janetzky,Michael Klar,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bayesian optimization can fail due to Monte Carlo estimation noise in acquisition functions. This paper proposes OrthoBO, which uses orthogonal acquisition estimators with control variates to reduce variance and stabilize candidate rankings, demonstrating strong performance in hyperparameter optimization tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bayesian optimization (BO) suffers from failure when finite-sample Monte Carlo error perturbs acquisition values, causing suboptimal decisions despite correctly specified surrogate models and acquisition targets.

Method: Proposed an orthogonal acquisition estimator using score-function control variates with optimal weights to create acquisition residuals orthogonal to posterior score directions, combined with ensemble surrogates and outer log transformation (OrthoBO framework).

Result: Theoretical analysis confirms the estimator preserves targets, reduces variance, and improves pairwise ranking stability. Empirical results show reduced acquisition estimation variance, stabilized candidate rankings, and strong downstream performance in neural network hyperparameter optimization.

Conclusion: OrthoBO framework effectively reduces acquisition estimation variance, stabilizes candidate rankings, and achieves strong performance in both synthetic and real-world hyperparameter optimization tasks.

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely used for hyperparameter optimization when model evaluations are expensive; however, noisy acquisition estimates can lead to unstable decisions. We identify acquisition estimation noise as a failure mode that was previously overlooked: even when the surrogate model and acquisition target are correctly specified, finite-sample Monte Carlo error can perturb acquisition values. This can, in turn, flip candidate rankings and lead to suboptimal BO decisions. As a remedy, we aim at variance reduction and propose an orthogonal acquisition estimator that subtracts an optimally weighted score-function control variate, which yields an acquisition residual orthogonal to posterior score directions and which thus reduces Monte Carlo variance. We further introduce OrthoBO: a Bayesian optimization framework that combines our orthogonal acquisition estimator with ensemble surrogates and an outer log transformation. We show theoretically that our estimator preserves the target, leads to variance reduction, and improves pairwise ranking stability. We further verify the theoretical properties of OrthoBO through numerical experiments where our framework reduces acquisition estimation variance, stabilizes candidate rankings, and achieves strong performance. We also demonstrate the downstream utility of OrthoBO in hyperparameter optimization for neural network training and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [205] [MINER: Mining Multimodal Internal Representation for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06460)
*Weien Li,Rui Song,Zeyu Li,Haochen Liu,Gonghao Zhang,Difan Jiao,Zhenwei Tang,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Xue Liu,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has become essential for accessing information in visually rich documents. Existing approaches fall into two camps. Late-interaction retrievers achieve strong quality through fine-grained token-level matching but store hundreds of vectors per page, incurring large index footprints and high serving costs. By contrast, dense single-vector retrievers retain storage and latency advantages but consistently lag in quality because they compress all information into a single final-layer embedding. In this work, we first conduct a layerwise diagnostic on single-vector retrievers, revealing that retrieval-relevant signal resides in internal representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose MINER (Mining Multimodal Internal RepreseNtation for Efficient Retrieval), a lightweight plug-in module that probes and fuses internal signals across transformer layers into a single compact embedding without modifying the backbone or sacrificing single-vector efficiency. The first Retrieval-Aligned Layer Probing stage attaches a lightweight probe at each layer, surfacing which dimensions carry retrieval-relevant information. The subsequent Adaptive Sparse Multi-Layer Fusion stage applies performance-adaptive neuron-level masking to the selected layers and fuses the surviving signals into the final dense vector. Across ViDoRe V1/V2/V3, MINER outperforms existing dense single-vector retrievers on the majority of benchmarks, with up to 4.5% nDCG@5 improvement over its corresponding backbone. Compared to strong late-interaction baselines, in some settings MINER substantially narrows the nDCG@$5$ gap to $0.2$ while preserving the storage and serving advantages of dense retrieval.

</details>


### [206] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"多样性曲线"（diversity curves）方法，通过边收缩粗化追踪图的散布度（spread of graphs）等距不变量，生成可解释、高效且跨粗化层级可比较的图级嵌入表示，用于解决不同规模图的比较问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无监督任务中，比较来自同一分布但具有不同节点数的图仍然具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏可解释性、可扩展性和可靠性，需要一种能够感知图大小的图级表示方法。

Method: 通过边收缩粗化（edge contraction coarsening）追踪图在不同粗化层级上的结构多样性，利用"图的散布度"（spread of graphs）——一种新的等距不变量——来编码图的度量多样性和几何特征。该方法通过跨粗化层级跟踪图的散布度分布，生成多样性曲线图嵌入。

Result: 实验表明，多样性曲线在（i）跨不同规模图的聚类与可视化、（ii）区分单细胞图的几何特性、（iii）比较分子图数据集结构、（iv）表征几何形状等任务中均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Diversity curves provide interpretable, efficient, and comparable graph representations, outperforming baseline methods in clustering graphs across sizes, distinguishing single-cell graph geometry, comparing molecular graph structures, and characterizing geometric shapes.

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [207] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于算子理论的新型表示学习框架，通过hitting-time观测恢复MDP的有向时间几何结构。该框架在Hilbert空间中学习位移几何，使得预期hitting-time表示为潜在位移的线性泛函。基于此理论提出IEL算法，在离线迷宫 locomotion 任务上达到最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
