Motivation: 现有方法产生的距离度量往往是对称的或不满足三角不等式，无法有效捕捉序贯决策过程中的方向性时间结构。

Method: 提出同构嵌入学习(IEL)算法，结合HILP风格一致性目标和显式hitting-time回归目标，通过基于图的多阶段规划实现长期导航任务。

Result: 理论证明：在潜在线性闭包条件下，表示存在且可辨识(至多差一个有界线性同构)；有限维实现中，全局hitting-time误差被单步转移误差放大环境暂态谱半径所界定；并提供有限样本 guarantees。

Conclusion: IEL algorithm通过显式 hitting-time 回归确保学习到的几何结构反映实际决策进度，非对称组合结构支持鲁棒的图基多阶段规划，在离线迷宫运动数据上取得了最先进的基础策略学习性能。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [208] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一个基于预测的KV-Cache管理系统,专为动态LLM工作流设计,通过预测未来agent调用来优化GPU内存中的缓存复用,从而提升推理效率。相比LRU最高提升1.85倍,相比KVFlow提升1.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-Cache管理方案存在局限: agent级管理无法利用工作流内部的复用机会,而工作流级管理假设调用序列静态不变。但实际工作流通常是动态的,调用序列随任务上下文变化,导致现有方案效果不佳。

Method: PBKV通过融合历史工作流指导和目标工作流上下文,预测工作流未来几步的agent调用;基于预测结果评估各缓存条目的复用潜力,优先保留高潜力条目;在缓存淘汰和预取阶段保守利用预测结果以应对预测误差。

Result: 在三个工作流基准测试上,PBKV相比LRU在动态工作流上最高加速1.85倍,相比SOTA基线KVFlow在静态工作流上最高加速1.26倍。

Conclusion: 通过预测驱动的KV-Cache管理策略,PBKV能有效应对动态工作流场景,兼顾缓存复用优化与预测鲁棒性,实现显著的性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [209] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-MMR proposes a novel off-policy evaluation framework for finite-horizon MDPs that learns reweighting weights through moment matching, achieving dimension-free finite-sample guarantees under Q^π realizability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) is a core challenge in offline reinforcement learning, requiring accurate estimation of expected returns under target policies using logged data without interaction.

Method: Q-MMR learns scalar weights for each data point via a top-down moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class, enabling reweighted rewards to approximate expected return under target policy.

Result: The framework establishes data-dependent finite-sample guarantees under only Q^π realizability with a dimension-free bound. It also connects to existing methods (importance sampling, linear FQE) and provides new theoretical insights into coverage.

Conclusion: Q-MMR provides a theoretically principled approach to off-policy evaluation with dimension-free finite-sample guarantees. The work offers new insights into the coverage concept, which is fundamental to offline RL.

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [210] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPSD-RL是一种用于连续强化学习中的值保持结构发现方法，基于李群作用和拉回算子理论，证明了值保持结构存在当且仅当价值函数拉回与动作推送满足特定交换条件。该框架可发现精确和近似值保持结构，通过学习无穷小生成元、ODE指数化和转换一致性正则化实现，并在理论上证明HJB不匹配小意味着值函数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间和状态/动作空间的强化学习通常数据密集，且在干扰变异和分布偏移下表现脆弱。现有方法多聚焦于特殊情形（如固定对称性、精确等变性），缺乏对需要非线性算子来发现更一般结构的方法。

Method: VPSD-RL将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过李群作用和关联的拉回算子定义值保持映射。通过拟合可微分漂移、扩散和奖励模型；利用决定方程残差最小化学习无穷小生成元；使用ODE流将其指数化得到有限变换；通过转换一致性正则化将结构集成到RL中。

Result: 当生成元/奖励不匹配有界时，最优值函数沿近似轨道具有定量稳定性，敏感性由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准上实现了更高的数据效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL通过在连续控制基准测试中展现出改进的数据效率和鲁棒性，验证了基于李群作用的值保持结构在连续强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [211] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero是一种PAC-私有零阶方法，通过符号量化和一致性步骤机制，首次实现在零互信息（I=0）的高隐私条件下对大语言模型进行微调并保持可用效用。PACZero-MI提供预算内MI控制，PACZero-ZPL通过硬币翻转实现完全零信息泄露，在SST-2和SQuAD任务上超越所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私零阶（DP-ZO）方法在高隐私 regime（ε<1）下无法产生可用效用。需要一种新的隐私框架，在零互信息（I=0）条件下仍能提供有意义的隐私保护同时保持模型实用性。

Method: 基于PAC隐私框架，仅在发布依赖于哪个候选子集是秘密时才收取互信息费用。关键创新是对子集聚合的零阶梯度进行符号量化，产生频繁的一致性步骤（unanimity steps），此时每个候选子集对更新方向达成一致，发布的符号代价为零条件互信息。提出两变体：PACZero-MI（通过二元发布的精确校准进行预算MI控制）和PACZero-ZPL（在分歧步骤使用均匀硬币翻转实现I=0）。

Result: 在SST-2上，使用OPT-1.3B全参数微调，PACZero-ZPL在I=0时达到88.99±0.91，仅比非私有MeZO基线（91.1 FT）低2.1个百分点。在SST-2和SQuAD上，PACZero-ZPL在I=0条件下对OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B均获得有竞争力的准确率和非平凡F1。隐私攻击后验成功率被限制在先验水平。

Conclusion: PACZero是首个在高隐私 regime (ε<1) 下实现可用实用性的方法。PACZero-ZPL通过均匀掷硬币机制在I=0条件下保持竞争力，PACZero-MI则提供预算内的互信息控制。两变体覆盖了隐私-效用的权衡权衡，为大模型微调中的隐私保护提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [212] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张基础模型（FM）的分布外（OOD）泛化问题具有结构独特性，无法仅通过模型中心范式解决，需要智能体（agentic）范式作为补充。研究通过四个步骤论证：(1)提出阶段感知的形式化框架；(2)证明参数覆盖天花板；(3)定义智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性并证明其超越天花板；(4)回应七个反驳论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界环境中部署，面临知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放式任务变化等OOD问题，这些问题与过去研究的设定有本质不同。此外，现代FM的预训练和后训练分布往往只是部分可观察的，传统的模型中心方法面临根本性局限。

Method: (1) 提出阶段感知的形式化方案以适应部分可观察的多阶段训练分布；(2) 证明参数覆盖天花板：存在实际相关的输入，对于任何模型中心方法（训练时或测试时）都无法在容差ε内处理；(3) 通过四个结构属性（感知、策略选择、外部行动、闭环验证）刻画智能体OOD系统，证明其严格超越参数覆盖天花板；(4) 系统回应七条反驳论点。

Result: 研究证明存在模型中心方法无法处理的输入（参数覆盖天花板），而智能体系统可超越该天花板。两者是互补而非替代关系，需要明确将智能体范式作为一流研究方向。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD问题在结构上不同于传统分布偏移问题，模型中心范式存在根本性局限。智能体系统通过感知、策略选择、外部行动和闭环验证四个属性，可超越参数覆盖天花板。论文倡导将智能体范式确立为FM-OOD研究的一流方向，同时强调两种范式的互补性。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [213] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过秩-1替换分析揭示了RLVR中隐性奖励过拟合现象，发现RL训练本质上优化单一奇异谱，rank-1组件特异性携带推理能力而非通用知识，为改进RL训练范式和持续学习提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明RLVR增强的推理能力主要集中于rank-1分量，但这一现象的深层机制尚不清晰。研究旨在探索RLVR对模型参数的塑造方式及其隐含的过拟合问题。

Method: 采用周期性秩-1替换（Periodic Rank-1 Substitution）方法观察模型性能变化，分析RLVR训练后线性层奇异值分布特征，以及训练过程中左奇异向量的对齐程度。

Result: 发现了RLVR中存在隐性奖励过拟合现象：模型在训练奖励较低时仍可在测试集上表现良好。进一步揭示了RL训练的三项特性：rank-1组件仅承载数学推理能力而不保留其他知识；RLVR本质上是优化特定奇异谱的过程，线性层奇异值呈重尾分布；左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐倾向。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了RLVR通过优化单一奇异谱来实现推理能力提升的本质，并发现模型在保持基础能力的同时仅通过rank-1组件获得数学推理能力。这一发现为改进RL训练范式和实现持续学习提供了新的理论依据和实践方向。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [214] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MELO方法，通过指数加权最小二乘多尺度适应专家与无参数聚合器MLpol的结合，在无需外部协变量和领域知识的情况下，实现对分布漂移的自适应处理，理论证明Oracle不等式并取得显著实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习方法在处理分布漂移时面临稳定性-适应性权衡的两难困境，且最优适应记忆尺度事先未知。

Method: 提出MELO方法，通过多层遗忘因子的EWLS专家与MLpol无参数聚合器的结合，实现自适应尺度的对冲。理论证明基于有界性条件的确定性Oracle不等式。

Result: 在法国电力负荷预测COVID-19数据集上，MELO相对基线MLpol RMSE降低34.7%，且优于使用外部政策协变量的TabICL参考模型。

Conclusion: MELO在无需人工标注、仅依赖递归更新的轻量级计算下即可应对分布漂移，且无需外部协变量或领域知识即可实现对COVID-19特殊时段的有效适应。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [215] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种序贯式框架，利用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳过程的模拟器模型结合强化学习策略，在存在内在随机性和跨实验室变异性的情况下优化遗传电路。核心创新在于"摊销"方法，通过预先训练来避免每次实验后的昂贵推理步骤，实现即时、基于观测的自适应设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计面临两大不确定性挑战：生物分子反应的内在随机性和实验室条件的可变性。现有的贝叶斯方法虽能处理不确定性，但每个实验轮次后都需要昂贵的推理和优化计算，导致设计周期延迟。

Method: 采用模拟器辅助的强化学习框架，结合：(1) 基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳过程的模拟器模型；(2) 强化学习策略驱动的方法；(3) 针对不确定性参数的预训练摊销方法，使系统能立即适应未知条件而无需显式参数推断。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和 repressilator 电路上的演示表明，该框架能有效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性。

Conclusion: 摊销方法成功克服了传统贝叶斯方法的计算瓶颈，为遗传电路的序贯设计提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [216] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY proposes a divergence-free guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes sample diversity by leveraging Fokker-Planck equation symmetries through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, without requiring additional training.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion and flow matching models tend to generate samples with limited diversity while maintaining quality, requiring a mechanism to promote diversity among generated samples.

Method: EDDY exploits Fokker-Planck equation symmetries using kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields constructed from repulsive directions, generating divergence-free dynamics that promote joint-level diversity while preserving marginal distributions. Practical approximations are proposed for computational efficiency.

Result: EDDY improves sample diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines, validated on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation.

Conclusion: EDDY provides an effective and efficient solution for improving diversity in generative models without additional training, validated on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation tasks.

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究树集成模型的最优反事实解释计算问题，提出约束规划方法CPCF，并与MaxSAT、MILP方法进行系统比较，分析了三种范式在不同场景下的相对优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次优的解释可能导致建议的改变大幅超出实际所需，影响用户信任度和公平性。次优和启发式错误会对不同个体产生不均匀影响，需要计算最优的反事实解释。

Method: CPCF约束规划方法：将数值特征编码为由分裂阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保持原生有限域表示；扩展MaxSAT公式以支持软投票集成；与MILP方法进行系统性比较。

Result: CPCF实现紧凑有限域公式，支持多种距离目标且无需连续分裂边界搜索。实验表明：CP方法整体性能最佳；MaxSAT擅长处理硬投票集成；MILP在中等级别分裂层级数的摊销推理场景下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: CP方法总体性能最佳，MaxSAT在硬投票森林表现突出，MILP在中等分裂层级数场景下具有竞争力。研究强调了不同数学规划范式在不同场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [218] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This study compared logistic regression, random forest, and SVM for breast cancer subtype classification using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. While overall accuracy was high across all models, macro F1 analysis revealed that simpler logistic regression models achieved more balanced performance across subtypes, particularly for rare classes, highlighting the importance of model selection and evaluation metrics in high-dimensional biomedical classification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurate breast cancer subtype classification from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment, but high dimensionality with limited sample sizes poses significant challenges for machine learning models.

Method: Evaluated logistic regression, random forest, and SVM models trained with varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518) on TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Performance assessed using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with accuracy and macro F1 score.

Result: Despite all models achieving high overall accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial performance differences: logistic regression showed most stable and balanced performance including better detection of rare classes; random forest underperformed on minority subtypes; SVM was sensitive to feature dimensionality.

Conclusion: Model simplicity, appropriate evaluation metrics, and careful feature selection are critical for high-dimensional biological classification tasks. Logistic regression's balanced performance suggests that simpler models may be preferable for clinical deployment in breast cancer subtype classification.

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [219] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了深度学习优化中梯度方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦的现象，设计了基于方向一致性信号自适应调整momentum的GONO优化器，在理论保证和实验验证两个层面证明了方向对齐作为优化信号的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（Adam/SGD/RMSprop）缺乏对梯度方向时间一致性的显式利用机制，仅依赖幅值信号无法有效区分plateaus、saddle points和真实收敛。

Method: 提出GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer)，基于梯度余弦相似性cc_t自适应调整Adam的momentum系数β₁：方向一致时增强momentum，振荡时抑制momentum。

Result: 理论证明GONO保持O(1/√T)收敛速率；cc_t振荡检测F1达1.00（vs 梯度范数0.45）；MNIST/CIFAR-10/ResNet-18性能与AdamW相当。

Conclusion: GONO在保持与AdamW competitive性能的同时，提供了理论上严格、实践上可操作的优化信号。通过cc_t实现振荡检测（F1=1.00），并通过自适应momentum机制提升优化效率。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [220] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [221] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒优化框架，用于学习一阶优化方法的超参数（如步长）。该方法在Wasserstein度量下对性能估计问题（PEP）进行鲁棒化，统一了经典的L2O学习和最坏情况最优算法设计两种范式。通过随机梯度下降求解，并提供有限样本风险界。在二次优化、LASSO和线性规划上的实验表明，该方法具有可认证的鲁棒性和优异样本外性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化方法（如学习率）通常在经验风险最小化或最坏情况分析两个极端之间缺乏平衡。L2O方法虽经验性能好但缺乏鲁棒性保证，而PEP方法提供最坏情况最优但可能过于保守。

Method: 1) 构建Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本的PEP；2) 当鲁棒半径趋于0时退化为经典L2O，当半径趋于无穷时收敛到PEP；3) 使用SGD求解，可微穿过内层半定规划；4) 提供高概率有限样本风险界。

Result: 理论证明：真实风险以高概率被样本内L2O最优值加上一项（随样本量收缩）所上界，且不差于PEP最坏情况上界。实验表明学习得到的算法在二次优化、LASSO、线性规划基准上具有可认证鲁棒性，优于最坏情况最优和vanilla L2O基线。

Conclusion: 该框架成功桥接了L2O和PEP两种范式，在经验性能和鲁棒性之间取得平衡，提供了可认证的样本外性能保证。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [222] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次从泛函分析角度严格刻画了带 L2 正则化的 Transformer 目标函数，证明其满足 Villani 的 coercive 能量函数准则，进而推导出 log-Sobolev 和 Poincaré 常数，为 noisy SGD 提供收敛保证并改进 PAC-Bayes 泛化界。实验验证了理论预测的有效性，表明 weight decay 建立了深度学习快速 Langevin 混合所需的数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weight decay 作为大语言模型中的常用正则化器，其对 Transformer 损失景观的精确作用机制尚未得到理论上的充分探索。该研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 证明正则化损失函数F满足 Villani 的 coercive 能量函数准则（无限光滑、至少二次增长、Gaussian 可积尾部、微分增长条件）；2. 从该结构推导出显式的 log-Sobolev 和 Poincaré 常数 C_LS ≤ λ⁻¹ + d/λ²；3. 引入可扩展的 Villani 诊断量 Ψ_s(θ) = -ΔF + s⁻¹||∇F||² 并使用 Hutchinson trace 探针有效估计。

Result: 1. 理论上建立了正则化损失与 Villani 判据的严格对应关系；2. 推导出与正则化强度 λ 和模型维度 d 相关的显式常数，并由此获得 noisy SGD 的有限时间收敛保证和 PAC-Bayes 泛化界；3. 实验验证（GPT-Neo-125M, Penn Treebank, WikiText-103）：确认 Ψ_s 的预测二次增长行为、Hessian 谱膨胀以及与 log-Sobolev 分析一致的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: Weight decay 不仅能改善泛化性能，还建立了深度学习中快速 Langevin 混合和理论上合理的曲率感知优化所需的数学条件。该研究为理解权重衰减的深层机制提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [223] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAPRO是首个为多轮LLM交互中事件时间预测提供理论有效的动态预算分配框架。它通过投影优化方法，在不依赖截断与事件时间条件独立性假设的前提下，以均方根截断权重取代最坏情况权重实现了更紧密的覆盖保证，并在多种LLM评估任务中验证了其有效性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）的多轮对话场景中进行性能评估和预测至关重要但计算成本高昂。关键事件（如越狱或智能体任务成功）往往需要重复交互后才能出现，且这些事件可能非常罕见，在有限计算预算下难以观察到。

Method: 提出了DAPRO（Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization）框架，通过投影优化实现动态预算分配。核心创新包括：1）无需传统共形生存方法所需的截断与事件时间条件独立性假设；2）提出新的覆盖范围界，以均方根截断权重而非最坏情况权重为尺度，理论证明更紧。

Result: 在保证满足预算约束的前提下，DAPRO提供了无分布、有限样本的覆盖保证。在Agentic任务成功率、对抗性越狱、毒性内容生成、RAG幻觉等多种评估指标上，显著优于静态基线方法，覆盖率更接近目标水平且方差更低。

Conclusion: 在多种LLM（Llama 3.1、Qwen 2.5）和多种评估场景（智能体任务成功率、对抗性越狱、毒性内容生成、RAG幻觉）中，DAPRO始终能够更接近目标覆盖水平且方差更低，同时满足预算约束。实验结果验证了该框架在有限计算资源下进行大规模LLM评估的实用价值。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [224] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了softmax注意力机制如何实现上下文学习。研究证明，通过监督学习训练的自注意力层可以隐式学习梯度下降步骤，多层堆叠或循环应用该层可实现上下文逻辑回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解transformer的上下文学习（ICL）机制——即其隐式执行上下文上算法以增强预测的能力缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 构建多层transformer执行上下文逻辑回归，每层执行一步归一化梯度下降；通过监督训练单层自注意力实现梯度下降步骤，然后循环应用该层构建loop模型；提供训练收敛和分布外泛化保证。

Result: 理论验证了多层transformer可实现上下文逻辑回归；单层自注意力可通过监督学习收敛到梯度下降算子；loop模型具有分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 揭示了softmax transformer作为上下文学习器的机制，为ICL的理论理解做出贡献。研究表明，通过监督学习训练单层自注意力，并在循环模型中迭代应用，可以实现对上下文数据的算法执行（如梯度下降）。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [225] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSAE，一种具有动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器，通过可微分的Soft Top-K算子实现输入依赖的稀疏度调整，解决传统SAE固定稀疏度无法适应不同输入复杂度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE（如TopK SAE）使用固定稀疏度K，忽略真实数据的复杂性差异。自然数据位于具有可变局部内在维度的流形上，不同样本的相关因素数量差异显著。固定K在简单输入上引入噪声，在复杂输入上遗漏重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE方法，使用可微分的Soft Top-K算子学习输入依赖的稀疏度k，使模型能根据每个输入的复杂性调整活跃特征数量。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征，表征更好地匹配数据结构，解释长度反映输入信息量。

Conclusion: SoftSAE通过动态稀疏度机制，有效解决了固定稀疏度的局限性，在特征可解释性和表征质量上均有提升。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [226] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型中注意力接收点（attention sink）现象的结构性起源——根源于自注意力机制中价值聚合过程的方差差异，并经由FFN层中超神经元激活大幅放大。通过注意力掩码修改和目标token表征方差放大两种干预手段验证了该因果链，并提出一种名为"头-wise RMSNorm"的架构修改以稳定预训练期间的价值聚合输出，显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中普遍存在注意力接收点现象，即初始token不成比例地垄断注意力分数，但这种现象的结构性起源尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的理解和控制方法。

Method: 1. 将注意力接收点追溯至自注意力机制中固有的价值聚合过程所导致的系统性方差差异；2. 证明FFN层中超神经元的激活会大幅放大该差异，具体是通道稀疏的下投影触发了第一个token表征的维度差异，迫使注意力接收点作为结构锚点形成；3. 通过两种受控干预验证因果链：使用注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应，以及放大目标token表征的方差；4. 提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改以稳定价值聚合输出。

Result: 两种干预手段均可在任意位置复现注意力接收点现象，验证了所揭示的因果链；head-wise RMSNorm通过恢复各位置统计对等性显著加速了模型收敛。

Conclusion: 注意力接收点现象具有明确的结构性机制解释，其根源在于价值聚合过程的方差差异和FFN层超神经元的放大作用。该理解为系统性地控制接收点形成提供了理论基础，head-wise RMSNorm是一种有效的架构级解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [227] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRPC框架，通过将无差异粒子更新与条件高斯过程更新分离，实现在线贝叶斯校准，并在检测到 regime shifts 时通过重启机制重置校正过程，从而同时处理渐变系统演化和突发 regime shifts，在合成数据和工厂仿真基准上验证了其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-偏差混淆问题，且传统上为离线稳态假设，难以适应数字孪生应用中系统的时变演化（包括渐变漂移和突变 regime shifts）；现有数据同化方法虽然支持序贯更新，但通常不显式建模系统偏差，在突变场景下效果不佳。

Method: BRPC将校正参数的无差异粒子更新与偏差的条件高斯过程更新分离；通过重启机制检测 regime shifts 并重置校正过程，从而兼顾渐变演化中的跟踪性能和突变检测能力。

Result: BRPC在渐变演化下改善了校准精度；结合重启机制的BRPC在突变 regime shifts 下进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能。

Conclusion: BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes; restart-augmented BRPC further enhances robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts, outperforming sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines on both synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks.

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [228] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper merges concept-based explanations with formal abductive/contrastive explanations to propose concept-based causal explanations. It uses concept erasure to establish causal relationships and enumerates minimal explanations that identify the smallest sets of high-level concepts responsible for model predictions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing concept-based explanation methods lack causal connections or are limited to single-concept explanations, while formal abductive explanations only consider low-level features (pixels). A unified approach is needed to capture minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes.

Method: The paper proposes a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations using concept erasure procedures to establish causal relationships between concepts and model predictions.

Result: The method can generate concept-based explanations for individual image predictions and for collections of images where the model exhibits user-specified common behaviors.

Conclusion: The approach is evaluated on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, demonstrating its effectiveness in providing helpful and user-friendly explanations for both individual predictions and common model behaviors across image collections.

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [229] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper extends Venn-Abers predictors (originally for binary classification) to unbounded regression using conformal prediction, showing improved point prediction efficiency for larger training sets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers predictors are limited to binary classification and bounded regression, lacking applicability to unbounded regression scenarios.

Method: Generalizes Venn-Abers framework to unbounded regression by incorporating conformal prediction elements; derives point regressors from Venn-Abers regressors.

Result: Simulation and empirical studies demonstrate improved predictive efficiency for larger training sets.

Conclusion: Results show that Venn-Abers regressors somewhat improve predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [230] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全量微调（SFT）能够取得更好的"学习-遗忘"权衡，称之为"优化器-模型一致性"。通过实验和理论分析表明，优化器会对激活值产生正则化效应，形成不同的损失景观；为减少预训练知识的遗忘，SFT权重更新需遵循特定结构，而使用相同优化器可自然满足这一条件。此外，Muon优化器在推理任务微调中表现不如AdamW，因其倾向于机械记忆而非模式获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究对预训练和微调阶段优化器选择的一致性问题关注不足，且缺乏对优化器如何影响微调过程中知识遗忘现象的系统性理解。

Method: 1) 对比实验：测试多种优化器（AdamW、Muon等）和LoRA在SFT阶段的表现；2) 控制变量实验：探究优化器对激活值正则化效应的影响；3) 理论分析：解释为何相同优化器能降低遗忘；4) 合成语言建模实验：分析Muon在少样本学习中的机械记忆倾向。

Result: 1) 使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全量微调显著优于使用不同优化器或LoRA；2) 优化器通过正则化激活值塑造模型，影响损失景观结构；3) Muon在推理任务微调中表现差于AdamW，因其过度依赖记忆而非泛化。

Conclusion: 优化器-模型一致性是影响LLM微调效果的重要因素。预训练和微调阶段使用相同优化器可通过保持正则化效应的一致性来减少知识遗忘。该发现对实际应用中的优化器选择策略具有指导意义，并提示需要设计更适合连续训练场景的优化器。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [231] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPool通过将各层隔离的专家集合替换为共享专家池，实现了专家容量的全局分配，配合池级辅助损失和NormRouter实现稳定训练。研究表明专家参数可亚线性增长且保持更高效率，为MoE架构的深度扩展提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构采用严格的逐层专家分配规则，导致深度扩展与专家参数线性增长耦合。研究通过路由探测发现：深层路由器的随机化仅导致1.0-1.6个点的精度下降，表明现有分配模式存在冗余，启发了UniPool的共享专家池设计思路。

Method: UniPool采用全局专家池替代原有的层隔离专家分配方式，由各层独立路由器共享访问。为实现稳定训练，引入了池级辅助损失函数平衡专家利用率，并采用NormRouter提供稀疏且尺度稳定的路由能力。

Result: 在5个LLaMA架构模型尺度（182M至978M参数）上，UniPool在Pile数据集30B tokens训练下持续优于匹配的 vanilla MoE基线，验证损失最大降低0.0386。更重要的是，仅使用41.6%-66.7%专家参数预算的reduced-pool变体即可达到或超越逐层MoE性能。

Conclusion: UniPool证明了专家参数可以与深度解耦，实现亚线性增长而非线性增长，同时保持优于传统MoE的性能表现。此外，UniPool的优势可与更细粒度的专家分解相叠加，表明共享专家池设计具有较强的可扩展性。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [DADL: A Declarative Description Language for Enterprise Tool Libraries in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05247)
*Axel Dunkel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DADL是一种YAML格式的API描述语言，旨在解决MCP在企业规模部署中的结构性缺陷。通过单一声明式文件描述REST API的所有细节，由运行时解释执行，无需为每个API部署独立服务器进程。实验表明，该方案将工具广告的上下文成本降低142倍（从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MCP（Model Context Protocol）在组织规模部署中的两个结构性问题：(1) 每个API集成都作为独立服务器进程部署，导致大量薄封装；(2) 每个工具的注册模型使上下文窗口随目录大小线性增长，迫使实际部署只能暴露组织实际使用的API的一小部分。

Method: 提出DADL（Dunkel API Description Language）：一种YAML格式，在单一声明式文件中描述REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应塑形和访问分类。由执行层在运行时解释DADL文件，无需为每个API部署独立服务器进程，也无需生成集成代码。所有工具共享同一运行时，实现凭证和授权的集中管理，LLM通过固定大小的Code Mode接口访问工具目录。

Result: DADL v0.1规范以CC BY-SA 4.0发布，公共注册库包含1,833个工具定义，涵盖20个服务。在该目录上，Code Mode将工具广告的上下文成本从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens，实现142倍 reduction；搜索和执行调用的每次调用成本额外计算，取决于任务类型。

Conclusion: DADL实现了Enterprise Tool Library（企业工具库）：一个版本化、可审计的API集成集合，任何团队都可以通过单一认证和授权边界扩展、共享和消费。有效解决了MCP在企业场景下的可扩展性和上下文窗口消耗问题。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is the standard interface between large language model (LLM) agents and external tools. At organizational scale, however, it exposes two structural problems. First, every API integration is shipped as a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependency tree, and credential handling; recent empirical work shows the overwhelming majority of these servers are thin wrappers around REST APIs. Second, the per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of the APIs an organization actually uses.
  We present DADL (Dunkel API Description Language), a YAML format describing a REST API's endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single declarative file. A DADL file is interpreted by an execution layer at runtime; no per-API server process is deployed and no integration code is generated, though the runtime is itself a server. Because all tools share that runtime, credentials and authorization are managed centrally, and the catalog reaches the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface independent of size. The result is an Enterprise Tool Library: a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary.
  The DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0, and a public registry contains 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. On this catalog, Code Mode reduces the context cost of tool advertisement from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000, a 142x reduction; the per-call cost of search and execute invocations is additional and depends on the task.

</details>


### [233] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI文档智能的大规模审计交易测试自动化框架，利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据（约20份标注文档即可），并与权威数据源进行对账，实现全量级别的审计测试，替代传统的抽样审查，提升审计覆盖范围并支持持续审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统审计交易测试依赖人工抽样审查非结构化PDF对账单，劳动强度大且无法扩展到百万级交易量，难以满足大规模审计需求。

Method: 使用Snowflake Document AI构建文档智能模型，利用约20份标注文档的小规模语料库从PDF对账单中提取结构化数据；将提取结果与权威“真相来源”数据集进行对账比对；通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现结果。

Result: 实现population-level（全量）审计测试，取代传统抽样方法，提升审计覆盖率，支持近实时的风险识别和持续审计保障。

Conclusion: 该框架证明文档智能与数据分析驱动审计框架的结合可实现大规模、可扩展的近实时风险识别，为持续审计提供技术支撑。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [234] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述114项研究，探讨训练数据质量问题如何影响代码生成质量。建立了统一的分类体系和包含18种机制的因果框架，并综合分析了检测与缓解技术。研究发现质量保证正从后生成过滤转向主动的数据治理和闭环修复，为通过集成数据策管开发可靠代码生成LLM提供了研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成任务中频繁产生有缺陷的输出，从逻辑错误到安全漏洞不等。尽管这些生成失败常被视为模型层面的问题，但经验证据表明其根本原因往往可追溯到训练语料库的缺陷。然而，训练数据质量问题与生成代码质量问题之间的具体关联机制尚未被清晰映射。

Method: 对该领域114项主要研究进行系统性文献综述，建立了统一的分类体系，将生成代码质量问题分为9个维度，训练数据质量问题分为代码和非代码属性两大类。在此基础上，形式化了一个包含18种典型传播映射机制的因果框架，并对数据、模型和生成生命周期中的最新检测与缓解技术进行综合分析。

Result: 建立了统一的分类体系：生成代码质量问题涵盖9个维度，训练数据质量问题分为代码属性和非代码属性两类；形式化了一个包含18种典型传播映射机制的因果框架；综合分析了跨越数据、模型和生成生命周期的检测与缓解技术。

Conclusion: 研究发现了明显的方法论转变：质量保证正从被动的、基于启发式的后生成过滤，转向主动的、以数据为中心的治理和闭环修复。最终识别了开放性挑战并概述了通过集成数据策管和持续评估开发可靠LLM的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [235] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对CI系统中无关构建失败的识别问题，基于7个Apache项目的77,000+构建失败数据，采用半监督PU学习建立预测模型，实现0.63-0.97的AUC性能，可有效辅助开发者快速判断构建失败是否与自身代码变更相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI系统并发运行多个构建时，合法构建失败可能与触发它的代码提交无关，导致开发者需要浪费大量时间（平均4小时）来确定失败是否与当前变更相关，造成不必要的人力浪费。

Method: 对7个Apache项目的77,354次CI构建失败进行分析；对371个已确认的无关构建失败进行文档分析；从问题报告、评论和提交中提取33个特征；使用半监督PU（正样本与未标记样本）学习构建预测模型。

Result: 无关测试失败占无关构建失败的20%；模型精确率0.70-0.88，召回率0.30-1.00，F1分数0.44-0.91，AUC 0.63-0.97；CI延迟、重复错误信息和前置评论数量是预测无关构建失败的重要指标。

Conclusion: PU learning可有效帮助开发者识别可能与当前代码提交无关的构建失败，从而减少不必要的工作量。关键特征包括CI延迟、重复错误信息和评论数量。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) systems often run many builds concurrently. In this setting, a legitimate build failure may not be caused by the code push that triggered it. Such unrelated build failures can waste developer effort because developers must determine whether the failure is actionable for their current change. We study 77,354 CI build failures from seven open source Apache projects to understand and predict unrelated build failures. We find that developers spend a median of 4 hours identifying whether a failure is related or unrelated to their push. We also perform a document analysis of 371 confirmed unrelated build failures sampled from 10,316 potentially unrelated failures. The analysis shows that unrelated test failures account for 20% of the cases in which developers classify build failures as unrelated. To predict unrelated build failures, we extract 33 features from issue reports, issue comments, and commits associated with the triggering push. We build semi-supervised Positive and Unlabeled (PU) learning models for seven Apache projects. The models achieve precision from 0.70 to 0.88, recall from 0.30 to 1.00, F1-score from 0.44 to 0.91, and AUC from 0.63 to 0.97. Feature importance analysis shows that CI latency, repeated error messages, and the number of preceding comments are useful indicators of unrelated build failures. These results show that PU learning can help developers identify build failures that are unlikely to be caused by their current push.

</details>


### [236] [Operationalizing Ethics for AI Agents: How Developers Encode Values into Repository Context Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05584)
*Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes,Marc Cheong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As AI coding agents become embedded in software development workflows, developers are beginning to operationalize ethical principles by encoding behavioral rules into repository-level context files for AI agents, such as AGENTS.md files. Rather than examining the ethics of AI agents in the abstract, this vision paper investigates how ethics and values are already being translated for AI agents into actionable instructions that shape agent behavior. Through a preliminary investigation, we find that developers are already embedding guidance related to fairness, accessibility, sustainability, tone, and privacy. These artifacts function as a developer-authored governance layer, translating abstract principles into situated, natural-language directives within development workflows. We outline a research agenda for studying this emerging practice, including how encoded values vary across communities, what governance dynamics emerge when multiple contributors negotiate these files, and whether agents reliably adhere to the constraints specified. Understanding how ethics and values are operationalized for AI agents is essential to ground AI governance in modern software engineering practice.

</details>


### [237] [An Empirical Study of Proactive Coding Assistants in Real-World Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05700)
*Lehui Li,Ruixuan Jia,Guo-Ye Yang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过大规模实证研究揭示了LLM模拟IDE轨迹与真实开发者行为的显著差距，提出ProCodeBench基准，发现当前方法在真实场景下表现不佳，强调真实数据的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动编码助手研究受限于大规模真实开发者行为数据的稀缺，现有研究依赖LLM模拟的IDE轨迹，但这些模拟数据与真实开发行为的保真度尚不清楚。

Method: 从1,246名资深行业开发者收集了连续三天的真实IDE交互轨迹（通过自定义VS Code扩展），并构建配对的LLM模拟轨迹用于对照研究。

Result: 分析表明模拟轨迹在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上与真实轨迹存在显著差异；ProCodeBench基准测试显示当前方法在真实IDE轨迹下远未达到可靠水平，模拟评估可能高估真实世界性能；训练研究表明模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可在真实数据微调前作为补充。

Conclusion: 这些发现突显了真实开发者行为数据对于评估和训练主动编码助手的重要性。模拟数据虽不能替代真实数据，但可在真实数据微调前作为补充。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding assistants have made substantial progress, yet most systems remain reactive, requiring developers to explicitly formulate their needs. Proactive coding assistants aim to infer latent developer intent from integrated development environment (IDE) interactions and repository context, thereby reducing interaction overhead and supporting more seamless assistance. However, research in this direction is limited by the scarcity of large-scale real-world developer behavior data. Existing studies therefore often rely on LLM-simulated IDE traces, whose fidelity to real development behavior remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate this simulation-to-reality gap through a large-scale empirical study. We collect real IDE interaction traces from 1{,}246 experienced industry developers over three consecutive days using a custom Visual Studio Code extension, and construct paired LLM-simulated traces for controlled comparison. Our analysis shows that simulated traces differ substantially from real traces in behavioral diversity, temporal structure, and exploratory patterns. Based on the collected data, we introduce \textbf{ProCodeBench}, a real-world benchmark for proactive intent prediction. Experiments with representative LLMs, retrieval-augmented methods, and agentic baselines show that current approaches remain far from reliable under real IDE traces, suggesting that simulation-based evaluation can overestimate real-world performance. Finally, our training study shows that simulated data cannot replace real data, but can complement it when used before real-world fine-tuning. These findings highlight the importance of real developer behavior data for evaluating and training proactive coding assistants.

</details>


### [238] [From Chat to Interview: Agentic Requirements Elicitation with an Experience Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05828)
*Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Yaotian Yang,Linyu Li,Zheng Fang,Yuanpeng He,Wenchun Jing,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews are crucial and time-consuming in requirements engineering, but heavily rely on the experience of requirements analysts. Although recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to automate this process, existing approaches rely solely on LLMs for free-form chat without taking into account the interview and development experience. That leads to the omission of implicit requirements and redundant questions. Practically, experienced analysts implicitly follow a structured cognitive framework when conducting requirements elicitation. Inspired by this observation, this paper proposes an interview agent named OntoAgent for the elicitation of requirements guided by an experience ontology. OntoAgent automatically analyzes domain-specific requirements descriptions to construct an experience ontology, which organizes requirements concerns into an ontology to support systematic and explainable interviews. During the interview, OntoAgent first performs four operations (i.e., ParseUser, ScoreOnto, ReRankOnto, GatePrune) guided by the ontology to identify the relevant requirement concerns. The selected concern is then combined with the current dialogue context to generate the elicitation question. To validate OntoAgent, we conduct comprehensive quantitative experiments using the widely adopted website application domain. The results show that OntoAgent significantly outperforms existing baselines in both elicitation effectiveness and questioning efficiency, achieving a 33% improvement in IRE and a 21% improvement in TKQR. Ablation studies further validate the contribution of each key design component. In addition, a qualitative user study demonstrates its practical advantages in real-world scenarios. We believe that OntoAgent can also be extended to requirements interview tasks in other domains.

</details>


### [239] [On Fixing Insecure AI-Generated Code through Model Fine-Tuning and Prompting Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05867)
*Ali Soltanian Fard Jahromi,Amjed Tahir,Peng Liang,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地调查了提升AI生成代码安全性的多种策略，评估其在不同模型和编程语言中的有效性。研究发现安全改进的效果高度依赖于具体策略和模型，且修复某些弱点可能引入新弱点，目前尚不存在通用的"防弹"解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码生成模型虽然在功能基准测试上表现良好，但其输出经常包含漏洞和安全弱点，这严重阻碍了AI生成代码的广泛采用。先前工作虽探索了静态分析引导生成和提示工程等方法，但在不同模型和设置下的效果差异显著。

Method: 采用微调（fine-tuning）和提示工程（prompting）两种方法对模型输出进行优化，通过系统性评估这些策略在跨模型和跨编程语言场景下对CWE安全性的改善效果。

Result: 安全改进高度依赖策略和模型选择；某些方法虽能减少特定类型的弱点，但常作为副作用引入新的弱点；未发现任何策略能在所有模型和场景中一致地消除弱点。

Conclusion: 研究表明没有任何策略能够在所有模型和场景中一致地消除弱点，凸显了安全AI生成代码领域缺乏通用有效的"防弹"解决方案。这一发现对推动AI代码生成的实用化部署具有重要的警示意义。

Abstract: The security of AI-generated code remains a major obstacle to its widespread adoption. Although code generation models achieve strong performance on functional benchmarks, their outputs frequently contain bugs and security weaknesses that undermine their trustworthiness. Prior work has explored a range of approaches to mitigate security issues in AI-generated code, e.g., using static analysis-guided generation and prompt engineering. However, their effectiveness varies widely across models and settings. This paper presents a systematic investigation of strategies for hardening model-generated code against a list of Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). We assess the extent to which these strategies improve security across models and programming languages, using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement. Beyond the prevalence of security weaknesses, we analyse the severity of identified CWEs, their co-occurrence, and the unintended consequences of remediation (i.e., whether fixing certain weaknesses introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the same code). Our results show that security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. Although some approaches reduce specific classes of weaknesses, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects of the fixes. Moreover, no strategy consistently eliminates weaknesses across all models and scenarios, highlighting the absence of a universally effective "bulletproof" solution for secure AI-generated code.

</details>


### [240] [Evaluating Non-English Developer Support in Machine Learning for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05902)
*Jonathan Katzy,Yongcheng Huang,Gopal-Raj Panchu,Maksym Ziemlewski,Paris Loizides,Sander Vermeulen,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地揭示了代码大模型在多语言代码注释生成与评估方面的双重缺陷。研究发现生成质量在非英语场景大幅下降，且现有所有自动评估方法均无法可靠检测错误，人工评判仍不可替代。

This study is the first to systematically reveal the dual defects of code LLMs in multilingual code comment generation and evaluation. The study finds that generation quality drops significantly in non-English settings, and all current automatic evaluation methods fail to reliably detect errors, making human judgment irreplaceable.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在软件工程中的应用日益广泛，但代码生成与评估均以英语为中心，存在显著的多语言支持缺口。非英语自然语言代码注释的质量及其自动评估的可靠性尚不清楚，这阻碍了多语言开发工具的发展。

The use of large language models in software engineering is increasingly widespread, but both code generation and evaluation are predominantly English-centric, creating a significant multilingual support gap. The quality of non-English natural language code comments and the reliability of their automatic evaluation remain unclear, hindering the development of multilingual development tools.

Method: 实验涵盖5个代码大模型（CodeGemma、CodeLlama、CodeQwen1.5、GraniteCode、StarCoder2）在5种自然语言（荷兰语、英语、希腊语、波兰语、汉语）下的代码注释生成任务。通过对12,500条生成注释进行开放式编码研究，建立包含26种错误类型的分类体系，并以此评估传统重叠度量、现成神经度量、扩展神经度量（多语言/语言专用/代码专用模型）及LLM评判等多种自动化评估方法的性能。

The experiment covers code comment generation tasks for 5 code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, StarCoder2) across 5 natural languages (Dutch, English, Greek, Polish, Chinese). Through open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, a taxonomy of 26 error types was established, and used to evaluate the performance of various automated evaluation methods including traditional overlap metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics (multilingual/language-specific/code-specific models), and LLM-as-a-judge.

Result: 生成层面：英语以外语言的生成质量显著下降，语言错误增加高达15.1倍，且频繁出现不连贯生成和语义错误。评估层面：所有自动评估方法均不可靠——神经度量无法区分正确注释与错误输出甚至随机噪声，且倾向于高估非英语场景的质量；LLM评判与人工标注的 agreement 最高，但仍无法可靠捕获语言相关错误和语义错误。
