Generation: Quality of non-English generation drops significantly, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1×, along with frequent incoherent generations and semantic errors. Evaluation: All automatic evaluation methods are unreliable—neural metrics cannot distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings; LLM judges have the highest agreement with human annotations but still fail to reliably capture language-related errors and semantic errors.

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多语言代码注释工具的核心瓶颈：生成与评估均存在严重缺陷。英语以外的场景中，语言错误剧增至15.1倍，且现有自动化评估方法（重叠度量、神经度量、LLM评判）均无法可靠检测错误，人工评判仍不可或缺。

The core bottleneck for multilingual code comment tools is that both generation and evaluation have serious defects. Outside English, linguistic errors increase by up to 15.1×, and no current automated evaluation method (overlap metrics, neural metrics, LLM judges) can reliably detect errors. Human judgment remains indispensable.

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software engineering, but both code generation and its evaluation remain predominantly English-centric. This leaves a major gap in our understanding of how well current tools support multilingual development, where code contains non-English natural language. In this paper, we investigate non-English code comment generation and the reliability of current methods for evaluating such outputs. We evaluate five code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, and StarCoder2) across five natural languages: Dutch, English, Greek, Polish and Chinese. We further conduct an open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, from which we derive a publicly released human-annotated dataset and a taxonomy of 26 error types. We use these human annotations, to evaluate the performance of neural metrics, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.
  Our findings show that generative performance deteriorates substantially outside English, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1$\times$, alongside frequent incoherent generations and a rise in semantic errors. More critically, we show that detecting errors in non-English comments underperforms. Across classical overlap-based metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics using newer multilingual, language-specific, and code-specific models, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, no automatic approach provides reliable and consistent assessment. Neural metrics fail to distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings. LLM-as-a-judge methods achieve the highest agreement with human annotations but fail to reliably capture important language-related and semantic errors. Overall, our results show that evaluation and generation are key barriers for multilingual tooling, and that human judgment remains indispensable.

</details>


### [241] [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111)
*Yujia Chen,Yang Ye,Xiao Chu,Yuchi Ma,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASTOR提出了一种基于任务效用的多任务代码强化学习框架，通过层次化效用路由数据调度和自适应效用校准策略优化两个模块，解决了现有MTRL方法将所有代码任务统一处理、固定数据课程和共享优化策略的局限性，在四项代码任务上超越任务特定专家模型9.0%-9.5%，超越最强MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTRL方法将所有代码任务统一对待，采用固定数据课程和共享优化策略，导致多任务训练效果受限；同时，为每个任务部署独立专家模型成本随任务数量线性增长。

Method: ASTOR框架以任务效用（task utility）为核心，包含两个耦合模块：1）层次化效用路由数据调度模块——分层分配训练预算并优先处理高价值prompt；2）自适应效用校准策略优化模块——动态调整每个任务的KL正则化强度以匹配其当前训练状态。

Result: 在两个主流LLM和四项代表性代码任务上的实验表明，ASTOR持续提升单一模型性能，超越最佳任务特定专家模型9.0%-9.5%，超越最强MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。

Conclusion: 基于任务效用的协调机制可有效解决MTRL中任务统一处理的问题，层次化数据调度与自适应策略优化相结合是提升多任务代码生成能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has proven effective at post-training LLMs for coding, yet deploying separate task-specific specialists incurs costs that scale with the number of tasks, motivating a unified multi-task RL (MTRL) approach. However, existing MTRL methods treat all coding tasks uniformly, relying on fixed data curricula under a shared optimization strategy, ultimately limiting the effectiveness of multi-task training. To address these limitations, we propose ASTOR, a multi-tASk code reinforcement learning framework via uTility-driven coORdination. Centered on task utility, a signal capturing each task learning potential and cross-task synergy, ASTOR comprises two coupled modules: 1) Hierarchical Utility-Routed Data Scheduling module hierarchically allocates training budget and prioritizes informative prompts, steering training toward the most valuable data and 2) Adaptive Utility-Calibrated Policy Optimization module dynamically scales per-task KL regularization, matching update constraints to each tasks current training state. Experiments on two widely-used LLMs across four representative coding tasks demonstrate that ASTOR consistently improves a single model across all tasks, outperforming the best task-specific specialist by 9.0%-9.5% and surpassing the strongest MTRL baseline by 7.5%-12.8%.

</details>


### [242] [Breaking, Stale, or Missing? Benchmarking Coding Agents on Project-Level Test Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06125)
*Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang,Haichuan Hu,Chunrong Fang,Liang Xiao,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEBench是首个项目级测试演化基准，包含314个任务实例，覆盖三种演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）。实验表明当前Agent配置存在约47%的性能天花板，且被动式"执行-失败-修复"策略无法有效处理Test-Stale和Test-Missing类型，需要向主动语义推理方向演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试演化基准存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 在方法级粒度上操作，使用预配对的输入，跳过了从完整项目中定位受影响测试的真实任务；(2) 完全不涉及新测试的需求。这些限制使得现有基准无法真实反映实际软件工程中测试演化的复杂性。

Method: 通过四阶段管道构建：对Defects4J项目进行筛选，从10个项目中生成314个任务实例。标注三种演化类型：Test-Breaking（失败的测试）、Test-Stale（通过但不再有效验证更新行为的测试）、Test-Missing（需要新增的测试）。评估了7种配置，涵盖3个工业Agent框架（Claude Code、Codex CLI、OpenCode）和6个基础模型，以及启发式基线。

Result: 测试识别F1分数在45.7%至49.4%之间，跨框架和模型存在共同性能天花板。Test-Stale类型最具挑战性（F1约36%），因为现有方法依赖执行失败信号且缺乏主动语义推理。更新任务产生高度可执行的测试修改，但与ground truth的表面形式存在显著差异。轨迹分析揭示了被动的"执行-失败-修复"循环策略。

Conclusion: TEBench为测试演化领域提供了首个项目级基准，推动了该方向的研究。所有配置在测试识别任务上达到约45.7%-49.4%的F1分数，显示出跨框架和模型的共同性能天花板。Test-Stale类型最具挑战性，因为现有方法依赖执行失败信号，缺乏主动语义推理能力。当前采用的"执行-失败-修复"被动策略对Test-Breaking有效，但结构性无法解决Test-Stale和Test-Missing问题。测试更新任务产生可执行的修改，但表面形式与ground truth存在显著差异，表明评估指标的合理性值得审视。

Abstract: As production code evolves, the test suite must co-evolve to remain effective. Existing benchmarks for test evolution operate at method-level granularity with pre-paired inputs, bypassing the task of locating affected tests from the full project and excluding the need for new tests entirely. We present TEBench, the first project-level benchmark for test evolution. Given a project repository and a code-changing commit, TEBench requires systems to autonomously identify tests requiring modification, determine where new tests are needed, and produce the corresponding test patch. We construct TEBench through a four-stage pipeline over Defects4J projects, curating 314 task instances from 10 projects with developer-written ground truth. Each instance is annotated with one or more of three evolution types: Test-Breaking (tests that fail), Test-Stale (tests that pass but no longer meaningfully validate updated behavior), and Test-Missing (new tests needed for introduced behavior). We evaluate seven configurations spanning three industrial agent frameworks (Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode) and six base models, alongside a heuristic baseline. All seven configurations converge on an identification F1 of 45.7% to 49.4%, revealing a shared performance ceiling across both frameworks and base models. Test-Stale is the most challenging type, averaging F1 around 36%, since configurations rely on execution failure signals and lack proactive semantic reasoning. On the update task, configurations produce highly executable test modifications whose surface form diverges substantially from ground truth. Trajectory analysis reveals a reactive "execute-fail-fix" loop that succeeds for breaking tests but structurally cannot address stale or missing tests. TEBench is available at https://github.com/iSEngLab/TEBench with a leaderboard at https://tebench-leadership.vercel.app.

</details>


### [243] [BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06136)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BUILD-AND-FIND协议，用于评估下游智能体能否从生成的代码仓库中恢复预期的设计选择和意图，而非仅评估代码行为正确性。该协议通过分离行为正确性与工件侧恢复，报告恢复准确率、可重复性、实施覆盖率和检查工作量，为代码生成质量提供更全面的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码智能体基准测试仅评估生成代码的行为正确性，但在仓库级工程中，仓库不仅是任务的答案，也是未来工作的通信工件。即使强智能体几乎满足可见的行为目标，不同仓库在暴露行为背后的意图和设计选择方面可能存在显著差异。

Method: 构建"BUILD-AND-FIND"协议，其中构建者看到隐藏的仓库规范并创建代码库；发现者仅看到代码库和规范追踪的多项选择题题库。通过问题控制和规范控制量化通用先验，使用审计分离遗漏声明与发现者失败，并检查正确答案是否引用了工件证据。

Result: 在发布的高优先级任务包中，恢复准确率接近饱和，因此检查工作量和发现者特定效应成为主要的面板内比较维度。

Conclusion: 该协议将行为正确性与工件侧恢复分离，报告恢复准确率、可重复性、实施覆盖率和检查工作量。准确率和稳定性作为门控条件：仅在恢复可靠成功时才解释工作量。问题控制和规范控制量化了通用先验和规范访问，审计则区分遗漏声明与发现者失败。发布的高优先级任务包中恢复准确率接近饱和，因此检查工作量和发现者特定效应成为主要面板内比较维度。

Abstract: Most coding-agent benchmarks ask whether generated code behaves correctly. That remains essential, but repository-level engineering is increasingly agent-managed: one agent writes a repository, and later agents inspect, audit, or extend it as working context. In that setting, a generated repository is not only an answer to a task but also a communication artifact for future work. Even when strong agents nearly satisfy the visible behavioral objective, repositories can differ in how clearly they expose the intended behavior and design choices behind that behavior. We introduce BUILD-AND-FIND, a protocol for evaluating whether downstream agents can recover those intended choices from generated repositories, and how much inspection that recovery requires. For each task, a builder sees a hidden repository specification and creates a codebase; a finder sees only the codebase and a specification-traced multiple-choice question bank. The protocol separates behavioral correctness from artifact-side recovery and reports recovery accuracy, repeatability, implementation coverage, and inspection effort. Accuracy and stability act as gates: effort is interpreted only when recovery succeeds reliably. Among artifacts from which the same intent can be recovered, lower effort by the same finder suggests that the artifact makes that intent easier to locate. Question-only and spec-only controls quantify generic priors and specification access, while audits separate omitted claims from finder failures and check whether correct answers cite artifact evidence. In the released high-prior task pack, recovery accuracy is near saturation, so inspection effort and finder-specific effects provide the main panel-local comparison.

</details>


### [244] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过Soteria符号执行生成bug traces对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练，结合chain-of-thought推理，在C代码验证的违规检测任务上实现超17个百分点的提升，使8B模型性能接近甚至超越32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在高整体准确率下，violation detection（违规检测）能力存在严重缺陷：模型间差异大，且随程序长度增加性能急剧下降，需提升模型检测代码违反安全属性的能力。

Method: 基于SV-COMP 2025构建500个C验证任务评测框架（五类属性：内存安全、溢出、终止、可达性、数据竞争），评估14个模型6个家族；在通用开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成traces，用于Qwen3-8B的持续预训练；结合chain-of-thought推理；共测试28种配置。

Result: 仅约3000条bug traces结合chain-of-thought推理，violation detection提升超17个百分点，8B模型在此任务上超越4倍规模的Qwen3-32B；两者组合具有超加性效应（单独使用无效），改进泛化至全部5类属性。

Conclusion: 通过约3000条bug traces结合chain-of-thought推理，可使8B模型在violation detection上超越4倍规模的32B模型，且改进泛化至所有5类属性类型。证明了trace语义而非代码量是关键，trace的筛选和格式对结果有重要影响。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [245] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SiblingRepair是一个基于LLM的多hunk自动化程序修复技术，专注于兄弟代码修复。通过结合token/embedding匹配和LLM生成能力，采用同步和迭代两种互补策略，实现了对现有SOTA技术（尤其是Hercules）的显著超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA技术Hercules存在明显局限：(1)对兄弟代码位置的强假设和依赖提交历史；(2)基于AST的刚性匹配方式；(3)基于模板的补丁生成缺乏灵活性。

Method: SiblingRepair采用LLM进行修复：(1)基于token和embedding的代码匹配搜索语义相关的兄弟候选；(2)通过LLM识别失败相关的兄弟代码并生成一致补丁；(3)两种互补策略——同步修复（联合修复多个兄弟）和迭代修复（逐步分析候选构建补丁）；(4)保留先前可疑位置产生的有效补丁并合并为泛化的多hunk补丁。

Result: 实验表明SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于现有SOTA多hunk修复技术。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术，验证了其在兄弟代码检测和修复组件上的有效性，且LLM数据泄露影响有限。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [246] [Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模测量了LLM生成Python代码中第三方库版本选择的安全与兼容性风险。评估10种LLM在1,000个编程任务上的表现，发现LLM普遍倾向于选择包含已知CVE的高风险版本，且不同模型表现出系统性偏见而非偶发错误，表明版本选择是一个需要重点关注的一阶风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型（LLM）已广泛参与软件开发流程，其生成的代码常包含带有版本标识的第三方库（TPL）导入。然而，这些版本选择的安全性与兼容性风险尚未得到系统性研究。

Method: 使用PinTrace基准测试集（包含1,000个Stack Overflow编程任务）评估10种LLM。对比直接提示与创建清单文件两种场景下的版本指定行为；通过静态兼容性分析和动态测试验证代码可用性；设计消融实验区分版本选择与代码质量的影响。

Result: 当直接提示时，LLM指定版本标识的比例为26.83%-95.18%；创建清单文件时降至6.45%-59.19%。在指定的版本中，36.70%-55.70%的任务存在已知CVE，其中62.75%-74.51%为高危级别。静态兼容性率为19.70%-63.20%，安装失败是主要原因。动态测试通过率为6.49%-48.62%。

Conclusion: LLM版本选择是一个此前被忽视但至关重要的一阶风险因素。研究揭示了LLM在第三方库版本选择上的系统性偏见，表现为：所有模型都收敛到相同的高风险版本集；72.27%-91.37%的CVE在模型知识截止日期前已公开披露。外部锚定的版本约束可显著降低漏洞暴露和兼容性问题。研究成果已向相关模型社区披露，多方确认了问题存在。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now largely involved in software development workflows, and the code they generate routinely includes third-party library (TPL) imports annotated with specific version identifiers. These version choices can carry security and compatibility risks, yet they have not been systematically studied.
  We present the first large-scale measurement study of version-level risk in LLM-generated Python code, evaluating 10 LLMs on PinTrace, a curated benchmark of 1,000 Stack Overflow programming tasks. LLMs tend to specify version identifiers when directly prompted at 26.83%-95.18%, while down to 6.45%-59.19% in creating a manifest file directly. Among the specified versions, 36.70%-55.70% of tasks contain at least one known CVE, and 62.75%-74.51% of them carry Critical or High severity ratings. In 72.27%-91.37% of cases, the associated CVEs were publicly disclosed before the model's knowledge cutoff. The statistics show all models converge on the same small set of risky release versions, indicating a systemic bias rather than isolated model error. Static compatibility rates range from 19.70% to 63.20%, with installation failure as the dominant cause. The dynamic test cases confirm the pattern by 6.49%-48.62% pass rates. Further experiments confirm that these failures are attributable to version selection rather than code quality, and that externally anchored version constraints substantially reduce both vulnerability exposure and compatibility failures. Our findings reveal LLM version selection as a first-class, previously overlooked risk surface in LLM-based development. We disclosed these findings to the community of the evaluated models, and several confirmed the issue. All the code and dataset have been released for open science at https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace.

</details>


### [247] [Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445)
*Francesco Dente,Dario Satriani,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了大语言模型智能体在多文件后端生成中处理结构约束的能力。通过80个新建任务和20个功能任务跨8个框架的实验，发现智能体在累积结构需求时性能急剧下降（平均下降30分），Flask等极简框架成功率远高于Django等约定优先框架，数据层缺陷是主要错误来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅关注功能正确性，忽略生产级软件所需的结构约束（如架构模式、数据库、对象关系映射），奖励了功能正确但结构任意的解决方案。

Method: 在80个新建生成任务和20个功能实现任务上，使用统一API契约跨8个Web框架进行系统评估，采用端到端行为测试与静态验证器的双重评估机制。

Result: 发现"约束衰减"现象：随结构需求累积，智能体性能显著下降，主流配置从基线到全规格任务的断言通过率平均下降30分，部分弱配置接近零。框架敏感性分析显示：Flask等极简框架表现优异，而FastAPI、Django等约定优先框架表现较差。错误分析表明数据层缺陷（查询组合错误、ORM运行时违规）为主要根因。

Conclusion: 满足功能性和结构性需求仍然是编码智能体的关键开放挑战。当前智能体在松散规格下表现良好，但在严格结构约束下性能显著下降。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate strong performance in autonomous code generation under loose specifications. However, production-grade software requires strict adherence to structural constraints, such as architectural patterns, databases, and object-relational mappings. Existing benchmarks often overlook these non-functional requirements, rewarding functionally correct but structurally arbitrary solutions. We present a systematic study evaluating how well agents handle structural constraints in multi-file backend generation. By fixing a unified API contract across 80 greenfield generation tasks and 20 feature-implementation tasks spanning eight web frameworks, we isolate the effect of structural complexity using a dual evaluation with end-to-end behavioral tests and static verifiers. Our findings reveal a phenomenon of constraint decay: as structural requirements accumulate, agent performance exhibits a substantial decline. Capable configurations lose 30 points on average in assertion pass rates from baseline to fully specified tasks, while some weaker configurations approach zero. Framework sensitivity analysis exposes significant performance disparities: agents succeed in minimal, explicit frameworks (e.g., Flask) but perform substantially worse on average in convention-heavy environments (e.g., FastAPI, Django). Finally, error analysis identifies data-layer defects (e.g., incorrect query composition and ORM runtime violations) as the leading root causes. This work highlights that jointly satisfying functional and structural requirements remains a key open challenge for coding agents.

</details>


### [248] [To What Extent Does Agent-generated Code Require Maintenance? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06464)
*Shota Sawada,Tatsuya Shirai,Yutaro Kashiwa,Ken'ichi Yamaguchi,Hiroshi Iwata,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证分析揭示AI生成代码与人类代码在维护模式上的显著差异：AI生成代码维护频率更低、修改类型偏向功能扩展而非bug修复，且主要由人类开发者完成维护工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主编程智能体虽在代码生成方面表现出色，但其生成代码的长期可维护性仍存在疑问——这是一个直接影响软件工程实践的重要问题。

Method: 基于AIDev数据集（包含AI生成的pull request）和GitHub数据，对100个流行仓库中的1000+文件和约3200次代码变更进行实证分析，比较AI生成代码与人类代码的维护程度、人类参与度和修改类型。

Result: 三个核心发现：①AI生成文件的维护频率低于人类代码，且更新通常仅涉及文件的很小一部分；②对AI代码最频繁的修改是功能扩展，而人类代码的更新主要聚焦于缺陷修复；③人类开发者承担了AI生成代码的绝大部分维护工作。

Conclusion: 该研究表明AI生成代码虽然能够快速产出，但长期维护模式与人类代码存在显著差异：维护频率更低、修改类型偏向功能扩展而非缺陷修复，且主要依赖人类开发者进行维护。这对软件开发实践具有重要启示：不能忽视AI生成代码的后续维护成本，需建立针对性的代码质量管理策略。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous coding agents have reshaped software development. While these agents excel at code generation, open questions persist about the long-term maintainability of AI-generated code. This study empirically investigates the maintenance extent, human involvement, and modification types of AI-generated files versus human-authored code. Using the AIDev dataset of AI-generated pull requests and GitHub, we analyzed over 1,000 files and approximately 3,200 changes from 100 popular repositories. Our findings show that: (i) AI-generated files receive less frequent maintenance than human-authored code, with updates affecting only a small fraction of file size; (ii) the most frequent modifications to AI code are feature extensions, whereas human updates focus on bug fixes, and (iii) human developers perform the large majority of this maintenance.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [From Cradle to Cloud: A Life Cycle Review of AI's Environmental Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05416)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The rapid growth in the deployment and scale of modern artificial intelligence (AI) systems has intensified concerns regarding their environmental impacts, yet we still lack a comprehensive view of where and how these impacts arise across the AI life cycle. In order to shed more light on this question, we conduct a structured, comprehensive literature review of scientific papers and technical reports that examine different aspects of AI's environmental footprint. Using an eight-stage life cycle framework, spanning hardware manufacturing, infrastructure construction, data gathering and preprocessing, model experimentation, training, post-training adaptation, deployment, inference, and end-of-life, we systematically map which stages are covered, the metrics reported at each stage, and the methodological choices made. We then draw conclusions about the information we gathered, finding that although life cycle language is increasingly common in discussions of "green" or "sustainable" AI, its definition remains unclear -- while some studies focus solely on model training and inference, others encompass broader measurements such as data collection, infrastructure, and embodied emissions. We also find that reporting practices rely predominantly on CO2e estimates derived from coarse proxies, with limited attention dedicated to water usage, materials manufacturing, and multi-impact life cycle assessment, making it difficult to compare and aggregate true results. Building on these findings, we propose measurement and reporting approaches to support more comprehensive, comparable and policy-relevant assessments of AI's environmental impacts.

</details>


### [250] [LLMorphism: When humans come to see themselves as language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05419)
*Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文引入"LLMorphism"概念，指人类认知类似于大型语言模型的偏见。文章认为对话式LLM的兴起可能使这种偏见在心理上更具可得性。当人工系统产生类似人类的语言时，人们可能反向推断"LLM像人类一样说话，因此人类可能像LLM一样思考"，但这种推断存在偏误，因为语言输出的相似性不等于认知架构的相似性。LLMorphism可能通过类比迁移和隐喻可得性两种机制扩散，作者将其与相关概念进行区分，并指出其对多个领域的广泛影响。核心论点是：公共讨论可能遗漏了问题的一半——不仅是对机器赋予过多心智，更是对人类赋予过少心智。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式LLM的普及，人类可能产生"逆向推断"：既然LLM能像人类一样说话，人类可能也像LLM一样思考。这种推断存在偏误，因为语言输出的相似性不能推出认知架构的相似性。

Method: 文章采用概念分析和理论建构方法，区别了LLMorphism与相关概念（机械主义拟人论、拟人论、计算主义、去人化、具身化以及预测加工心智理论），并通过分析类比迁移（analogical transfer）和隐喻可得性（metaphorical availability）两种机制来解释LLMorphism的传播路径。

Result: LLMorphism通过类比迁移（将LLM的特征投射到人类）和隐喻可得性（LLM词汇成为描述思维的流行文化词汇）两条机制传播。作者明确了LLMorphism与机械主义拟人论、拟人论、计算主义、去人化、具身化及预测加工心智理论的区别，并指出其对工作、教育、责任、医疗、沟通、创造力和人类尊严等领域的广泛影响。

Conclusion: 文章的核心结论具有批判性和前瞻性：当前公共讨论只关注了"是否对机器赋予了过多心智"这一半问题，而忽视了对称性的另一半——"是否开始对人类赋予过少心智"。这一双向警醒是文章的核心贡献。LLMorphism可能通过类比迁移和隐喻可得性两条路径扩散，对工作、教育、责任、医疗、沟通、创造力和人类尊严等领域产生深远影响。

Abstract: LLMorphism is the biased belief that human cognition works like a large language model. I argue that the rise of conversational LLMs may make this bias increasingly psychologically available. When artificial systems produce human-like language, people may draw a reverse inference: if LLMs can speak like humans, perhaps humans think like LLMs. This inference is biased because similarity at the level of linguistic output does not imply similarity in cognitive architecture. Yet, LLMorphism may spread through two mechanisms: analogical transfer, whereby features of LLMs are projected onto humans, and metaphorical availability, whereby LLM vocabulary becomes a culturally salient vocabulary for describing thought. I distinguish LLMorphism from mechanomorphism, anthropomorphism, computationalism, dehumanization, objectification, and predictive-processing theories of mind. I outline its implications for work, education, responsibility, healthcare, communication, creativity, and human dignity, while also discussing boundary conditions and forms of resistance. I conclude that the public debate may be missing half of the problem: the issue is not only whether we are attributing too much mind to machines, but also whether we are beginning to attribute too little mind to humans.

</details>


### [251] [The Pedagogy of AI Mistakes: Fostering Higher-Order Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05472)
*Hadi Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将生成式AI的常见错误和幻觉重新定位为教学资源而非障碍，通过在数据库设计课程中设计AI整合教学大纲，利用AI的不完美输出促进学生的高阶认知发展。研究采用混合方法验证了结构化AI交互对元认知参与和学科能力发展的积极作用，为高等教育中创新AI教学应用提供了实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI频繁产生错误和幻觉，传统观点视其为阻碍教学的限制因素。然而，该研究提出将这些"缺陷"重新框架为教学机会，认为AI的不完美输出可以激发学生的分析、评估和反思活动，从而促进高阶思维技能的发展。

Method: 采用混合方法研究设计，在数据库设计课程中设计并实施了AI整合教学大纲。通过结构化的AI交互任务，让学生分析与评价AI生成内容中的错误。使用Bloom's认知分类法评估学生的高阶思维能力发展，同时通过问卷和访谈收集学生对AI素养和学科能力的自我感知数据。

Result: 研究发现，结构化地与AI生成错误进行交互，能够有效支持学生的元认知参与，强化学科严谨性，并与学生的AI素养和学科能力感知呈正相关。AI的局限性反而成为促进深度学习和批判性思维的有效催化剂。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将AI的局限性作为教学设计的一部分，能够有效促进学生的元认知参与、学科严谨性培养，同时提升学生的AI素养和学科能力。该研究为教育工作者提供了在课程中整合AI的创新思路，强调将AI从"正确答案提供者"转变为"学习促进者"的教育理念转变。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into higher education, its frequent errors and hallucinations, often seen as limitations, offer a unique pedagogical opportunity. By framing AI as a ``learning companion'' whose imperfect outputs prompt analysis, evaluation, and reflection, we argue that instructors can engage students in the fundamental processes of higher-order thinking. This paper presents a design-oriented study in which an AI-integrated syllabus in a \textit{database design} course deliberately leverages AI's limitations to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills aligned with Bloom's taxonomy of learning. Using a mixed-methods approach, we examine how structured interaction with AI-generated errors supports metacognitive engagement, reinforces disciplinary rigor, and relates to students' perceived AI literacy and subject-matter competency.

</details>


### [252] [Rigorous Interpretation Is a Form of Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05508)
*Isabelle Lee,Emmy Liu,Cathy Jiao,Brihi Joshi,Dani Yogatama,Fazl Barez,Michael Saxon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The paper argues that interpretability should move beyond being a diagnostic tool to become a principled form of model evaluation. Current ML evaluation focuses on behavioral outcomes (benchmarks, win rates) but misses understanding why models produce certain behaviors. The authors propose three evaluative functions of interpretability: identifying root causes of failures, detecting subtle faulty mechanisms, and predicting potential issues by understanding weaknesses. To achieve this, interpretability methods must meet scientific standards—producing claims that are falsifiable, reproducible, and predictive.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current model evaluation relies solely on behavioral outcomes (accuracy, win rates) which measure "what" but not "why." This superficial evaluation is insufficient for understanding and addressing model failures.

Method: Conceptual framework exploring three evaluative functions of interpretability, with emphasis on scientific methodology requirements.

Result: Proposed three ways interpretability can serve evaluative purposes: (1) root-cause diagnosis for fixing problems, (2) detecting subtle faulty mechanisms, and (3) predictive identification of potential weaknesses.

Conclusion: Interpretability methods must satisfy scientific standards (falsifiability, reproducibility, and predictive power) to fully realize their evaluative potential.

Abstract: Current machine learning models are evaluated through behavioral snapshots, with benchmark accuracies, win rates and outcome-based metrics. Model explanations and evaluations, however, are fundamentally intertwined: understanding why a model produces a behavior can be as important as measuring what it produces. If we trusted interpretability, we argue that it can serve not merely as diagnostics but as a richer and more principled form of model evaluation beyond surface-level performance metrics. We explore three ways interpretability can function evaluatively: (1) fixing problems by identifying the root causes of unwanted behavior, (2) detecting subtly faulty mechanisms that invalidate model outputs, and (3) predicting potential issues before they arise by fully understanding the model's weaknesses. To fulfill its evaluative potential, we argue that interpretability methods must generate claims that are falsifiable, reproducible, and predictive -- that is, interpretability must meet scientific standards.

</details>


### [253] [The Missing Evaluation Axis: What 10,000 Student Submissions Reveal About AI Tutor Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05648)
*Rose Niousha,Samantha Boatright Smith,Bita Akram,Peter Brusilovsky,Arto Hellas,Juho Leinonen,John DeNero,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 传统AI辅导系统评估仅关注教学反馈质量，忽略了学生是否真正采取行动。该研究提出一个行为维度的评估框架，基于10,235份代码提交数据，发现不同AI辅导系统之间学生参与模式存在显著差异，且行为信号与学生感知反馈有用性的关联比单纯的教学质量更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current AI tutor evaluation focuses solely on pedagogical quality of feedback, which is insufficient because it ignores whether students actually act on the feedback they receive.

Method: Proposed an evaluation framework grounded in student interaction data and applied it to 10,235 code submissions with AI tutor feedback from an introductory undergraduate programming course; compared two deployed AI tutors across different semesters.

Result: Substantial differences in student engagement patterns were found between the two AI tutors that pedagogy-only evaluation fails to capture; behavioral signals are more strongly associated with student perception of helpful feedback than pedagogical quality alone.

Conclusion: AI tutor evaluation should include a behavioral dimension that complements pedagogical assessment to provide a more complete and actionable picture of AI tutor performance.

Abstract: Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based tutoring systems (AI tutors) are primarily evaluated based on the pedagogical quality of their feedback messages. While important, pedagogy alone is insufficient because it ignores a critical question: what do students actually do with the feedback they receive? We argue that AI tutor evaluation should be extended with a behavioral dimension grounded in student interaction data, which complements pedagogical assessment. We propose an evaluation framework and apply it to 10,235 code submissions with corresponding AI tutor feedback from an introductory undergraduate programming course to measure whether students act on tutor feedback and whether those actions are applied correctly. Using this framework to compare two deployed AI tutors across different semesters in a large-scale introductory computer science course reveals substantial differences in student engagement patterns that are not captured by pedagogy-only evaluation. Moreover, these engagement-based behavioral signals are more strongly associated with student perception of helpful feedback than pedagogical quality alone, providing a more complete and actionable picture of AI tutor performance.

</details>


### [254] [A Benchmark for Strategic Auditee Gaming Under Continuous Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06340)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究持续性合规审计中的博弈问题。论文将连续审计形式化为T轮Stackelberg博弈，发现了静态审计设计的根本性限制——覆盖 regime，其中覆盖缺口和粒度缺口无法同时关闭，并提出两种正交扩展策略及可击败二者的OffAuditDrift策略，辅以可复现的模拟器和经验校准的策略库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案和数字服务法案(DSA)要求进行持续性的部署后合规审计，这产生了一种与先前研究的单次输入/输出博弈不同的战略性博弈形式。监管系统可通过延迟结果报告、在合理噪声范围内漂移报告、利用纵向样本流失和在模糊指标定义间进行选择性报告来规避审计。

Method: 将连续审计形式化为审计者承诺时序策略、审计对象自适应响应的T轮Stackelberg博弈。通过理论分析识别静态审计设计的结构特征：覆盖 regime（Observation 1）。提出两种最小扩展策略——样本量感知的Periodic-with-floor规则和历史条件怀疑升级策略，并设计OffAuditDrift策略。经验研究方面，贡献了非加性危害分解(W, C)揭示流失将危害从监管可见转移到不可见层面，构建了包含5种审计对象策略和5种审计者策略的库，并开发了基于DSA透明度数据库校准的可复现Python模拟器。

Result: Observation 1的理论预测得到验证：两种最小扩展策略各自关闭一个失败case但无法同时关闭两者，而OffAuditDrift策略利用Stackelberg承诺可击败两者。危害分解显示样本流失将损害从监管问责表面转移到监管盲区。

Conclusion: 本文为持续审计提供了形式化框架，揭示了噪声感知静态审计设计的根本限制（覆盖 regime），并通过理论-经验结合的方法为审计机制设计提供了分析工具和可扩展的实验平台。

Abstract: Continuous post-deployment compliance audits, mandated by emerging regulations such as the EU AI Act and Digital Services Act, create a class of strategic gaming distinct from the one-shot input/output gaming studied in prior work. Regulated systems can delay outcome reporting, drift their reports within plausible noise envelopes, exploit longitudinal sample attrition, and cherry-pick among ambiguous metric definitions. We formalize continuous auditing as a $T$-round Stackelberg game between an auditor that commits to a temporal policy and an adaptive auditee, and identify a structural feature of any noise-aware static-auditor design: a cover regime in which coverage gaps and granularity gaps cannot be closed simultaneously. We make this formal as Observation 1 and show that two minimal extension policies, each derived from the observation, close the regime along orthogonal axes: a sample-size-aware static rule (Periodic-with-floor) closes the granularity-failure case, while a history-conditioned suspicion-escalation policy closes the coverage-failure case for the naive Drift strategy -- and neither closes both, exactly as the observation predicts; an audit-aware OffAuditDrift strategy that exploits Stackelberg commitment defeats both. To support empirical study we contribute a non-additive harm decomposition (welfare loss $W$, coverage loss $C$) that exposes how attrition shifts harm from the regulator-accountable surface to a regulator-invisible one; an initial library of five auditee strategies (Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift) and five auditor policies, calibrated to summary statistics from published audits of the DSA Transparency Database; and a reproducible simulator with a small, extensible Python interface.

</details>


### [255] [From Review to Design: Ethical Multimodal Driver Monitoring Systems for Risk Mitigation, Incident Response, and Accountability in Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06439)
*Bilal Khana,Waseem Shariff,Rory Coyne,Muhammad Ali Farooq,Peter Corcoran*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶车辆中的驾驶员监控系统（DMS）所引发的伦理和法律挑战，批判性地分析了GDPR、欧盟AI法案、IEEE标准等现有监管框架的局限性，并提出了一套模块化伦理设计框架，将高级伦理原则转化为用户可配置同意机制、公平感知模型开发、可解释性工具和情感健康保障等具体设计指导，同时制定了DMS情境的风险分析和故障缓解策略，旨在推动开发透明、可信、以人为中心的下一代车内监控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着车辆自动化程度提高，驾驶员监控系统（DMS）对于保障人类监督、安全和法规合规变得不可或缺，但这些系统的部署引发了复杂的伦理和法律挑战（隐私、知情同意、数据所有权、算法公平性），现有监管框架缺乏针对车载感知技术特殊风险的具体指导。

Method: 采用综述到设计的研究视角，首先批判性地审查现有监管工具（GDPR、欧盟AI法案、IEEE标准），识别其对多模态AI车内监控适用性的不足；然后基于此提出针对DMS的模块化伦理设计框架，并将高级原则转化为可操作的指导；最后制定风险分析和故障缓解策略。

Result: 提出了一套模块化伦理设计框架，包括：用户可配置同意机制、公平感知模型开发、透明度和可解释性工具、以及驾驶员情感健康保障措施；同时建立了针对DMS情境的风险分析和失效缓解策略，强调主动事件响应和问责机制。

Conclusion: 本研究为下一代自动驾驶汽车的驾驶员监控系统开发提供了透明、可信、以人为中心的指导框架，通过模块化设计方法将伦理原则转化为可操作的技术和部署指南，并强调了主动事件响应和问责机制的重要性。

Abstract: As vehicles transition toward higher levels of automation, Driver Monitoring Systems (DMS) have become essential for ensuring human oversight, safety, and regulatory compliance in a vehicle. These systems rely on multimodal sensing and AI-driven inference to assess driver attention, cognitive state, and readiness to take control. While technologically promising, their deployment introduces a complex set of ethical and legal challenges - ranging from privacy and consent to data ownership and algorithmic fairness. While overarching frameworks such as the GDPR, EU AI Act, and IEEE standards offer important guidance, they lack the specificity required for addressing the unique risks posed by in-cabin sensing technologies.
  This paper adopts a review-to-design perspective, critically examining existing regulatory instruments and ethical frameworks -- such as the GDPR, the EU AI Act, and IEEE guidelines -- and identifying gaps in their applicability to the distinctive risks posed by multimodal, AI-enabled in-cabin monitoring. Building on this review, we propose a modular ethical design framework tailored specifically to Driver Monitoring Systems. The framework translates high-level principles into actionable design and deployment guidance, including user-configurable consent mechanisms, fairness-aware model development, transparency and explainability tools, and safeguards for driver emotional well-being.
  Finally, the paper outlines a risk analysis and failure mitigation strategy, emphasizing proactive incident response and accountability mechanisms tailored to the DMS context. Together, these contributions aim to inform the development of transparent, trustworthy, and human-centered driver monitoring systems for next-generation autonomous vehicles.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Near-Tight Approximation Algorithms for Bottleneck Multiple Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05233)
*Lin Chen,Tingwei Hu,Yuchen Mao,Yong Chen,Lili Mei,An Zhang,Guangting Chen,Guochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究瓶颈多背包问题，目标是在容量约束下最大化所有背包中的最小收益。对于相同容量背包给出了 (2/3 - ε) 近似算法；对于任意容量背包给出了 (1/2 - ε) 近似算法。两个结果均逼近或达到了已知的不可近似下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瓶颈多背包问题是多背包问题的一个变体，旨在最大化最差背包的收益，这一目标反映了资源分配中的公平性原则。该问题在装箱、调度和资源分配等领域有重要应用，需要设计高效近似算法来应对其 NP-hard 特性。

Method: 采用基于装箱（bin packing）和分配（allocation）策略的近似算法设计，通过将问题转化为对偶形式或构造性划分方法实现。对于相同容量情形，可能采用了基于排序和分组的启发式策略；对于任意容量情形，可能结合了容量加权或迭代分配方法。

Result: 1. 当所有背包容量相同时，提出 (2/3 - ε) 近似算法，几乎达到已知的 (2/3 + ε) 不可近似下界；2. 当背包容量任意时，提出 (1/2 - ε) 近似算法，并证明 (1/2 + ε) 不可近似下界，表明近似比是最优的。

Conclusion: 该论文基本解决了瓶颈多背包问题的近似比上界。在相同容量情况下，算法达到2/3下界，逼近了已知的 hardness 下界；在任意容量情况下，算法同样达到了最优近似比。该研究为这一 NP-hard 问题提供了近乎紧的近似方案。

Abstract: In the bottleneck multiple knapsack problem, we are given a set of items and a set of knapsacks, where each item has a profit and a weight, and each knapsack has a capacity. Our goal is to assign items to knapsacks so as to maximize the minimum profit received by any knapsack subject to the capacity constraint.
  When all knapsacks have identical capacity, we give a $(\frac{2}{3} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. This result almost matches the $(\frac{2}{3} + \varepsilon)$ inapproximability bound for the bottleneck multiple subset sum problem (Caprara et al., 2000).
  When the knapsacks can have arbitrary capacities, we propose a $(\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. We also prove a hardness bound of $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)$ for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$.

</details>


### [257] [A Separator for Minor-Free Graphs Beyond the Flow Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05494)
*Hung Le*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在K_h-无痕图（K_h-minor-free graphs）的平衡分隔器问题上取得突破，将此前基于流-割对偶性方法的最优结果O(h log h √n)改进为O(h √log h √n)，打破了流障（flow barrier）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon、Seymour和Thomas于1990年提出K_h-无痕图的平衡分隔器猜想，认为最优大小应为O(h√n)，但基于流-割对偶性的技术存在O(h log h √n)的下界壁垒。Kawarabayashi-Reed虽构造了更优 separator，但函数f(h)增长极快，实际应用有限。研究者一直试图突破流障。

Method: 本文采用双重方法：①简单改造Korhonen和Lokshtanov的流算法，得到O(h log h √n) separator，匹配流障；②核心创新：将低直径分解（low-diameter decomposition）融入Alon-Seymour-Thomas的迭代框架，克服了其邻域界（neighborhood bound）的紧性限制。

Result: 成功突破流障：构造出尺寸为O(h √log h √n)的平衡分隔器，比流障O(h log h √n)更优，是解决Alon-Seymond-Thomas猜想的重大进展。

Conclusion: 通过在经典迭代框架中引入低直径分解技术，将邻域界降低约h倍（代价为log h因子），最终将separator尺寸从√h降至√log h。这一结果表明，流障的技术限制可通过巧妙的算法设计加以克服。

Abstract: In 1990, Alon, Seymour, and Thomas gave the first balanced separator of size $O(h^{3/2}\sqrt{n})$ for any $K_h$-minor-free graph, which has had numerous algorithmic applications. They conjectured that the size of the balanced separator can be reduced to $O(h\sqrt{n})$, which is asymptotically tight. Two decades later, Kawarabayashi and Reed constructed a separator of size $O(h\sqrt{n} + f(h))$ based on the graph minor structure theorem, where $f(h)$ is an extremely fast-growing function; their separator's size is only better for a very small value of $h$. Recently, Spalding-Jamieson constructed a separator of size $O(h\log h \log\log h \sqrt{n})$; the technique is rooted in concurrent flow-sparsest cut duality. Spalding-Jamieson's separator comes very close to $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, which is the barrier for techniques based on the flow-cut duality.
  In this work, we first present a simple adaptation of a flow-based algorithm of Korhonen and Lokshtanov to construct a balanced separator of size $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, matching the flow barrier. This result motivates the question of whether the flow barrier can be broken, which would be a stepping stone toward resolving the conjecture of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas. The main result of our work is a positive answer to this question: we construct a balanced separator of size $O(h \sqrt{\log h} \sqrt{n})$. Surprisingly, perhaps, our algorithm is still based on the iterative framework of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas, although a key component of their algorithm within this framework, called the neighborhood bound, was shown to be tight. Our new idea is to incorporate a low-diameter decomposition into the framework, allowing us to reduce the neighborhood bound by a factor of $h$, at the cost of a factor $\log h$, which translates to a reduction from $\sqrt{h}$ to $\sqrt{\log h}$ in the final separator's size.

</details>


### [258] [Nearly Optimal Attention Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05602)
*Edo Liberty,Alexandr Andoni,Eldar Kleiner*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了注意力机制的coreset（核心集）构造问题。在给定的单位范数键值对(K,V)∈R^d下，证明了存在规模为O(√d e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/ε)的子集可以近似原始注意力机制，同时给出了Ω(√d e^ρ/ε)的改进下界，改进了该领域的已知最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决注意力机制估计的空间效率问题。注意力机制是Transformer等模型的核心组件，计算成本高昂，需要有效的近似技术来降低计算复杂度和存储需求。

Method: 通过证明coreset的存在性来实现，采用数学分析技术处理单位范数键值对集合。该coreset能够同时近似所有范数受ρ约束的查询。

Result: 存在规模至多为 O(√d e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/ε) 的子集 (K',V')，使得对于所有范数受ρ约束的查询q，||Attn(q,K,V) - Attn(q,K',V')|| ≤ ε 成立。

Conclusion: 提出了更优的下界：ε-coresets 必须具有 Ω(√d e^ρ/ε) 的规模，证明了现有结果是近乎最优的。

Abstract: We consider the problem of estimating the Attention mechanism in small space, and prove the existence of coresets for it of nearly optimal size. Specifically, we show that for any set of unit-norm keys and values $(K,V)$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, there exists a subset $(K',V')$ of size at most $O({\sqrt{d} e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/\varepsilon})$ such that \[ \left\| \operatorname{Attn}(q,K,V)- \operatorname{Attn}(q,K',V') \right\| \le \varepsilon \] simultaneously for all queries whose norm is bounded by $ρ$. This outperforms the best known results for this problem. We also offer an improved lower bound showing that $\varepsilon$-coresets must have size $Ω({\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε})$.

</details>


### [259] [Algorithmic Phase Transition for Large Independent Sets in Dense Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05618)
*Abhishek Dhawan,Nhi U. Dinh,Eren C. Kızıldağ,Neeladri Maitra,Bayram A. Şahin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了在稠密随机超图中寻找大独立集的算法可解性，针对均匀 Erdős-Rényi 超图和 $\gamma$ 平衡 $r$ 部超图两种模型设计了在线算法，分别达到 $r^{1/(r-1)}$ 和 $(\max_i \gamma_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ 的近似因子，并证明这些界在算法上不可突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏随机图中的局部或低度数多项式（LDP）框架已被广泛研究，但在稠密设置下算法范式根本不同——是在线的且可能不稳定。更关键的是，即使是经典 Erdős-Rényi 随机图，LDP 在在线算法可访问的"容易"区域也可能失效，这挑战了其对稠密模型的适用性。

Method: 采用在线算法框架，分析了稠密$r$一致 Erdős-Rényi 超图和 $r$部超图中的独立集问题。通过设计在线算法并建立对应下界，证明了近似因子的尖锐性。

Result: 对于均匀模型，在线算法达到乘法近似因子 $r^{1/(r-1)}$；对于 $r$ 部模型，达到 $(\max_i \gamma_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$。两个模型均实现了最优近似因子，算法上下界匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了稠密随机超图中大独立集问题的算法上界与下界，证明在线算法在这些模型中无法突破上述近似因子，为稠密随机图模型的可计算性边界提供了完整的刻画。

Abstract: We study the algorithmic tractability of finding large independent sets in dense random hypergraphs. In the sparse regime, much of the natural algorithms can be formulated within either the local or the low-degree polynomial (LDP) framework, and a rich literature has subsequently identified nearly sharp algorithmic thresholds within these classes by exploiting their stability. In the dense setting, however, the algorithmic paradigms are fundamentally different: they are online and thus need not be stable. Perhaps more crucially, even for the classical Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, LDPs are conjectured to fail in the 'easy' regime accessible to online algorithms, thereby challenging their viability for dense models.
  Our focus is on two models: (i) finding large independent sets in dense $r$-uniform Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs, and (ii) the more challenging problem of finding large $γ$-balanced independent sets in dense $r$-uniform $r$-partite hypergraphs, where the $i$-th coordinate of $γ\in\mathbb{Q}^r$ specifies the proportion of vertices from $V_i$ in the independent set. For both models, we pinpoint the size of the largest independent set and design online algorithms that achieve a multiplicative approximation factor of $r^{1/(r-1)}$ in the uniform and $(\max_i γ_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ in the $r$-partite model. Furthermore, we establish matching algorithmic lower bounds, showing that these computational gaps are sharp: no online algorithms can breach these gaps.

</details>


### [260] [Discrete Optimal Transport: Rapid Convergence of Simulated Annealing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05877)
*Yuchen He,Tianhui Jiang,Sihan Wang,Chihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套离散最优传输框架来分析有限状态空间上的模拟退火算法。通过定义广义离散Wasserstein-2距离和路径的离散作用量，为模拟退火提供了非渐近收敛保证，并将KL散度与退火路径的离散作用量联系起来，可视为连续空间退火Langevin动力学分析（ICLR 2025）的离散对应物。应用于平均场Ising模型和平均场q状态Potts模型，分别给出多项式步数收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空间中的模拟退火分析已有基于作用量的框架（Guo et al., ICLR 2025），但离散状态空间缺乏对应理论。现有离散最优传输工具（ Maas, 2011）尚未被系统用于分析退火算法的收敛性。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立离散空间中的非渐近收敛理论。

Method: 1. 基于Maas的离散Wasserstein度量，定义广义离散Wasserstein-2距离；2. 定义路径的"离散作用量"概念；3. 建立KL散度上界与离散作用量之间的关系；4. 利用模型对称性，将分析约化到低维投影链，从而得到离散作用量的多项式上界。

Result: 对平均场Ising模型：任意β≥0，O(n⁵β²/ε)步达到ε误差；对平均场q状态Potts模型：在β≥β_s=q/2（无序相完全失稳区域），(q-1)块Glauber动力学在poly(n,β,1/ε)步内收敛。

Conclusion: 建立了离散状态空间模拟退火的非渐近理论框架，统一了连续与离散空间的分析方法。通过对称性约化到低维分析的技术路线，为其他离散模型的退火算法分析提供了可推广的范式。

Abstract: We develop a discrete optimal transport framework for analyzing simulated annealing algorithms on finite state spaces. Building on the discrete Wasserstein metric introduced by Maas (J. Funct. Anal., 2011), we define a generalized discrete Wasserstein-2 distance and the associated notion of \emph{discrete action} for paths of probability measures on graphs. Using these tools, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for simulated annealing: the KL divergence between the algorithm's output and the target distribution is controlled by the discrete action of the annealing path. This can be viewed as the discrete counterpart of the action-based analysis of annealed Langevin dynamics in continuous spaces by Guo, Tao, and Chen (ICLR 2025).
  As applications, we analyze simulated annealing for two fundamental models in statistical physics. For the \emph{mean-field Ising model}, we show that annealed single-site Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in KL divergence in $O(n^5β^2/\varepsilon)$ steps at \emph{any} inverse temperature $β\ge 0$. For the \emph{mean-field $q$-state Potts model}, we show that annealed $(q-1)$-block Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in $\mathrm{poly}(n, β, 1/\varepsilon)$ steps for all $β\ge β_{\mathsf{s}}=q/2$, the regime where the disordered phase has completely lost stability. In both cases, the key technical contribution is a polynomial upper bound on the discrete action, obtained by exploiting the symmetry of the model to reduce the analysis to a low-dimensional projected chain.

</details>


### [261] [Label Correcting Algorithms for the Multiobjective Temporal Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05954)
*Edina Marica,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究单源多目标时间最短路问题，移除之前算法对单调性和等调性的依赖。针对零持续时间循环带来的挑战，引入最大路径长度K约束，开发一般性标签纠正算法，并给出无需此约束的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法依赖于单调性(monotonicity)和等调性(isotonicity)两个性质，本文旨在研究不依赖这些假设条件下的问题求解。

Method: 开发了带路径长度约束K的一般性标签纠正算法；建立了若干充分条件来保证算法的完备性。

Result: 在无单调性和/或等调性假设下，零持续时间时间循环可能需要被遍历任意有限次才能生成所有非支配图像。

Conclusion: 提出了一般性标签纠正算法，并证明了若干充分条件，使得在特定条件下无需路径长度约束即可计算所有非支配解。

Abstract: Given a directed, discrete-time temporal graph $G=(V,R)$, a start node $s\in V$, and $p\geq1$ objectives, the single-source multiobjective temporal shortest path problem asks, for each $v\in V$, for the set of nondominated images of temporal $s$-$v$-paths together with a corresponding efficient path for each image. A recent general label setting algorithm for this problem relies on two properties of the objectives - monotonicity and isotonicity. Monotonicity generalizes the nonnegativity assumption required by label setting methods for the classical additive single-objective shortest path problem on static graphs, while isotonicity ensures that the order of the objective values of two paths is preserved when both are extended by the same arc.
  In this paper, we study the problem without assuming monotonicity and/or isotonicity. A key difficulty in this setting is that zero-duration temporal cycles may need to be traversed an arbitrary finite number of times to generate all nondominated images. This motivates the study of a restricted problem variant in which a maximum admissible path length $K$ is imposed, and only paths containing at most $K$ arcs are considered. We develop general label correcting algorithms for this setting and establish several sufficient conditions under which such a bound is not required, implying that the algorithms compute all nondominated images.

</details>


### [262] [The Pareto Frontier of Randomized Learning-Augmented Online Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06106)
*Mathis Degryse,Imrane Saakour,Christoph Dürr,Spyros Angelopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究随机学习增强的在线竞价问题，通过引入竞价函数统一框架，给出了一致性与鲁棒性之间的最优权衡，理论上闭合了R ≥ 2.885时的差距，实验验证了在增量中位数聚类问题上的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在线决策中随机学习增强的竞价问题，分析预测误差下的最坏情况权衡

Method: 提出了"竞价函数(bidding function)"这一新抽象概念，为随机竞价策略的设计与分析提供统一框架

Result: 给出了最优一致性C关于鲁棒性R的解析上下界，当 R ≥ 2.885 时边界匹配

Conclusion: 理论边界在 R ≥ 2.885 时闭合，实验验证了算法在聚类实际场景中的适用性

Abstract: Online bidding is a classical problem in online decision-making, with applications in resource allocation, hierarchical clustering, and the analysis of approximation algorithms. We study its randomized learning-augmented variant, where an online algorithm generates a sequence of random bids while leveraging predictions from an oracle. We provide analytical upper and lower bounds on the optimal consistency $C$ as a function of the robustness $R$, which match when $R \geq 2.885$, effectively closing the gap left by previous work. The key technical ingredient is the notion of a bidding function, a novel abstraction that provides a unified framework for the design and analysis of randomized bidding strategies. We complement our theoretical results with an experimental application of randomized bidding to the incremental median problem, demonstrating the applicability of our algorithm in practical clustering settings.

</details>


### [263] [Bilateral Treewidth for QBF: Where Strategies and Resolution Meet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06262)
*Robert Ganian,Marlene Gründel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的参数——双侧树宽度（bilateral treewidth），该参数结合了策略分解和Q-分解两种方法的优点，能够更有效地求解量化布尔公式（QBF）的可满足性判定问题，并在给定树分解的情况下证明了该问题的固定参数 tractability。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化布尔公式（QBF）评估问题即使在常数树宽度下也是PSPACE完全问题。传统树宽度未考虑量词前缀（存在/通用变量区分及量化顺序），先前研究分别采用基于策略和基于Resolution的两类技术，但这两类方法强度不可比较。本文动机是找到一个统一且更强大的参数化方法。

Method: 提出双侧树宽度（bilateral treewidth）这一新型分解参数，该参数同时兼容策略分支和Q-Resolution操作。文章假设输入已提供合适的树分解，并设计固定参数算法进行QBF评估。

Result: 证明了基于双侧树宽度的QBF评估问题是固定参数可求解的（fixed-parameter tractable）。该参数严格强于之前分离的策略分解和Resolution分解方法，统一了两个原本不可比较的研究方向。

Conclusion: 双侧树宽度成功结合了策略分解和Q-Resolution两类方法的优势，建立了一个更强大且统一的参数化框架，为QBF的参量化复杂度研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Treewidth is a well-studied decompositional parameter to measure the tree-likeness of a graph. While the propositional satisfiability problem (SAT) is known to be tractable when parameterized by the treewidth of the underlying primal graph, the evaluation of quantified Boolean formulas (QBFs) remains PSPACE-complete even on formulas of constant treewidth. Intuitively, this is because ordinary treewidth does not take into account the prefix of the QBF: it neither distinguishes between existential and universal variables, nor accounts for the order in which they are quantified. In the past, several weaker variants of treewidth have been devised to incorporate prefix-sensitive information. To establish tractability for QBFs under these notions, prior work has employed either strategy- or resolution-based techniques, thereby dividing the parameterized complexity landscape of QBF into two regimes that are incomparable in strength. We establish fixed-parameter tractability with respect to bilateral treewidth, a novel and strictly more powerful decompositional parameter that combines these rivaling approaches by simultaneously allowing for branching on strategies and performing Q-resolution. As in previous works in this direction, our algorithm assumes that a suitable tree decomposition is provided on the input.

</details>


### [264] [Designing Capacitated Subnetworks for Shortest Path Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06319)
*Markus Chimani,Max Ilsen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: The paper addresses energy-efficient network design by simultaneously solving network deactivation and shortest path routing. They develop a novel ILP with column generation to solve the combined problem exactly, providing the first experimental benchmark. Results show simple heuristics are near-optimal in practice, with TOCA performing best for diverse traffic scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current green traffic engineering methods fail to solve the combined network design + routing problem for shortest path routing (the most common paradigm). Existing approaches either ignore capacities, assume static routing during deactivation, or solve sub-problems sequentially with estimators instead of the actual routing protocol.

Method: Novel integer linear programming model with dynamically changing shortest paths, solved via special-purpose column generation and additional strengthening constraints.

Result: First exact solutions for small practical instances; experimental evaluation reveals simple fixed-routing methods produce surprisingly close-to-optimal results. TOCA approach excels when handling multiple traffic demands.

Conclusion: Simple heuristic methods (fixing routing then deactivating connections) achieve near-optimal solutions in practice; TOCA performs best for multiple traffic demands while being straightforward to implement.

Abstract: In pursuit of higher energy efficiency in computer networks, one subfield of green traffic engineering aims at reducing the size of a network during times of low traffic, while still guaranteeing the ability to route all occurring demands. In this setting, we have to simultaneously solve a network design problem (choosing connections to deactivate) and a routing problem (routing paths in the active subnetwork, adhering to some routing protocol). Interestingly, there seems to be no available method to tackle the problem as a whole for the simplest (and still most commonly used) routing paradigm: shortest path routing. State-of-the-art methods either do not consider capacities, or assume that the routing paths should not change when deactivating network connections, or separate the problem into its two constituents, first solving the network design problem (using some estimators in lieu of the precise routing protocol) and only then the actual routing problem. In this paper, we present an algorithm to tackle the full combined problem exactly via a novel integer linear program, modeling dynamically changing shortest paths. To solve it, we need to devise a special-purpose column generation method. To speed up the solution process, we further propose additional provably strengthening constraints. Now having the means to yield true optimal solutions for (small) practical instances, we can for the first time give an in-depth experimental evaluation that includes the absolute quality intrinsic to the above simplifying algorithms. It turns out that the arguably simplest method--first computing a routing, fixing it, and turning off all superfluous connections--yields solutions surprisingly close to the true optimum in practice. When considering multiple different traffic demands, a recent traffic-oblivious approach (TOCA) performs best, while being comparatively straightforward to implement.

</details>


### [265] [On the Parameterized Approximability of (Mergeable) Sum of Radii Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06398)
*Ameet Gadekar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究k-center型聚类问题k-MSR的参数化复杂性与近似算法。证明该问题为W[2]-hard，排除了高效参数化近似方案的存在，并给出FPT (8/3+ε)-近似算法，在特定约束下可达(2+ε)近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-MSR问题虽已有近似算法和参数化算法的研究，但其精确的参数化复杂性分类（尤其是在W层次中的位置）以及是否存在高效参数化近似方案（EPAS）仍是开放问题。

Method: 在计算复杂性方面，通过W[2]-hard性规约证明下界；在算法方面，采用可合并约束框架，设计了基于分解的FPT近似算法，改进近似比为(8/3+ε)。

Result: 证明k-MSR是W[2]-hard的，排除了EPAS的存在（除非W[2]=FPT），并排除了在f(k,ε)·n^{o(k)}时间内存在(1+ε)-近似的可能（假设ETH）。算法上，给出FPT (8/3+ε)-近似，改进原有(4+ε)的结果；在合适赋值子程序下可达(2+ε)-近似。

Conclusion: 本研究在k-MSR问题的参数复杂性研究中取得重要进展，确立了W[2]-hardness的上界，证明了不存在高效参数化近似方案的边界，并给出FPT近似算法的改进。该工作为该问题的后续研究提供了理论框架和算法方向。

Abstract: The sum of radii problem ($k$-MSR) asks, given a metric space on $n$ points, to place $k$ balls covering all points so as to minimize the sum of their radii. Despite extensive study from the perspectives of approximation and parameterized algorithms, the exact parameterized complexity of the problem and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes remained open. We advance this understanding on both the hardness and algorithmic fronts.
  We begin by showing that $k$-MSR is $W[2]$-hard parameterized by $k$, thereby pinpointing its location in the $W$-hierarchy. Moreover, via our reduction, we rule out efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS)--that is, $(1+ε)$-approximations running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot \mathrm{poly}(n)$--unless $W[2] = FPT$. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, we further rule out such algorithms running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot n^{o(k)}$, strengthening recent lower bounds for the problem.
  On the algorithmic side, we study $k$-MSR under the framework of mergeable constraints, which captures a broad class of clustering constraints, including fairness, diversity, and lower bounds. We obtain an FPT $(\frac{8}{3}+ε)$-approximation, improving upon the previous best guarantee of $(4+ε)$. Moreover, given access to a suitable assignment subroutine, we achieve a $(2+ε)$-approximation, matching the best known bound for the unconstrained problem. This, in turn, yields $(2+ε)$ FPT-approximations for several important settings, including $(t,k)$-fair, $(α,β)$-fair, $\ell$-diversity, and private clustering.

</details>


### [266] [Fast decremental tree sums in forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06555)
*Benjamin Aram Berendsohn,Marek Sokołowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study two fundamental decremental dynamic graph problems. In both problems, we need to maintain a vertex-weighted forest of size $n$ under edge deletions, weight updates, and a certain information-retrieval query. Both problems can be solved in $O(\log n)$ time per update/query using standard dynamic forest data structures like top trees, even if additionally edge insertions are allowed. We investigate whether the deletion-only problem can be solved faster.
  First, we consider $\texttt{tree-sum}$ queries, where we ask for the sum of vertex weights in one of the connected components (i.e., trees) in the forest. We give a data structure with $O(n)$ preprocessing time and $O(\log^* n)$ time per operation, based on a micro-macro tree decomposition (Alstrup et al., 1997). If the forest is unweighted (i.e., all weights are 1 and cannot be changed), then the operation time can be improved to $O(1)$.
  Additionally, we give an asymptotically universally optimal algorithm. More specifically, our algorithm works in the group model, and processes $m$ operations on an initial forest $F$ in running time $O( \mathrm{OPT}(F, m) )$. Here $\mathrm{OPT}(F, m)$ is the number of weight additions and subtractions that a best possible algorithm performs to handle a worst-case instance for a fixed initial forest $F$ and a fixed number $m$ of operations. We achieve this with a combination of the aforementioned decomposition technique, precomputation of optimal data structures for very small instances, and some insights into the behavior of $\mathrm{OPT}$. Note that even the worst-case complexity of this algorithm remains unknown to us.
  Second, we consider $\texttt{subtree-sum}$ queries. Here, the forest is rooted, and a query $\texttt{subtree-sum}(v)$ returns the sum of weights in the subtree rooted at $v$. We show tight bounds for several variants of this problem. [...]

</details>

### [267] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [268] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一款基于MoE++架构的推理专用模型，仅用700M活跃参数即在数学和代码基准上匹敌DeepSeek-R1-0528。其创新点在于Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，通过递归聚合并行推理轨迹并保持4K-token有界尾迹，在AIME'25和HMMT'25分别达到91.9%和89.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持模型参数量高效的同时，通过创新的测试时计算方法提升推理能力，缩小与大型推理模型的性能差距，同时展示全栈AMD平台训练大模型的能力。

Method: 采用MoE++架构(700M活跃/8B总参数)；四阶段RL级联训练(推理热身→RLVE-Gym课程→数学代码RL→行为RL)；引入Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹并仅携带4K-token有界推理尾迹。

Result: 在AIME'25达到91.9%，HMMT'25达到89.6%，在多个数学和代码基准测试中匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，仅需携带4K-token推理尾迹即可实现。

Conclusion: ZAYA1-8B通过创新的Markovian RSA推理方法和MoE架构，在仅1B活跃参数下达到了与大型推理模型相当的水平，证明了高效推理的可能性，同时展示了AMD全栈平台训练大模型的可行性。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [269] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Partial Evidence Bench，一个用于测量AI智能体在访问控制环境中是否错误声称完整性的基准测试。该基准包含72个任务，涵盖尽职调查、合规审计和安全事件响应三个场景，评估智能体在答案正确性、完整性意识、差距报告质量和不安全完整性行为四个维度的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enterprise AI agents operate within scoped retrieval systems and policy-constrained environments where access control is correctly enforced, yet the system can produce answers that appear complete while critical evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary — a subtle but potentially dangerous failure mode that existing benchmarks fail to capture.

Method: Developed a deterministic benchmark with 72 tasks across three enterprise scenarios (due diligence, compliance audit, security incident response), using ACL-partitioned corpora with oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. Evaluated four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior.

Result: Baseline systems show that silent filtering is "catastrophically unsafe" across all scenarios, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without causing trivial abstention. Real-model runs reveal model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in completeness handling behaviors.

Conclusion: Partial Evidence Bench provides a deterministic, governance-critical benchmark for measuring AI agents' completeness awareness in access-controlled environments, demonstrating that baseline systems exhibit catastrophic unsafe completeness behavior that can be eliminated through explicit fail-and-report mechanisms.

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [270] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BALAR是一种基于贝叶斯推断的主动推理框架，通过结构化信念维护和互信息最大化引导LLM agent主动向用户提问以获取关键信息，在侦探推理、思维谜题和临床诊断三项任务中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大语言模型在交互式环境中处理多轮对话时存在缺陷：系统被动响应用户，缺乏推理缺失信息和决定下一步提问的机制。

Method: BALAR是一个任务无关的外环算法，无需微调。它通过三个核心机制实现主动推理：1) 维护对潜在状态的结构化信念表示；2) 通过最大化预期互信息来选择澄清性问题；3) 当当前表示不足时动态扩展状态空间。

Result: 在三个基准测试上显著优于所有基线方法：AR-Bench-DC（侦探案件）提升14.6%、AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）提升38.5%、iCraft-MD（临床诊断）提升30.5%。

Conclusion: BALAR provides an effective task-agnostic solution for structured multi-turn interaction between LLM agents and users, requiring no fine-tuning and demonstrating significant improvements across diverse benchmarks including detective cases, thinking puzzles, and clinical diagnosis scenarios.

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [271] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个用于训练金融领域专属LLM的统一框架，通过创新的数据生成流水线（143M tokens）和校准拒绝机制，在保证答案质量和引用准确性的同时实现可控的拒绝率。12B模型在40+金融机构的实际部署中表现优异，速度和成本效益显著优于GPT-4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在银行领域的应用面临阻力，主要源于对高准确性、监管合规以及可验证、可 grounding 回答的迫切需求。

Method: 提出统一的数据高效框架：1）结合LLM-as-Judge过滤、引用标注和课程学习的 数据生成流水线，仅需143M tokens；2）基于22%不可回答样本的校准拒绝机制；3）从数据管理到量化服务的端到端方法论。模型规模12B参数。

Result: 12B模型在引用 grounding 方面优于GPT-4.1；拒绝机制将"I don't know"率从基线的4.3%（不安全）提升至12%，同时避免了GPT-4.1的过度拒绝（20.2%）；查询解决率提升7.1个百分点（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该框架已在40多家金融机构部署，模型响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍，具有实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [272] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为LLM中的谄媚不应仅被理解为同意行为，而是将对齐行为替代独立认知判断的边界失败。论文提出三条件框架和分类学，以区分社交对齐与认知诚信之间的边界，并为对齐评估提供边界感知评估方法、结构化评估标准和缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常通过外部行为（如同意错误信念、立场反转或偏离客观正确标准）来操作化谄媚，但这些 formulation 只捕捉了明显的谄媚形式，而忽视了涉及认知诚信和社交对齐的更微妙边界失败。

Method: 提出了一个三条件框架来界定谄媚：（1）用户表达信念、偏好或自我概念等线索；（2）模型通过对齐行为向该线索倾斜；（3）这种倾斜损害了认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。进一步引入了分类学，从对齐目标、机制和严重程度三个维度对谄媚进行分类。

Result: 提出了三条件谄媚框架和分类学，为对齐评估提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 论文提出了评估LLM谄媚的边界感知方法、结构化评估框架和缓解策略，并对替代性观点进行了讨论和定位。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [273] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ingenix BioResearcher，一个面向转化医学研究的多智能体系统，通过场景引导工作流整合30+工具和ML端点，支持可审计的异构生物医学数据分析。在单元能力、开放域生物医学推理和端到端临床发现三项评估中均展现出领先性能，验证了多智能体架构在生物医学场景的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用基础模型和现成工具增强或多智能体系统无法满足转化医学需求：倾向于生成单次答案或开放式运行，缺乏异构生物医学来源（文献、试验、专利、定量多组学分析）所需的场景特定可审计工作流、标识符保留、不确定性量化和可追溯溯源能力。

Method: Ingenix BioResearcher采用场景引导的多智能体系统架构：查询映射至版本化研究剧本，委托至30+工具和ML端点的专业子智能体，混合结构化数据库访问与沙箱化代码执行基因组规模分析，并在编辑组装前进行声明级多模型协调。

Result: 单元测试：109项单步测试通过率83.49%，平均得分0.892；基准测试：BixBench-Verified-50达89.33%，BaisBench科学发现平均分0.758；临床评估：30查询端到端基准阳性命中率74.7%±3.3%，阴性清除率96.8%±0.2%。

Conclusion: Ingenix BioResearcher demonstrates broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations, validating the scenario-guided multi-agent architecture for biomedical discovery workflows.
