Result: 在CodeFlow和ProofFlow基准上的实验表明，MCPP在广泛的预算-期限约束组合下，持续优于强基线方法，显著提升约束完成概率。

Conclusion: MCPP为智能体工作流提供了一种有效的约束驱动资源分配方案，能够在有限预算和时间下最大化工作流成功完成的可能性。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly solve complex user requests by executing orchestrated workflows, where subtasks are assigned to specialized models or tools and coordinated according to their dependencies. While recent work improves agent efficiency by optimizing the performance--cost--latency frontier, real deployments often impose concrete requirements: a workflow must be completed within a specified budget and before a specified deadline. This shifts the goal from average efficiency optimization to maximizing the probability that the entire workflow completes successfully under explicit budget and deadline constraints. We study \emph{constraint-driven online resource allocation for agentic workflows}. Given a dependency-structured workflow and estimates of success rates and generation lengths for each subtask--model pair, the executor allocates models and parallel samples across simultaneously executable subtasks while managing the remaining budget and time. We formulate this setting as a finite-horizon stochastic online allocation problem and propose \emph{Monte Carlo Portfolio Planning} (MCPP), a lightweight closed-loop planner that directly estimates constrained completion probability through simulated workflow executions and replans after observed outcomes. Experiments on CodeFlow and ProofFlow demonstrate that MCPP consistently improves constrained completion probability over strong baselines across a wide range of budget--deadline constraints.

</details>


### [341] [CrossCult-KIBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Cultural Knowledge Insertion in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06115)
*Zhen Zeng,Leijiang Gu,Feng Li,Jing Yu,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在跨文化场景中的文化错位问题，提出跨文化知识插入任务及CrossCult-KIBench评估基准（含9,800个案例，覆盖49个文化视觉场景，支持英语、中文、阿拉伯语），并引入MCKI基线方法。实验表明现有方法难以平衡文化适应与行为保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLM主要在英语中心数据上训练，在跨文化场景中经常产生文化不当或错位的响应，需要在保持模型在非目标文化中原有行为的同时，适应特定文化上下文

Method: 提出Memory-Conditioned Knowledge Insertion (MCKI)方法，从外部记忆库中检索相关文化知识，使用冻结的MLLM表示进行检索，并将匹配条目作为条件提示进行添加

Result: 当前方法难以在有效文化适应与行为保持之间取得平衡，揭示了开发文化感知MLLM的关键挑战

Conclusion: 开发更文化适应性且负责任的MLLM的重要研究方向

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), trained primarily on English-centric data, frequently generate culturally inappropriate or misaligned responses in cross-cultural settings. To mitigate this, we introduce the task of cross-cultural knowledge insertion, which focuses on adapting models to specific cultural contexts while preserving their original behavior in other cultures. To facilitate research in this area, we introduce CrossCult-KIBench, a comprehensive evaluation benchmark for assessing both the effectiveness of knowledge insertion and its unintended side effects on non-target cultures. The benchmark includes 9,800 image-grounded cases covering 49 culturally relevant visual scenarios across English, Chinese, and Arabic language-culture groups. It supports evaluation in both single-insert and sequential-insert settings. We also propose Memory-Conditioned Knowledge Insertion (MCKI) as a baseline method. MCKI retrieves relevant cultural knowledge from an external memory using frozen MLLM representations, prepending matched entries as conditional prompts when applicable. Extensive experiments on CrossCult-KIBench reveal that current approaches struggle to balance effective cultural adaptation with behavioral preservation, highlighting a key challenge in developing culturally-aware MLLMs. Our work thus underscores an important research direction for developing more culturally adaptive and responsible MLLMs.

</details>


### [342] [Back to the Beginning of Heuristic Design: Bridging Code and Knowledge with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06123)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Bui Dinh Pham,Dao Van Tung,Tran Cong Dao,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种互补的"自顶向下"自动启发式设计范式，将知识作为主要搜索对象，代码仅作为知识的实例化和测试载体。通过统计学习框架揭示失真-压缩权衡，在种群和树两种AHD框架中实现知识优先搜索，显著提升发现效率、迁移性和泛化能力，并证明两种范式结合可带来额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型自动启发式设计（AHD）方法大多遵循"自底向上"范式，从低层代码实现逐步提炼高层原理。作者认为这一视角不够完整，缺少对知识本身作为搜索对象的关注，导致学习到的知识难以跨问题迁移和复用。

Method: 本文通过统计学习视角形式化这一转变，揭示了失真-压缩权衡，并将该方法分别在基于种群的AHD框架和基于树的AHD框架中实现。研究涵盖组合优化及组合优化以外的任务，验证了知识优先范式的广泛适用性。

Result: 知识优先搜索在多个组合优化任务及更广泛领域均表现出更优的发现效率、迁移能力和泛化性能，通常优于传统的代码中心管道。将知识优先与代码优先策略相结合可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出知识优先搜索范式，强调迭代构建和演化可解释假设的重要性，这些假设能够超越单一搜索轨迹保持价值。结合自底向上和自顶向下两种策略可进一步提升性能，表明组合优化中启发式设计的进步取决于知识的显式表达和跨问题迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently advanced automatic heuristic design (AHD) for combinatorial optimization (CO), where candidate heuristics are iteratively proposed, evaluated, and refined. Most existing approaches search over executable programs and distill insights from execution feedback to guide later iterations. Because this process moves from low-level implementations to high-level principles, we refer to it as a bottom-up paradigm. We argue that this view is incomplete and introduce a complementary top-down perspective: knowledge becomes the primary search object and code merely instantiates and tests it, making what is learned explicit and reusable across problems and trajectories. We formalize this shift through a statistical-learning view that exposes a distortion--compression trade-off, and instantiate it in both population-based and tree-based AHD frameworks. Across CO and tasks beyond it, knowledge-first search improves discovery efficiency, transfer, and generalization, often outperforming code-centric pipelines, while combining both strategies yields further gains. Our results suggest that progress in AHD depends on iteratively constructing and evolving interpretable hypotheses that retain value beyond a single search trajectory.

</details>


### [343] [P-Guide: Parameter-Efficient Prior Steering for Single-Pass CFG Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06124)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: P-Guide proposes a single-pass alternative to dual-pass Classifier-Free Guidance in flow matching by modulating initial latent state, achieving ~50% latency reduction with competitive generation quality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is essential for high-fidelity conditional generation but imposes significant computational overhead by requiring dual forward passes at each sampling step.

Method: P-Guide modulates only the initial latent state to achieve guidance effects in a single inference pass. Under first-order approximation, it maintains equivalence to CFG by steering generation from the prior space without explicit velocity field extrapolation. The approach extends to heteroscedastic priors by jointly modeling mean and variance for adaptive loss attenuation.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that P-Guide reduces inference latency by approximately 50% while maintaining fidelity and prompt alignment competitive with standard dual-pass CFG baselines.

Conclusion: P-Guide provides an efficient alternative to standard CFG by modulating only the initial latent state, achieving comparable generation quality with approximately 50% latency reduction, and joint mean-variance modeling enhances robustness to data uncertainty.

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is essential for high-fidelity conditional generation in flow matching, yet it imposes significant computational overhead by requiring dual forward passes at each sampling step. In this work, we address this bottleneck by introducing \textbf{P-Guide}, a framework that achieves high-quality guidance through a single inference pass by modulating only the initial latent state. We further show that, under a first-order approximation, P-Guide is equivalent to CFG in the sense that it steers generation from the prior space, without requiring explicit velocity field extrapolation during sampling. We consider both homoscedastic and \textbf{heteroscedastic} priors, and find that jointly modeling the mean and variance enables adaptive loss attenuation and improved robustness to data uncertainty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that P-Guide reduces inference latency by approximately 50\% while maintaining fidelity and prompt alignment competitive with standard dual-pass CFG baselines.

</details>


### [344] [Rethinking Adapter Placement: A Dominant Adaptation Module Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06183)
*Suoxin Zhang,Run He,Di Fang,Xiang Tan,Kaixuan Chen,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现LoRA适配器的梯度能量高度集中于单个浅层FFN下投影模块，提出DomLoRA方法仅在该主导适配模块放置单个适配器，以约0.7%的可训练参数超越vanilla LoRA在多种任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法广泛分布适配器，但使用更少的LoRA适配器仍可能保持或提升性能。然而如何放置有限数量的适配器以最大化性能仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出PAGE（投影适配器梯度能量）方法，这是一种基于梯度的敏感性探测工具，用于估算每个候选LoRA适配器位置的初始可训练梯度能量。进一步基于发现提出DomLoRA方法，仅在主导适配模块位置放置单个适配器。

Result: 发现PAGE在两个模型家族和四个下游任务中高度集中于单个浅层FFN下投影模块。这种主导适配模块的层索引具有架构依赖性但任务稳定性。DomLoRA以约0.7%的vanilla LoRA可训练参数，在指令跟随、数学推理、代码生成、多轮对话等多种下游任务上平均优于LoRA，并能改进其他LoRA变体。

Conclusion: DomLoRA方法将单个适配器放置在主导适配模块位置，仅使用约0.7%的vanilla LoRA可训练参数即可在多种下游任务（指令跟随、数学推理、代码生成、多轮对话）上优于vanilla LoRA，同时也能改进其他LoRA变体，为适配器放置提供了实用的放置指南。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that places trainable low-rank adapters into frozen pre-trained models. Recent studies show that using fewer LoRA adapters may still maintain or even improve performance, but existing methods still distribute adapters broadly, leaving where to place a limited number of adapters to maximize performance largely open. To investigate this, we introduce PAGE (Projected Adapter Gradient Energy), a gradient-based sensitivity probe that estimates the initial trainable gradient energy available to each candidate LoRA adapter. Surprisingly, we find that PAGE is highly concentrated on a single shallow FFN down-projection across two model families and four downstream tasks. We term this module the dominant adaptation module and show that its layer index is architecture-dependent but task-stable. Motivated by this finding, we propose DomLoRA, a placement method that places a single adapter at the dominant adaptation module. With only ~0.7% of vanilla LoRA's trainable parameters, DomLoRA outperforms it on average across various downstream tasks, including instruction following, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-turn conversation. This method also improves other LoRA variants, supporting the dominant adaptation module perspective as a practical placement guideline.

</details>


### [345] [OPSD Compresses What RLVR Teaches: A Post-RL Compaction Stage for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06188)
*Jaehoon Kim,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了OPSD在思维增强数学推理任务中失效的根本原因：对于长思维链输出，OPSD只能识别冗余而非提供更好的替代方案，因此应作为压缩工具而非纠正工具使用，推荐的后训练流程为SFT→RLVR→OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在简单RLVR任务中表现优异，但在思维增强的数学推理中准确率提升消失甚至变为负值，这一反常现象需要解释。

Method: 将OPSD分别应用于正确rollouts组和错误rollouts组，隔离观察压缩效应和纠正效应。

Result: OPSD在思维增强数学推理中主要表现为压缩机制而非纠正机制：仅在正确rollouts上训练可在保持准确率的同时显著缩短响应长度，仅在错误rollouts上训练则损害准确率。

Conclusion: 提出改进的后训练流程：SFT → RLVR → OPSD，适用于思维增强的数学推理任务。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has recently emerged as an alternative to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), promising higher accuracy and shorter responses through token-level credit assignment from a self-teacher conditioned on privileged context. However, this promise does not carry over to thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, where reported accuracy gains shrink and sometimes turn negative. We hypothesize that hindsight supervision can specify better token-level alternatives in short thinking-disabled outputs, but in long thinking-enabled traces it more readily identifies redundancy than supplies better replacements. To test this, we applied OPSD separately to correct and incorrect rollout groups, so that compression and correction can be observed in isolation. Our results show that in thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, OPSD behaves most reliably as a compression mechanism rather than a correction mechanism: training only on correct rollouts preserves accuracy while substantially shortening responses, whereas training only on incorrect rollouts damages accuracy. In light of these findings, we propose a revised post-training pipeline for thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning: SFT then RLVR then OPSD.

</details>


### [346] [Graphlets as Building Blocks for Structural Vocabulary in Knowledge Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06154)
*Kossi Amouzouvi,Robert Wardenga,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将图元（graphlets）作为结构化token来处理知识图谱缺乏固定几何结构的挑战，通过模式匹配挖掘知识图谱中的关系间结构模式，在51个知识图谱上验证了零样本链接预测任务中优于先前的知识图谱基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言和视觉领域的基础模型依赖离散符号在固定网格上的表示，但知识图谱虽具有离散性却缺乏统一几何结构，其不规则、非欧几里得的拓扑结构使得跨图谱迁移表示困难。当前知识图谱基础模型（KGFM）缺乏通用token集，限制了模型在未见知识图谱上的迁移能力。

Method: 提出基于图元词汇的模型无关框架，通过模式匹配在异构图谱中挖掘关系间的结构模式。具体采用闭合/开放2-路径、3-路径及星形图元获取鲁棒的结构不变量。

Result: 在51个来自不同领域的知识图谱上进行了零样本归纳式和直推式链接预测评估。结果表明，向词汇表添加简单图元即可使模型性能超越先前知识图谱基础模型。

Conclusion: 图元可作为知识图谱基础模型的通用结构token，有效解决了知识图谱缺乏固定几何结构的问题，为跨图谱迁移学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Foundation models excel at language, where sentences become tokens, and vision, where images become pixels, because both reduce to discrete symbols on a shared, fixed grid. Knowledge Graphs share the discreteness, but not the geometry. Their entities and relations are discrete symbols, yet their arrangement is relational and lacks a common, fixed grid. Knowledge Graphs (KGs) share the discreteness, but not the geometry. They form irregular, non-Euclidean topologies whose local neighborhoods differ from graph to graph. Therefore, Knowledge Graph Foundation Models (KGFMs) rely on identifying structural invariances to produce transferable representations. Without a universal token set, KGFMs are limited in their ability to transfer representations across unseen KGs. We close this gap by treating graphlets, small connected graphs, as structural tokens that recur in heterogeneous KGs. In this paper, We introduce a model-agnostic framework based on a vocabulary of graphlets that mines a KG between relations via pattern matching. In particular, we considered closed and open 2- and 3-path, and star graphlets, to obtain robust invariances. The framework is evaluated on 51 KGs from a wide range of domains, for zero-shot inductive and transductive link prediction. Experiments show that adding simple graphlets to the vocabulary yields models that outperform prior KGFMs.

</details>


### [347] [The Granularity Axis: A Micro-to-Macro Latent Direction for Social Roles in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06196)
*Chonghan Qin,Xiachong Feng,Ziyun Song,Xiaocheng Feng,Jing Xiong,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明大语言模型内部表征编码了社会角色的粒度维度，从微观个人到宏观机构/国家角色形成了有序且可操控的潜在方向。这一发现基于几何分析、跨模型迁移和因果干预实验得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被普遍赋予从个人到机构的各种社会角色，但尚不清楚其内部表征是否编码这些角色的粒度差异（从微观个体经验到宏观组织/制度/国家推理）。

Method: 定义对比粒度轴（macro与micro角色隐状态均值之差）；构建75个跨5个粒度级别的社会角色，收集91,200个角色条件响应；提取角色级隐状态并投影到粒度轴；进行激活 steering 实验验证因果相关性。

Result: Qwen3-8B中，粒度轴与角色表征空间主轴PC1余弦相似度达0.972，解释了52.6%方差，证明粒度是组织提示社会角色的主导几何轴。角色投影在所有五个层级单调递增，在层间、提示变体、端点定义、held-out分割及分数过滤子集中保持稳定，并能迁移到Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct。激活steering可因果性转移响应粒度。

Conclusion: 社会角色粒度不是表面风格特征，而是角色条件语言模型行为中一个结构化、有序且可因果操控的潜在维度。研究为理解LLM如何表征和应用社会角色提供了新框架，并对可解释性和可控AI系统设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are routinely prompted to take on social roles ranging from individuals to institutions, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations encode the granularity of such roles, from micro-level individual experience to macro-level organizational, institutional, or national reasoning. We show that they do. We define a contrast-based Granularity Axis as the difference between mean macro- and micro-role hidden states. In Qwen3-8B, this axis aligns with the principal axis (PC1) of the role representation space at cosine 0.972 and accounts for 52.6% of its variance, indicating that granularity is the dominant geometric axis organizing prompted social roles. We construct 75 social roles across five granularity levels and collect 91,200 role-conditioned responses over shared questions and prompt variants, then extract role-level hidden states and project them onto the axis. Role projections increase monotonically across all five levels, remain stable across layers, prompt variants, endpoint definitions, held-out splits, and score-filtered subsets, and transfer to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The axis is also causally relevant: activation steering along it shifts response granularity in the predicted direction, with Llama moving from 2.00 to 3.17 on a five-point macro scale under positive steering on prompts that admit local responses. The two models differ in controllability, suggesting that steering depends on each model's default operating regime. Overall, our findings suggest that social role granularity is not merely a stylistic surface feature, but a structured, ordered, and causally manipulable latent direction in role-conditioned language model behavior.

</details>


### [348] [BioMedArena: An Open-source Toolkit for Building and Evaluating Biomedical Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06177)
*Jinge Wu,Hongjian Zhou,Mingde Zeng,Jiayuan Zhu,Junde Wu,Jiazhen Pan,Sean Wu,Honghan Wu,Fenglin Liu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioMedArena是一个开源工具包，通过解耦生物医学智能体评估的六个层次（基准加载、工具暴露、工具选择、执行模式、上下文管理、评分），解决了深度研究智能体评估中的"每篇论文工程税"问题。该工具包提供147个生物医学基准和75个工具，支持6种评估框架和策略，使新模型/基准/工具的集成成本大幅降低，并在8个基准上实现SOTA性能，平均提升15.03个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前构建深度研究智能体面临"每篇论文工程税"问题：相同的骨干模型在相同基准上因测试框架和工具注册表不同而导致不同论文报告的准确率存在差异。将新基础模型集成到可比评估环境中需要数周的模型特定工程工作。

Method: BioMedArena将生物医学智能体评估解耦为六个层次：基准加载、工具暴露、工具选择、执行模式、上下文管理和评分。提供147个生物医学基准测试和75个生物医学工具（涵盖9个功能家族），以及6种智能体测试框架和6种上下文管理策略。添加新模型、基准或工具仅需注册几行代码的提供者适配器。

Result: BioMedArena为12个具有竞争性研究能力的骨干模型提供支持，在8个代表性生物医学基准上达到SOTA水平，平均提升+15.03个百分点。

Conclusion: BioMedArena是一个开源工具包，通过模块化设计将评估流程解耦为6个层次，显著降低了深度研究智能体的工程成本。该工具包为生物医学领域提供了标准化、可比较的评估框架，使新模型、基准或工具的集成仅需注册少量代码即可完成。

Abstract: Building a deep research agent today is an exercise in glue code: the same backbone evaluated on the same benchmark can report different accuracies in different papers because harness and tool registry all differ, and integrating a new foundation model into a comparable evaluation surface costs weeks of model-specific engineering. We call this the per-paper engineering tax and release BioMedArena, an open-source toolkit that not only alleviates it but also provides an arena for fair comparison of different foundation models when evaluating them as deep-research agents. BioMedArena decouples six layers of biomedical agent evaluation -- benchmark loading, tool exposure, tool selection, execution mode, context management, and scoring -- and exposes 147 biomedical benchmarks and 75 biomedical tools across 9 functional families. Adding a new model, benchmark, or tool reduces to registering a few-line provider adapter. We further provide 6 agent harnesses with 6 context-management strategies, which provide 12 backbones with competitive research capabilities and significantly improved performance, achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on 8 representative biomedical benchmarks, with an average lift of +15.03 percentage points over prior SOTA. The toolkit, configurations, and per-task traces are available at https://github.com/AI-in-Health/BioMedArena

</details>


### [349] [Event-Causal RAG: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Long Video Reasoning in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06185)
*Peizheng Yan,Yu Zhao,Liang Xie,Juntong Qi,Mingming Wang,Erwei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Event-Causal RAG，一个用于无限长视频推理的轻量级检索增强框架。该方法通过将视频按语义事件分割、表示为结构化 SES 图并构建全局 Event Knowledge Graph，结合双存储记忆和双向检索策略，有效解决了现有方法在超长视频推理中记忆碎片化、因果建模弱及计算成本高的问题，在长视频理解基准上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大视觉-语言模型在短视频和中等长度视频理解上表现良好，但在超长甚至无限长视频推理方面存在明显不足，主要挑战在于：需要在长时序上保持连贯记忆，以及推断跨时间距离的事件间因果依赖关系。现有端到端方法受限于自注意力的 O(n²) 复杂度，而基于 RAG 的方法仍存在碎片化的片段级记忆、弱的时间/因果结构建模以及高存储和在线推理成本等问题。

Method: 方法层面，首先将流式视频按语义连贯性分割为事件，每个事件表示为 State-Event-State (SES) 结构化图以捕捉状态转换关系；随后将这些局部事件图合并为全局 Event Knowledge Graph，存储于双存储记忆系统中，支持语义匹配和因果-拓扑检索；在记忆系统之上设计双向检索策略，定位最相关的因果链并连同视频证据一同输入视频基础模型生成答案。

Result: 在长视频理解基准测试上的实验表明，Event-Causal RAG 在需要多事件整合和跨长时间间隔因果推理的问题上持续优于基于片段的检索基线和长上下文视频模型，同时实现了更好的记忆效率和稳健的流式处理性能。

Conclusion: Event-Causal RAG 是一种针对超长视频推理的高效检索增强框架，通过事件分割和结构化 SES 图表示，结合双向检索策略，在保持记忆连贯性和因果建模能力的同时显著降低了存储与推理开销，为无限长视频推理提供了轻量且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent large vision-language models have achieved strong performance on short- and medium-length video understanding, yet they remain inadequate for ultra-long or even infinite video reasoning, where models must preserve coherent memory over extended durations and infer causal dependencies across temporally distant events. Existing end-to-end video understanding methods are fundamentally limited by the $O(n^2)$ complexity of self-attention, while recent retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches still suffer from fragmented clip-level memory, weak modeling of temporal and causal structure, and high storage and online inference costs. We present Event-Causal RAG, a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework for infinite long-video reasoning. Instead of indexing fixed-length clips, our method segments streaming videos into semantically coherent events and represents each event as a structured State-Event-State (SES) graph, capturing the event together with its surrounding state transitions. These graphs are merged into a global Event Knowledge Graph and stored in a dual-store memory that supports both semantic matching and causal-topological retrieval. On top of this memory, we design a bidirectional retrieval strategy to efficiently identify the most relevant event causal chains and provide them, together with the associated video evidence, to a backbone video foundation model for answer generation. Experiments on long-video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that Event-Causal RAG consistently outperforms strong clip-based retrieval baselines and long-context video models, particularly on questions requiring multi-event integration and causal inference across long temporal gaps, while also achieving improved memory efficiency and robust streaming performance.

</details>


### [350] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将激活修补分析重新定义为图机器学习问题，通过修补效应图表示模型组件间的激活影响关系。提出三种图构建方法（直接影响力、偏相关、共影响），在GPT-2 Small的IOI任务上验证了图的判别能力，并提供了受控基准以区分结构化电路证据与基线信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究中，将transformer计算反向工程为因果电路时，激活修补实验的缩放面临挑战：跨多样化提示和任务家族的干预产生高维、非结构化数据集，难以系统性比较。现有方法缺乏统一的表示框架和可控基准来评估电路级claims的有效性。

Method: 将激活修补概况表示为模型组件上的修补效应图（Patched-Effect Graphs），并提出三种图构建方法：直接影响力（通过因果中介分析）、偏相关（PC）和共影响（CI）。应用图核分析这些结构，并在GPT-2 Small上使用间接对象识别（IOI）及相关任务进行评估。

Result: （1）修补效应图保留了判别性结构信号；（2）局部边缘槽特征比全局图形状描述符提供更高分类准确率；（3）CI和PC筛选的候选边缘比随机或低秩候选具有更强的激活影响效应；（4）图特征可压缩结构化修补信号，而原始张量和表面线索构成强有力的基线。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的图框架为激活修补数据的压缩与评估提供了系统性方法论，分离了任务相关的切片判别证据与更强的任务通用因果电路证据。通过直接影响力、偏相关和共影响三种图构建方法与图核结合，该框架在GPT-2 Small的IOI任务上验证了结构信号保留能力，为电路级研究提供了可复现的基准框架。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [351] [Systematic Evaluation of Large Language Models for Post-Discharge Clinical Action Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06191)
*Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi,Prasanna Desikan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了零样本和少样本大语言模型在临床行动提取任务中的表现，特别是在出院小结中识别安全关键性临床行动。研究引入两阶段提取框架，将叙述性出院记录分解为可操作的临床任务。结果表明，当代LLM在二元行动检测上可媲美或超越监督模型，但在细粒度多标签分类上仍落后于监督模型。研究指出许多失败源于模型推理与标注规范之间的不一致，强调需要带推理理由的标注数据集来正确评估模型的临床理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床文档的复杂性使得从叙述性出院记录中提取安全关键性行动面临挑战。传统监督模型需要大量标注数据且缺乏泛化能力，而临床场景下的数据隐私限制进一步制约了模型开发。需要系统评估现代LLM在此类安全关键任务中的可行性。

Method: 1) 引入两阶段提取框架，通过分层提示策略将出院记录分解为细粒度可操作任务；2) 系统评估生成式LLM在临床行动提取中的表现；3) 对比通用LLM与特定任务监督BERT模型；4) 分析不同行动类别间的标注不一致性。

Result: 当代LLM在二元行动检测任务上达到或超越监督模型性能；但在细粒度多标签分类任务上，监督基线仍保持显著优势。定性错误分析表明，许多失败源于模型推理与数据集标注规范之间的不一致，尤其涉及隐含临床行动和刚性结构化标注规则的情况。

Conclusion: 当前性能指标未能充分反映模型缺乏临床推理能力的局限性，因为标注仅包含"什么被标注"而缺乏"为什么"的推理依据。推进临床NLP发展需要构建包含推理理由的标注数据集，使研究者能够区分模型临床推理失败与标注规范不一致，从而正确评估模型对临床内容的理解能力。

Abstract: The work in this paper evaluates zero-shot and few-shot large language models (LLMs) for safety-critical clinical action extraction using the CLIP discharge-note dataset, with particular emphasis on transitions of care and post-discharge patient safety. To manage the complexity of clinical documentation, we introduce a two-stage extraction framework that decomposes discharge notes, that are written in narrative form, into fine-grained, explicitly actionable clinical tasks through a staged prompting strategy. Our contributions include a systematic assessment of generative LLMs for clinical action extraction, a detailed comparison between general-purpose LLMs and task-specific supervised BERT-based models, and an analysis of annotation inconsistencies across different action categories. We show that contemporary LLMs achieve performance comparable to or exceeding supervised models on binary actionability detection, while supervised baselines retain a meaningful advantage on fine-grained multi-label category classification, despite the absence of task-specific fine-tuning and under strict data-privacy constraints. Qualitative error analysis reveals that many failures stem from misalignment between model reasoning and dataset annotation conventions, particularly in cases involving implicit clinical actions and rigid structural labeling rules. These results indicate that reported performance reflects model limitations due to lack of clinical reasoning, that is not captured by plain annotations. Labels without rationales make it impossible to distinguish clinical reasoning failures from annotation convention mismatches. Advancing clinical NLP requires reasoning-annotated datasets that document why specific spans are actionable, not merely which spans were labeled, enabling proper evaluation of model clinical understanding.

</details>


### [352] [Towards Annotation-Free Validation of MLLMs: A Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06201)
*Ying Gu,Mei Chee Leong,Hui Li Tan,Shangbo Mao,Liyuan Li,Nancy Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VL-LCM框架，通过基本逻辑原则评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的视觉-语言逻辑一致性，可无需ground-truth标注对新任务进行评估。实验涵盖MMMU和NaturalBench等11个开源模型，结果显示当前模型逻辑一致性显著落后于准确率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主导的准确率评估可能奖励模型的不正当猜测，且在没有ground-truth标注的新任务上不适用。需提出新的评估框架来衡量模型的逻辑一致性。

Method: 提出Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric (VL-LCM)框架，基于充分和必要因果关系的基本逻辑原则，评估MLLMs的视觉-语言逻辑一致性。该指标可应用于传统MC-VQA测试和无需gt标注的NaturalBench测试。

Result: 尽管近期MLLMs在准确率上取得显著进步，但其逻辑一致性严重滞后。VL-LCM与gt标注指标的关联性、LCM的可靠性以及VL-LCM与响应分布的关系均验证了该指标的有效性。

Conclusion: VL-LCM可有效用于MLLM选择、验证及新颖任务中的可靠答案解释，且无需ground-truth标注即可评估模型的可靠性和逻辑一致性。

Abstract: Dominant accuracy evaluation might reward unwarranted guessing of Large Language Models, and it might not be applicable to novel tasks for model validation without ground-truth (gt) annotation. Based on basic logic principle, we propose a novel framework to evaluate the vision-language logical consistency of MLLMs on both sufficient and necessary cause-effect relations. We define Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric (VL-LCM) on traditional MC-VQA tests, and recent NaturalBench tests without the need for gt annotation. Through systematic experiments on representative VL benchmark MMMU and recent VL challenges like NaturalBench, we evaluated 11 recent open-source MLLMs from 4 frontier families. Our findings reveal that, despite significant progress of recent MLLMs on accuracy, logical consistency lags behind significantly. Extensive evaluations on the correlations of VL-LCM with metrics on gt, the reliability of LCM, and the relation of VL-LCM with response distribution justify the validity and applicability of VL-LCM even without gt annotation. Our findings suggest that, beyond accuracy, logical consistency could be employed for both accuracy and reliability. VL-LCM can also be employed for MLLM selection, validation, and reliable answer justification in novel tasks without gt annotation.

</details>


### [353] [SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614)
*Siru Ouyang,Jun Yan,Yanfei Chen,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Yizhu Jiao,Kaiwen Zha,Maohao Shen,Vishy Tirumalashetty,George Lee,Jiawei Han,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOS提出了一个基于经验驱动的强化学习训练方案,用于自我进化智能体中的技能策展学习。其核心创新在于将技能执行器(冻结)与可训练的技能策展器分离,通过组合奖励信号和基于技能相关任务依赖的分组训练流,在多轮智能体任务和单轮推理任务中均优于基线方法,且习得的策展器具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体多为一次性问题求解器,缺乏从过去交互中学习的能力。技能策展是自我进化的关键瓶颈,但现有方法存在依赖人工策展、使用启发式操作或仅训练短期技能操作的局限,难以从间接和延迟反馈中学习复杂的长期策展策略。

Method: SkillOS采用经验驱动的强化学习训练方案:
1. **架构分离**: 冻结的智能体执行器(负责技能检索与应用) + 可训练技能策展器(从累积经验更新外部SkillRepo)
2. **奖励设计**: 设计组合奖励信号为策展提供学习信号
3. **训练策略**: 基于技能相关任务依赖对任务流进行分组,早期轨迹更新SkillRepo,后续相关任务评估这些更新

Result: 在多轮智能体任务和单轮推理任务中,SkillOS在有效性和效率上均优于无记忆和强基于记忆的基线方法。习得的技能策展器可泛化到不同执行器骨干和任务领域。

Conclusion: 1. 习得策展器产生更有针对性的技能使用
2. SkillRepo中的技能演化为结构更丰富的Markdown文件,随时间编码更高层次的元技能
3. 策展策略具有良好的跨执行器骨干和跨任务领域的泛化能力

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.

</details>


### [354] [Beyond Fixed Benchmarks and Worst-Case Attacks: Dynamic Boundary Evaluation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06213)
*Haoxiang Wang,Da Yu,Huishuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态边界评估(DBE)方法，通过在每个模型特有的"边界"处(随机采样解码下通过率接近0.5的题目)进行评估，并结合经过校准的题库、技能引导边界搜索算法和自适应扩展协议，实现了对不同能力水平LLM的精准、灵活的动态评估，解决了传统固定基准测试的天花板/地板效应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估依赖固定基准测试，对所有模型应用相同的题目集，导致天花板效应和地板效应，掩盖了模型之间的能力差异。

Method: 提出了动态边界评估(DBE)框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 经过校准的题库，覆盖安全、能力、诚实性三个维度，难度标签在9个参考LLM上验证；2) 技能引导边界搜索(SGBS)算法，仅需API级别的查询访问即可为给定目标LLM寻找边界项；3) 评估协议，将新LLM置于统一能力标尺上，并在目标超出题库覆盖范围时自适应扩展评估集。

Result: DBE能够覆盖更广泛的模型能力范围而不产生饱和，同时与现有数据集保持兼容性。

Conclusion: DBE通过在动态边界处定位模型，实现了更广泛的模型能力覆盖，避免了饱和效应，同时保持了与现有数据集的兼容性，为LLM评估提供了一种更加灵活和精准的范式。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) today rests on fixed benchmarks that apply the same set of items to any model, producing ceiling and floor effects that mask capability gaps. We argue that the most informative evaluation signal lies at the boundary, where the per-prompt pass probability is near $0.5$ under random-sampling decoding, and propose Dynamic Boundary Evaluation (DBE), which actively locates each model's boundary and places it on a globally comparable difficulty scale. DBE delivers three artifacts: (i) a calibrated item bank covering safety, capability, and truthfulness, with per-item difficulty labels validated across $9$ reference LLMs; (ii) Skill-Guided Boundary Search (SGBS), a search algorithm that finds boundary items for a given target LLM using only API-level query access; and (iii) an evaluation protocol that places a new LLM on a unified ability scale and grows the evaluation set adaptively when the target falls outside the bank's coverage. We instantiate DBE on four categories spanning safety (harmful request refusal and over-refusal), capability (constrained instruction following), and truthfulness (multi-turn sycophancy resistance). The resulting evaluation covers a broader model spectrum without saturation while remaining compatible with existing datasets.

</details>


### [355] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASPO是一个用于自动迭代优化多智能体系统中LLM提示词的新框架，通过联合评估机制和进化束搜索方法，在6个不同任务上平均准确率提升2.9个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统中，智能体通过角色特定提示词协调工作，但提示词的质量对系统性能至关重要。现有方法难以联合优化交互智能体间的提示词，主要因为局部目标与整体系统目标存在对齐偏差，且缺乏真值标签监督。

Method: MASPO框架包含两个核心创新：1）联合评估机制，不仅评估提示词的局部有效性，还评估其对后续智能体下游成功率的促进作用，从而弥合局部交互与全局结果间的差距；2）数据驱动的进化束搜索，在高维提示空间中高效搜索。

Result: 在6个多样化任务上的实验表明，MASPO持续优于现有最先进的提示词优化方法，平均准确率提升2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种无需真值标签即可联合优化多智能体系统提示词的有效方法，为复杂协作任务的自动化提示词工程开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


### [356] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScaleLogic框架揭示了RL训练中计算量与任务难度的幂律 scaling 规律，证明逻辑表达性越强Scaling exponent越大，且训练数据的形式显著影响迁移效果，课程学习可提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练中任务难度与计算量的 scaling 规律缺乏系统研究，主要因缺乏可控、可 scale 的实验环境。

Method: 提出ScaleLogic合成推理框架，操控两个难度维度：证明深度D和逻辑表达性。采用幂律拟合 T∝D^γ 分析多RL方法，并设计课程训练策略。

Result: （1）训练计算量与推理深度呈幂律关系（R²>0.99）；（2）表达性从蕴含扩展至一阶逻辑时，γ从1.04增至2.60；（3）更表达性训练设置在下游任务提升达+10.66分，且计算效率更高；（4）幂律关系跨多种RL方法成立；（5）课程学习显著改善scaling效率。

Conclusion: 研究证实了逻辑表达性与训练难度之间存在可量化的幂律关系（γ: 1.04→2.60），且训练数据的形式（表达性）而非仅数量决定迁移效果。课程学习可显著提升 scaling 效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [357] [A Versatile AI Agent for Rare Disease Diagnosis and Risk Gene Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06226)
*Tianyu Liu,Wangjie Zheng,Rui Yang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Hui Zhang,Jeffries Lauran,Jianlei Gu,Botao Yu,Weihao Xuan,Kexin Huang,Nan Liu,James Zou,Yonghui Jiang,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hygieia是一个多模态AI诊断系统，通过整合表型特征、基因图谱和临床记录，并采用基于路由器和知识增强的框架，专门针对罕见病诊断进行了优化（如优先考虑风险相关的基因组因素），提供置信度评分以辅助临床决策。在耶鲁医学院和Duke-NUS医学院的验证中，Hygieia相比医生表现出12%-60%的诊断性能提升，并显著降低了临床医生的工作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current diagnostic workflows for rare diseases suffer from prolonged assessment times and low accuracy, necessitating improved AI-driven diagnostic systems.

Method: Multi-modal AI agent system integrating phenotypic features, genetic profiles, and clinical records with a router-based, knowledge-enhanced framework that prioritizes risk-related genomic factors and provides confidence scores.

Result: SOTA performance across multiple benchmarks; 12%-60% improvement over physicians in real-world validation.

Conclusion: Hygieia demonstrates superior diagnostic performance (12%-60% improvement over physicians), validated through real-world clinical cases with reduced clinician workload, positioning it as a valuable clinical decision support tool.

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnosis is essential for effective treatment, particularly in the context of rare diseases. However, current diagnostic workflows often lead to prolonged assessment times and low accuracy. To address these limitations, we introduce Hygieia, a multi-modal AI agent system designed to support precision disease diagnosis by integrating diverse data sources, including phenotypic features, genetic profiles, and clinical records. Hygieia features a router-based and knowledge-enhanced framework that mitigates hallucination and tailors diagnostic strategies to different disease categories. Notably, it prioritizes risk-related genomic factors for rare diseases and provides confidence scores to assist clinical decision-making. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation demonstrating that Hygieia achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple diagnostic benchmarks. In collaboration with clinical experts from Yale School of Medicine and Duke-NUS Medical School, we further validated its practical utility by showing (1) Hygieia's superior diagnostic performance compared to physicians with an improvement from 12%-60% and (2) its effectiveness in assisting clinicians with medical records for handling real-world cases. Our findings indicate that Hygieia not only enhances diagnostic accuracy and interpretability but also significantly reduces clinician workload, highlighting its potential as a valuable tool in clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [358] [Data Language Models: A New Foundation Model Class for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290)
*Eda Erol,Giuliano Pezzoli,Ozer Cem Kelahmet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper introduces Schema-1, the first Data Language Model (DLM) — a 140M parameter foundation model for tabular data that processes raw cell values natively without preprocessing, outperforming existing tabular models on prediction and imputation tasks while enabling novel capabilities like domain identification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tabular data is critical for real-world AI decisions but lacks a native foundation model; all existing approaches require preprocessing pipelines, unlike text (language models), images (vision models), or audio (audio models) which have native foundation models.

Method: Schema-1: a 140M parameter model trained on 2.3M+ synthetic and real-world tabular datasets, operating directly on raw cell values without serialization or preprocessing.

Result: Outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and existing tabular foundation models on row-level prediction benchmarks; achieves lowest reconstruction error on missing value imputation tasks; reliably identifies industry sector from raw cell values alone across any domain.

Conclusion: DLM (Schema-1) is the missing foundation model for tabular data, providing native understanding that eliminates preprocessing pipelines and enabling row-level prediction, missing value imputation, and domain identification directly from raw cell values.

Abstract: Every major data modality now has a foundation model that understands it natively: text has language models, images have vision models, audio has audio models. Tabular data, the modality on which many consequential real-world AI decisions are made, does not. Every approach to tabular AI today, from gradient-boosted trees to the latest tabular foundation models, requires a preprocessing pipeline before any model can consume the data. None of them understand tabular data as a modality. We introduce the Data Language Model (DLM), the missing foundation model for tabular data. A DLM understands tables the way a language model understands sentences: natively, without serialization or preprocessing, directly from raw cell values. It is the tabular data layer on which AI models, agents, and vertical AI applications can be built, eliminating the preprocessing pipelines that currently stand between raw data and every AI system that consumes it. We present Schema-1, the first DLM: a 140M parameter model trained on more than 2.3M synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. Schema-1 outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and the tabular foundation models we evaluate on established row-level prediction benchmarks. On missing value reconstruction it achieves lower reconstruction error than all classical statistical methods and frontier large language models on mean performance across conditions, establishing that structural understanding of a dataset's own distributional geometry is more useful for imputation than world knowledge encoded in language. It identifies the industry sector of any unseen dataset from raw cell values alone, reliably across any domain, a task no prior tabular model can perform. It is the native tabular understanding layer that has been missing from the AI stack.

</details>


### [359] [Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
*Richard Csaky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在确定性物理或模拟世界中研究部分可观测性下的智能体理论，通过桥接接口框架证明预测、压缩与 empowerment 的分离，揭示完美预测的两种机制（识别 vs 覆写），并为现代AI智能体设计与人机对齐提供接口设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体面临的核心挑战：在确定性世界中处理部分可观测性，理解预测、压缩与 empowerment（赋能/控制能力）之间的关系。现有方法往往混淆这些概念，导致设计不当。需要建立严格的理论框架来区分：(1) 完美预测的机制、(2)  empowerment的真正来源与局限、(3) 人机对齐的接口设计本质。

This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: 建模方法：将感知与执行建模为桥接接口，分离智能体控制参数与环境控制信道状态，通过潜在微观状态的先验分布与多对一观测粗粒化，构建确定性POMDP。证明策略：信息论分析（香农熵、信息预算守恒）与接口精化理论的结合。

This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: 主要发现：① 完美预测可通过两种方式实现——识别与目标家族相关的隐藏商空间，或通过覆写控制使未来目标动作确定；② 高 empowerment 本身不足以实现预测；③ 在可细化接口与充足记忆下，动作条件观测压缩进度可减少关于潜在商的后验不确定度；④ 当细化需要引导世界侧信道条件时，产生目标条件接口 empowerment；⑤ 比特殊化下，识别预测需要至少等于相关潜在熵的内部容量，覆写控制需要关于受控商的动作容量。

This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: 论文为现代AI智能体提供设计原则而非必然定理：目标应区分隐藏状态识别、接口细化、任务相关可控性与纯覆写/干扰控制；人机对齐可视为接口设计问题，涉及人类意图、智能体内部状态、外部工具与世界侧信道条件之间的桥接。

This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We study agency under partial observability in deterministic physical or simulated worlds, where apparent randomness arises from uncertainty over initial conditions, fixed law bits, and unrolled exogenous noise. We model sensing and actuation as bridge interfaces split between agent-controlled parameters and environment-controlled channel state, inducing a deterministic POMDP through a prior over latent microstates and many-to-one observation coarsening. Within this framework, we prove a separation between prediction, compression, and empowerment. Perfect prediction can be achieved either by identifying the hidden quotient relevant to the target family or by overwrite control that makes the future target action-determined; high empowerment alone is insufficient. Under refinable interfaces and sufficient memory, action-conditioned observation-compression progress reduces posterior uncertainty about the latent quotient, and when refinement requires steering world-side channel conditions, this creates target-conditioned interface empowerment. A bit-string specialization with a conserved information budget makes the resulting tradeoff explicit: prediction by identification requires internal capacity at least the relevant latent entropy, whereas overwrite control requires terminal action capacity over the controlled quotient. For modern AI agents, the results suggest a design principle rather than a theorem of inevitability: objectives should distinguish hidden-state identification, interface refinement, task-relevant controllability, and mere overwrite or distractor control. Human--AI alignment is partly an interface-design problem, where the relevant bridge is between human intent, agent internal state, external tools, and world-side channel conditions. This is a working draft: feedback and criticism is most welcome.

</details>


### [360] [Debiased Multimodal Personality Understanding through Dual Causal Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06371)
*Yangfu Zhu,Zitong Han,Nianwen Ning,Yuting Wei,Yuandong Wang,Hang Feng,Zhenzhou Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态人格理解中的主体偏差问题提出DCAN框架，通过因果推断中的后门和前门调整机制解耦人口统计学因素的干扰，在保证预测精度的同时显著提升公平性，并构建了首个带有人口统计学标注的学生人格多模态数据集DMSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在学习多模态表征时容易受到主体偏差（如年龄、心理状态等人口统计学属性）的干扰，导致多模态特征与性格特征之间产生虚假关联，从而引发不公平的人格理解问题。

Method: 提出Dual Causal Adjustment Network (DCAN)，包含两个核心模块：1) 后门调整因果学习(BACL)模块，通过基于原型的主题混淆字典阻塞可观测人口统计学因素导致的虚假相关性；2) 前门调整因果学习(FACL)模块，通过学习的中间变量字典干预处理潜在不可观测的偏差。

Result: 在CFI-V2和DMSP数据集上准确率分别达92.11%和92.90%；公平性指标方面，CFI-V2上Equal opportunity和Demographic parity分别提升6.57%和7.97%，DMSP数据集上分别提升15.38%和20.06%。

Conclusion: DCAN通过后门调整和前门调整的双重因果干预机制，有效解耦了人口统计学因素对人格理解的影响，在提升预测准确率的同时显著改善了公平性指标。实验表明该方法在两个数据集上均达到了92%以上的准确率，且公平性指标提升显著。

Abstract: Multimodalpersonalityunderstandingplaysacriticalroleinhuman centered artificial intelligence. Previous work mainly focus on learn-ing rich multimodal representations for video personality under standing. However, they often suffer from potential harm caused by subject bias (e.g., observable age and unobservable mental states), as subjects originate from diverse demographic backgrounds. Learn ing such spurious associations between multimodal features and traits may lead to unfair personality understanding. In this work, weconstruct aStructural Causal Model (SCM)toanalyze theimpact of these biases from a causal perspective, and propose a novel Dual Causal Adjustment Network (DCAN) to mitigate the interference of subject attributes on personality understanding. Specifically, we design a Back-door Adjustment Causal Learning (BACL) module to block spurious correlations from observable demographic factors via a prototype-based confounder dictionary, and subsequently ap ply a Front-door Adjustment Causal Learning (FACL) module to ad dress latent and unobservable biases throughalearnedmediatordic tionary intervention, thereby achieving causal disentanglement of representations for deconfounded reasoning. Importantly, we con struct a Demographic-annotated Multimodal Student Personality (DMSP) dataset to support the analysis and discussion of fairness related factors. Extensive experiments on the benchmark dataset CFI-V2 and our DMSPdataset demonstrate that DCAN consistently improves prediction accuracy, reaching 92.11% and 92.90%, respec tively. Meanwhile, the improvementsinthefairnessmetricsofequal opportunity and demographic parity are 6.57% and 7.97% on CFI-V2, and 15.38% and 20.06% on the DMSP dataset. Our code and DMSP dataset are available at https://github.com/Sabrina-han/DCAN

</details>


### [361] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Evidential Deep Learning (EDL)中基于Vacuity/Uncertainty Mass (UM)的OOD检测指标对类别基数K的变化高度敏感这一关键问题。当ID和OOD数据集的类别数K不同时，AUROC和AUPR指标会产生显著偏差（AUROC偏差高达0.318-0.360，AUPR偏差高达0.613-0.683），这可能是一种评估伪影（evaluation artefact），而非模型真实性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EDL研究中，Vacuity（不确定性质量）被广泛用作OOD检测的评估指标。该指标通过K/S计算，其中S是Dirichlet参数的总和。由于EDL中错误分配的证据会被抑制，K和S之间不太可能存在线性关系，因此当ID和OOD的类别基数K不同时，指标比较存在不公平性，但这一影响因素常被忽视。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明当K在ID和OOD之间存在差异时，AUROC/AUPR可能被人工夸大；2. 实证验证：在标准EDL和IB-EDL上，通过控制变量（仅改变K值）展示指标偏差程度；3. 应用探索：将EDL评估扩展到因果语言模型的MCQA数据集场景。

Result: 实验表明，当ID和OOD的类别数仅相差1时，AUROC差异可达0.318（标准EDL）和0.360（IB-EDL），AUPR差异可达0.613（标准EDL）和0.683（IB-EDL）。这种偏差完全由K值差异引起，而非模型预测改变。

Conclusion: 基于Vacuity的EDL微调LLM的OOD检测对评估时类别基数的不可控差异高度敏感。研究呼吁在EDL的OOD检测评估中明确定义ID和OOD边界，并确保K_ID = K_OOD以获得可靠的比较结果。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [362] [Automated alignment is harder than you think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06390)
*Aleksandr Bowkis,Marie Davidsen Buhl,Jacob Pfau,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A leading proposal for aligning artificial superintelligence (ASI) is to use AI agents to automate an increasing fraction of alignment research as capabilities improve. We argue that, even when research agents are not scheming to deliberately sabotage alignment work, this plan could produce compelling but catastrophically misleading safety assessments resulting in the unintentional deployment of misaligned AI. This could happen because alignment research involves many hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks (tasks without clear evaluation criteria, for which human judgement is systematically flawed). Consequently, research outputs will contain systematic, undetected errors, and even correct outputs could be incorrectly aggregated into overconfident safety assessments. This problem is likely to be worse for automated alignment research than for human-generated alignment research for several reasons: 1) optimisation pressure means agent-generated mistakes are concentrated among those that human reviewers are least likely to catch; 2) agents are likely to produce errors that do not resemble human mistakes; 3) AI-generated alignment solutions may involve arguments humans cannot evaluate; and 4) shared weights, data and training processes may make AI outputs more correlated than human equivalents. Therefore, agents must be trained to reliably perform hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks. Generalisation and scalable oversight are the leading candidates for achieving this but both face novel challenges in the context of automated alignment.

</details>


### [363] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于验证导向知识图谱（KG）的多智能体工作流，用于从自然语言规范和RTL中生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA）。该方法通过结构化中间表示构建KG，结合语法修复、CEX引导修正和覆盖率引导增强三条反馈回路，在7个基准设计上实现了78.5%–99.4%的形式验证覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based SVA synthesis suffers from loose specification-RTL grounding due to treating inputs as unstructured text, leading to semantic mismatches and syntax failures when formal tools attempt to parse the generated assertions.

Method: Constructs a verification-centric Knowledge Graph from structured IRs of specifications, RTL, and formal-tool feedback; uses a multi-agent workflow to query and update this KG for SVA generation, with three refinement loops driven by tool diagnostics, counterexamples, and coverage reports.

Result: KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding, consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead, and achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4% across seven benchmark designs.

Conclusion: KG-based multi-agent workflow with three refinement loops (syntax repair, CEX-guided correction, coverage-directed augmentation) achieves formal coverage of 78.5%–99.4% across seven benchmarks, though complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remains challenging for current LLMs, with design-dependent convergence.

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [364] [PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455)
*Xinmiao Huang,Jinwei Hu,Rajarshi Roy,Changshun Wu,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrefixGuard是一种用于LLM智能体轨迹的轻量级前缀监控框架，通过离线StepView归纳和监督式监控器训练实现事件抽象和风险评分。在四个基准测试中验证有效，平均AUPRC提升+0.137，且提供了可观测性上限和DFA可解释性等诊断工具，但强排名性能并不直接转化为低误报部署实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体现在执行长序列工具使用任务时，最终结果检查往往太晚而无法干预。在线预警需要轻量级前缀监控器处理异构轨迹，但手工编写的事件模式脆弱，部署时LLM评判成本高昂。

Method: 采用离线StepView归纳步骤从原始轨迹样本中诱导确定性类型化步骤适配器，然后进行监督式监控器训练从终端结果学习事件抽象和前缀风险评分器。

Result: 在WebArena、τ²-Bench、SkillsBench和TerminalBench四个基准上，最强PrefixGuard监控器AUPRC分别达到0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557；相比原始文本对照平均提升+0.137 AUPRC；LLM评判者在相同协议下表现显著较弱；可观测性上限可分离监控错误与缺乏证据的失败；DFA提取在简单领域紧凑（20-29状态），在复杂领域扩展（151-187状态）；首次警报诊断表明强排名不等于部署实用性。

Conclusion: PrefixGuard定位为实用的监控器综合方案，提供明确的诊断能力，用于判断前缀警告是否能转化为可操作干预。方法包括离线StepView归纳（从原始轨迹样本诱导确定性类型化步骤适配器）和监督式监控器训练（从终端结果学习事件抽象和前缀风险评分器）。在四个基准测试中，最强PrefixGuard监控器达到0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC，相比原始文本对照平均提升+0.137 AUPRC。LLM评判者在相同前缀警告协议下表现显著较弱。研究还推导了基于分数的AUPRC可观测性上限，用于分离监控错误与缺乏证据的失败。有限状态审计的DFA提取在WebArena和τ²-Bench上保持紧凑（29和20状态），但在SkillsBench和TerminalBench上扩展至151和187状态。首次警报诊断表明，强排名并不等同于部署实用性：WebArena排名良好但无法支持低误报警报，而τ²-Bench和TerminalBench保留更多可操作的早期警报。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now execute long, tool-using tasks where final outcome checks can arrive too late for intervention. Online warning requires lightweight prefix monitors over heterogeneous traces, but hand-authored event schemas are brittle and deployment-time LLM judging is costly. We introduce PrefixGuard, a trace-to-monitor framework with an offline StepView induction step followed by supervised monitor training. StepView induces deterministic typed-step adapters from raw trace samples, and the monitor learns an event abstraction and prefix-risk scorer from terminal outcomes. Across WebArena, $τ^2$-Bench, SkillsBench, and TerminalBench, the strongest PrefixGuard monitors reach 0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC. Using the strongest backend within each representation, they improve over raw-text controls by an average of +0.137 AUPRC. LLM judges remain substantially weaker under the same prefix-warning protocol. We also derive an observability ceiling on score-based area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) that separates monitor error from failures lacking evidence in the observed prefix. For finite-state audit, post-hoc deterministic finite automaton (DFA) extraction remains compact on WebArena and $τ^2$-Bench (29 and 20 states) but expands to 151 and 187 states on SkillsBench and TerminalBench. Finally, first-alert diagnostics show that strong ranking does not imply deployment utility: WebArena ranks well yet fails to support low-false-alarm alerts, whereas $τ^2$-Bench and TerminalBench retain more actionable early alerts. Together, these results position PrefixGuard as a practical monitor-synthesis recipe with explicit diagnostics for when prefix warnings translate into actionable interventions.

</details>


### [365] [Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06457)
*Donghao Huang,Joon Kiat Chua,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ASR（Agentic Success Rate）指标，用于评估LLM多智能体系统中智能体的执行轨迹保真度，解决了传统指标TSR和HF1无法捕捉执行过程偏差的问题。在HMASP系统、18个LLM和90,000个任务实例上的实验表明，ASR能够发现10个模型系统性跳过支付确认检查点的行为，而这一偏差被TSR和HF1完全遗漏。基于ASR诊断的优化使部分模型的TSR提升高达93.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支付工作流的多智能体系统评估指标存在根本缺陷：TSR仅关注最终任务成功率，HF1仅评估无序的路由决策，二者都无法捕捉智能体执行序列中的中间步骤偏差。在受监管的支付领域，确认检查点等安全机制的缺失可能带来合规风险，但现有指标无法有效检测。

Method: 提出ASR指标，通过比较观察到的与预期的智能体执行序列，在转换级别进行对齐评估。ASR分解为两个子指标：Transition Recall（召回率）和Transition Precision（精确率）。实验在HMASP（分层多智能体支付系统）上进行，涵盖18个LLM模型和90,000个任务实例。

Result: ASR揭示了关键发现：18个模型中有10个系统性跳过支付结账时的确认检查点，这一偏差对TSR和HF1完全不可见；8个模型则正确执行检查点。GPT-4.1尽管在TSR和HF1上达到完美得分，却存在隐藏的工作流捷径；GPT-5.2则实现了完美的ASR。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估在受监管领域至关重要。基于ASR诊断的提示优化和确定性路由保护机制可带来显著改进，对表现欠佳的模型TSR提升高达93.8个百分点。研究强调了在合规敏感场景中，需要超越最终结果的细粒度执行轨迹评估。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for payment workflows, yet prevailing metrics, Task Success Rate (TSR) and Agent Handoff F1-Score (HF1), capture only final outcomes or unordered routing decisions. We introduce the Agentic Success Rate (ASR), a trajectory-fidelity metric that compares observed and expected agent execution sequences at the transition level, decomposing performance into Transition Recall and Transition Precision. Applied to the Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP) across 18 LLMs and 90,000 task instances, ASR reveals that 10 of 18 models systematically skip a confirmation checkpoint during payment checkout, a deviation invisible to both TSR and HF1, while 8 models enforce the checkpoint perfectly. Notably, GPT-4.1 exhibits hidden workflow shortcuts despite achieving perfect TSR and HF1, while GPT-5.2 achieves perfect ASR. Prompt refinements and deterministic routing guards guided by ASR diagnostics yield substantial TSR improvements, with gains up to +93.8 percentage points for previously struggling models, demonstrating that trajectory-level evaluation is essential in regulated domains.

</details>


### [366] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonSTL是一个工具增强框架，通过显式推理、确定性工具调用和过程奖励训练，使本地开源语言模型能够将自然语言转换为Signal Temporal Logic (STL)公式。该方案在保护隐私和降低成本的同时，在4B参数规模下实现了最先进的翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自然语言到STL的自动翻译面临三重困境：(1) 手动规范需掌握时间逻辑专业知识且无法扩展；(2) 调用商业LLM API产生高昂的token成本；(3) 向第三方服务暴露敏感系统需求带来隐私风险，阻碍工业部署。

Method: ReasonSTL采用工具增强框架，将自然语言到STL的翻译分解为显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建三个阶段。模型通过过程奖励训练同时监督工具使用轨迹和最终公式的正确性。评估使用了STL-Bench基准测试，该基准包含双语评估样本并基于真实世界信号。

Result: 实验表明，经过ReasonSTL训练的4B参数模型在自动评估指标和人工评估中均达到最优性能，证明了该方法在透明度、成本效益和隐私保护方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL represents a significant step toward practical, privacy-preserving formal specification authoring. By combining tool-augmented reasoning with process-rewarded training and a rigorous benchmark, it demonstrates that local open-source LLMs can effectively serve industrial-grade formal requirement drafting at a fraction of the cost and privacy risk of commercial API solutions.

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [367] [From Token Lists to Graph Motifs: Weisfeiler-Lehman Analysis of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06494)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pablo Magariños-Docampo,Javier Perez-Robles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于token共现图的SAE特征图结构表示方法，通过WL风格图核度量特征相似性，验证了其在发现token频率和decoder权重视角无法捕捉的结构关系方面的互补价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征分析方法几乎仅通过激活token列表或decoder权重向量表征特征，忽略了特征之间更高阶的共现结构。

Method: 将每个SAE特征建模为token共现图（节点为强激活附近的频繁token，边为局部上下文窗口内共现的token对），并设计了一种自定义的WL风格、频率分箱的图核作为相似性度量。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small的大规模SAE上验证表明，图结构聚类能够识别标点符号模式、语言/脚本聚类、代码模板等启发式motif家族（decoder余弦相似度聚类无法捕获），但token直方图基线整体纯度更高，说明图视角的贡献是互补性的。

Conclusion: 图结构视角提供了互补而非替代的方法：token共现图能够捕捉decoder权重和token频率方法遗漏的结构关系，聚类结果对超参数和随机种子具有良好的稳定性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become central to mechanistic interpretability, decomposing transformer activations into monosemantic features. Yet existing analyses characterise features almost exclusively through top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined. We introduce a graph-structured representation in which each SAE feature is modelled as a token co-occurrence graph: nodes are the tokens most frequent near strong activations, and edges connect pairs that co-occur within local context windows. A custom WL-style, frequency-binned graph kernel then provides a similarity measure over this structural space. Applied as a proof of concept to features from a large SAE trained on GPT-2 Small and probed with a synthetic mixed-domain corpus, our clustering recovers heuristic motif families (punctuation-heavy patterns, language and script clusters, and code-like templates) that are not recovered by clustering on decoder cosine similarity. A token-histogram baseline achieves higher overall purity, so the contribution of the graph view is complementary rather than dominant: it surfaces structural relationships that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone do not capture. Cluster assignments are stable across graph-construction hyperparameters and random seeds.

</details>


### [368] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpatialEpiBench是一个针对时空流行病预测的基准数据集，包含11个流行病数据集，采用标准化滚动评估和疫情特定指标。研究发现，即使使用了流行病学先验知识，大多数方法在1天到1个月的前瞻预测中仍不如简单的"上次观测值"基线模型，存在三大失败模式：疫情预警不足、数据稀疏和噪声处理困难、地理邻接信息在流行病学空间建模中作用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流行病预测研究缺乏标准化基准，且现有评估方法多采用简单的时序切分，未能反映实时预测的实际场景。空间信息虽被广泛关注，但缺乏系统化的基准测试和方法对比。

Method: 构建SpatialEpiBench基准，包含11个流行病数据集，采用标准化滚动评估（rolling evaluation）方式，设计疫情特定评估指标，评估邻域感知模型与流行病学先验方法的组合表现。

Result: 大多数预测方法（包括使用流行病学先验的模型）在1天至1个月前瞻预测中表现均不如简单基线模型last-value baseline。

Conclusion: 当前时空流行病预测模型存在三个主要失败模式：疫情预警能力不足、难以处理数据稀疏和噪声、地理邻接信息在流行病学空间建模中的实用价值有限。研究强调了建立真实公共卫生场景基准的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [369] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估AI对人类创意多样性影响的框架，通过将创意概念化为"可拥塞资源"，引入过剩拥塞系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ三个前沿LLM在多个任务中均低于平等条件（ρ < 1），表明当前AI系统存在多样性崩溃风险，但该风险可通过针对性的生成协议设计来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current creative AI evaluation focuses on individual utility, ignoring population-level effects. When many produce similar ideas, value decreases (congestion). AI may improve individual outputs while worsening population-level crowding, creating an "evaluation blind spot" that requires a new assessment framework.

Method: Model ideas as congestible resources; within-distribution comparisons to identify source-level crowding; excess-crowding coefficient (Δ) and human-relative diversity ratio (ρ) as metrics; theoretical connection to adoption games with exposure-dependent redundancy costs.

Result: Three frontier LLMs fell below parity (ρ < 1) across crowding kernels in short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Crowding can be reduced through targeted generation-protocol design.

Conclusion: Generation-protocol variants can reduce crowding effects, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI. The framework provides practical tools for ex ante assessment of crowding risk.

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [370] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A deterministic adjoint matching framework is proposed that reformulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem, featuring a truncated adjoint scheme for computational efficiency and flexible alignment regularization beyond KL constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-based generative models require effective human preference alignment, but existing approaches may suffer from instability and computational inefficiency. The authors seek to leverage optimal control theory to provide a more principled and stable framework for this alignment task.

Method: The paper proposes a deterministic adjoint matching framework that reformulates human preference alignment as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. The method involves direct regression of control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, and introduces a truncated adjoint scheme that concentrates computation on the terminal portion of trajectories. Additionally, the framework generalizes beyond standard KL-based regularization to allow more flexible alignment trade-offs.

Result: Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially enhanced diversity and mode preservation.

Conclusion: The proposed adjoint matching framework successfully bridges optimal control theory with generative model alignment, achieving a balanced trade-off between alignment quality and distributional preservation while maintaining computational efficiency.

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [371] [NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06584)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Jianwei Zhang,Shuo huang,Jiaxin Yue,Mingyang Xia,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroAgent是一个LLM驱动的多智能体框架，通过层级架构和生成-执行-验证引擎自动化多模态神经影像预处理和分析流程，在ADNI数据集上实现84.8%的端到端预处理正确率和0.9518的四模态分类AUC，显著降低人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像分析涉及复杂且模态特异性的预处理工作流程，需要跨异构工具链的精细配置和质量控制；下游统计分析和疾病分类需要任务特定的代码、评估协议和数据格式约定，导致从原始采集到可重复科学分析之间存在严重障碍。

Method: 采用层级式多智能体架构，结合反馈驱动的生成-执行-验证引擎，智能体能够自主生成可执行预处理代码、检测并恢复运行时错误、验证输出完整性，支持自然语言查询进行交互式下游分析。

Result: 在ADNI所有阶段汇总的1,470例受试者中评估，最强后端模型(Qwen3.5-27B)达到100%的意图解析准确率和84.8%的端到端预处理步骤正确率；自动化恢复机制将人工干预限制在仅需人工审查的边缘案例；阿尔茨海默病分类任务中，四模态融合实现AUC=0.9518，优于所有单模态基线模型。

Conclusion: NeuroAgent能够显著减少神经影像预处理所需的人工工作，并实现端到端的自动化分析流程，为神经影像研究提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.

</details>
