### [372] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GlazyBench，首个用于AI辅助釉料设计的数据集，包含23,148个真实釉料配方，支持属性预测和视觉生成两项任务，建立了全面的基线模型，实验显示该任务具有挑战性但前景可观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发陶瓷釉料是一个成本高昂、耗时且依赖试错的过程，涉及复杂的化学成分，这对独立艺术家构成了重大负担。尽管多模态AI提供了现代解决方案，但该领域缺乏训练这些模型所需的大规模数据集。

Method: 该数据集支持两项主要任务：1）从原材料预测烧制后的表面属性（如颜色和透明度）；2）基于这些属性生成准确的视觉表征。研究团队建立了全面的基线模型，包括使用传统机器学习和大语言模型进行属性预测，以及使用深度生成模型和大语言多模态模型进行图像生成基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明模型性能有前景但仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: GlazyBench为AI辅助材料设计开辟了新的研究方向，提供了标准化的基准测试用于系统性评估，为独立艺术家提供了降低陶瓷釉料开发成本的潜在途径。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [373] [AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651)
*Daniel Zheng,Ingrid von Glehn,Yori Zwols,Iuliya Beloshapka,Lars Buesing,Daniel M. Roy,Martin Wattenberg,Bogdan Georgiev,Tatiana Schmidt,Andrew Cowie,Fernanda Viegas,Dimitri Kanevsky,Vineet Kahlon,Hartmut Maennel,Sophia Alj,George Holland,Alex Davies,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An AI workbench for mathematicians that provides integrated support across the full mathematical research workflow, from ideation to theorem proving, achieving state-of-the-art results on hard benchmarks like FrontierMath.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mathematicians need holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows (ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving, and theory building), rather than isolated tools.

Method: An asynchronous, stateful workbench that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts to mirror human collaborative workflows.

Result: The system helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. It achieved 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

Conclusion: The system demonstrates a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, while also achieving state-of-the-art performance on hard problem-solving benchmarks (48% on FrontierMath Tier 4).

Abstract: We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [374] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nitsum是一种分布式LLM服务系统，将张量并行从静态部署参数转变为运行时动态控制面，通过联合优化TP级别、GPU分配和调度，并配合高效的权重重用与KV迁移机制，在多租户混合SLO场景下实现显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务多租户化面临混合SLO挑战，需同时处理延迟敏感型交互请求和宽松的后台工作负载。现有系统主要在请求层面进行优化（队列、批处理），而执行配置保持静态，难以适应多层级竞争下的动态负载变化。

Method: Nitsum将张量并行(TP)作为运行时一等控制面，联合优化TP级别、prefill/decode GPU分配和请求调度；引入TP感知的权重重用和快速KV迁移技术以降低TP切换开销。

Result: 在真实轨迹和针对性微基准测试中，Nitsum相比现有最优系统SLO达标吞吐量提升最高达5.3倍。

Conclusion: Nitsum通过将张量并行作为运行时控制面而非静态部署参数，结合权重复用和KV迁移等优化技术，有效解决了多租户场景下混合SLO服务的难题，显著提升系统吞吐量。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [375] [A Scalable Digital Twin Framework for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05581)
*Raphael Hendrigo de Souza Gonçalves,Wendel Marcos dos Santos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个用于数据中心能源优化的可扩展数字孪生框架，该框架整合物联网数据采集、云计算和LSTM机器学习模型，实现实时监控、能源预测和智能管理。在小型受控环境中的实验结果表明，该方案能够有效降低功耗并改善PUE，展现出作为可持续数据中心管理解决方案的可扩展性和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的能源消耗持续增长，亟需有效的能源优化解决方案来实现可持续运营。

Method: 该框架集成了基于物联网的数据采集、云计算和机器学习技术。实验中开发了一个小型受控数据中心环境，监测功耗、温度和计算工作负载等变量，并采用长短期记忆(LSTM)模型预测能源需求以支持运营决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架实现了能源效率的提升，包括功耗降低和PUE（电源使用效率）改善。

Conclusion: 该框架在受控环境中表现出色，展现出作为数据中心的可扩展且经济有效的可持续管理解决方案的潜力。

Abstract: This study proposes a scalable Digital Twin framework for energy optimization in data centers.The framework integrates IoT-based data acquisition, cloud computing, and machine learning techniques to enable real-time monitoring, forecasting, and intelligent energy management. A controlled small-scale data center environment was developed to monitor variables such as power consumption, temperature, and computational workload. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models were employed to predict energy demand and support operational decision-making. Experimental results demonstrated improvements in energy efficiency, including reductions in power consumption and enhancements in Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE). Despite being evaluated in a constrained environment, the proposed framework demonstrates strong potential as a scalable and cost-effective solution for sustainable data center management.

</details>


### [376] [Irminsul: MLA-Native Position-Independent Caching for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05696)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Irminsul是一个基于MLA架构内容寻址特性设计的缓存系统，通过内容哈希键控和k_r的δ旋转规则扩展SGLang基数缓存，在三个主流MLA-MoE模型上实现了83%的提示令牌恢复率和63%预填充能耗节省，为LLM服务系统提供了一种新的缓存范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM工作负载每轮将位相同的令牌置于偏移位置，导致前缀缓存在首个分歧字节处失效，产生10-16秒的TTFT尖峰；现有位置无关缓存系统需对GQA全d_K维Key进行RoPE校正，带来不必要架构开销。

Method: Irminsul通过内容哈希键控CDC分块段和k_r的δ旋转规则扩展SGLang基数缓存，利用MLA中c_KV位置无关性与k_r的64维可封闭形式校正特性，实现跨请求缓存复用。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite(16B/2.4B)、Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B、JoyAI-Flash(48B/3B)三个MLA-MoE部署上，Irminsul在Agentic流量中恢复了约83%的精确前缀以上提示令牌，每次缓存命中节省63%预填充能耗。

Conclusion: Irminsul将内容寻址缓存定位为服务栈的一等公民(First-Class Primitive)，而非前缀匹配的后备方案，实现了83%的提示令牌恢复率和63%的预填充能耗节省。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workloads put bit-identical tokens at shifted positions every turn, voiding prefix caches at the first byte of divergence. Operators report cache-hit regressions ranging from moderate slowdowns to severe TTFT spikes of 10-16s on unchanged content. Prior position-independent caching systems correct RoPE on the full $d_K$-dimensional key, an architectural cost imposed by GQA, not by caching itself. Multi-Head Latent Attention, deployed at scale in DeepSeek-V2/V3/R1, Kimi-K2/Moonlight, GLM-5, and Mistral Large 3, factors each KV row into a position-free $c_{KV}$ and a 64-dim $k_r$ correctable in closed form; this structure motivates content-addressed caching as a natural fit rather than a GQA workaround. We present Irminsul, which extends SGLang's radix cache with content-hash keying over CDC-chunked segments and a $δ$-rotation rule for $k_r$. We evaluate three native MLA-MoE deployments - DeepSeek-V2-Lite (16B/2.4B), Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B, and JoyAI-Flash (48B/3B) - with output-consistency on all three and recovery measured on the two endpoints; Irminsul recovers up to ~83% of prompt tokens above exact-prefix on agentic traffic while delivering 63% prefill energy savings per cache hit. We argue that content-addressed caching belongs in the serving stack as a first-class primitive, not a retrofit over prefix matching.

</details>


### [377] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FalconGEMM是一个跨平台框架，通过代码生成、Group-Parallel优化和自适应性能模型选择，在GPU和CPU异构硬件上实现LCMA的峰值突破性能，超越现有GEMM库和LCMA竞品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEMM库和低复杂度矩阵乘法算法(LCMA)在跨平台部署、性能优化和策略选择方面存在瓶颈，亟需一套自动化、可移植且高效的解决方案来释放LCMA在LLM训练推理中的理论性能优势。

Method: 提出三模块协同架构：(1) Deployment Module通过代码生成实现跨平台可移植部署；(2) Execution Module采用Group-Parallel优化策略最大化数据复用、并行资源和带宽效率；(3) Decision Module基于轻量级解析性能模型实现自适应策略选择。

Result: FalconGEMM较GEMM库(cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL等)提升7.59%-17.85%，较LCMA竞品(如AlphaTensor)提升12.41%-55.61%。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM成功将LCMA理论优势转化为实际生产部署，在GPU（H20, A100）和CPU（ARM, x86）异构平台上均实现峰值突破性能。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [378] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出BalanceRoute系列在线路由算法解决大规模LLM推理中的数据并行负载均衡瓶颈。该算法通过分段线性F-score捕获请求接纳的收益-代价非对称性，支持无需预测基础设施的快速调度（BR-0）和带常数视界预测的增强版本（BR-H）。实验表明该方案能有效降低DP不平衡度并提升服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模LLM服务中，数据并行（DP）负载均衡已成为首要瓶颈。当模型通过张量并行（TP）或专家并行（EP）分片并跨多个DP worker复制时，每个解码步骤末尾的同步屏障延迟由负载最重的worker决定，即使 modest的持续不平衡也会在每一步中累积成大量计算浪费。

Method: 提出BalanceRoute算法族，包含两个变体：BR-0使用分段线性F-score捕获请求接纳的收益与溢出代价之间的非对称性，采用两阶段分解保证每步调度成本适用于毫秒级调度；BR-H引入常数视界H和轻量级终止分类器，将F-score扩展为视界折扣形式，实现更前瞻的负载预测。

Result: 在144-NPU集群上部署BalanceRoute，对比vLLM基线，在生产追踪和公开Azure-2024追踪上均显著降低了平均DP不平衡度，提升了端到端服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute通过实用的在线路由算法有效解决了大规模LLM推理中的数据并行负载均衡瓶颈问题，在144-NPU集群上验证了其在生产环境和公开数据集上均能显著改善端到端服务吞吐量。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [379] [TACO: A Toolsuite for the Verification of Threshold Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06118)
*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TACO是一个用于开发与自动验证容错及阈值分布式算法的工具套件，实现了三种可判定片段的模型检查方法以及两种超越这些片段的半决策程序。该工具套件具有模块化、可扩展和文档完善的特点，并提供了算法概览与实验性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式系统的发展，对容错和基于阈值的分布式算法的正确性验证需求日益增长，自动化验证工具的缺乏限制了算法开发和验证的效率。

Method: 该工具套件实现了三种模型检查方法（用于已知的可判定片段）以及两种半决策程序（用于超出可判定范围的情况）。

Result: TACO提供了一个模块化、可扩展且文档完善的研究框架，成功实现了多种验证方法，并经过实验验证了性能。

Conclusion: TACO为阈值自动机的验证提供了从可判定到半可判定的完整工具链，是一个模块化、可扩展、文档完善的研究框架，并经过实验验证了性能表现。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [380] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ResiHP是一个面向大规模LLM训练（数万个GPU）的弹性训练系统，通过工作负载感知的故障检测器和动态调度器解决混合并行训练中的硬件故障和性能偏斜问题，在256-GPU集群上将训练吞吐量提升1.04-4.39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模LLM训练中（数万个GPU），硬件故障导致设备间性能偏斜，降低训练效率。现有弹性系统忽视了数据集序列长度可变性和混合并行下的性能偏斜，导致：(1) 序列长度变化引发的迭代时间波动触发虚假慢故障检测；(2) 故障缓解仅在混合并行内部进行个别调整，造成不必要的检测开销和低效的弹性训练。

Method: 提出ResiHP系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) Detector（检测器）：开发工作负载感知的执行时间预测器，将故障从迭代时间波动中解耦出来，同时保持轻量级以实现在线检测；(2) Scheduler（调度器）：动态调整并行组大小、模型划分和工作负载调度策略以提升故障下的训练效率。

Result: 在256-GPU集群上针对多种故障场景的实验表明，与最先进的弹性训练系统相比，ResiHP将训练吞吐量提升1.04-4.39倍。

Conclusion: ResiHP通过细粒度故障检测和自适应混合并行调整，有效解决了大规模LLM训练中的弹性训练问题，显著提升了系统鲁棒性和训练效率。

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [381] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ADELIA是首个利用反向模式自动微分和多GPU结构 exploit 的INLA实现，在保持计算精度的同时将梯度计算复杂度与超参数维度解耦，显著提升大规模时空贝叶斯推断的效率与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INLA实现依赖中心有限差分（FD）近似导数，需2d+1次评估，随着超参数维度d增长，总工作量和能耗显著增加，限制了固定算力预算下的时间收敛。

Method: 提出ADELIA框架，首次将反向模式自动微分（AD）应用于INLA，并设计结构 exploitation 的多GPU反向传播算法以利用模型稀疏性。该方法计算精确梯度的代价与超参数维度d无关。

Result: 在10个基准模型（包括真实空气污染监测）上验证，ADELIA实现单梯度4.2-7.9倍加速；在高达190万潜在变量的生产规模模型上可靠收敛（FD难以完成）；在16-32 GPU上匹配ADELIA wall-clock时间时，FD能耗高出5-8倍。

Conclusion: ADELIA通过结构化AD和多GPU优化，在生产规模问题上实现了FD方法无法达到的可靠收敛性，且能效提升5-8倍，为INLA在环境与健康科学中的大规模应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [382] [ROSE: Rollout On Serving GPUs via Cooperative Elasticity for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06534)
*Wei Gao,Yuheng Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Dakai An,Xuchun Shang,Tianyuan Wu,Lunxi Cao,Shaopan Xiong,Weixun Wang,Ju Huang,Teng Ma,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Bo Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ROSE是一个协同弹性后训练系统，通过安全利用服务GPU上的闲置计算和内存资源，加速agentic RL的rollout执行，包含SLO安全执行器、跨集群权重传输引擎和弹性调度器三个组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RL训练效率受制于长尾rollout和多轮环境交互，静态GPU配置难以适配：过度配置造成GPU资源浪费在落后者身上，而配置不足则增加竞争并拖慢训练。

Method: 提出ROSE系统，包含三个核心组件：1) SLO安全的共服务执行器，通过高效的GPU内存和计算共享提升rollout吞吐量；2) 利用权重分片和稀疏性的跨集群权重传输引擎；3) 动态调度协同容量并路由轨迹rollout的弹性调度器。

Result: 实验表明，ROSE在多种模型规模和集群规模下，相比最先进的资源固定和弹性基线方法，实现了1.20-3.31倍的平均端到端吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: ROSE通过协同弹性机制实现了1.20-3.31倍的端到端吞吐量提升，证明了利用闲置服务GPU加速agentic RL训练的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key driver for improving the multi-step reasoning and tool-use capabilities of LLMs. However, its efficiency is bottlenecked by long-tail rollouts with multi-turn environment interactions, making static GPU provisioning a poor fit: overprovisioning wastes GPUs on stragglers, while underprovisioning increases contention and slows training.
  We observe that production serving clusters routinely leave substantial GPU compute and memory headroom. Based on this observation, we argue for cooperative elasticity: opportunistically repurposing underutilized serving GPUs to execute rollouts. Realizing cooperative elasticity is non-trivial because it must preserve serving Service Level Objectives (SLOs) under bursty traffic and minimize communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present ROSE, a cooperative, resource-elastic post-training system that safely harvests idle compute and memory on serving GPUs to accelerate agentic RL rollouts. ROSE consists of three components: (1) an SLO-safe co-serving executor that improves rollout throughput while preserving serving SLOs through efficient GPU memory and compute sharing; (2) a cross-cluster weight transfer engine that leverages weight shards and sparsity for fast weight synchronization across clusters; and (3) an elastic rollout scheduler that dynamically provisions cooperative capacity and routes trajectory rollouts across dedicated rollout GPUs and opportunistic serving GPUs. Experiments across multiple model sizes and cluster scales show that ROSE improves average end-to-end throughput by 1.20-3.31 x compared with state-of-the-art resource-fixed and elastic baselines.

</details>


### [383] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CCL-Bench是一个基于追踪的基准测试框架，用于评估LLM基础设施配置的性能，解决了现有基准测试仅提供端到端数字而无法解释性能差异原因的问题。该框架通过记录可重用的执行追踪、YAML工作负载卡片和启动脚本，为ML工作负载提供细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基础设施评估基准（如benchmark）仅公布少量端到端数字，无法解释为何某一配置优于另一配置。这导致无法验证诸如"工作负载X在硬件Y上使用软件Z最快"这类评估性声明的准确性，因为配置空间涉及硬件加速器、互联带宽、软件框架、并行策略和通信库等多个维度。

Method: CCL-Bench采用trace-based方法，每个数据点包含：执行追踪(execution trace)、YAML工作负载卡片(workload card)、启动脚本(launch scripts)。开发了社区可扩展的工具包，用于计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标。

Result: 通过CCL-Bench揭示了三个summary-statistic基准无法支持的结论：1) 更高的计算-通信重叠可能与更长的训练步时间重合，揭示了低效的并行化选择；2) 在小型和中型工作负载上，将TPU互联带宽翻倍比将GPU互联带宽翻倍带来更高的端到端步时间改进；3) 在相同硬件上，一个训练框架的最优调优配置可能比peer框架的最优配置慢最多3倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench通过提供可解释的细粒度证据，超越了传统端到端基准测试，能够深入分析配置空间中各因素对性能的具体影响，为基础设施选择提供更可靠的依据。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [384] [Anatomy of a Query: W5H Dimensions and FAR Patterns for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05525)
*Vicki Stover Hertzberg,Eduardo Valverde,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST框架用于评估文本到SQL系统，包含FAR结构不变量（Filter-Aggregate-Return）和W5H语义维度框架。通过120,464条查询验证发现：FAR符合性普遍成立，但W5H维度分布差异显著，医疗领域查询高度集中于时间和人物维度，因果推理和机制性推理几乎为零，表明当前系统仍缺乏真正的机器推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据库接口日益普及，但评估和设计这些系统的理论基础仍然薄弱。现有系统在实际应用中表现出色，但缺乏系统性的理论框架来理解和比较其能力边界。

Method: 提出QUEST框架，包含两个独立动机的组成部分：(1) FAR结构不变量——每个格式良好的查询都可归约为Filter、Aggregate、Return操作；(2) W5H维度框架——所有过滤条件映射到六个语义维度（Who、What、Where、When、Why、How）。在5个文本转SQL数据集（n=120,464）上进行验证。

Result: FAR符合性在所有领域和模式类型中普遍成立；W5H维度分布存在显著差异。医疗查询强烈集中于时间维度（WHEN: 80.4%）和人物维度（WHO: 73.0%），远超高领域基准；因果（WHY）和机制性（HOW）推理在所有数据集中几乎为零，表面上的HOW例外实际上反映的是定量聚合而非真正的程序性推理。

Conclusion: 研究识别出通向真正结构化数据机器推理必须跨越的前沿：当前系统虽能处理表层语义转换，但缺乏深层因果和机制推理能力。FAR-W5H框架为评估和设计下一代NLIDB系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to databases have gained popularity, yet the theoretical foundations for evaluating and designing these systems remain underdeveloped. We present QUEST (Query Understanding Evaluation through Semantic Translation), a framework resting on two independently motivated components: the FAR structural invariant, which holds that every well-formed query reduces to Filter, Aggregate, and Return operations; and the W5H dimensional framework, which holds that all filtering criteria map to six semantic dimensions (Who, What, Where, When, Why, and How). Validated across five text-to-SQL datasets (n = 120,464), FAR conformance is universal across all domains and schema types, while W5H dimensional profiles vary substantially. Healthcare queries are strongly concentrated in temporal (WHEN: 80.4%) and person-centric (WHO: 73.0%) dimensions far exceeding general-domain benchmarks, and causal (WHY) and mechanistic (HOW) reasoning are near-zero everywhere, with apparent HOW exceptions reflecting quantitative aggregation rather than genuine procedural reasoning. These results identify a frontier that must be crossed for genuine machine reasoning over structured data.

</details>


### [385] [An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)
*Sicheng Pan,Shuxian Wang,Wesley Zheng,Zirong Zeng,Vijay Sharma,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rulescript是一种与引擎无关的领域特定语言，旨在解决数据库查询计划重写规则开发中的紧耦合、缺乏可移植性和正确性保证等问题。通过关系代数核心语言、显式规则分解和形式化验证，Rulescript实现了"写一次，处处部署"的范式，可轻松移植到不同数据引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询重写系统存在三个核心问题：(1)规则需要手动实现且与执行引擎紧耦合；(2)缺乏形式化正确性保证；(3)开发新引擎需重新实现所有规则，导致巨大工程成本、可移植性受限且易引入新bug。

Method: 采用关系代数启发的核心语言，将规则显式分解为匹配和转换两个阶段，并通过轻量级适配器将核心/自定义操作符映射到目标引擎的操作符。

Result: 成功从Apache Calcite移植33条重写规则，并在CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion两个底层架构显著不同的引擎上自动部署，验证了Rulescript的可移植性和有效性。

Conclusion: Rulescript通过实现"写一次，处处部署"的查询计划重写范式，显著降低了为新数据引擎开发重写规则所需的工程成本，同时通过形式化验证确保了正确性。

Abstract: Logical query plan rewriting transforms a relational database query into an equivalent but more efficient form and is crucial to the performance of database-backed applications. In existing systems, rewrite rules are typically implemented manually, tightly coupled to specific execution engines, and often lack formal correctness guarantees. Consequently, developing a new engine requires reimplementing both legacy and new rules, incurring significant engineering cost, limiting portability, and every new implementation is an opportunity for introducing new bugs.
  We introduce Rulescript, an engine-agnostic domain-specific language (DSL) for developing query rewrite rules. Rulescript separates rule definition from execution infrastructure via a relational algebra-inspired core language and an explicit decomposition of rules into matching and transformation phases. Developers express rewrites by pattern-matching query plans using Rulescript's core operators and constructing semantically equivalent transformed plans, with all rewrites automatically verified formally to ensure correctness. Rulescript is extensible: users can define custom operators in terms of the core language to capture engine-specific semantics. To integrate with an existing system, developers need only implement a lightweight adapter that maps Rulescript's core and custom operators to the operators implemented in the target engine.
  We evaluate Rulescript by reimplementing 33 rewrite rules from Apache Calcite and extending the language with several custom operators. To demonstrate portability, we automatically deploy these rules to CockroachDB and Apache Data Fusion, two engines with substantially different backends. Our results show that Rulescript enables "write once, deploy everywhere" paradigm for query plan rewriting, with minimal effort required to deploy previously written rules on a new data engine.

</details>


### [386] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SkipDisk，一种磁盘-内存混合的近似最近邻搜索方法，通过设计专用pivot改进下界精度、三层数据修剪减少内存占用、以及异步I/O解耦磁盘访问，在大幅降低内存占用（10%~20%内存）的同时实现了与HNSW相当甚至更低的搜索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于磁盘的ANN方法存在高磁盘访问延迟问题，而现有基于下界的过滤方法过于宽松，无法有效减少磁盘访问，导致搜索性能受限。

Method: 1) 为每个点设计专用pivot以改进三角形不等式的下界，提升过滤效果；2) 采用基于该下界的估计方法；3) 采用三层数据修剪策略在内存中保留关键信息；4) 通过将邻居节点存储在内存中实现异步I/O策略，解耦内存搜索与磁盘访问。

Result: 在约10%内存占用下实现HNSW的85%搜索延迟；在约20%内存占用下实现HNSW的63%搜索延迟。

Conclusion: SkipDisk通过设计专用pivot提高下界精度、采用三层数据修剪策略减少内存占用、引入异步I/O策略解耦内存搜索与磁盘访问，在仅使用HNSW约10%内存的情况下实现其85%的搜索延迟，在约20%内存下实现63%的延迟，兼顾了内存效率与低延迟搜索性能。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering. We further design an estimation-based approach based on this lower bound. Second, to reduce the memory footprint, we employ a three-level data pruning strategy to preserve informative data in memory. Third, to further reduce search latency, we design an asynchronous I/O strategy based on the decoupling of in-memory search and disk access by storing neighbor nodes in memory. Experiments show that our method achieves a latency of 85 of HNSW's latency with approximately 10 memory footprint, and a latency to 63 of HNSW's with a slightly higher memory footprint of around 20.

</details>
